### [1] [FRAGMENT: Factorized Graph Representations for Document Generation and Editing via Entity-Aware Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18679)
*Ayoub El Bouchtili,Guilhaume Leroy-Meline*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FRAGMENT：将结构化文档表示为带类型的关系图，并把分布分解为 p(structure)·p(content|structure)，用两阶段生成（Architect生成拓扑与空间关系，Builder用GATv2填充边界框、文本与视觉样式），可给出文档级似然用于伪造检测，并支持指令条件下的可控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发票、表单、报告、科学文章等结构化文档的语义来自空间布局、文本内容与逻辑结构的交互，而像素级或token级生成模型难以有效捕捉这些依赖关系。

Method: 以带类型的关系图表示文档并做分布因式分解。第一阶段Architect为因果掩码Transformer，以文档类别为条件自回归生成图拓扑与带类型的空间关系；第二阶段Builder为基于GATv2的图注意力网络，为图补充归一化边界框、文本与视觉样式属性。两阶段均为显式似然模型，得到可计算的文档级似然并用作伪造检测的异常分数；另以提示条件扩展，通过交叉注意力将指令嵌入注入Builder实现语义与实体感知的编辑。训练于DocLayNet，并在FUNSD与SROIE上微调。

Result: 在DocLayNet、FUNSD、SROIE上与代表性的自回归、仅布局及图方法基线进行对比评测，给出对该因式化图生成框架特性与权衡的实证分析（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 因式化的图表示与两阶段显式似然建模可统一文档生成、伪造检测与可控编辑三类任务，其性能特点与取舍需通过与自回归、仅布局及图基线的对照实验来刻画。

Abstract: Structured documents such as invoices, forms, reports, and scientific articles derive meaning from the interplay between spatial layout, textual content, and logical structure. Generative models operating at the pixel or token level often struggle to capture these dependencies effectively. We explore FRAGMENT, a generative framework that represents a document as a typed relational graph and factorizes its distribution as p(structure, content) = p(structure) * p(content | structure). The framework consists of two stages. The first stage, the Architect, is a causally masked Transformer conditioned on document category that autoregressively generates the graph topology and typed spatial relations. The second stage, the Builder, is a GATv2-based graph attention network that enriches the graph with normalized bounding boxes, text, and visual style attributes. Both stages define explicit likelihood models, yielding a tractable document-level likelihood that serves as an anomaly score for forgery detection. For controlled editing, a prompt-conditioned extension injects instruction embeddings into the Builder through cross-attention, enabling semantic and entity-aware modifications. We describe training on DocLayNet and fine-tuning on FUNSD and SROIE. Experiments on DocLayNet, FUNSD, and SROIE evaluate FRAGMENT alongside representative autoregressive, layout-only, and graph-based baselines, providing an empirical analysis of the characteristics and trade-offs of the proposed factorized graph generation framework.

</details>


### [2] [SciFigBench: A Benchmark for Scientific Method Figure Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27087)
*Lijia Wu,Zheng Li,Yuxuan Liu,Jingwen Zhang,Penghai Zhao,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SciFigBench基准，用于评估VLM理解科学方法图的三项核心能力（密集内容感知、视觉关系理解、信息流追踪），含4,352张图与40,670条QA；并提出免训练视觉提示模块SciFigCue。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学方法图是读者进入论文方法与核心贡献的直观入口，但其密集布局与复杂信息流使VLM难以解读。现有基准多聚焦粗粒度任务（如基于上下文的检索或整体理解），忽视了复杂方法图内部的细粒度内容与模块间关系。

Method: 构建SciFigBench：从真实论文中筛选4,352张科学方法图，设计专用流水线生成40,670条QA对并经人工校验，将能力操作化为可测量的问答任务，覆盖密集内容感知、视觉关系理解、信息流追踪三方面；同时提出SciFigCue——免训练的视觉提示模块，将箭头隐含的依赖关系外化为视觉关系证据以辅助方法图推理。

Result: 在开源与闭源VLM上的实验表明，即便强模型在视觉关系理解上仍显著受限；SciFigCue在无需参数更新情况下提升了多项任务的表现。

Conclusion: SciFigBench填补了细粒度科学方法图理解的评测空白，揭示当前VLM在模块关系与信息流推理上的短板；SciFigCue验证了以视觉提示注入结构化关系证据的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Scientific method figures often provide researchers with a clear visual entry point into a paper's methodology and core contributions, yet their dense layouts and complex information flows are difficult for Vision Language Models (VLMs) to interpret. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on coarse-grained tasks, such as context-based retrieval or holistic comprehension, overlooking the fine-grained content and inter-module relations within complex methodological diagrams. To address these overlooked aspects, we introduce Scientific Figure Benchmark (SciFigBench), a benchmark for evaluating core capabilities required for scientific method figure understanding. Specifically, it targets three capabilities: dense content perception, visual relation comprehension, and information-flow tracing. For this purpose, we curate 4,352 scientific method figures from real-world research papers and construct 40,670 QA pairs to operationalize these capabilities as measurable question-answering tasks. All annotations are generated by dedicated pipelines and then manually verified. We further propose SciFigCue, a training-free visual prompting module that externalizes arrow-induced dependencies as visual relational evidence for method-figure reasoning. Experiments across open-source and proprietary VLMs show that even strong models remain substantially limited in visual relation comprehension, while SciFigCue improves performance across several tasks without parameter updates. The code and related resources are available at https://github.com/LeNcyer/SciFigBench.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond the Evidence Boundary: Behavioral Mirage Under Progressive Degradation in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28000)
*Edric Castel C. Hao,Rez Samantha Z. Floresca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“以基线为锚”的框架，追踪基线正确样本在渐进图像退化下的表现，定义“边界”与“幻影（mirage）”现象，并发现尽管行为异质，残差流探针可高精度解码边界处的“坚持作答 vs. 弃答”行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM医学图像解读的鲁棒性研究多在干净输入上评估，虽有证据表明高证据损失下性能下降但置信度仍高，却无法判定一个原本可靠的“模型-样本对”何时开始不再可靠。论文希望建立可操作的经验证据边界与可靠性判据。

Method: 构建基线锚定框架：先筛选基线正确样本，再对其施加渐进图像退化，追踪至经验证据边界；将“边界处仍坚持给出答案（无论对错）”定义为mirage。在3个VLM×3个医学数据集上评估，并用残差流探针+嵌套交叉验证解码边界处的commit/abstain行为；同时检验语言可答性与基线正确性、边界承诺的关联。

Result: 仅MedGemma具备足够大的基线正确池用于轨迹分析。VQA-RAD上（唯一问题文本变化的数据集）语言可答性与基线正确性相关（AUROC 0.677），与边界承诺相关（AUROC 0.653–0.670）。边界后行为异质：直接mirage、正确恢复、mirage消解、持续弃答、振荡，各数据集-模态对mirage率16.0%–100.0%。残差流探针在嵌套交叉验证下以AUROC 0.895–0.994解码commit vs. abstain。

Conclusion: 渐进证据损失会导致持续承诺行为，而输出熵与弃答行为无法一致反映该现象；支持面向边界感知的评估、选项顺序控制以及更优弃答机制，以提升部分观测下医学基础模型的可靠性。

Abstract: VLMs for medical image interpretation are typically evaluated on clean inputs, despite the progressive evidence loss that can occur in high stakes clinical settings. Existing robustness studies show that performance can degrade while confidence remains high, but do not establish when a previously reliable model-case pair ceases to behave reliably. We introduce a baseline-anchored framework that traces baseline-correct cases through progressive image degradation to identify an empirical evidence boundary, and define mirage as continued commitment at this boundary, regardless of correctness. Across three VLMs on three medical datasets, only MedGemma provides a sufficiently large baseline-correct pool for trajectory analysis. On VQA-RAD, the only dataset in which the question text varies, language-only answerability is associated with baseline correctness (AUROC 0.677) and with commitment at the boundary (AUROC 0.653 to 0.670). MedGemma's post-boundary behavior is heterogeneous, including direct mirage, correct recovery, mirage dissolution, consistent abstention, and oscillation, with mirage rates of 16.0 to 100.0% across dataset-modality pairs. Despite this behavioral heterogeneity, residual-stream probes decode commit-versus-abstain behavior at the boundary with AUROC of 0.895 to 0.994 under nested cross-validation. These results show that progressive evidence loss can produce persistent commitment that output entropy and abstention behavior do not consistently reflect, motivating boundary-aware evaluation, option-order controls, and improved abstention mechanisms for reliable medical foundation models operating under partial observation.

</details>


### [4] [AnomalyJev: Towards Evaluating Image Anomaly Explanations via Claim-Level Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27565)
*Qizhou Wang,Jiawen Zhu,Guansong Pang,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnomalyJev，用"可验证声明抽取+真值核验"替代 LLM 生成式评判来评估 MLLM 异常检测的解释，在极低开销下达到接近旗舰 LLM 评判者的人类一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 MLLM 的异常检测方法能生成文本解释，但现有评估手段存在瓶颈：LLM-as-judge 虽可用结构化评分量表评价解释，却需大量 token 生成，成本高、难扩展；且评估应更直接地锚定在已有真值上。

Method: 提出 AnomalyJev 轻量框架：从上下文表示中用由真值定义的查询和指针式（pointer-based）预测头抽取可验证声明，全程不做 token 生成；再将声明与对应标注核验，据此为解释的忠实性（faithfulness）与完整性（completeness）打分。

Result: 构建了含 640 条解释、1,600 余条专家评分的人工标注评测集，覆盖域内与跨数据集设置。AnomalyJev 与人类标注者的一致性优于多数开源 MLLM 评判者，接近旗舰 MLLM 评判者，而运行时间与资源消耗仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 将解释评估重构为"声明抽取—真值核验"的判别式任务，可在不牺牲与人类判断一致性的前提下大幅降低评估成本，为 MLLM 解释的可扩展评估提供可行路径。

Abstract: Recent multimodal LLM (MLLM)-based anomaly detection (AD) methods can generate textual explanations that help interpret their predictions. However, reliably and efficiently evaluating these explanations remains challenging. While LLM judges can assess explanations through structured rubrics, they often require substantial token generation, making evaluation costly and difficult to scale. We instead propose to anchor evaluation in the available ground truth by extracting verifiable claims from each explanation and verifying them against the corresponding annotations. We instantiate this idea as AnomalyJev, a lightweight framework that extracts verifiable claims from contextualised representations using ground-truth-defined queries and pointer based heads, without token generation. The claims are then verified against the ground truth to score faithfulness and completeness. For evaluation, we construct a human-annotated set of 640 explanations with over 1,600 expert ratings across in-domain and cross-dataset settings. Despite its compact design, AnomalyJev achieves stronger agreement with human annotators than most open MLLM judges and approaches that of flagship MLLM judges, at a fraction of their runtime and resources.

</details>


### [5] [GTSM: Gaussian Splatting with Thermal State-Space Dynamics for Spatio-Temporal Thermal Field Reconstruction and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26918)
*Yunpeng Zhao,Haoxiang Zhang,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出高斯热状态空间模型（GTSM），从二维观测直接重建并外推四维时空热场，无需预定义3D模型、热力学方程或加热/冷却轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度场重建与预测对工业与动态环境的状态监测、过程分析和状态评估至关重要，但现有方法依赖预定义3D模型、显式热力学方程或已知加热/冷却轨迹，限制了直接由2D观测恢复4D热状态的能力。

Method: GTSM以共享的场景级隐过程与高斯特定的局部热状态表征热演化，用可微RK2积分传播连续动态；作为与骨干无关的热动态模块，可嵌入静态3DGS与动态4DGS，从多视角、多时刻观测推断热演化并连续外推，支持新视角热渲染与未来时刻热状态预测。

Result: 在真实红外序列与合成动态热数据上，重建精度优于代表性基线，时间外推能力显著更强，并验证了GTSM对形变目标的适用性。

Conclusion: GTSM实现了无需先验模型与物理方程的数据驱动4D热场重建与预测，具备骨干无关的通用性，适用于静态与时变几何目标的热状态监测与预测任务。

Abstract: Temperature-field reconstruction and prediction are important for condition monitoring, process analysis, and state assessment in industrial and dynamic environments. This work aims to recover a four-dimensional spatio-temporal thermal state field directly from two-dimensional observations, without requiring a predefined 3D model, prescribed thermodynamic equation, or predefined heating/cooling trajectory. To this end, we propose the Gaussian Thermal State-Space Model (GTSM), which represents thermal evolution through a shared scene-level latent process and Gaussian-specific local thermal states. The resulting continuous dynamics are propagated using differentiable RK2 integration, allowing thermal evolution to be inferred from multi-view and multi-temporal observations and continuously extrapolated beyond the observed interval. GTSM is designed as a backbone-agnostic thermal dynamics module for Gaussian representations and can be integrated with both static 3D Gaussian Splatting and dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting, supporting thermal reconstruction and prediction under both static and time-varying target geometry. The reconstructed representation further enables novel-view thermal rendering and future-time thermal-state prediction. Experiments on real infrared sequences and synthetic dynamic thermal data demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy and substantially stronger temporal extrapolation than representative baselines, while also validating the applicability of GTSM to deforming targets.

</details>


### [6] [Beckmann World Models: Direct Terminal-Map Learning for One-Step Action-Conditioned Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26903)
*Adam Lee,Shobhit Agarwal,June Pyo Suh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Beckmann World Models（BWM），将 Beckmann 传输模型的终端映射守恒律迁移到动作条件视频预测，训练时直接从噪声–数据对学习，并通过端点对齐训练弥补插值一致性与纯噪声推理之间的差距，实现高效训练与准确一步视觉预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型依赖准确的一步预测器进行规划与策略评估，但训练此类预测器需要大量计算，且通常依赖预训练的生成式教师模型。

Method: 学习从噪声、视觉历史与动作到未来帧的直接映射，无需预训练生成教师；将 Beckmann 传输模型的终端映射守恒律适配到动作条件视频预测，直接由噪声–数据对训练，避免 DriftWorld 所需的条件生成样本群；引入端点对齐训练直接监督源预测，强调运动与空间结构，并教会模型从生成历史中恢复；所有组件训练同一映射，每个未来 chunk 仅需一次网络调用；并扩展到潜空间（RT-1、Bridge-V2）。

Result: 在 PushT 与 Robomimic Can 上，64 帧预测 MSE 相较同骨干回归基线分别降低 68% 与 57%；策略评估中平均 IoU 误差最低（0.0100），PushT 四帧预测耗时 24.8 ms；Robomimic Can 上采用运动监督与生成历史的 BWM 阶段相比局部一步 DriftWorld 吞吐高 3.3 倍、峰值内存低 78%；等更新对照显示潜空间下生成历史中的特征监督提升感知预测。

Conclusion: 终端映射学习能够在保持每 chunk 一次网络调用的前提下，将高效训练与准确的一步视觉预测结合起来。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models support planning and policy evaluation by predicting future states. However, learning an accurate one-step predictor can require substantial training computation. We introduce Beckmann World Models (BWM), which learn a direct map from noise, visual history, and actions to future frames without a pretrained generative teacher. We adapt the terminal-map conservation law of Beckmann Transport Models to action-conditioned video prediction, training directly from noise-data pairs without conditional populations of generated samples required by DriftWorld. However, transport consistency on interpolated inputs does not ensure accurate prediction from the pure noise used at inference. We address this gap with endpoint-aligned training that directly supervises the source prediction, emphasizes motion and spatial structure, and teaches recovery from generated histories. All components train the same map and retain one network call per future chunk. On PushT and Robomimic Can, the final model reduces 64-frame prediction MSE by 68% and 57%, respectively, relative to same-backbone regression baseline. For policy evaluation, BWM achieves the lowest mean IoU error among the evaluated models, 0.0100, at 24.8 ms per four-frame PushT prediction. On Robomimic Can, the BWM stage trained with motion supervision and generated histories achieves 3.3 times higher throughput and 78% lower peak memory than local one-step DriftWorld. In a latent-space extension to RT-1 and Bridge-V2, equal-update controls show that feature supervision under generated histories improves perceptual prediction. These findings show how terminal-map learning can combine efficient training with accurate one-step visual prediction.

</details>


### [7] [PerQ: Inverse Generative Modeling for Neural Image Compression via Quantization Error Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27374)
*Tianhao Peng,Ho Man Kwan,Fan Zhang,Shan Liu,Dave Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerQ将感知重建重构为有界支撑的逆向生成建模，把生成采样限制在码本诱导的量化单元内，通过为冻结的多码率失真优化骨干附加码率条件流匹配补偿器，在保持PSNR竞争力的同时达到生成式编解码器的LPIPS/FID水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经图像编解码器存在像素保真与感知质量的根本权衡：失真优化编解码器低码率下重建模糊；生成式编解码器感知质量好但高码率下PSNR饱和，且不同码率目标需分别训练模型。

Method: 将感知重建重构为有界支撑的逆向生成建模。把量化单元定义为产生相同传输符号的潜变量集合，推导传输界证明该单元内重建结果与编解码器输出保持在由码率决定的半径内。据此在编解码器诱导的量化单元内（而非无约束图像先验）进行生成式重建。实现上在冻结的多码率失真优化骨干上附加码率条件流匹配补偿器。

Result: 在Kodak和CLIC2020上，LPIPS与FID与MS-ILLM等生成式编解码器相当，PSNR相对ELIC等失真优化编解码器保持竞争力；单一模型检查点支持宽码率范围，解码快速；补偿器在三种不同骨干上均带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 以量化单元约束生成采样的流匹配补偿方案，可解耦感知增强与骨干编码性能，兼顾保真与感知、统一多码率且具骨干通用性。

Abstract: Neural image codecs face a fundamental trade-off between pixel fidelity and perceptual quality. Distortion-optimized codecs preserve pixel-wise fidelity but produce blurred reconstructions at low bitrates, whereas generative codecs leverage learned priors to improve perceptual quality but tend to exhibit saturating PSNR at higher bitrates and require separate models for different bitrate targets. We propose PerQ, which reframes perceptual reconstruction as inverse generative modeling with bounded support. Specifically, we define the quantization cell as the set of latents that yield the same transmitted symbols and derive a transport bound showing that reconstructions from this cell remain within a rate-determined radius of the codec output. This motivates performing generative reconstruction within the codec-induced quantization cell, rather than sampling from an unconstrained image prior. PerQ implements this principle by attaching a rate-conditioned flow-matching compensator to a frozen, multi-rate distortion-optimized backbone, enabling perceptual enhancement while preserving the backbone's coding performance. Experiments on Kodak and CLIC2020 show that PerQ achieves perceptual performance comparable to generative codecs such as MS-ILLM in LPIPS and FID, while maintaining competitive PSNR relative to distortion-optimized codecs such as ELIC. A single model checkpoint supports a wide bitrate range with fast decoding, and the compensator provides consistent gains across three different backbones. The implementation is available at https://jasminepp.github.io/PerQ/.

</details>


### [8] [What Not to Erase: Saliency-Constrained Augmentation for Low-Resource Handwritten Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27309)
*Paraskevi Platanou,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究低资源历史手写字符识别(HCR)中显著图引导的数据增强，提出解释一致性指标ECI，发现显著图约束增强可提升识别性能且不唯一依赖精度，无单一显著策略始终最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源历史材料中的OCR/HCR常依赖数据增强，但增强策略几乎只用预测精度评估，无法判断性能提升是否伴随显著图（saliency）稳定性。在字形多变、部分退化的历史HCR任务中，精度相近的模型可能在显著模式稳定性上存在差异，因此需要解释感知的评估维度。

Method: 将显著图从仅作事后解释转为用于定义增强变体：对字符图像中视觉信息丰富区域进行扰动、保留或约束。比较经典文档风格变换、随机空洞（random lacunae）及多种显著约束空洞变体，并提出解释一致性指数（ECI）量化受控扰动下显著模式的稳定性。

Result: 显著约束增强可在经典增强基础上提升识别性能，但没有任何单一显著策略始终最优；最优约束形式随架构与语料而变；在某些设置下即使预测性能相近，显著约束训练仍能提升解释一致性。

Conclusion: 显著图更适合作为控制增强的机制而非固定的增强策略；仅用预测指标会掩盖模型在扰动下的敏感性差异，解释一致性应作为互补的评估维度。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) and handwritten character recognition (HCR) in low-resource historical material collections often rely on data augmentation, yet augmentation strategies are typically evaluated almost exclusively through predictive accuracy. This leaves open whether a model's improved performance is accompanied by saliency stability. This question is important in low-resource historical HCR, where recognition is performed on variable and partially degraded letterforms and two models with similar predictive performance may differ in how consistently their saliency patterns respond to such variation. We therefore study saliency-guided augmentation as an explanation-aware strategy for low-resource HCR. Rather than treating saliency maps only as post-hoc interpretations, we use them to define augmentation variants that perturb, preserve, or constrain visually informative regions of character images. We compare classical document-style transformations, random lacunae, and several saliency-constrained lacuna variants. To evaluate the effect of augmentation beyond accuracy, we introduce the Explainability Consistency Index (ECI), a metric that quantifies the stability of saliency patterns under controlled perturbations. We find that saliency-constrained augmentation can improve recognition over classical augmentation, but no single saliency strategy is consistently best. The preferred form of the constraint varies with both architecture and corpus, indicating that saliency is better treated as a mechanism for controlling augmentation than as a fixed augmentation strategy. In some settings, saliency-constrained training improves explanation consistency even when predictive performance is similar. This shows that predictive metrics alone can obscure differences in model sensitivity under perturbation, motivating explanation consistency as a complementary evaluation dimension.

</details>


### [9] [Long-wave Infrared Polarimetric Remote Sensing Image Fusion for Vehicle Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27018)
*Wenjuan Zhang,Xiangrui Peng,Zhen Li,Caimeng Yi,Xuesong Ma,Jianwei Wang,Jie Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Mamba的红外偏振遥感图像融合模型，结合物理先验与双向状态空间扫描融合S0与DoLP，抑制偏振噪声并服务车辆检测，同时构建了AIR-LWIR-Pol数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外遥感图像中车辆轮廓模糊、对比度低，限制检测性能；现有融合方法易引入偏振噪声，且未能有效整合互补信息。红外偏振信息反映表面材质属性与几何结构，可为红外强度提供互补信息。

Method: 1) 融合长波红外总强度S0与线偏振度DoLP，借助物理启发的偏振响应代理与空间结构连续性提取引导特征；2) 沿水平、垂直及两条对角线方向进行双向状态空间扫描，实现全图跨模态特征融合；3) 联合引入先验引导的多窗口结构相似性、重建约束及偏振显著性相对下界损失，在抑制噪声的同时保持全局背景结构与目标偏振特征；4) 构建AIR-LWIR-Pol数据集（1901对精确配准图像，机载分孔径长波红外偏振成像系统采集），并用YOLO11评估下游车辆检测。

Result: 在包括熵和结构相似性在内的四项融合指标上优于对比方法；车辆检测mAP50达55.0%，mAP50-95达29.4%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了将物理先验与状态空间建模相结合用于长波红外偏振图像融合的有效性，可支撑复杂遥感场景下的目标检测应用。

Abstract: Blurred vehicle contours and low contrast limit detection performance in infrared remote sensing imagery, while existing fusion methods may introduce polarization noise and fail to integrate complementary information effectively. To address these limitations, we propose a Mamba-based infrared polarimetric remote sensing image fusion model. Infrared polarization information reflects surface material properties and geometry, providing complementary information to infrared intensity. The proposed method first combines long-wave infrared total intensity S0 and the degree of linear polarization (DoLP), using a physics-inspired polarization-response proxy and spatial structural continuity to extract guidance features. It then employs bidirectional state space scanning along horizontal, vertical, and both diagonal orientations to enable cross-modal feature fusion across the entire image. Prior-guided multi-window structural similarity, reconstruction constraints, and a relative lower-bound loss for polarization saliency are jointly introduced to suppress noise while preserving global background structures and target polarization features. In addition, we construct AIR-LWIR-Pol, a dataset of 1,901 accurately registered image pairs acquired using an airborne division-of-aperture long-wave infrared polarimetric imaging system, and evaluate downstream vehicle detection using YOLO11. The experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms the comparison methods on four fusion metrics, including entropy and structural similarity. Vehicle detection mAP50 and mAP50-95 reach 55.0% and 29.4%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining physical priors with state space modeling for long-wave infrared polarization image fusion and support its use for target detection in complex remote sensing scenes.

</details>


### [10] [REST: Reliability-Guided Spatial Transcriptomics Supervision for Gene Expression Prediction from Histopathological HE-Stained Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26988)
*Lanfeng Zhong,Xiangyu Sun,Guixuan Xu,Rongzhao Zhang,Jie Xu,Shaoting Zhang,Guotai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REST框架，利用生物程序库(BPB)实现程序结构化、可靠性引导的ST监督训练，从H&E图像预测分子信息，在基因表达、通路活性和细胞特征预测上取得最优宏相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H&E形态不直接反映分子状态，而空间转录组(ST)可提供空间分辨的基因表达；但不同检测的覆盖度与spot信息量差异导致配对H&E–ST观测对表征学习的可靠性不均，需要按可靠性加权利用ST监督。

Method: 构建由签名、通路和细胞类型基因集组成的生物程序库(BPB)，为ST编码、可靠性估计与分子重建提供共享结构；在此之上提出Spot可靠性对比对齐(SRCA)，基于检测层面程序覆盖度与spot层面活性离散度度量每个spot的可靠性并对H&E–ST对齐重新加权；以及生物信息引导的程序重建(BIPR)，用可学习的程序查询从组织形态重建成员基因表达。

Result: 训练语料含209个样本(407,475个spot，覆盖16个器官)，评估使用70个互斥样本(230,056个spot，覆盖8个器官)；在基因表达、通路活性与细胞特征预测任务上，REST的Macro相关优于现有ST监督训练方法，并超过现有病理基础模型。

Conclusion: 以生物程序为共享结构、按spot可靠性加权ST监督，可有效提升H&E图像驱动的分子预测；代码已开源。

Abstract: Histopathology is central to cancer diagnosis, but morphology in Hematoxylin and Eosin (HE)-stained images does not directly reveal the molecular states that characterize disease heterogeneity and inform tumor characterization, prognostic assessment, and biomarker discovery. Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) measures spatially resolved gene expression, providing molecular information within tissue architecture, yet differences in assay coverage and spot informativeness make paired HE--ST observations unequally reliable for representation learning. We propose Reliability-Guided Spatial Transcriptomics (REST) supervision with Biological Program Bank (BPB), a program-structured and reliability-guided ST-supervised training framework for predicting molecular information from H\&E images. REST constructs BPB from curated signature, pathway, and cell-type gene sets to provide a shared structure for ST encoding, reliability estimation, and molecular reconstruction. Guided by BPB, Spot-Reliability Contrastive Alignment (SRCA) measures the reliability of each spot from assay-level program coverage and spot-level activity dispersion, and reweights HE--ST alignment accordingly. Biology-Informed Program Reconstruction (BIPR) further uses learnable program queries to reconstruct member-gene expression from histological morphology. REST's training corpus comprised 209 samples (407,475 spots across 16 organs); evaluation used 70 disjoint samples (230,056 spots across eight organs) for gene-expression, pathway-activity, and cell-signature prediction. REST achieved the highest Macro correlation among state-of-the-art ST-supervised training methods and outperformed existing pathology foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/lanfz2000/REST.

</details>


### [11] [Gaze2HOI: Gaze-Aware Intention Disambiguation for Egocentric Hand-Object Interaction Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27877)
*Chuanyang Ma,Chenye Wang,Yixuan Wang,Huiling Zhou,Xingqun Qi,Muyi Sun,Man Zhang,Caifeng Shan,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaze2HOI 提出一种融合注视线索的分层手-物交互预测框架，将预测分解为意图推断、接触定位与耦合运动生成，并构建 EgoAIM 阶段化基准，取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接从历史观测预测手-物交互，意图与接触几何被隐式化；而相似的观测历史可能对应语义或几何上迥异的未来，存在语义歧义与几何歧义。行为学证据表明注视可提供意图的早期线索。

Method: 将手-物交互预测建模为分层问题：(1) 按注视强度与时间近因加权注视线索，经 Temporal Gaze Saliency Modulation (TGSM) 调制 VLM 视觉表征以降低意图推断的语义歧义；(2) Anticipatory Contact Grounding (ACG) 模块将结构化意图映射为显式的接触场、支撑场与放置场，缓解几何歧义；(3) 以这些显式条件驱动扩散模型联合生成物体 6-DoF 轨迹与双手未来关节运动。同时构建 EgoAIM 阶段化基准，按手-物关系演化划分四阶段并提供细粒度意图标注。

Result: 大量实验表明 Gaze2HOI 在意图推断与运动预测上均达到SOTA，并在特定交互阶段接近 oracle 级端到端性能。

Conclusion: 显式引入注视先验并将交互预测分层分解为意图—接触—运动，可有效缓解歧义并提升预测性能，为 AR/VR 与具身场景的主动式辅助提供支持。

Abstract: Anticipating how users will interact with their surroundings is essential for proactive systems that provide timely assistance in AR, VR, and embodied applications. Prior work typically forecasts hand-object interactions directly from historical observations, leaving intention and contact geometry implicit. However, similar observation histories can precede semantically or geometrically distinct futures. Inspired by behavioral evidence that gaze can provide early cues for underlying intention, we propose Gaze2HOI, a gaze-aware framework that formulates hand-object interaction forecasting as a hierarchical problem comprising intention inference, contact grounding, and coupled hand-object motion generation. Specifically, Gaze2HOI first weights gaze cues by fixation strength and recency, and uses them to modulate the visual representations of a vision-language model (VLM) through Temporal Gaze Saliency Modulation (TGSM), thereby reducing semantic ambiguity in intention inference. Subsequently, an Anticipatory Contact Grounding (ACG) module maps these structured intentions into explicit contact, support, and placement fields, effectively mitigating geometric ambiguity. Driven by these explicit conditions, a diffusion-based model jointly generates the 6-DoF object trajectories and future articulated motion of both hands. To evaluate Gaze2HOI, we further introduce EgoAIM, a phase-aware benchmark that organizes egocentric hand-object interactions into four phases defined by evolving hand-object relations and provides fine-grained intention annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Gaze2HOI achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intention inference and motion forecasting, even approaching oracle-level end-to-end performance in specific interaction phases. Project page: https://gaze2hoi.github.io/.

</details>


### [12] [PixelSR: Pixel-Space Orthogonal Flow Decoupling for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27759)
*Xiang Lv,Kai Zhao,Qiang Wang,Qiao Mo,Chao Zhou,Mingwen Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单阶段像素扩散Transformer——PixelSR，直接在像素空间做真实图像超分，缓解保真度与真实感的跷跷板效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Real-SR方法存在"跷跷板效应"：提升感知真实感会牺牲结构保真度。作者认为该问题并非扩散模型固有，而主要源于主流两阶段框架（VAE压缩+扩散）的架构约束。

Method: PixelSR包含两个核心设计：(1) 像素空间条件扩散（Pixel-space Conditional Diffusion）：融合空间对齐的LR条件、视觉语义引导与轻量纹理细化，绕过VAE压缩，在RGB空间端到端优化；(2) 正交流解耦（Orthogonal Flow Decoupling）：将非对称流目标分解为低秩语义分量与无噪声的正交纹理分量，分别路由至专用分支，实现低相互干扰的解耦优化。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，PixelSR取得有竞争力的性能，并能有效缓解保真度—真实感的跷跷板效应。

Conclusion: 单阶段像素空间扩散Transformer是缓解Real-SR中结构保真与感知真实感权衡的有效路径。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models have recently achieved remarkable progress in Real-world Image Super-Resolution (Real-SR). However, existing methods consistently exhibit a persistent seesaw effect: enhancing perceptual realism compromises structural fidelity. This effect, we argue, is not intrinsic to diffusion models, but largely stems from the architectural constraints of prevailing two-stage frameworks. To address this challenge, we propose PixelSR, a novel single-stage Pixel Diffusion Transformer for Real-SR that operates directly in pixel space. Specifically, PixelSR consists of two core designs: Pixel-space Conditional Diffusion and Orthogonal Flow Decoupling. The former establishes an effective pixel-space restoration framework by integrating spatially aligned LR conditioning, visual semantic guidance, and lightweight texture refinement, thereby bypassing VAE compression and enabling end-to-end optimization directly in RGB space. Complementarily, the latter decomposes the asymmetric flow target into a low-rank semantic component and a noise-free orthogonal texture component. By routing these components to specialized branches, it achieves decoupled optimization with reduced mutual interference. Benefiting from these designs, our PixelSR restores perceptually realistic details while faithfully preserving structural content. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PixelSR not only achieves competitive performance, but also effectively mitigates the fidelity--realism seesaw effect.

</details>


### [13] [Revealing Inter-Channel Modality Discriminability: Channel Selective Interaction Fusion Network for Optical-SAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27757)
*Haojie Li,Yuanfei Huang,Lingfei Song,Jia Li,Lizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通道选择交互融合网络CSIF-Net，通过频域分解、跨注意力通道选择与双分支融合实现SAR-光学选择性跨模态融合，在OGSOD 1.0与M4-SAR上分别取得4.01%与3.18%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR与光学图像在结构与外观信息上互补，但存在显著的跨模态差异，且不加区分的通道交互会引入冗余或冲突特征。由于不同通道编码异质语义且对融合贡献不均，需选择性强化任务相关通道、抑制冗余通道。

Method: CSIF-Net包含三个核心环节：（1）频率分解模块构建频率感知SAR输入，将结构信息与噪声响应分离；（2）跨注意力通道选择模块建模跨模态通道关系，动态划分共享特征与模态特有特征；（3）双分支融合模块对共享特征做跨模态交互与通道重对齐，同时独立增强SAR特有特征。

Result: 在OGSOD 1.0与M4-SAR数据集上，水平框与旋转框检测均达到SOTA，分别较现有方法提升4.01%和3.18%。

Conclusion: 通道级选择性交互能有效利用SAR-光学互补信息并抑制模态干扰，验证了"先分解、再选择、后分支融合"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) and optical imagery provide complementary structural and appearance information for remote sensing object detection. However, substantial cross-modal discrepancies and indiscriminate channel interaction inevitably introduce redundant or conflicting features. Since different feature channels encode heterogeneous semantic information and contribute unequally to cross-modal fusion, selectively highlighting task-relevant channels while suppressing redundant ones is essential. To address this issue, we propose the Channel Selective Interaction Fusion Network (CSIF-Net), which performs selective channel-level cross-modal interaction. CSIF-Net first employs a frequency decomposition module to construct frequency-aware SAR inputs, facilitating the separation of structural information from noise-related responses. It then models cross-modal channel relationships through a cross-attention channel selection module and dynamically partitions the features into shared and modality-specific components. Finally, a dual-branch fusion module performs cross-modal interaction and channel realignment for shared features while independently enhancing SAR-specific features, thereby exploiting complementary information and suppressing modality interference. Extensive experiments on the OGSOD 1.0 and M4-SAR datasets demonstrate that CSIF-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance in both horizontal and oriented object detection, surpassing the existing methods by 4.01% and 3.18% gains, respectively.

</details>


### [14] [A Principled Optimal Transport Framework for Frame Selection in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27692)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yuhang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 OTFS，将长视频帧选择建模为结构化证据分配问题，用半不平衡熵最优传输把多证据源映射到视频帧，配合精确动态规划做覆盖感知子集抽取，构成免训练两阶段流程，在三个长视频理解基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解受限于视频长度与模型视觉上下文预算的不匹配，帧选择至关重要；现有查询感知方法依赖启发式流程（一维查询条件打分为帧，再做采样/排序/抽取规则），缺乏显式优化目标，难以捕捉复杂推理所需的异质证据。

Method: 从优化视角重构帧选择：将其形式化为结构化证据分配问题，提出最优传输框架 OTFS，将多个证据源映射到视频帧；针对“重要证据应被保留、冗余帧无需吸收质量”的非对称性，采用半不平衡熵正则最优传输目标，并设计高效求解器 OTFS-Sinkhorn；结合覆盖感知子集抽取的精确动态规划，形成免训练两阶段流水线。理论分析表明单源情形下该形式化诱导出帧上的 Gibbs 型分布，Q-Frame 的温度缩放分布为其特例。

Result: 在三个长视频理解基准实验中，OTFS 一致优于强的免训练基线方法。

Conclusion: 将帧选择转化为最优传输驱动的证据分配提供了有原理依据的建模方式，统一并推广了既有启发式方法（如 Q-Frame），且免训练即可取得更优性能；代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-video understanding with multimodal LLMs is fundamentally constrained by the mismatch between video length and the model's limited visual context budget, making frame selection essential for practical reasoning. Existing query-aware methods typically rely on heuristic pipelines: they assign one-dimensional query-conditioned scores to frames and then apply sampling, ranking, or extraction rules to form the final subset. While effective in practice, such methods are generally not derived from an explicit optimization objective and struggle to capture the heterogeneous evidence required for complex reasoning. In this paper, we revisit long-video frame selection from a principled optimization perspective. We formulate it as a structured evidence-allocation problem and propose OTFS, an optimal transport framework that maps multiple evidence sources onto video frames. To reflect the asymmetry of this problem---important evidence should be preserved, whereas redundant frames need not absorb mass---we instantiate this view with a semi-unbalanced entropic optimal transport objective and an efficient solver, OTFS-Sinkhorn. Combined with an exact dynamic program for coverage-aware subset extraction, OTFS yields a practical two-stage training-free pipeline. We further show that, in the single-source case, our formulation induces a Gibbs-type distribution over frames, making Q-Frame's temperature-scaled distribution a special case of our framework while also providing a broader perspective for understanding related prior methods. Experiments on three long-video understanding benchmarks show that OTFS consistently outperforms strong training-free baselines. Codes are available at https://github.com/barry664/OTFS.

</details>


### [15] [NeoWorld-3: From Pixels to Code-Defined Physical Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27690)
*Eachen Soong,Zijian Niu,Haoran Xu,Yihong Dong,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeoWorld-3 是一个编码智能体框架，通过可调、可执行的程序从视觉观测重建可交互的 3D 世界，实现从物体级到场景级的物理就绪重建，并完成 Real-Sim-Real 闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒式 3D 生成只能达到视觉真实感，缺乏物理可交互性，无法满足仿真就绪的世界重建需求。

Method: 核心组件 NeoSDK 提供底层几何、部件组合、可微参数优化与物理验证的计算引擎；NeoMCP 将其封装为结构化、可工具调用的 API，供视觉语言智能体进行视觉检查、代码编辑与程序迭代精修；采用分层 coordinator-worker 架构，从独立铰接物体扩展至复杂组合场景。

Result: 在物体级与场景级重建基准上取得最优的几何保真度与物理就绪性；在重建数字孪生中规划的抓取操作轨迹可在真实双臂机器人上零样本执行成功。

Conclusion: 基于代码智能体与可执行程序范式的重建方法能有效弥合视觉生成与物理仿真之间的鸿沟，支持真实机器人零样本迁移，验证了 Real-Sim-Real 闭环的可行性。

Abstract: Simulation-ready world reconstruction demands physical interactivity beyond visual realism, which existing black-box 3D generation fails to deliver. We present NeoWorld-3, a coding-agent framework that reconstructs interactive 3D worlds from visual observations via tunable, executable programs. At its core, NeoSDK provides the underlying computational engine for low-level geometries, part composition, differentiable parameter refinement, and physics validation. At the same time, NeoMCP exposes these capabilities as structured, tool-callable APIs for vision-language agents to visually inspect, edit code, and iteratively refine programs. Through a hierarchical coordinator-worker architecture, the framework scales seamlessly from standalone articulated objects to complex compositional scenes. Evaluations across object-level and scene-level reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate that NeoWorld-3 achieves state-of-the-art geometric fidelity and physical readiness. Crucially, manipulation trajectories planned within our reconstructed twins achieve successful zero-shot execution on a physical dual-arm robot, completing the Real-Sim-Real loop.

</details>


### [16] [PACE-MoE: Adaptive Domain Sampling and Domain-Specific LoRA for Medical Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27656)
*Zhenxuan Wang,Jisheng Dang,Ronghao Lin,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Wenjun Ma,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PACE-MoE框架，将策略引导的自适应域采样（PACE）与域特定LoRA适配器结合，用于多域医学视觉定位的后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多域后训练策略依赖固定域采样与共享参数更新，忽视了"哪些域需要训练"和"更新施加在何处"两方面的自适应性需求。

Method: PACE基于当前策略反馈动态评估各影像域的训练价值，通过域准入、域回放与最终全域平衡逐步调整域覆盖；同时使用域特定LoRA适配器将更新路由至对应影像域，冻结预训练骨干与视觉编码器；不改动原始奖励函数与GRPO目标。

Result: 在七个医学影像域上，相比固定域训练持续提升定位性能，平均box IoU相对Vanilla基线提升3.64%；在相同域特定适配器架构下，仅自适应采样即带来2.07%的平均box IoU提升。

Conclusion: 协同的数据—参数路由是多域医学视觉定位的有效策略。

Abstract: Medical visual grounding requires models to align clinical descriptions with spatial evidence across heterogeneous imaging domains. However, existing multi-domain post-training strategies typically rely on fixed domain sampling and shared parameter updates, overlooking the need to adapt both which domains receive training and where their updates are applied. To address this issue, we propose PACE-MoE, a policy-guided framework that jointly performs adaptive domain sampling and domain-specific adaptation for medical visual grounding. PACE dynamically assesses the training value of each imaging domain from current-policy feedback and progressively adjusts domain coverage through domain admission, replay, and final all-domain balancing. Meanwhile, domain-specific LoRA adapters route updates to the corresponding imaging domains while preserving the pretrained backbone and visual encoder. This coordinated design aligns training-data allocation with parameter adaptation without modifying the original reward formulation or Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective. Experiments across seven medical imaging domains show that PACE-MoE consistently improves grounding performance over fixed-domain training, achieving a 3.64% relative improvement in mean box IoU over the Vanilla baseline. Under matched domain-specific adapter architectures, adaptive sampling alone further improves mean box IoU by 2.07%, demonstrating the benefit of policy-guided domain allocation. These results establish coordinated data-parameter routing as an effective strategy for multi-domain medical visual grounding.

</details>


### [17] [Text-Anchored Modality Experts with Uncertainty-Guided Routing for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27571)
*Xiaohua Huang,Bingrui Lei,Xiuyan hao,Ling Zhou,Yuan Zong,Wenming Zheng,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAME框架，将多模态情感分析重构为"以语言为先验、由不确定性引导的非言语证据校准"范式：文本作为固定激活的共享专家，音-文/视-文分支作为动态路由的辅助专家，并用轻量Prophet Heads量化模态不确定性以驱动路由聚合，在三个数据集上取得多数指标SOTA且大幅降低参数量与计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法将各模态视为对称，忽视了语言提供主导语义先验、而非言语信号仅具互补性且易受噪声干扰的事实，导致融合时噪声模态可能污染语义判断。

Method: 采用非对称Mixture-of-Modality-Experts结构：文本特征作为固定且始终激活的共享专家，音-文与视-文跨模态分支作为可动态路由的辅助专家；引入轻量Prophet Heads估计各模态的样本级预测不确定性，并将该不确定性注入模态路由器，实现可靠性感知的专家聚合与融合。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI与CH-SIMS上，TAME在多数指标上达到SOTA；相比MoE基线，参数量最多减少78.7%，FLOPs最多减少41.2%。

Conclusion: 非对称专家构建结合不确定性引导路由，是多模态情感分析中兼顾性能与效率的有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to understand user-generated videos by integrating multiple modalities. Existing methods often treat modalities as symmetric, overlooking the fact that language provides a dominant semantic prior while nonverbal signals are complementary and noise-prone. To address this, we propose a Text-Anchored Modality Experts with Uncertainty-Guided Routing, termed TAME, that reformulates MSA as an uncertainty-guided refinement paradigm, where the language prior is calibrated and enhanced by selectively trusted nonverbal evidence. Specifically, TAME adopts an asymmetric Mixture-of-Modality-Experts framework, in which textual features act as a fixed and always-activated shared expert, while audio-textual and video-textual cross-modal branches serve as dynamically routed auxiliary experts. Furthermore, we introduce lightweight Prophet Heads to quantify the sample-level predictive uncertainty of each individual modality, and this uncertainty metric is further incorporated into the modality router to realize reliability-aware expert aggregation and fusion. Experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS datasets demonstrate that TAME achieves state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics while reducing parameter count by up to 78.7% and FLOPs by up to 41.2% over the mixture-of-expert baseline. %These results confirm that asymmetric expert construction with uncertainty-guided routing is an effective paradigm for MSA.

</details>


### [18] [CityPaint: A Free Lunch from 2D Diffusion for City-Scale 3D Texturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27564)
*Yuchen Zou,Lihuang Fang,Siyu Zhu,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CityPaint 提出一种无需城市级3D训练数据的框架，利用2D扩散模型为城市尺度3D环境生成全局一致的纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市尺度3D训练数据稀缺，现有方法只能拼接独立生成资产或依赖合成城市语料学习，牺牲了真实感与全局一致性；而2D图像及其生成模型丰富、蕴含强视觉先验，故希望将2D生成能力迁移到城市级3D纹理生成。

Method: 将几何先验与2D扩散模型的生成能力结合，引入稳健的 top-K 相机烘焙（camera baking）策略进行迭代纹理投影；针对2D到3D投影产生的伪影，提出解耦优化地形与建筑纹理的细化机制。

Result: 大量实验表明，CityPaint 生成的纹理具有全局连贯性与精细局部细节，在视觉质量和跨视角一致性上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 2D扩散模型可作为城市尺度3D纹理生成的有效先验来源，配合 top-K 相机烘焙与解耦细化可缓解投影伪影，实现无需城市级3D数据的全局一致纹理生成。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity, city-scale 3D environments plays a crucial role in applications such as virtual reality, gaming, and urban digital twins. However, the scarcity of real-world city-scale 3D training data forces existing methods to either stitch independently generated assets or learn from synthetic urban corpora, often sacrificing realism and global consistency. In contrast, 2D image data and their corresponding generative models are abundant and encode rich visual priors. To bridge this gap, we propose CityPaint, a novel framework that leverages 2D diffusion models to generate globally consistent city-scale 3D textures without relying on city-scale 3D training data. Specifically, CityPaint integrates geometric priors with the generative power of 2D diffusion models, introducing a robust top-$K$ camera baking strategy for iterative texture projection. To address the artifacts of 2D-to-3D projection, we further propose a decoupled refinement mechanism that separately optimizes terrain and building textures. Extensive experiments show that CityPaint produces globally coherent textures with fine local details and achieves favorable results against strong baselines in both visual quality and cross-view consistency.

</details>


### [19] [HealBench: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Psychological Comfort in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27516)
*Junjie Xu,Yuzhe Yang,Rui Yang,Luwei Xiao,Wenxin Wu,Xingjiao Wu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HealBench：面向"心理舒适感"的3万图文对细粒度基准与HealBench-Eval评测集，揭示视觉质量与情感疗愈性之间的gap，并验证HealScorer可作为RL奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图与图像编辑研究已在照片级真实感、语义保真与美学上取得进展，但"输出是否令人心理上感到舒适/疗愈"这一以人为中心的维度仍缺乏研究。作者基于认知评价理论，将心理舒适定义为观看者对修复性与情感支持性视觉线索进行评价后产生的情绪反应，并指出该维度缺少基准与量化手段。

Method: 构建HealBench：从10万候选中，经视觉语言模型匹配、人类偏好模型、专家过滤与样例引导增强的人机协同流程，筛选出3万高质量图文对，覆盖19大类、92子类；另构建1,095例特征聚类的HealBench-Eval评测集。在此基础上对14个代表性生成模型及提示词改写策略进行客观与主观评测，并开展强化学习实验检验HealScorer作为奖励信号的可行性。

Result: 评测显示传统视觉质量与感知舒适度之间存在明显落差：强语义对齐与高美学并不能保证图像具备情感修复性；提示词改写策略亦难以弥合该差距。强化学习实验表明HealScorer可作为奖励信号，用于评估并提升生成图像的情感与修复质量。

Conclusion: 论文将"心理舒适感"确立为人本图像生成的独立评价维度，并将HealBench定位为情感感知生成模型的研究基础，为后续情感导向的生成与对齐工作提供数据与评测支撑。

Abstract: Despite advances in photorealism, semantic fidelity, and aesthetics, text-to-image generation and image editing remain understudied in a human-centered dimension: whether their outputs feel psychologically comforting. Grounded in cognitive evaluation theory, we define psychological comfort as an affective response shaped by viewers' appraisal of restorative and emotionally supportive visual cues. We introduce HealBench, a fine-grained benchmark of 30,000 high-quality image-text pairs spanning 19 categories and 92 subcategories, selected from 100,000 candidates through a human- and expert-in-the-loop pipeline combining vision-language model matching, human preference models, expert filtering, and exemplar-guided augmentation. We also construct HealBench-Eval, a feature-clustered evaluation set of 1,095 instances. Using HealBench-Eval, we conduct objective and subjective evaluations of 14 representative image generation models and prompt-rewriting strategies. Results reveal a gap between conventional visual quality and perceived comfort: strong semantic alignment and aesthetics do not guarantee emotionally restorative images. Reinforcement learning experiments further show that HealScorer can serve as a reward signal for assessing and improving the emotional and restorative quality of generated images. These findings establish psychological comfort as a distinct dimension of human-centered image generation and position HealBench as a foundation for affect-aware generative models.

</details>


### [20] [Progressive Subset Selection and Cascaded Consistency for Unsupervised Vision-Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27515)
*Hao Yan,Guanghui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级无监督PVLM适配框架，结合渐进式基于margin的子集选择与级联一致性学习，仅更新归一化参数与文本原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型（PVLM）零样本识别强，但在目标域偏移下性能下降。无监督适配无需标注数据，但伪标签不可靠且缺乏带标签验证集用于模型选择/早停。

Method: 1）基于预测margin构造类别平衡的伪标签子集，并随训练逐步扩展，从可靠监督锚点起步再纳入更广目标域数据；2）级联一致性学习：用弱增强视图上时间平滑的教师预测监督强增强视图的学生预测，并以置信度过滤降低不确定伪标签影响；3）为保持预训练嵌入结构，仅更新视觉编码器的归一化参数及由文本提示初始化的类原型。

Result: 在多个识别基准上，使用CLIP与SigLIP骨干均相较零样本基线取得一致提升，并与近期无监督PVLM适配方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 渐进式伪标签选择与教师引导一致性是无标注目标数据条件下适配PVLM分类器的有效且轻量策略。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language Models (PVLMs) achieve strong zero-shot recognition but often suffer performance degradation under target-domain shift. Unsupervised adaptation provides a practical way to improve target-domain performance without labeled data, but it remains challenging because pseudo-labels generated by the model can be unreliable and no labeled validation set is available for model selection or early stopping. We propose a simple framework for unsupervised PVLM adaptation that combines progressive margin-based subset selection with cascaded consistency learning. The subset selection strategy constructs a class-balanced pseudo-labeled set using prediction margins and gradually expands it during training, enabling adaptation to start from reliable supervision anchors before incorporating broader target-domain data. Cascaded consistency learning further exploits unlabeled samples by using temporally smoothed teacher predictions on weakly augmented views to supervise student predictions on strongly augmented views, with confidence filtering to reduce the influence of uncertain pseudo-labels. To preserve the pretrained embedding structure, the framework updates only normalization parameters in the visual encoder and class prototypes initialized from text prompts. Experiments across multiple recognition benchmarks with both CLIP and SigLIP backbones show consistent improvements over zero-shot baselines and competitive performance against recent unsupervised PVLM adaptation methods. These results suggest that progressive pseudo-label selection and teacher-guided consistency provide an effective and lightweight strategy for adapting PVLM classifiers without labeled target data.

</details>


### [21] [Assessing Representation Semantics Under Language Pretraining in Fine-Grained Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28051)
*Md Raisul Kibria,Sébastien Lafond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个由可解释性驱动的紧凑框架，在CUB-200上对比"仅分类"与"图像描述生成"两种监督所塑造的视觉表征，用显著图多指标量化其结构与定位差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭下游准确率难以刻画细粒度、少数据场景下监督信号对语义视觉表征的结构性影响，需要超越准确率的表征评估手段。

Method: 采用受控两阶段协议，使两种监督训练的编码器最终都优化于同一分类任务，从而可直接比较表征；用attention rollout与gradient-weighted rollout生成显著图，并以五项指标评估：忠实性（正/负扰动）与定位性（关键点强调、背景归因、Earth Mover's Distance）；跨随机种子与多种解释方法验证，并在Oxford-IIIT Pet上做消融。

Result: 描述生成监督的表征对显著区域移除更敏感、与标注部件更对齐、更少依赖背景；分类监督的表征对低显著区域移除略更鲁棒。Oxford-IIIT Pet消融在关键指标上呈一致趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架使监督引起的表征差异可观察、可量化，突破仅用下游准确率的评估局限，为表征评估及训练/迁移学习策略比较提供系统化基础。

Abstract: Understanding how supervision signals shape semantic visual representations remains challenging, particularly in fine-grained, low-data settings. Standard evaluations of vision models rely on downstream accuracy, which often fails to capture structural differences in learned representations. We propose a compact explainability-driven framework for comparing encoders trained with classification-only and caption-generation supervision on CUB-200. Using a controlled two-stage protocol in which both encoders are ultimately optimized for the same classification task, we enable a direct comparison of the resulting representations. Saliency maps are generated using attention rollout and gradient-weighted rollout and evaluated with five metrics covering faithfulness (positive and negative perturbation) and localization (keypoint emphasis, background attribution, and Earth Mover's Distance). Across random seeds and explanation methods, caption supervision yields representations that are more sensitive to the removal of salient regions, better aligned with annotated object parts, and less reliant on background regions, whereas classification supervision produces representations that are slightly more robust to the removal of low-saliency regions. An ablation study on the Oxford-IIIT Pet dataset shows consistent trends across key metrics. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework makes supervision-induced representational differences observable and quantifiable beyond downstream accuracy alone. More broadly, it provides a systematic basis for assessing learned representations and comparing alternative training and transfer-learning strategies.

</details>


### [22] [P$^3$-VLM: A Point-based Alternative for Grounded 3D Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28018)
*Anna-Maria Halacheva,Jan-Nico Zaech,Sombit Dey,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示3D VLM基准中因过度依赖边界框而产生的"语义泄漏"捷径，提出以单点提示替代框提示的point-based grounding协议，并设计无检测器的三通路3D VLM（P³-VLM），在点提示定位描述任务上达52.1% SOTA，且具备更强鲁棒性与OOD泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉语言模型（VLM）的grounding评测与提示协议普遍以边界框作为空间提示或表示原语，而边界框高度暴露物体类别信息，形成"语义泄漏"捷径，使模型在训练中忽略视觉信息。作者希望量化该捷径并消除其影响。

Method: 1）用仅接收框坐标的"盲"LLM验证捷径存在；2）提出point-based grounding prompt协议，即用单个3D点替代框进行查询；3）构建P³-VLM——一种无检测器、以场景为中心、支持3D GS输入的三通路3D VLM（位置感知、任务感知、上下文感知通路），完全不依赖边界框。

Result: 在ScanNet Nr3D上，盲LLM准确率由36.4%（框提示）降至18.0%（点提示），3D VLM（如LL3DA）亦同等退化；P³-VLM在点提示的位置定位描述任务上取得52.1%的SOTA准确率，并在鲁棒性、OOD泛化及非grounding场景中心3D VQA基准上均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 边界框提示是3D VLM评估中被低估的语义捷径来源；采用点提示可有效抑制该捷径。P³-VLM证明，摆脱边界框依赖的点式学习能带来更强的视觉-空间推理能力与跨设定一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Examining existing benchmarks for grounding in 3D VLMs reveals a pervasive "semantic leakage" in the evaluation and prompting protocols: a heavy reliance on bounding boxes as spatial prompts or representation primitives. Because bounding boxes are highly informative of object categories, they introduce a shortcut for the models to neglect visual information during 3D VLM training. We demonstrate this with a "blind" LLM that, prompted only with the box coordinates, can perform surprisingly well. To further analyze this shortcut, we propose point-based grounding prompting - querying models with a single 3D point instead of a box. Under this protocol, the accuracy of the blind LLM drops from 36.4% to 18.0% on ScanNet's Nr3D, and similarly degrades for 3D VLMs (e.g. LL3DA).
  Building on this insight, we introduce P$^3$-VLM, a novel tri-pathway 3D VLM that supports point-based prompts and achieves 52.1% state-of-the-art accuracy on location-grounded captioning under this setting. To achieve this, we redesign the architecture while keeping it detector-free, scene-centric, and suitable for 3D GS inputs. P$^3$-VLM utilizes three complementary pathways (location-, task-, and context-aware), enabling strong reasoning about scenes without any bounding box dependencies. The key benefit of point-based learning is a consistent improvement across settings: P$^3$-VLM achieves state-of-the-art performance under point-based grounding while also improving robustness, OOD generalization, and performance on ungrounded scene-centric 3D VQA benchmarks, reflecting its reduced reliance on bounding-box shortcuts and resulting stronger visual-spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond the Frame: Redundancy, Not Trajectory, Carries the Privacy Leak in Video Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27982)
*Kavyansh Tyagi,Paolo Russo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 逐帧评估会严重低估视频分割类遗忘的泄漏；泄漏主要源于跨帧冗余而非轨迹。PACE用置换不变的池化对抗判别器和双向时序一致性抑制冗余，在VSPW与Virtual KITTI 2上将汇聚攻击者AP显著降低并保持可用精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类遗忘泄漏评估逐帧进行，无法发现攻击者通过多帧输出场平均可重建被遗忘类别的问题，导致泄漏被低估。

Method: 提出两秒片段上输出场平均的汇聚攻击评估；用时间打乱对照实验证明泄漏源于跨帧冗余而非目标轨迹；提出PACE，采用严格置换不变的池化对抗判别器与双向时序一致性目标直接抑制冗余。

Result: VSPW上person与car的汇聚攻击AP降至18.79与12.10，Virtual KITTI 2上car为9.11；后者在保持可用效用的判决安全方法中抑制最强；VSPW car保留19.8 mIoU，而达到相当擦除效果的最佳基线至多8.3。

Conclusion: 多帧汇聚评估揭示了被逐帧评估掩盖的严重泄漏；冗余是核心泄漏源，PACE在擦除强度与保留效用间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Class unlearning for video segmentation aims to ensure that a removed category cannot be reconstructed from a deployed model's outputs. Current leakage evaluation algorithms inspect one frame at a time, and we show that this understates leakage by a wide margin: an attacker that averages a model's output field over a two-second clip recovers the forgotten class from every method we evaluate on VSPW and most on Virtual KITTI~2, including models whose per-frame predictions are visually indistinguishable from a retrain oracle (a model trained from scratch without the forgotten class). We attribute the recoverable signal to cross-frame redundancy rather than object trajectory, establish this with a time-shuffled control that leaves leakage intact, and introduce PACE, an unlearning method that suppresses redundancy directly using an exactly permutation-invariant pooled adversarial discriminator and a two-sided temporal consistency objective. Across VSPW and Virtual KITTI~2, PACE lowers pooled attacker AP to $18.79$ (\texttt{person}) and $12.10$ (\texttt{car}) on VSPW and $9.11$ (\texttt{car}) on Virtual KITTI~2, achieving on the latter the strongest suppression among verdict-safe methods retaining usable utility, while retaining $19.8$ mIoU on VSPW \texttt{car} against at most $8.3$ for any baseline achieving comparable erasure.

</details>


### [24] [Decoupling Action from Egocentric Observation for World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27946)
*Yue Ma,Pengjie Song,Xinyu Wang,Yi He,Zeqian Long,Fangneng Zhan,Kaichen Zhou,Hongyu Liu,Hongfa Wang,Peihao Li,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoACT——一个无需附加几何估计器的测试时框架，直接在视频扩散模型去噪过程中实现自我中心动作迁移，并构建EgoActionBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心动作迁移方法分两类：条件引导的视频生成将观测转为显式几何控制（手部姿态/网格），但几何估计误差导致新场景中交互物理合理性差；运动迁移方法直接提取运动模式，但运动级特征无法编码接触动力学，常出现手部结构崩塌和接触布局不合理。

Method: EgoACT在视频扩散模型去噪过程中直接操作：早期去噪阶段采用速度引导的结构锚定（Velocity-guided Structure Anchoring）稳定参考一致的手-物结构；中后期去噪阶段采用稀疏对应校准（Sparse Correspondence Calibration）精炼可靠的局部对应关系。同时建立EgoActionBench基准，评估动作保持、视觉质量与手-物合理性。

Result: 实验表明，在多样化自我中心操作场景下，EgoACT相比强基线生成更连贯、物理更合理的交互视频。

Conclusion: 通过在去噪过程中注入结构锚定与稀疏对应校准，可在不依赖几何估计的前提下兼顾动作语义保持、时序连贯性与交互真实性，为可扩展的世界模拟与机器人数据生成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Egocentric action transfer aims to decouple the semantic action from egocentric observations and reproduce them in new visual contexts, enabling scalable world simulation, embodied policy learning, and robotic data generation. Existing approaches to transferring actions rely on condition-guided video generation, which converts the observation video into explicit geometric controls (e.g., hand poses or meshes) to drive synthesis. However, these methods introduce geometric estimation errors, making it difficult to preserve physically plausible interactions when transferred to new scenes. Alternatively, motion transfer methods directly extract motion patterns from the observation video, yet motion-level features alone cannot encode rich contact dynamics, frequently leading to severe hand structural collapse and implausible contact layout. To address both limitations, we present EgoACT, a test-time framework for egocentric action transfer that operates directly on the denoising process of video diffusion models without requiring additional geometric estimators. EgoACT uses Velocity-guided Structure Anchoring to stabilize reference-consistent hand-object structure in the early denoising stage, and Sparse Correspondence Calibration to refine reliable local correspondences in the mid-to-late denoising stages. Together, these two components preserve transferable action semantics while improving temporal coherence and interaction realism. We further establish EgoActionBench, a benchmark for evaluating action preservation, visual quality, and hand-object plausibility across diverse egocentric manipulation scenarios. Experiments show that EgoACT generates more coherent and physically plausible interaction videos than strong baselines.

</details>


### [25] [Unified RAW Image Exposure Correction and Restoration via Linear Illumination and Frequency-Adaptive Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28191)
*Yingkai Zhang,Yunhao Zou,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的RAW域曝光校正与复原框架RIECR，将问题分解为线性光照校正与残余退化复原两个耦合子问题，构建223场景/1784对真实配对RAW基准，在16种SOTA方法对比中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误曝光同时损害图像亮度与可恢复的场景细节。现有sRGB方法受非线性ISP管线限制；RAW域方法通常只针对单一退化类型（如低光去噪或高光恢复）。因此需要一个在RAW域直接处理欠曝与过曝的统一框架。

Method: 依据RAW成像模型将问题分解为两个耦合子问题：(1) 线性光照校正网络LICN，利用通道特异性RAW线索估计曝光感知的线性增益——借助G通道高信噪比实现稳健提亮，利用R/B相对未饱和响应实现高光压暗；(2) 双分支频域增强网络FEN，含频率动态卷积模块FDC，将卷积响应分解为低频与高频分量，按曝光上下文自适应抑制放大噪声或恢复被截断纹理。此外构建真实配对RAW基准：223个场景、1784对，覆盖复杂多重曝光错误。

Result: 与16种最先进方法对比，RIECR在真实RAW曝光校正上取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 在RAW域统一建模曝光校正与退化复原是有效路径；曝光感知的通道特异线性增益与频率自适应增强相结合，可同时处理欠曝与过曝场景，并获得优于现有方法的真实数据表现。

Abstract: Incorrect exposure degrades both image brightness and recoverable scene detail. Existing sRGB-based correction methods are limited by the non-linear image signal processing pipeline, whereas RAW-based methods are usually developed for a single degradation type, such as low-light denoising or high-light recovery. In this paper, we focus on joint exposure correction and restoration directly in the RAW domain and propose a unified RAW Image Exposure Correction and Restoration (RIECR) framework for both under- and over-exposed images. RIECR decomposes the problem into two coupled sub-problems that follow the RAW imaging model: linear illumination correction and residual degradation restoration. First, a Linear Illumination Correction Network (LICN) estimates exposure-aware linear gains by using channel-specific RAW cues. It exploits the high signal-to-noise ratio of Green channels for robust brightening and the relatively unsaturated Red/Blue responses for high-light darkening. Second, a dual-branch Frequency-based Enhancement Network (FEN) restores the remaining degradation with a Frequency Dynamic Convolution (FDC) module. FDC decomposes convolutional responses into low- and high-frequency components and adaptively suppresses amplified noise or recovers clipped textures according to the exposure context. We further build a real paired RAW benchmark containing 223 scenes and 1,784 pairs covering complex multi-exposure errors. Experiments against sixteen state-of-the-art methods show that RIECR achieves state-of-the-art performance on real RAW exposure correction.

</details>


### [26] [MatchCityLoc: Aerial Localization in LoD City Models via Surface Normal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27110)
*Oussema Dhaouadi,Zuria Bauer,Johannes Michael Meier,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Cremers,Olaf Wysocki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MatchCityLoc，将无纹理 LoD 城市模型定位从 2D 轮廓重叠评分转向基于法向特征的直接匹配，从含噪 6-DoF 先验出发建立 3D-2D 对应并 PnP 求解，配合 render-and-match 迭代优化，在极少渲染次数下实现高姿态召回与精确平移/旋转估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境下 GNSS 不可靠，视觉定位成为关键补充；无纹理 LoD 城市模型轻量且保护隐私，可替代笨重的带纹理 3D 网格。但基于抽象几何的定位困难，现有流程通常对 2D 轮廓重叠度打分，易受严重几何歧义影响。

Method: 1) 利用零样本基础模型从查询图像中提取表面法向；2) 将图像法向与渲染的 LoD 法向进行匹配，建立显式 3D-2D 对应关系；3) 基于对应关系执行 PnP 位姿求解；4) 通过 render-and-match 循环迭代精化位姿；整个流程以含噪 6-DoF 位姿先验为起点。

Result: 在评估的现成 LoD 方法中，表现出较强的姿态召回率以及准确的平移与旋转精度，且所需渲染次数极少。

Conclusion: MatchCityLoc 弥合了航空影像与无纹理几何之间的模态鸿沟，可借助轻量、隐私保护的城市模型实现精确定位。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) navigation in urban environments requires precise pose estimation. Because Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals are often unreliable in cities, visual localization serves as a crucial complement. Within this domain, textureless Level-of-Detail (LoD) city models provide a lightweight and privacy-preserving substitute for heavy 3D textured meshes. However, localizing against abstract geometry is challenging. Existing pipelines typically score 2D silhouette overlaps, which can suffer from severe geometric ambiguities. We present MatchCityLoc, a framework that shifts this paradigm to direct feature matching from a noisy 6-DoF pose prior. By extracting surface normals from the query image using a zero-shot foundation model and matching them against rendered LoD normals, we establish explicit 3D-to-2D correspondences. This enables a Perspective-n-Point (PnP) pose solve, which we iteratively refine through a render-and-match loop. Experiments demonstrate strong pose recall and accurate translation and rotation among the evaluated off-the-shelf LoD-based methods with very few renderings. By bridging the modality gap between aerial imagery and texture-free geometry, MatchCityLoc enables accurate localization using lightweight, privacy-preserving city models.

</details>


### [27] [OmniVLoc: Topo-metric Visual Localization for Omnidirectional Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27522)
*Zhuo Yu,Shuyang Zhang,Huihua Zhu,Wenjia Ouyang,Zuo Zuo,Jianhao Jiao,Yilong Zhu,Xiaopin Zhong,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniVLoc，一种基于拓扑度量框架的全向相机（环视）定位系统，通过融合全局场景识别与单目深度估计，构建多约束视觉定位范式，实现尺度感知的位姿图求解与球面光束法平差，并配合分层验证机制提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向相机可提供环视信息，显著克服传统视觉定位系统的视场限制；但现有定位系统未能充分利用全向相机的特性，在场景内容变化与光照引起的表观变化下难以实现准确、鲁棒的定位。

Method: 1) 基于 topo-metric 框架构建环视定位系统；2) 集成先进的全局场景识别与单目深度估计方法，提出多约束视觉定位范式；3) 求解尺度感知位姿图（scale-aware pose graph）并进行精确的球面光束法平差（spherical bundle adjustment），恢复查询帧的度量级位姿；4) 设计分层验证机制，在不同阶段过滤错误输出。

Result: 在公开数据集与自建数据集上的大量实验验证了 OmniVLoc 在精度与可靠性上的优越性；源码与数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Conclusion: OmniVLoc 充分发挥全向相机的环视优势，结合全局识别、深度估计与几何优化，在场景与光照变化条件下实现了准确且鲁棒的度量级定位，并通过分层验证机制保障系统整体鲁棒性。

Abstract: Omnidirectional cameras provide surround-view information, significantly overcoming the field-of-view constraints for traditional visual localization systems. However, existing localization systems fail to fully exploit the characteristics of omnidirectional cameras, struggling to deliver accurate and robust localization performance amidst scene content variations and illumination-induced appearance changes. In this work, we propose OmniVLoc, a surround-view localization system based on a topo-metric framework. By integrating state-of-the-art global place recognition and monocular depth estimation methods, we introduce a multi-constrained visual localization paradigm. By solving a scale-aware pose graph and performing precise spherical bundle adjustment, we accurately recover the metric-level pose for each query frame.Furthermore, a hierarchical verification mechanism is designed to filter out erroneous outputs across different stages, effectively bolstering the overall system robustness. Extensive experiments on both public and our custom datasets validate the superior accuracy and reliability of OmniVLoc. To benefit the community, our source code and dataset will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [28] [FocusOVD: Geometric and Contextual Feature Refinement for Open-Vocabulary Small-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27479)
*Hanlin Kou,Beiqi Bai,Li Chen,Youjia Jing,Shupan Li,Mingyuan Jiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FocusOVD，一种不改动冻结 VLM 主干、面向小目标的开放词表目标检测器，通过三个互补模块增强小目标表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词表检测方法在小目标上表现不佳，原因有三：高分辨率细节保留不足、跨层级信息聚合低效、区域级判别性有限。

Method: 在冻结 VLM 主干之外引入三个模块：(1) Reweighted Cross-scale Feature Fusion，学习归一化的层级权重，在特征金字塔聚合时自适应平衡浅层外观细节与深层语义；(2) Residual Pinwheel-shaped Gated Convolution，增强高分辨率特征的局部几何响应；(3) Residual Convolutional Gated Linear Unit，在预测前抑制背景主导的激活、提升 RoI 判别性。

Result: COCO-OVD 上新类 AP 36.3%，分别超过 CORA、UniDetector、SAMP 1.3%、1.1%、1.5%；LVIS-OVD 上总体 AP 33.7%，比最强对比方法高 0.6%。

Conclusion: FocusOVD 在开放词表小目标检测上具备较强泛化能力，验证了在冻结 VLM 前提下改进特征融合与区域判别性的有效性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection aims to detect both seen and unseen categories by leveraging the semantic knowledge of vision-language models. However, existing methods often struggle with small objects due to insufficient preservation of high-resolution details, ineffective information aggregation across feature levels, and limited region-level discriminability. To address these challenges, we propose FocusOVD, an open-vocabulary object detector that enhances small-object representations without modifying the frozen VLM backbone. FocusOVD introduces three complementary components: a Reweighted Cross-scale Feature Fusion module, which learns normalized level-wise weights to adaptively balance shallow appearance details and deep semantic cues during feature pyramid aggregation; a Residual Pinwheel-shaped Gated Convolution module, which strengthens local geometric responses in high-resolution features; and a Residual Convolutional Gated Linear Unit module, which suppresses background-dominated activations and enhances RoI discriminability before prediction. Extensive experiments on COCO-OVD and LVIS-OVD demonstrate the effectiveness of FocusOVD. On COCO-OVD, FocusOVD achieves a novel-category AP of 36.3%, outperforming CORA, UniDetector, and SAMP by 1.3%, 1.1%, and 1.5%, respectively; on LVIS-OVD, it achieves an overall AP of 33.7%, surpassing the strongest competing method by 0.6%. These results demonstrate the strong generalization ability of FocusOVD for open-vocabulary small-object detection.

</details>


### [29] [TokenFlow: Latent Token Rectified Flow Matching for 3D Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27418)
*Linhao Wang,Dongjin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenFlow：以M个潜在token表示形状，用token级rectified flow与点级条件流耦合的分层flow matching框架，兼顾部件级布局与表面细节并保持置换等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜变量生成模型将整个形状编码为单一向量，难以保留部件级结构；而3D点云生成需同时刻画部件布局与表面细节，并保持置换等变性。

Method: 分层flow matching：将形状表示为潜在token集合（M≪N个可学习token，经cross-attention以O(NM)代价聚合全局上下文）；对token施加rectified flow，并耦合一个点级条件flow。

Result: ShapeNet上在airplane、car、chair三类取得最优MMD与coverage，1-NNA与最强基线相当；ModelNet上其潜在集合在ModelNet10取得已报告最佳线性评测精度。

Conclusion: 以潜在token集合表示形状、以O(NM)代价聚合，是3D点云生成的有效表示方式。

Abstract: 3D point clouds generation requires capturing both part-level layout and surface detail while preserving permutation equivariance. Existing latent generative models often encode an entire shape into a single vector, which limits their ability to retain part-level structure. We propose TokenFlow, a hierarchical flow matching framework that represents each shape as a set of latent tokens and couples a rectified flow over these tokens with a conditional flow over points. A bottleneck of $M \ll N$ learnable tokens aggregates global context through cross-attention at $O(NM)$ cost. On ShapeNet, TokenFlow achieves the best MMD and coverage across airplane, car, and chair, while obtaining 1-NNA comparable to the strongest baselines. On ModelNet, its learned latent set reaches the best reported linear-evaluation accuracy on ModelNet10. These results show that representing a shape as a set of latent tokens, aggregated at $O(NM)$ cost, provides an effective representation for 3D point cloud generation.

</details>


### [30] [Generalizing Multimodal Foundational Models in Context-Driven Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27298)
*Avi Gupta,Nilotpal Sinha,Vishnu Raj,Sambuddha Saha,Pratik Joshi,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Tammam Tillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出冻结多模态基础模型（MFM）的视听类增量学习框架，以音频表征作为任务稳定的上下文引导视觉表征，并结合特征级与logit级蒸馏，在多个视听CIL基准上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视听交叉模态下的类增量学习（CIL）研究不足；虽然多模态基础模型（MFM）表征富含静态先验，但初步分析显示其在增量场景下表现不佳，需要一种将冻结MFM泛化到视听CIL的方法。

Method: 利用冻结MFM的稠密音频与视觉表征，设计基于注意力的上下文引导模块，将音频表征作为任务稳定的上下文引导视觉表征以提升增量类识别；同时采用特征级与logit级蒸馏以缓解灾难性遗忘、维持跨任务稳定性。

Result: 跨多个视听CIL基准的广泛评估显示，该方法一致优于现有SOTA；消融表明特征级与logit级蒸馏是降低遗忘的主要贡献来源。

Conclusion: 冻结MFM结合音频引导的上下文引导与双层蒸馏可有效实现视听类增量学习，验证了将多模态基础模型迁移至增量场景的可行性。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to learn new classes continuously without forgetting the previously learned ones. Recent works in CIL have significantly advanced across various modalities; however, at the intersection of audio-visual modality, this problem remains underexplored. While representations from multimodal foundational models (MFMs) encapsulate rich static priors, our preliminary analysis shows that these representations struggle in incremental settings. Motivated by this, we propose a framework that generalizes frozen MFMs to audio-visual CIL. Specifically, we leverage their dense audio and visual representations and employ an attention-based contextual guidance module, in which the audio representations serve as task-stable contextual guidance for the visual representations, enabling effective class recognition in the incremental setting. To further mitigate catastrophic forgetting and preserve stability across tasks, we employ feature- and logit-level distillation, which are primarily responsible for the reduced forgetting. Extensive evaluations across various audio-visual CIL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/avigupta2798/MFM-CIL.

</details>


### [31] [Manifold Prior Guided Deep Unfolding for Hyperspectral Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27874)
*Shuqi Liu,Yingkai Zhang,Lin Gu,Tao Zhang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以两阶段流形学习为核心的深度展开框架，将无退化结构先验注入投影与去噪模块，实现高光谱图像重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度展开方法存在两方面不足：1）数据一致性投影模块采用固定梯度下降步长，忽略HSI的谱段内容自适应性；2）去噪模块或因计算复杂度高、或因感受野受限，难以高效捕获全局空谱依赖。

Method: 提出两阶段流形学习策略获取无退化结构先验，并策略性地注入投影与去噪两个模块：投影模块引入先验引导的谱权重机制，自适应调制各谱段权重；去噪模块提出流形引导的Mamba，通过先验调制实现结构感知的空谱特征提取。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的定量与视觉比较表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持展开框架的结构可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过流形先验引导的自适应投影与结构感知去噪，可在保留展开框架可解释性的前提下显著提升HSI重建性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral Image (HSI) reconstruction has made substantial progress with the deep unfolding framework by decomposing the problem into a projection module and a denoiser module. Nevertheless, existing methods still exhibit limitations of insufficient matching with HSI data. The issues lie in two aspects: 1) the data-consistency projection module applies a fixed gradient descent step while ignoring the spectral content adaptivity of HSI; 2) the denoiser module either suffers from high computational complexity or is limited to the local receptive field, failing to efficiently capture the global spatial-spectral dependencies. In this paper, we propose a deep unfolding framework centered on a two-stage manifold learning strategy to obtain a degradation-free structural prior, which is then strategically injected into both the projection and denoiser modules. In the data-consistency projection, a prior-guided spectral weight mechanism is introduced to adaptively modulate the weight of spectral band. In the denoiser module, a manifold-guided Mamba is proposed to achieve the structure-aware extraction of spatial-spectral features through prior modulation. Quantitative and visual comparisons on synthetic and real-world datasets show that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining the structural interpretability of the unfolding framework. The code is available at https://github.com/SqLiu67/MPG-DU.

</details>


### [32] [Decoupled Representation Learning for Face Presentation Attack Detection under Smart Glass Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27800)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra,Narayan Vetrekar,Neha Naik,Rajendra Gad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜采集的人脸呈现攻击数据库SmaGla-FPAD（含真伪与硅胶面具攻击共297,000张图像），并给出解耦表征学习框架DeCoupPAD-FAS++，在域内与跨场景评估中取得攻击拒绝与真伪接受的更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Face PAD多在固定相机或手持采集条件下研究，而智能眼镜采集引入自我运动、视角变化、背景杂乱、距离变化与光照不可控等更难的因素，缺乏相应数据集与方法。

Method: 构建SmaGla-FPAD数据库（室内/室外/车内三类场景，真伪与硅胶面具攻击）；提出DeCoupPAD-FAS++，通过互补的attack分支与style分支、伪域监督、对抗抑制及提示引导的语义对齐，将伪装相关证据与采集扰动解耦。

Result: 在SmaGla-FPAD上，域内与跨场景评估中攻击拒绝与真伪接受的平衡最优；消融实验验证解耦、伪域监督与提示正则的贡献。

Conclusion: 智能眼镜采集是Face PAD的新挑战场景，所构建数据库与解耦式表征学习方法有效提升该条件下的检测鲁棒性与平衡性。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection is typically studied under fixed camera or handheld acquisition, whereas smart glass capture introduces a more challenging setting with ego motion, changing viewpoint, background clutter, distance variation, and less controlled illumination. To address this challenge, we present \emph{SmaGla-FPAD}, a smart glass based face presentation attack database acquired using Ray-Ban Meta Wayfarer Smart Glasses across indoor, outdoor, and in-car settings. The database contains 297,000 sample images with both bona fide and silicone mask based attacks. We further propose \emph{DeCoupPAD-FAS++}, a decoupled representation learning framework that separates spoof related evidence from nuisance sensitive capture variation through complementary attack and style branches, pseudo-domain supervision, adversarial suppression, and prompt guided semantic alignment. Experiments on SmaGla-FPAD show that the proposed method achieves the best balance between attack rejection and bona fide acceptance in both in domain and cross scenario evaluation. Ablation results further confirm the contribution of disentanglement, pseudo-domain supervision, and prompt based regularisation under smart glass capture.

</details>


### [33] [Geometry Regularization for Vanilla NeRF via Self-Supervised Cross-Ray Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27776)
*Zongji Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过自监督跨射线一致性分支增强NeRF的几何优化，无需求解深度/法向真值，显著提升隐式几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始NeRF的密度场所编码的几何存在噪声且不准确，因为不同的形状与辐射组合可以解释相同的输入图像，导致几何歧义。

Method: 在vanilla NeRF基础上增加几何优化分支：从密度场采样候选表面点，在不同方向上生成经过该点的虚拟射线并用NeRF网络渲染其外观；基于可见性加权的跨射线颜色一致性约束隐式几何；并以外观预测不一致处的透明度惩罚和局部法向平滑正则化进一步约束几何，全程无需真值深度或表面法向监督。

Result: 在八个Replica场景上，平均Chamfer距离相比vanilla NeRF降低30.8%。

Conclusion: 自监督跨射线一致性可作为有效的几何正则手段，在不引入额外监督的前提下显著改善NeRF重建几何质量。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve high-quality novel-view synthesis by representing a scene as a continuous volumetric radiance field. However, the geometry encoded by the density field of vanilla NeRF can be noisy and inaccurate, since different combinations of shape and radiance may explain the same input images. To reduce this ambiguity, we augment vanilla NeRF with a geometry-optimization branch based on self-supervised cross-ray consistency. Specifically, this branch samples candidate surface points from the density field, generates virtual rays that intersect at each point from different directions, and renders their appearance using the NeRF network. Based on the visibility-weighted color consistency across these rays, we constrain the implicit geometry. We further regularize the implicit geometry by penalizing opacity at candidates with inconsistent appearance predictions and encouraging local normal smoothness. These geometric constraints are derived from the NeRF predictions and require no ground-truth depth or surface-normal supervision. Experiments on eight Replica scenes show that the proposed method reduces the mean Chamfer distance by 30.8% compared with vanilla NeRF.

</details>


### [34] [CILA: Confusion-based Instance-wise Logit Adjustment for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27728)
*Jiyoung Lim,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对长尾数据中现有logit调整方法仅在类级别调整、忽略实例级混淆的问题，提出实例级混淆感知的logit调整(CILA)与批自适应噪声注入(BNI)两种互补方法，作为即插即用模块提升长尾学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据存在类别不平衡，模型偏向多数类、难以学习少数类。现有logit调整方法基于类频率做类级别调整，忽略实例间的混淆情况，常抑制来自易混淆非目标类的有信息信号，导致这些类别学习不充分、预测不确定性升高、泛化受限。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：1) CILA，根据目标类与非目标类之间的实例级混淆程度自适应调整logit，恢复被类级别调整压制的有信息梯度；2) BNI，训练时扰动目标logit，将对混淆敏感的更新扩展到分类正确但靠近边界的样本。CILA为与损失函数无关的即插即用模块，可无缝集成到现有长尾学习方法中。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上一致提升性能；代码将于 https://github.com/maya0395/CILA 公开。

Conclusion: 实例级混淆感知的logit调整能弥补类级别调整的不足，CILA与BNI互补结合可有效改善长尾场景下易混淆类别的学习与泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world datasets are inherently class-imbalanced, leading models to favor majority classes and struggle with minority ones. Existing logit adjustment methods address this by modifying logits based on class frequencies, but their class-wise adjustments ignore instance-wise confusion and often suppress informative signals from confusing non-target classes. This results in insufficient learning of such classes, increased prediction uncertainty, and limited generalization.
  To address this, we propose two complementary methods. First, \textbf{Confusion-based Instance-wise Logit Adjustment (CILA)} adaptively adjusts logits based on instance-wise confusion between the target and non-target classes, restoring informative gradients that class-wise adjustment suppresses. Second, \textbf{Batch-adaptation Noise-Injection (BNI)} perturbs the target logit during training, extending the confusion-aware update to correctly classified but near-boundary samples that the penalty alone cannot reach. CILA is a loss-agnostic plug-in module that can be seamlessly integrated into existing long-tailed learning methods, and consistently improves performance across multiple benchmarks. Our code will be available at https://github.com/maya0395/CILA.

</details>


### [35] [VLA-Retain: Benchmarking Cross-Dimension Effects of Vision Tuning in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27698)
*Borui Xu,Han Chen,Bingsheng He,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VLA-Retain 基准，通过在 LIBERO-Plus 七个扰动维度上对比五个开源 VLA 模型的目标域微调检查点与各自基座，发现靶向视觉微调并非局部效应：目标增益无法预测保留性，10 个"模型–域"配置中 8 个非目标维度平均增益为负，且微调策略的效果依赖具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型的操控性能对观测条件变化敏感，现有做法是在目标域数据上微调视觉模块，但已有鲁棒性基准只评测未微调模型，而适配研究只报告目标域增益，因此"跨维度保留性"（目标增益之外的非目标维度变化）无人量化。要测这一效应需解决四项挑战：分离目标增益与非目标变化、将适配效应与架构差异隔离、排除通用遗忘、并比较不同调参选择。

Method: 构建 VLA-Retain 基准：选取 5 个开源 VLA 模型，分别在 Camera-view 与 Sensor-noise 两类目标域数据上微调，将微调检查点与其自身基座检查点在 LIBERO-Plus 数据集的 7 个扰动维度上逐一对比，以自基座为参照隔离架构差异并控制通用遗忘，同时对比不同微调策略（如 DELTA 式特征保持）。
