Result: 四项发现：(1) 靶向视觉微调非局部，目标增益不能预测保留性，10 个"模型–域"配置中 8 个非目标平均增益为负；Camera-view 微调下 X-VLA 目标域 +69.4 个百分点但其余平均 −4.6，OpenVLA-OFT 分别为 +39.3 与 +1.4。(2) 这些回退不能仅用同任务无扰动条件下的性能损失解释。(3) 更广的训练覆盖相比目标专用训练能提升总体增益，但未消除维度级回退。(4) 微调策略具模型依赖性：DELTA 式特征保持使 X-VLA 非目标平均增益升至 +1.2，却使 OpenVLA-OFT 降至 −23.1。

Conclusion: 仅以目标域成功率衡量 VLA 适配是不充分的；跨维度保留性应作为靶向 VLA 适配的关键评价准则，且微调方案需针对具体模型验证其非目标代价。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models transfer knowledge from large-scale pretraining into robot control, but their manipulation performance remains sensitive to changes in observation conditions, which recent work addresses by fine-tuning the visual module on target-domain data. However, it remains unclear how such adaptation affects robustness beyond the target condition, as existing robustness benchmarks only evaluate models without fine-tuning and adaptation studies only report the target gain. Measuring this cross-dimension effect requires separating target gains from off-target changes, isolating adaptation effects from architectural differences, ruling out generic forgetting, and comparing tuning choices. Therefore, we introduce VLA-Retain, a benchmark that compares five open VLA models, tuned on Camera-view or Sensor-noise data, with their own base checkpoints on LIBERO-Plus dataset spanning seven perturbation dimensions. Our study reveals four findings. First, targeted vision tuning is not local, and its target gain does not predict retention. Mean off-target gain is negative in 8 of 10 model--domain configurations. Under Camera-view tuning, X-VLA gains $69.4$ percentage points on the target but loses $4.6$ points on average elsewhere, whereas OpenVLA-OFT gains $39.3$ and $1.4$ points, respectively. Second, these regressions are not explained only by performance loss on the same tasks without perturbations. Third, broader training coverage raises overall gain over target-specific training but does not eliminate dimension-level regressions. Fourth, tuning strategies are model-dependent, as DELTA-style feature preservation raises X-VLA's mean off-target gain to $+1.2$ points but lowers OpenVLA-OFT's to $-23.1$. Overall, these findings show that target-domain success alone is insufficient and highlight cross-dimensional retention as a key criterion for targeted VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [36] [Media Intelligence Platform: Multimodal Detection of Synthetic and Manipulated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27974)
*David Fischinger,Marcel Hasenbalg,Silvia Poletti,Davide Antonutti,Mina Schütz,Lam Pham,Justin Ilyes,Péter Rjabcsenkó,Simon Freitter,Martin Boyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的网页化多模态媒体取证框架MIP，集成视频伪造、人脸变形、AI生成图像、音频深伪、局部图像篡改与文本分析等模块，在多个基准数据集上实现可靠检测并提供公开演示系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使合成人脸、伪造视频、AI生成图像、伪造音频及局部编辑图像等被操纵内容激增，而现有检测方法通常只针对单一篡改类型，难以应对真实场景中异构媒体并存的取证需求。

Method: 构建基于Web的Media Intelligence Platform（MIP）统一框架，采用模块化架构集成六类专用检测模块：伪造视频检测、人脸morphing检测、AI生成图像检测、音频深伪检测、局部图像篡改检测和文本分析；模块化设计支持异构模型的灵活接入，并便于后续扩展到新出现的篡改技术。

Result: 在代表性基准数据集上的实验表明，平台在多种媒体模态上均取得可靠的检测性能，同时为取证调查提供实用且易用的界面；随文发布公开演示系统以支持可复现性与社区评测。

Conclusion: 该平台验证了统一多模态系统在真实环境中进行综合深伪检测的潜力，其模块化、可扩展与可复现的设计为多模态媒体取证的一体化实践提供了可行范式。

Abstract: The rapid progress of generative artificial intelligence has led to a significant increase in manipulated multimedia content, including synthetic faces, forged videos, AI-generated images, fake audio, and locally edited images. Existing detection approaches are typically designed for a single manipulation type, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios involving heterogeneous media sources. In this work, we present the Media Intelligence Platform (MIP), a unified web-based framework for multimodal media forensics integrating specialized modules for fake video detection, face morphing detection, AI-generated image detection, audio deepfake detection, localized image manipulation detection, and text analysis. Its modular architecture enables flexible integration of heterogeneous models and supports future extensions for emerging manipulation techniques. Experimental results on representative benchmark datasets demonstrate reliable detection performance across multiple media modalities while providing a practical and user-friendly interface for forensic investigations. The platform highlights the potential of unified multimodal systems for comprehensive deepfake detection in real-world environments, and is accompanied by a public demonstrator released with this workshop submission to support reproducibility and community evaluation.

</details>


### [37] [CaMeWM: A Causal Medical World Model for Generative Tumor Evolution and Treatment Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26923)
*Huanlong Gao,Xuelei He,Fengjun Zhao,Xiaowei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果医学世界模型 CaMeWM，以"干预—决策"单环替代三项独立预测任务，实现支持域受限的 TACE 术后肿瘤演化模拟与治疗方案排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗规划本质是反事实的：需比较不同方案下肿瘤与生存的演化，但回顾性队列仅记录实际接受的方案与一次随访影像。在此类数据上训练的"治疗条件生成器"易复现历史治疗分配（混杂/选择偏差），而非模拟可用于方案排序的干预性病程动力学。

Method: CaMeWM 三阶段闭环：(1) 支持域准入——仅对具经验治疗支持的临床可比病史接受查询方案，依赖预处理调整与正值性（positivity）；(2) 病灶局部随访世界生成——动作条件响应描述子 + 确定性锚点 + 残差式三维扩散转移生成随访世界分布；(3) 效用映射——将采样世界映射至限制性平均生存时间（RMST），采用 response-Cox 效用模型。该环路估计预先设定的、支持域受限的 g-formula 目标；所有选择与 nuisance 估计均为 fold-local，策略以交叉拟合、删失校正的双稳健估计评价（factual-only AIPW）。

Result: 79 个配对 out-of-fold 回合中，随访 CT 转移 SSIM 达 0.618，变化图 SSIM 0.528；生成世界的生存通路 C-index 为 0.615；factual-only AIPW 显示冻结策略获得 9.60 周 RMST 增益（95% CI 0.61–18.13）。

Conclusion: CaMeWM 为回顾性、支持域受限的治疗规划提供了可检视的依据，通过显式建模干预支持与因果识别假设，缓解治疗条件生成器的分配复现问题。

Abstract: Treatment planning is intrinsically counterfactual: clinicians must compare how a patient's tumor and survival would evolve under competing regimens, whereas a retrospective cohort records only the regimen received and one follow-up scan. A treatment-conditioned generator trained on such data can therefore reproduce historical treatment allocation rather than simulate the interventional disease dynamics needed to rank regimens. We introduce \textbf{CaMeWM}, a causal medical world model for supported post-TACE tumor evolution and treatment planning. CaMeWM implements one intervention-to-decision loop rather than three independent prediction tasks: it first admits a queried regimen only for clinically comparable histories with empirical treatment support; it then generates a distribution of lesion-local follow-up worlds through an action-conditioned response descriptor, deterministic anchor, and residual 3D diffusion transition; and it finally maps sampled worlds to restricted mean survival time (RMST) with a response-Cox utility model. Under pre-treatment adjustment and positivity, this loop estimates a prespecified, support-restricted g-formula target; all selection and nuisance estimation are fold-local, and the induced policy is evaluated by cross-fitted, censoring-adjusted doubly robust estimation. Across 79 paired out-of-fold episodes, CaMeWM achieves 0.618 SSIM and 0.528 change-map SSIM for follow-up CT transition, while its generated-world survival pathway attains a 0.615 C-index; factual-only AIPW estimates a 9.60-week RMST gain (95\% CI 0.61--18.13) for the frozen policy. CaMeWM provides an inspectable basis for retrospective, support-restricted treatment planning.

</details>


### [38] [CodecMotion-OAD: Efficient Online Action Detection with Compressed-Domain Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26915)
*Zepeng Sun,Naichuan Zheng,Hailun Xia,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CodecMotion-OAD，用解码器运动矢量替代光流做在线动作检测，两阶段训练（无标签特征对齐+RGB+MV蒸馏）在 THUMOS'14 达 68.3 mAP（RGB+Flow 为 70.9），处理时间 CPU 加速 11.48 倍、GPU 2.04 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OAD 系统通常融合 RGB 外观与光流，但稠密光流提取是显著的计算瓶颈。编解码器运动矢量（MVs）在解码过程中天然可得，无需额外计算光流。作者量化了二者差距：在 THUMOS'14 上仅用运动输入时，光流比 MVs 高 11.2 mAP；与 RGB 融合后差距缩小至 3.7 点，且缩减主要发生在动作类别判别层面，说明 MVs 具备可挖掘的替代潜力。

Method: 提出 CodecMotion-OAD：以解码器提供的 MVs 替代光流，同时保留现有时间检测器架构。采用两阶段训练：第一阶段不使用动作标签，将 MV 特征对齐到预训练的光流特征接口；第二阶段使用真值标签与冻结的 RGB+Flow 教师模型的软预测，训练 RGB+MV 检测器（匹配蒸馏）。

Result: 在 THUMOS'14 上以 MiniROAD 为检测器，CodecMotion-OAD 达到 68.3 mAP，匹配蒸馏的 RGB+Flow 基线为 70.9 mAP；从解码到预测的处理时间在 CPU 上降低 11.48 倍，GPU 上降低 2.04 倍。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首次将编解码器运动矢量应用于帧级在线动作检测。结果表明 MVs 可在接近光流精度的前提下大幅降低计算开销，是替代稠密光流的可行方案。

Abstract: Online action detection (OAD) identifies ongoing actions in streaming videos. Many existing OAD systems combine RGB appearance with optical flow, but dense-flow extraction remains a major computational bottleneck. Codec motion vectors (MVs) are available during decoding, avoiding a separate optical-flow computation. On THUMOS'14, optical flow exceeds MVs by 11.2 mAP points with motion-only inputs, whereas the gap narrows to 3.7 points after RGB fusion, with the largest reduction in action-category discrimination. We propose CodecMotion-OAD, which replaces optical flow with decoder-provided MVs while retaining existing temporal detector architectures. Its two-stage training first aligns MV features with a pretrained flow-feature interface without action labels and then trains RGB+MV detectors using ground-truth labels and soft predictions from a frozen RGB+Flow teacher. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first application of codec MVs to frame-level OAD. On THUMOS'14 with MiniROAD, CodecMotion-OAD achieves 68.3 mAP versus 70.9 mAP for matched-distillation RGB+Flow, while reducing decoder-to-prediction processing time by 11.48-fold on CPU and 2.04-fold on GPU.

</details>


### [39] [EGGHead: Efficient Generalizable Gaussian Heads from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26898)
*Pilseo Park,Andrew Hou,Michel Sarkis,Ning Bi,Yiying Tong,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EGGHead，一种基于高斯泼溅的单图人脸重建方法，通过固定高斯预算下的语义区域高斯分配算法，在单次前向中同时提升重建质量、新视角合成与渲染速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DMM网格高效但表达能力有限；NeRF表达力与保真度高但渲染低效；Gaussian splatting虽兼顾两者，但其效率依赖光栅化高斯数量，且头部优化与准备时间（enrollment time）通常很长。

Method: 给定固定高斯预算，设计新的高斯分配算法，依据头部语义区域高效分配高斯，从而在尽量不损失表达能力的前提下减少所需高斯数量；整个建模过程以单次前向传播完成。

Result: 在重建质量、新视角合成以及渲染速度上均优于以往的单图像人脸建模方法。

Conclusion: 语义感知的高斯预算分配可有效缓解表达能力、效率与泛化性之间的权衡，EGGHead为高效高保真人头建模提供了单前向的可行方案。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic human heads typically involves trade-offs between representation power, efficiency, and generalizability. Traditional mesh-based solutions relying on 3DMMs generally sacrifice representation power but are efficient to render. On the other hand, NeRF-based methods improve representation power and fidelity while sacrificing efficiency. Gaussian splatting methods attempt to resolve these trade-offs by offering both high representation power and efficiency with real-time rendering. However, their efficiency nonetheless depends on the number of rasterized Gaussians, and the time needed to optimize and prepare the head (the enrollment time) is often high. In light of this, we propose EGGHead, which introduces a novel Gaussian allocation algorithm that efficiently assigns Gaussians to the head's semantic regions given a fixed Gaussian budget. Our algorithm reduces the number of Gaussians needed while minimizing the impact on representation power. EGGHead operates in a single forward pass and outperforms previous single-image face modeling methods in reconstruction quality, novel view synthesis, and rendering speeds.

</details>


### [40] [GAMF-Net: Grouped Availability-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Smartphone Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28129)
*Yue Zhuo,Jingjia Yan,Fangyu Liu,Lingjun Liu,Zongjun Chen,Zhisheng Luo,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分组可用性感知多模态融合网络GAMF-Net，将10个临床变量分为人口学、病史风险、皮损表现三组并联合编码取值与可用性掩码，以图像分支输出基础logits、各临床组输出类别级残差证据，仅在可用组间做可用性归一化的等权融合；在PAD-UFES-20上患者不交叉五折实验中取得Acc 74.17%、BACC 70.11%、Macro-F1 68.85%、Macro-AUROC 93.42%，全面优于图像单模态与多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机临床图像是资源受限场景下低成本皮肤病变分类数据源，但图像无法完整反映病史与症状；异构临床变量缺乏结构化组织、观测值与被插补值界限模糊，且临床信息可能干扰视觉证据，因此需设计能处理变量缺失与模态冲突的融合方案。

Method: 将10个临床变量按语义分为人口学、病史相关风险、皮损表现三组，每组同时编码字段值与字段级可用性掩码；图像分支产生基础分类logits，各临床分组生成以图像为条件的类别级残差证据；仅可用分组参与可用性归一化等权融合，临床信息全缺失时模型退化为纯图像通路；在PAD-UFES-20上采用患者不交叉五折协议，划分独立训练/验证/测试集。

Result: Accuracy 74.17±2.85%、BACC 70.11±2.11%、Macro-F1 68.85±0.85%、Macro-AUROC 93.42±1.20%；相较最强基线MetaBlock分别提升2.37、2.59、4.14、1.02个百分点；消融显示临床信息显著提升类平衡性能，约束等权融合优于动态融合，皮损表现组提供最有信息量的临床补充。

Conclusion: 分组可用性感知的残差式融合能在临床变量异构、缺失与潜在干扰条件下稳健引入临床信息，且保持图像单模态的可退化性；等权约束融合与语义分组是有效设计，皮损表现类变量为关键互补信息源。

Abstract: Smartphone clinical images provide a low-cost data source for computer-aided skin lesion classification in resource-constrained settings, but images alone cannot fully capture patients' medical histories and lesion symptoms. To address the lack of structured organization among heterogeneous clinical variables, the ambiguity between observed and imputed values, and the risk that clinical information may interfere with visual evidence, we propose GAMF-Net, a grouped availability-aware multimodal fusion network. Ten clinical variables are divided into three semantic groups---demographics, history-related risk, and lesion presentation---and each group jointly encodes field values and field-level availability masks. The image branch produces the base classification logits, while each clinical group generates image-conditioned class-level residual evidence. Only available groups participate in availability-normalized equal-weight fusion; when all clinical information is unavailable, the model reduces exactly to the image-only pathway. We evaluate the method on PAD-UFES-20 under a patient-disjoint five-fold protocol with separate training, validation, and test partitions. GAMF-Net achieves an Accuracy of $74.17\pm2.85\%$, a balanced accuracy (BACC) of $70.11\pm2.11\%$, a Macro-F1 of $68.85\pm0.85\%$, and a Macro-AUROC of $93.42\pm1.20\%$, outperforming all image-only and multimodal baselines reimplemented under a unified protocol. Compared with the strongest baseline, MetaBlock, the corresponding improvements are 2.37, 2.59, 4.14, and 1.02 percentage points, respectively. Ablation results show that clinical information substantially improves class-balanced performance, constrained equal-weight fusion is more effective than dynamic fusion, and the lesion-presentation group provides the most informative clinical complement.

</details>


### [41] [MoDDiT: Simple Token Routing in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28086)
*Jarek Liesen,Matthew T. Jackson,Benjamin Ellis,Shimon Whiteson,Jakob N. Foerster*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoDDiT 通过在 MLP 与自注意力中独立地对 token 进行动态路由，实现 DiT 的计算稀疏化分配，最低仅处理 12.5% 的 token，在降低训练与推理成本的同时提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频生成、交互式世界模型与端侧图像合成即将落地，DiT 采样质量优异但模型吞吐成为瓶颈。自然图像本身具有稀疏性（大量背景区域对场景贡献小、易去噪），而 DiT 对所有图像 token 采用静态、均匀处理，未能利用这种稀疏性。

Method: 提出 MoDDiT，一种简洁的架构修改：让 token 分别通过 MLP 与自注意力两条操作路径进行路由。由于两处路由相互独立，MLP 中的计算分配可随 token、图像及去噪时间步动态变化，从而实现 token 级的动态算力分配。

Result: 从零训练图像生成模型时显著降低训练与推理算力，最少仅处理 12.5% 的 token；在提高稀疏度与模型深度后，成本下降且生成性能优于无路由基线。学到的分配具有可解释性，能反映计算量随视觉内容与噪声水平的变化。该路由同样用于视频生成模型训练，其中分配与运动相关；并可将稠密双向视频模型蒸馏为稀疏自回归学生模型。

Conclusion: MoDDiT 表明在 DiT 中引入 token 级动态稀疏路由是提升生成模型效率与性能的有效途径，并具备良好的可解释性与向视频生成、模型蒸馏场景的迁移能力。

Abstract: Real-time video generation, interactive world models, and on-device image synthesis are almost a reality. Diffusion Transformers (DiT) achieve excellent sample quality, shifting the bottleneck to the throughput of the models themselves. Natural images are inherently sparse, as large portions of an image are often background, contribute little to the scene content, and are easy to denoise. DiTs fail to exploit this sparsity, as they process image tokens statically and uniformly. We propose MoDDiT, a simple architecture modification that learns to route tokens through both MLP and self-attention operations. By routing tokens through both blocks independently, MoDDiT allows for dynamic compute allocation in MLPs that varies across tokens, images, and denoising timesteps. Using MoDDiT to train image generation models from scratch significantly reduces computational costs of training and inference, processing as few as 12.5% of the tokens. By increasing sparsity and model depth, MoDDiT reduces costs and improves generation performance over unrouted baselines. The learned allocation is interpretable, revealing how computation varies with visual content and noise level. We also use MoDDiT to train video generation models, where allocation correlates with motion, and to distill a dense bidirectional video model into a sparse autoregressive student.

</details>


### [42] [Reusing Assurance Evidence in AI Pipelines: Validity Conditions, a Fail-Closed Release Gate, and a Replay of 714 Public Data Version Control Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27672)
*Atanu Mukhopadhyay,Shravya Reddy Nalla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文形式化了AI流水线中"证据复用"的两个安全条件（依赖声明完备性、门禁对候选对象的绑定校验），并实证测量前者在实践中失效的频率：按声明依赖复用可省去83.4%的阶段执行，但3.3%的提交发生"陈旧复用"，31%的流水线受影响；将阶段静态导入闭包纳入依赖可将陈旧复用降至0.4%，同时仍省去81.6%执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次变更后重跑全部治理控制成本高昂，团队倾向复用此前的证据记录。但复用仅在两个前提成立时才安全：各控制声明的依赖覆盖所有能改变其结果的因素；发布门禁能校验被复用证据确实描述待发布候选。作者指出这两个条件在现有实践中均缺乏显式化与度量，尤其是"未声明依赖"这一失效模式从未被量化，故欲将其形式化并实证测量其发生频率与影响范围。

Method: 1) 形式化定义：给出签名证据记录对某个候选有效的判定条件，并给出"变更局部重执行"与"全量重跑"得到相同发布决策的充分条件。2) 原型门禁：基于Ed25519数字签名的参考门禁实现上述校验，并与两类基线（信任规划器的门禁、仅验签的门禁）在9个操纵场景下对比。3) 大规模回放：对714个公开DVC流水线的38,083次提交进行重放，按声明依赖复用阶段结果，统计陈旧复用率；并对40个抽样事件做人工审计。4) 缓解方案对照评估：静态导入闭包、抽样影子重执行、对无法解释变更全量重跑三种策略在复用节省率与陈旧复用率上的权衡。

Result: 参考门禁在9个操纵场景中拦截8个；信任规划器的门禁拦截1个，仅验签的门禁拦截3个；而策略从未声明的依赖可通过所有门禁。回放结果显示：按声明依赖复用避免了83.4%的阶段执行，但3.3%的提交复用了其导入代码或引用配置已变更的阶段，31%的流水线至少受影响一次；40个抽样事件中人工审计确认36例存在未声明依赖。以输出为证据的评估阶段暴露度最高。加入各阶段静态导入闭包后，陈旧复用降至0.4%，仍避免81.6%的执行；抽样影子重执行降幅远小，对无法解释变更全量重跑仅避免45.3%。

Conclusion: 证据复用的安全性依赖于依赖声明的完备性与门禁对候选绑定的校验，二者缺一不可；仅依赖数字签名或规划器自述无法防御未声明依赖。实践中未声明依赖并非罕见边缘情况，而是系统性失效模式，且集中于产出证据的评估阶段。静态导入闭包是一种低成本、高收益的缓解手段，在保留大部分复用收益的同时几乎消除陈旧复用，优于抽样影子重执行与全量重跑等保守策略。

Abstract: Rerunning every governance control after every change to an artificial intelligence (AI) pipeline is expensive, so teams reuse earlier results. Reuse is safe only if each control's declared dependencies cover everything that can change its outcome, and only if the release gate checks that reused evidence describes the candidate being released. This article makes both conditions explicit and measures how often the first fails in practice. It defines when a signed evidence record is valid for a candidate and gives sufficient conditions under which change-local re-execution reaches the same release decision as rerunning every control. A reference gate implementing these checks with Ed25519 digital signatures blocks eight of nine manipulation scenarios, against one for a gate that trusts the planner and three for a gate that only verifies signatures; a dependency the policy never declared passes every gate. A replay of 38,083 commits from 714 public Data Version Control (DVC) pipelines measures that failure mode. Reusing stage results according to declared dependencies avoids 83.4% of stage executions, but in 3.3% of commits it reuses a stage whose imported code or referenced configuration changed, and 31% of pipelines are affected. A manual audit confirms the undeclared dependency in 36 of 40 sampled events. Evaluation stages, whose outputs are the evidence, are the most exposed. Adding each stage's static import closure lowers stale reuse to 0.4% while still avoiding 81.6% of executions; sampled shadow re-execution removes far less, and rerunning everything after unexplained changes avoids only 45.3%.

</details>


### [43] [Offline Makes Online Better: Calibrated Cache Compression for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28192)
*Zhen Wang,Ning Lai,Qingtao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CALM：通过离线校准构建层-头风险图，用动态规划在给定缓存预算下分配 token 合并比例，实现自回归视频生成 KV-cache 的非均匀压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 token 合并方法在所有注意力层与头上采用统一合并比例，忽略了不同层/头对合并的敏感度差异；作者观察到这种敏感度差异显著但在不同提示下保持一致，因而存在按层-头差异化分配的空间。

Method: 以注意力输出误差作为端到端视频质量的轻量代理指标用于风险评估；离线在此代理下评估多种分配策略并构建固定的层-头风险图；推理时在给定缓存预算下用动态规划求解最优合并比例分配。

Result: 在三个自回归视频模型上，相同内存预算下 CALM 持续优于统一 token 合并，将相对原视频的 LPIPS 降低约 21%，并获得更高的视频质量评分。

Conclusion: 层-头敏感度可作为可离线标定、跨提示稳定的先验，配合代理风险指标与动态规划，可在低开销下实现更优的 KV-cache 压缩；风险图离线固定，在线开销仅为一次 DP 求解。

Abstract: Token merging is an effective approach to reducing KV-cache memory consumption in autoregressive video generation by clustering temporally redundant tokens and retaining only a small set of representatives. Existing methods, however, typically apply a uniform merging ratio across all attention layers and heads. We observe that different layers and heads exhibit substantially different yet jointly correlated sensitivities to token merging, and that these sensitivity patterns remain consistent across prompts. Based on this observation, we introduce CALM, a Calibrated Allocation for Layer-Head Merging that uses offline calibration to improve online cache compression. Exhaustively evaluating all possible layer-head allocations is prohibitively expensive. To enable efficient calibration, we show that attention-output error strongly correlates with end-to-end video quality and can therefore serve as a lightweight proxy for allocation risk assessment. We evaluate different allocation strategies using this proxy and construct a fixed layer-head risk map offline. Given a cache budget at inference time, CALM uses dynamic programming to determine the best allocation of merging ratios. Experiments across three different autoregressive video models demonstrate that CALM consistently outperforms uniform token merging under the same memory budget, by reducing the LPIPS to original videos by around 21% with higher video-quality scores.

</details>


### [44] [Uncertainty Gates the Gradient: Adaptive Updates for Generalized Few-Shot 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26909)
*Yifei Zhao,Fanyu Zhao,Shengtang Wu,Han Zhang,Ruike Cao,Yinsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UGA框架，首次将不确定性提升到"更新级门控"，用于广义少样本3D点云分割，在ScanNet200与ScanNet++上平均提升+3.08 HM IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义少样本3D点云分割中，支持集标注常受稀疏与遮挡影响，直接适配低质量样本会学到伪几何模式而非鲁棒的新类概念；虽有3D视觉-语言模型补充监督，但不确定性用于主动调控适配动态的潜力尚未被充分挖掘，且损失重加权等信号级方法无法阻止不可靠episode主导优化轨迹。

Method: 核心模块Uncertainty-Gated Update (UG-Update)：在合并的支持集与伪标签新类监督上通过类内离散度估计episode可靠性，并选择性地门控残差梯度更新——可靠episode学习新类语义，不可靠时衰减更新；辅以Uncertainty-Based Pseudo Label Filter (UB-Filter)，强制从高置信、稠密信号中学习。

Result: 在ScanNet200与ScanNet++（1-shot/5-shot设置）上取得新的SOTA，相比最强先前基线平均提升+3.08 HM IoU。

Conclusion: 不确定性可作为更新级门控机制有效抑制低质量支持样本带来的噪声适配，显著提升广义少样本3D点云分割性能，为不确定性驱动的适配调控提供了新范式。

Abstract: In generalized few-shot 3D point cloud segmentation, support annotations are often compromised by sparsity and occlusion. Blindly adapting to such low quality samples risks learning spurious geometric patterns rather than robust novel concepts. Although 3D Vision-Language Models have been introduced to sup plement supervision, the potential of uncertainty to actively govern adaptation dynamics remains underexplored. Existing signal-level methods like loss reweight ing fail to prevent unreliable episodes from steering the optimization trajectory. To address this, we propose Uncertainty-Gated Adaptive (UGA), to the best of our knowledge the first framework that elevates uncertainty to update-level gating for generalized few-shot 3D point cloud segmentation. The core mod ule, Uncertainty-Gated Update (UG-Update), estimates episodic reliability via intra-class dispersion over merged support and pseudo-labeled novel supervision, and selectively gates residual gradient updates. This encourages the model to learn novel semantics from reliable episodes while attenuating updates during unreliable ones. Complementing this, we introduce an Uncertainty-Based Pseudo Label Filter (UB-Filter) to enforce learning from high-confidence dense signals. On ScanNet200 and ScanNet++ (1-/5-shot), UGA sets new state of the art with an average +3.08 HM IoU over the strongest prior baseline.

</details>


### [45] [OV-Foam: How 3D Representations Shape Semantic Feature Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28131)
*Yousef Rajeh,Alejandra Esparza Pelcastre,Ivan Viola,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OV-Foam，用 PowerFoam（基于幂图的零重叠空间划分表征）替代 3D Gaussian splatting 进行开放词汇分割的特征提升，使反投影更接近最优分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割的特征提升依赖简单反投影为基元分配特征。3DGS 基元高度重叠，每条射线需处理大量基元，导致提升结果次优；只有当同一射线不被多个基元共享时，反投影才退化为可最小二乘求解的问题。

Method: 采用 PowerFoam 作为底层表征，用幂图（power diagram）对场景做空间划分，使基元之间零重叠，并显著减少每条射线上的基元数量，从而简化特征提升问题、获得更接近最优的特征分配。

Result: 理论上表明 OV-Foam 的空间划分基元可达到近最优分配；实验中开放词汇分割性能优于基于 Gaussian 的基线方法。

Conclusion: 以零重叠的空间划分表征替代重叠高斯基元，是缓解反投影次优性、提升开放词汇分割特征提升质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Existing feature lifting approaches for open-vocabulary segmentation rely on simple backprojection to assign features to primitives. In the case of 3D Gaussian splatting reconstruction, the primitives overlap heavily, leading to suboptimal reconstructions, governed by many primitives per ray. Backprojection, however, reduces to a least-squares solvable problem, exactly when no two primitives share a ray. Motivated by this insight, we propose OV-Foam, which uses PowerFoam as the underlying representation. This representation uses a power-diagram to partition the scene, resulting in zero overlap between primitives and a substantial reduction in primitives per ray, which ultimately simplifies the lifting problem and allows for closer-to-optimal feature assignment. We show that the space partitioning primitives of OV-Foam reach near-optimal assignment, and our experiments demonstrate improved open-vocabulary segmentation over Gaussian baselines.

</details>


### [46] [Reliability-Guided Vision-Language Decoding with Text-Aware Skip Refinement for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28118)
*Sivan Talaei,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Mustaqeem Khan,Abdulhady Abas Abdullah,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RELiF视觉-语言分割框架，通过可靠性引导解码融合医学文本，缓解跨模态不可靠引导问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像目标视觉区分度低、结构不规则、跨模态差异大，导致分割困难；文本可提供补充信息降低歧义，但无差别融合会引入不可靠引导。

Method: RELiF框架含两个核心模块：可靠性引导融合门（RFG）自适应控制跨模态更新；文本引导跳跃细化适配器（TSRA）利用文本引导细化高分辨率特征；可靠性响应可导出互补的不确定性表征。

Result: 在X射线、CT与内窥镜分割任务上表现具竞争力，适用于多种成像特性与目标形态；额外分析考察效率与稳定性；不确定性值与分割错误像素相关，错误像素处高不确定性更频繁。

Conclusion: 可靠性引导的文本融合可在多模态医学分割中兼顾性能与稳健性，并提供与预测难度相关的内在不确定性信号。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging when targets exhibit limited visual distinction, irregular structure, or substantial variation across imaging modalities. Medical text can reduce this ambiguity, but indiscriminate fusion may introduce unreliable guidance. We present RELiF, a vision--language framework that integrates text through reliability-guided decoding. Its Reliability-guided Fusion Gate (RFG) adaptively controls cross-modal updates, while the Text-guided Skip Refinement Adapter (TSRA) refines high-resolution features using text guidance. RELiF is evaluated on radiographic, CT, and endoscopic segmentation tasks, where it shows competitive performance across diverse imaging characteristics and target appearances. Additional analyses examine efficiency and stability. The complementary uncertainty representation derived from the reliability response also shows an association with prediction difficulty, with higher uncertainty values occurring more frequently in pixels associated with segmentation errors. Code: https://github.com/SeivanTalaie/RELiF.

</details>


### [47] [Differentiable Task-Driven Coded Aperture Camera Design with 3D Gaussian Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28004)
*Chengyang Yan,Donald G. Dansereau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可微分框架，联合设计相机系统与下游感知任务，并以3D高斯溅射作为可微场景表示来实现端到端的编码孔径深度估计相机设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机设计通常由领域专家经昂贵实验完成，且与所服务的任务脱节。现有联合设计方法要么依赖限于预采集2D图像的可微仿真（仅能设计光学或图像处理管线），要么使用不可微仿真器配合无导数或强化学习方法，随设计参数增多而扩展性差。

Method: 构建可微的联合设计框架：场景由真实拍摄重建的3D高斯溅射表示，提供可微成像过程，从而支持基于梯度的优化，覆盖此前可微方法难以处理的设计空间。用该框架设计两款编码孔径深度估计（depth-from-defocus）相机——单目与立体配置，端到端联合优化编码孔径掩模、相机参数与深度估计网络。

Result: 在两个编码孔径深度估计相机的设计任务上，该方法优于现有编码孔径设计基线与不可微相机设计基线。

Conclusion: 可微、端到端的联合设计在可行性与效果上均优于既有方法，是面向自动系统实现自动化、原理化相机设计的一步进展。

Abstract: Camera design heavily influences perception task performance, yet cameras are typically designed by domain experts through costly experimentation, in isolation from the tasks they serve. Existing joint-design methods either rely on differentiable simulation limited to pre-captured 2D images, restricting them to optics or image-processing pipeline design, or use non-differentiable camera simulators optimised with derivative-free or reinforcement-learning methods that scale poorly with the number of design parameters. We present a differentiable framework for jointly designing complex camera systems and downstream perception tasks. We use it to design two coded-aperture depth-from-defocus cameras, a monocular and a stereo configuration, by jointly optimising the coded aperture mask, camera parameters, and a depth estimation network end-to-end. Our framework represents scenes with 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructed from real-world captures, providing a differentiable image formation process for gradient-based optimisation over design spaces intractable for prior differentiable approaches. Results demonstrate that our differentiable method improves over existing coded aperture design and non-differentiable camera design baselines, offering a step forward in automated and principled camera design for autonomous systems.

</details>


### [48] [Matrix-Game 4.0: Harnessing Video Models for Real-Time Playable Game Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27999)
*Jihai Zhang,Runjia Qian,Kaiqin Kong,Biao Jiang,Puyi Wang,Siru Chen,Tianle Li,Guanjie Chen,Junyi Wu,Chubin Chen,Yuanhang Li,Kaichen Huang,Haobo Zhang,Yangguang Li,Wei Yu,Guibin Chen,Yu Cheng,Yahui Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将游戏执行与视觉生成解耦的可执行游戏框架，并发布Matrix-Game 4.0，在单张RTX5090上实现480p、60FPS、按键到显示112ms延迟的可玩世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从"可控视频"迈向"真正可玩的世界"需要持久世界状态、可执行游戏规则和低延迟交互，现有方法难以同时满足。

Method: 以轻量游戏引擎维护世界状态并执行交互规则；多模态编码智能体将玩家想法转为游戏代码与动态场景描述；基于自回归视频模型的渲染器在动态描述与记忆参考条件下，将引擎渲染的低多边形代理转为高保真画面；渲染器在低多边形/高保真对齐视频对上训练，蒸馏为因果生成器，按四帧分块、1–2步去噪生成，并配合推理优化。

Result: 单卡RTX5090上实现480p、60FPS，按键到显示延迟112ms；单步生成稳态吞吐83FPS，峰值显存18.2GiB；固定提示下RTX5080达67FPS、RTX5070Ti达58FPS。

Conclusion: 展示了具备引擎强制机制的可定制射击与驾驶游戏，并开源代码、模型权重与低多边形游戏资产，支持可玩游戏世界的研究与创作。

Abstract: Moving from controllable videos to genuinely playable worlds requires persistent world state, executable game rules, and low-latency interaction. We outline a roadmap for the next generation of Matrix-Game that separates game execution from visual generation through an executable game harness, and introduce Matrix-Game 4.0 as the first system built on this design. A lightweight game engine maintains world state and executes interaction rules, a multimodal coding agent translates player ideas into game code and dynamic scene captions, and a renderer based on autoregressive video model transforms engine-rendered low-poly proxies into detailed visuals, conditioned on dynamic captions and memory references. This framework provides continuous guidance for visual generation and enables natural-language creation and modification of scenes and gameplay. Our renderer is trained on aligned low-poly and high-fidelity video pairs and distilled into a causal generator that generates in four-frame chunks with one or two denoising steps. Together with targeted inference optimizations, Matrix-Game 4.0 enables 480p, 60 FPS gameplay on a single NVIDIA RTX5090 with a key-press-to-display latency of 112ms. Single-step generation achieves 83FPS steady-state throughput with 18.2GiB of peak GPU memory usage. With fixed prompts, steady-state throughput also reaches 67FPS on an RTX5080 and 58FPS on an RTX5070Ti. We demonstrate customizable shooter and driving games with engine-enforced mechanics, and release code, model weights, and low-poly game assets to support research and creation of playable game worlds.

</details>


### [49] [Efficient SAM3 Adaptation for Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation via Dense Competitive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27960)
*Wenbin Liao,Hao Zhu,Yike Ma,Hao Jiang,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DCR-SAM 框架，解决 SAM3 在多概念同时分割时的语义重叠误分类、冗余掩码与推理延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3 在单概念可提示概念分割（PCS）上表现出色，但在真实场景中需同时分割多个概念时存在三方面不足：语义重叠的概念难以并行处理，导致语义误分类与冗余掩码预测；概念数量增加时，独立推理范式带来难以接受的推理延迟。

Method: DCR-SAM 采用三项关键设计：(1) 解耦跨模态交互以缓解推理延迟；(2) 动态对齐文本概念与视觉表示，并引入稠密竞争头（dense competition head）显式施加类间竞争以纠正语义误分类；(3) 引入可学习背景 token 动态吸收非目标对象，从而抑制重叠预测、生成高保真掩码。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明：(1) 相较 SOTA 方法性能提升 2.3%~10.7%；(2) 相比原生 SAM3 实现 33× 推理加速；(3) 在多样场景下具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦跨模态交互、稠密竞争与可学习背景 token 的协同设计，DCR-SAM 在精度、效率与鲁棒性上同时优于现有方法，为多概念可提示分割提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) has achieved significant progress in Promptable Concept Segmentation (PCS) by processing short noun phrases to generate segmentation masks with unique instance identifiers. While SAM3 excels at single-concept segmentation, it falls short in complex, real-world environments where multiple concepts must be segmented simultaneously. In particular, SAM3 typically fails to simultaneously process concepts with semantic overlap, leading to semantic misclassifications and redundant mask predictions. Moreover, as the number of concepts increases, the independent inference paradigm introduces prohibitive inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose the Dense Competitive Representations SAM (DCR-SAM) framework. By decoupling cross-modal interactions, the proposed method alleviates the inference latency. To resolve semantic misclassifications, DCR-SAM dynamically aligns textual concepts with visual representations and incorporates a dense competition head to enforce explicit inter-class competition. Furthermore, a learnable background token is applied to absorb non-target objects dynamically. These mechanisms effectively suppress overlapping predictions and generate high-fidelity masks. Extensive experimental evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework: (1) achieves a 2.3% $\sim$ 10.7% performance improvement over state-of-the-art methods, (2) delivers a 33$\times$ inference speedup compared to the native SAM3, and (3) exhibits strong robustness across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [From Proper Scoring Rules and Edge Biases to Mumford-Shah Models: Robust Unsupervised Hyperspectral Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27958)
*Fabian Conrad,Benjamin Berkels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分段常数Mumford-Shah泛函的特征无关无监督高光谱分割框架，将严格一致评分规则与参数化概率分布族转化为指示函数，重点使用重尾椭圆分布、负对数似然与beta散度评分以应对离群点，并引入边缘偏置先验，在四个标准数据集中的三个上取得最优全无监督整体精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱标注数据稀缺且获取耗时，无监督分割尤为重要；但光谱变异性、混合像元、噪声及相邻波段高相关性使该任务困难，因此需要一个通用、鲁棒的无监督框架。

Method: 构建特征无关的分段常数Mumford-Shah无监督分割框架：将严格一致评分规则与参数化概率分布族映射为Mumford-Shah泛函的指示函数，从而自然推广若干既有Mumford-Shah分割模型；采用重尾椭圆分布、负对数似然评分及基于beta散度的评分处理离群点；结合变分法与统计学的相互作用提出参数估计的鲁棒步骤；并在数据保真项与周长项中同时引入含边界先验信息的边缘偏置。

Result: 在Indian Pines、Kennedy Space Center、Pavia University和Salinas四个标准数据集的全场景评估中，该方法在其中三个上取得全无监督方法中最高的整体精度，并优于作者此前的Mumford-Shah模型。

Conclusion: 该框架将评分规则与Mumford-Shah变分模型统一，具备特征无关性与鲁棒性，结合边缘先验可有效提升无监督高光谱分割性能，并可作为已有Mumford-Shah模型的推广。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images provide a rich representation of the underlying spectrum for each pixel, allowing for a pixel-wise classification/segmentation into different classes. As the availability of labeled hyperspectral images is limited and the acquisition of labeled training data is time-consuming, unsupervised segmentation methods are important in hyperspectral image segmentation. Spectral variability, mixed pixels, noise as well as high correlation between neighboring spectral bands make this task challenging. We present a feature-agnostic, unsupervised hyperspectral segmentation framework based on the piecewise-constant Mumford-Shah functional. Our framework turns a strictly proper scoring rule and a family of parametric probability distributions into an indicator function for the Mumford-Shah functional, and we naturally generalize some previous Mumford-Shah based models for image segmentation this way. Our focus will be on heavy-tailed elliptical distributions, the negative log-likelihood score and the score based on the beta-divergence to deal with the heavy outliers in hyperspectral imaging. Using the interplay between the calculus of variations and statistics, we also propose a robustness step to estimate the statistical parameters of these heavy-tailed elliptical distributions. In addition, an edge bias, which contains prior boundary information, is incorporated into both the data fidelity term and the perimeter term of the Mumford-Shah functional. Numerical experiments show that, to the best of our knowledge, our method achieves the highest overall accuracy among fully unsupervised methods on three of the four standard benchmarks (Indian Pines, Kennedy Space Center, Pavia University and Salinas) evaluated on the full scenes. It also improves upon our previous Mumford-Shah based model.

</details>


### [51] [WaveSpec: Multi-Spectral Iris Presentation Attack Detection via Wavelength-Conditioned Spectral Coherence Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27608)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaveSpec多光谱虹膜活体检测框架，通过特征级光谱一致性适配显式建模跨波段光谱响应一致性，在跨攻击仪器协议下显著降低D-EER与BPCER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多光谱虹膜呈现攻击检测方法主要将波长信息用于条件调制或特征融合，难以显式建模样本光谱响应在各波段间是否一致，限制了对跨波段光谱连贯性的判别能力。

Method: 每个近红外波段用DINOv2骨干编码；提出集合条件光谱一致性适配器（SCSA），利用可用光谱集合的共识、各波段相对共识的偏差及波长身份更新各波段特征；适配特征投影为结构、光谱与异常三种表示，经自适应池化后分类为真实或攻击。

Result: 在MSIrPAD基准的严格跨攻击仪器协议下（一种攻击仪器训练、未见攻击测试），平均D-EER由SpectraIrisPAD的8.45%降至3.33%，平均BPCER@APCER=1%由43.16%降至12.07%；消融显示SCSA适配器与光谱残差是性能提升关键；波段子集分析表明部分光谱观测下仍有效，五波段齐全时最佳；五波段样本端到端延迟11.15 ms（NVIDIA L40S-48Q GPU），开销适度。

Conclusion: 特征级光谱一致性适配是提升多光谱虹膜PAD跨攻击泛化性的有效途径，且以较低计算代价实现稳健性能。

Abstract: Multispectral iris presentation attack detection benefits from observing the same iris across multiple near-infrared wavelengths, but existing methods often use wavelength information mainly for conditioning or fusion. This can limit their ability to explicitly model whether the spectral response of a sample is coherent across bands. In this paper, we propose \textbf{WaveSpec}, a multispectral iris PAD framework that performs feature-level spectral coherence adaptation. Each NIR band is first encoded with a DINOv2 backbone. The proposed Set Conditioned Spectral Coherence Adapter then updates each band feature using the consensus of the available spectral set, the band-specific deviation from that consensus, and wavelength identity. The adapted features are projected into structural, spectral, and anomaly representations, adaptively pooled, and classified as bona fide or attack. We evaluate WaveSpec on the MSIrPAD benchmark under a strict cross-artefact protocol, where the model is trained with one presentation attack instrument and tested on unseen attacks. WaveSpec reduces the average D-EER from 8.45\% for SpectraIrisPAD to 3.33\%, and reduces average BPCER@APCER=1\% from 43.16\% to 12.07\%. Ablation results show that the SCSA adapter and spectral residual are central to the improvement, while a band-subset analysis shows that WaveSpec remains effective under partial spectral observations and performs best when all five bands are available. The method adds only modest computational overhead, with 11.15 ms end-to-end latency per five-band sample on an NVIDIA L40S-48Q GPU.

</details>


### [52] [HiQGS: Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Querying for Feed-Forward Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27604)
*Qiaowei Miao,Qifeng Cheng,Yijing Guo,Yuru Xiao,Ziqian Ni,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiQGS：面向稀疏单目帧的前馈高斯重建的分层查询框架，用分层时空编码器组织多分辨率几何-外观特征，并用时间自适应解码器按目标时间戳查询证据、预测高斯场景状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏有位姿单目帧的前馈高斯重建需处理视角与时间联合变化的观测。现有方法存在两类局限：一是从与观测对齐的特征直接预测高斯基元，将高斯分配与单帧强耦合；二是用紧凑隐式查询聚合多帧信息，但未按与目标时间戳的时间关系显式组织观测证据。因此跨空间尺度、视角与时间戳的证据难以对齐与整合。

Method: 提出HiQGS框架，包含两部分：(1) 分层时空编码器（Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Encoder），按图像位置、观测时间与相机位姿组织多分辨率的几何-外观视觉特征，保留互补的外观与结构线索；(2) 时间自适应解码器（Time-Adaptive Decoder），以目标时间戳查询该分层表示，检索相关的时空证据并预测对应的高斯场景状态。

Result: 在静态与动态场景重建基准上，实验显示良好的渲染质量与几何精度，验证了分层视觉编码与目标时间感知的高斯查询的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将观测证据按空间尺度、视角与时间显式分层结构化，并以目标时间戳驱动查询，HiQGS缓解了高斯分配与单帧耦合及证据难以对齐的问题，为稀疏单目输入的前馈高斯重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction from sparse posed monocular frames requires reasoning over observations that vary jointly in viewpoint and time. Existing methods either predict Gaussian primitives from observation-aligned features or aggregate multi-frame information through compact latent queries. However, the former tightly couples Gaussian allocation to individual frames, while the latter does not explicitly structure observed evidence according to its temporal relation to the requested timestamp. Consequently, evidence across spatial scales, viewpoints, and timestamps can be difficult to align and integrate. We present \textbf{HiQGS}, a query-based hierarchical framework for feed-forward Gaussian scene reconstruction from sparse monocular frames. HiQGS introduces a \textbf{Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Encoder} that organizes multi-resolution geometry-appearance visual features according to image location, observation time, and camera pose, thereby preserving complementary appearance and structural cues. Our \textbf{Time-Adaptive Decoder} use target timestamp queries the resulting hierarchical representation to retrieve relevant spatiotemporal evidence and predicts the corresponding Gaussian scene state. Experiments on static and dynamic scene reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate strong rendering quality and geometric accuracy, validating the effectiveness of hierarchical visual encoding and target-time-aware Gaussian querying.

</details>


### [53] [Less is More: Concise Retrieval and Critic-based Re-ranking for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27538)
*Yuqian Zheng,Hyoju Ahn,Hyeongjin Ahn,Eunil Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Less-is-More CIR（LM-CIR），一个两阶段、免训练框架，用于零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）：先以“精简检索”生成只保留关键修改语义的目标导向描述以减少噪声，再以“批判式重排序”评估候选的修改一致性与参考保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ZS-CIR 方法依赖大型视觉-语言模型生成目标图像描述进行检索，但生成的描述常包含冗余语义细节，在检索时引入噪声，导致检索结果次优。

Method: 两阶段、无需训练的框架：(1) Concise Retrieval——生成精简的、面向目标的描述，仅保留最具判别性的修改语义，降低检索中的语义噪声；(2) Critic Re-ranking——引入 critic 重排模块，从“修改一致性”和“参考图像保持性”两个角度对已检索候选进行再评估与重排序。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架在 ZS-CIR 任务上具有有效性与鲁棒性（摘要未给出具体数值指标）。

Conclusion: 通过“少即是多”的精简描述加批判式重排策略，可在免训练条件下提升零样本组合图像检索的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Zero-shot composed image retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image based on a reference image and modification text. Recent approaches commonly rely on large vision-language models to generate target image captions for retrieval. However, these captions may include redundant semantic details, introducing noise when retrieving the target image and leading to suboptimal retrieval results. To address this issue, we propose Less-is-More CIR, a two-stage training-free framework that combines Concise Retrieval and Critic Re-ranking. In the first stage, concise target-oriented captions are generated that preserve only the most discriminative modification semantics, thereby reducing semantic noise in the retrieval. In the second stage, a critic re-rank module further evaluates the retrieved candidates from the perspectives of modification consistency and reference preservation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework for the ZS-CIR. Our code is available at: https://github.com/dxlabskku/LM-CIR.

</details>


### [54] [Language Guidance for Supervised Vision Training: An Empirical Study of Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27208)
*Shruthi Gowda,Nikita Jain,Elahe Arani,Bahram Zonooz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究是否可以用预训练语言模型作为视觉训练的轻量辅助监督（无需图文配对数据、提示工程或对比目标），并系统比较显式语言引导（ExLG）与隐式语言引导（ImLG）在六类泛化场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督视觉训练依赖one-hot标签，标签所含语义结构信息有限，导致视觉表征易受分布偏移、虚假相关、纹理偏置、对抗扰动及序贯学习遗忘等问题影响；作者希望在不引入图文配对数据与复杂设计的前提下，利用另一模态（语言）的预训练模型提供轻量辅助监督。

Method: 提出并评估两种语言引导形式：显式语言引导（ExLG）与隐式语言引导（ImLG），并给出统一评估框架，覆盖六类泛化设置：分布内、分布外泛化、捷径与虚假相关抵抗、纹理/形状偏置、对抗鲁棒性、持续学习。

Result: 两种机制呈现互补优势：ExLG在分布内性能、低数据场景与持续学习保持性上稳定受益；ImLG在捷径敏感场景及更强对抗扰动下更有用。两者均参数与训练开销极小。

Conclusion: 语言导出的语义结构可为监督视觉训练提供有效补充，两种语言引导机制各有适用场景，并可形成利用现成跨模态预训练模型作为辅助监督的实用路线图。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have achieved remarkable success on vision benchmarks, yet they continue to struggle with many generalization challenges. Supervised vision training relies on one-hot labels, which provide limited information about semantic structure and shared attributes between classes. This limited supervision can leave visual representations vulnerable to distribution shifts, spurious correlations, texture bias, adversarial perturbations, and forgetting in sequential learning settings. We study whether pretrained language models can serve as lightweight auxiliary supervision for vision training without requiring paired image-text data, prompt engineering, or contrastive objectives. Specifically, we evaluate two forms of language guidance, Explicit Language Guidance (ExLG) and Implicit Language Guidance (ImLG). We conduct a comprehensive evaluation across six generalization regimes, including in-distribution, out-of-distribution generalization, shortcut and spurious correlation resistance, texture and shape bias, adversarial robustness, and continual learning. Our analyses show that the two mechanisms have complementary strengths, with explicit guidance consistently benefiting in-distribution, low-data performance, and continual learning retention, while implicit guidance is often more useful in shortcut-sensitive settings and under stronger adversarial perturbations. Importantly, both are lightweight and add minimal parameters and training overhead. The analyses characterize when language-derived structure helps supervised vision training and provides a practical roadmap for using off-the-shelf pretrained models from another modality as auxiliary supervision.

</details>


### [55] [World Models with Configurable Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27914)
*Yijun Shen,Delong Chen,Xianming Hu,Kai Zhang,Pascale Fung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConfigWM，以语言任务描述作为配置信号，将视觉嵌入映射到任务特定状态空间，实现可配置的感知抽象，从而在有干扰的真实环境中稳健地进行世界建模与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实观测噪声大且含大量任务无关信息；有效世界建模需将观测抽象为状态表示并滤除无关信息。但现有世界模型的编码器通常是非自适应的，状态表示易被任务无关信息干扰，无法按不同任务需求选择性地过滤观测。

Method: ConfigWM 通过任务条件化的状态提取实现可配置感知：给定任务的自然语言描述，将其作为配置信号，引导视觉嵌入映射到任务特定的状态空间，选择性地保留与当前任务相关的信息、抑制无关变化；随后在该任务特定状态空间中学习动作条件动力学用于规划。

Result: 在干扰程度递增的环境中评测：现有方法随噪声增强规划成功率大幅下降，而ConfigWM相对稳定，在多种环境上平均比最强基线高22.3%；物理量探测提升+0.365 Pearson r，干扰到干净检索提升+75.5%。

Conclusion: ConfigWM 能学到既保留任务相关信息又对干扰鲁棒的状态，支持噪声世界中的可靠世界建模与规划；任务条件化配置是提升编码器自适应性、缓解任务无关信息干扰的有效途径。

Abstract: Real-world observations are noisy and often contain excessive task-irrelevant information. Effective world modeling and planning need to abstract observations into world state representations while filtering out irrelevant information. However, encoders in existing world models are typically non-adaptive, leading to state representations that are easily distracted by task-irrelevant information. As a result, the model may not selectively filter observations to cater to different task demands. To address this problem, we introduce ConfigWM, which realizes configurable perception through task-conditioned state extraction. Given a language description of the task at hand, ConfigWM uses it as a configuration signal to steer how visual embeddings are mapped into a task-specific state space, selectively preserving information relevant to the current task while suppressing task-irrelevant variations. Action-conditioned dynamics are then learned in this task-specific state space for planning. We evaluate ConfigWM in environments with increasing levels of distraction. While existing methods exhibit substantial declines in planning success rate as the environments become increasingly noisy, ConfigWM remains comparatively stable and outperforms the strongest competing method by 22.3% on average across diverse environments. Consistent gains in physical-quantity probing (+0.365 Pearson r) and distracted-to-clean retrieval (+75.5%) further indicate that ConfigWM learns states that preserve task-relevant information while remaining robust to distractors, supporting reliable world modeling and planning in noisy worlds. Project Page: https://configwm.github.io

</details>


### [56] [S$^3$E-Det: A Strict Spatial-Spectral Equivariant Detector for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27882)
*Peng Zhang,Tingfa Xu,Jinrui Sui,Yuyang Tian,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出严格空间-光谱等变检测器S³E-Det，通过SOSC、CDEF、GAH三个模块实现有序光谱建模与变换一致预测，在MODA、HOD3K、DroneVehicle上兼顾高精度、小参数量与旋转稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多光谱目标检测器虽能学习判别性光谱表征，但缺乏统一机制来同时保持光谱顺序、跨金字塔层级自适应融合光谱上下文，并在旋转下约束几何响应的一致性。

Method: Spectral Order-aware Sampling Calibration (SOSC) 通过一阶变化对输入导出的光谱特征施加局部顺序约束；Common-Differential Equivariant Fusion (CDEF) 在金字塔各层级自适应聚合光谱上下文；紧凑的Geometric Alignment Head (GAH) 在统一预测阶段协调不变性与等变性输出属性。

Result: 在MODA、HOD3K和DroneVehicle数据集上，S³E-Det同时实现高检测精度、较小参数规模以及更低的旋转引起的变化。

Conclusion: 将有序光谱建模与变换一致预测耦合为严格空间-光谱等变的检测框架，可有效提升多光谱目标检测的精度、效率与几何鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multispectral object detection requires detectors to exploit material-dependent spectral responses while maintaining consistent localization under geometric variations. Existing MOD detectors learn discriminative spectral representations, yet lack a unified mechanism that preserves spectral order, adapts spectral-context fusion across pyramid levels, and regulates geometric responses under rotation. To address these limitations, we propose S$^3$E-Det, a strict spatial-spectral equivariant detector that couples ordered spectral modeling with transformation-consistent prediction. Spectral Order-aware Sampling Calibration (SOSC) imposes local order constraints on input-derived spectral features through first-order variation, while Common-Differential Equivariant Fusion (CDEF) adapts spectral-context aggregation across pyramid levels. A compact Geometric Alignment Head (GAH) reconciles invariant and equivariant output properties within a unified prediction stage. Across MODA, HOD3K, and DroneVehicle, S$^3$E-Det combines high detection accuracy with a compact parameter footprint and reduced rotation-induced variation. Code is available at https://github.com/Riiluo/S3E-Det.

</details>


### [57] [GARRET: Generative Feedback for Region Retrieval in Visual Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27829)
*Weijian Jian,Siyang Chen,Yuguang Yang,Yajun Zhang,Chunyu Xie,Dawei Leng,Yuhui Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GARRET：仅用(page, question, answer)三元组、通过可微生成反馈训练的区域级视觉检索框架，并构建ViDoRe-V3-Region基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉富文档的多模态RAG需要比整页更细粒度的证据，但现有检索器多索引/返回整页，而区域级训练依赖昂贵的query-to-region标注或含噪的模型生成标签。

Method: 共享的上下文保持检索器输出页面与区域多向量表示；训练时用二者检索分数构成候选区域裁剪的软路由分布，辅助VLM分别注意各裁剪块，按路由权重聚合上下文，以next-token预测目标生成"问题+答案"；梯度经路由分布回传，为区域表示分配查询条件化的信用（无需区域标注）。推理时移除辅助模型；并用上下文化区域表示加页面摘要token构造紧凑页面表示。

Result: 构建ViDoRe-V3-Region（8个领域、200,682个固定候选区域）；区域检索macro nDCG@10达0.4126，超最强视觉基线2.94个点；页面检索与ColQwen2.5-v0.2相当，但每页索引向量少47.8%。

Conclusion: 生成式可微反馈可替代区域级标注实现弱监督区域检索，并在更少索引开销下兼顾区域与页面检索性能。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation over visually rich documents requires evidence at a finer granularity than entire pages. Existing visual retrievers typically index or return full pages, whereas training region-level retrievers often requires costly query-to-region annotations or noisy model-generated labels. We introduce GARRET, a region-retrieval framework trained from (page, question, answer) triplets through differentiable generative feedback. A shared context-preserving retriever produces page and region multi-vector representations. During training, their retrieval scores define a soft routing distribution over candidate region crops. An auxiliary vision-language model attends to each crop separately, aggregates crop-specific contexts using the routing weights, and predicts the question followed by the answer under a next-token prediction objective. Gradients through the routing distribution assign query-conditioned credit to the indexed region representations, without requiring query-to-region annotations. At inference, the auxiliary model is removed. GARRET also reuses the contextualized region representations, together with page summary tokens, to construct a compact page representation. We introduce ViDoRe-V3-Region, a corpus-wide benchmark containing 200,682 fixed candidate regions across eight domains. GARRET achieves a macro nDCG@10 of 0.4126, outperforming the strongest visual baseline by 2.94 points. On page retrieval, it remains comparable to ColQwen2.5-v0.2 while indexing 47.8% fewer vectors per page.

</details>


### [58] [Radar-Based 3D Human Pose Estimation via Motion-Aware Pose Initialization and Observation-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27774)
*Woo-Jin Jung,Chan-Bin Lim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段框架 I2R-Pose，利用毫米波雷达实现室内 3D 人体姿态估计，并构建多模态同步数据集 RaMoS-Pose。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内（如智能家居）场景需要分析人体运动与状态；毫米波雷达对光照鲁棒且不采集人体外观图像，适合隐私敏感场景。但雷达观测对部分身体部位信息不足，且存在多部位反射重叠，限制了 3D 关节定位精度。

Method: 提出 I2R-Pose，含两阶段：(1) 运动感知姿态初始化，融合连续雷达观测的时序关系与人体骨架结构学习关节级特征，补偿关节信息不足并估计初始 3D 姿态序列；(2) 观测引导精化，用单独提取的雷达观测特征预测关节级位置修正量，精化初始姿态得到最终序列。框架支持时多普勒/时距(TD/TR)图与雷达点云(PC)的单模态及融合输入。

Result: 构建 RaMoS-Pose 数据集（同步 TD/TR 图、点云与 3D 关节 GT）。在 RaMoS-Pose 和公开 MVDoppler-Pose 上评估，融合输入下相较各自最优现有方法，MPJPE 分别降低 39.50% 与 15.13%。

Conclusion: 两阶段"初始化—精化"设计结合时序与骨架先验可有效缓解雷达信息不足与反射重叠问题，多模态融合进一步提升精度，验证了方法在室内 3D 人体姿态估计中的有效性。

Abstract: 3D Human Pose Estimation (3D HPE) is used in indoor environments, such as smart homes, to analyze human movement and monitor human states. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is suitable for this task because it is robust to lighting variations and does not capture images of human appearance. However, radar observations often provide insufficient information about certain body parts or contain overlapping reflections from multiple body parts, limiting accurate 3D joint localization. To address these limitations, we propose the Initialization-to-Refinement Pose Framework (I2R-Pose), comprising two stages: Motion-Aware Pose Initialization and Observation-Guided Refinement. In the first stage, joint-wise features are learned by incorporating temporal relationships across consecutive radar observations and human skeletal structure to compensate for insufficient joint information and estimate an initial 3D pose sequence. In the second stage, separately extracted radar observation features predict joint-wise positional corrections that refine the initial pose and generate the final pose sequence. The framework supports individual and fused inputs of time-Doppler/time-range (TD/TR) maps and radar point clouds (PCs). To evaluate performance across indoor activities, we construct the Radar Motion and Spatial Information for 3D Human Pose Estimation (RaMoS-Pose) dataset, providing synchronized TD/TR maps, radar PCs, and 3D joint ground truth (GT). Evaluations on RaMoS-Pose and the publicly available MVDoppler-Pose dataset show that I2R-Pose reduces mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) by 39.50% on MVDoppler-Pose and by 15.13% on RaMoS-Pose with fused inputs, compared with the best-performing existing methods evaluated on each dataset.

</details>


### [59] [VEOcc++: Learning Persistent Voxel Feature Maps for Embodied Online Semantic Occupancy Mapping and Offline Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27704)
*Ruoyu Wang,Jiahan Li,Yong Liu,Kexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEOcc++ 是一个以体素为中心的稀疏特征图框架，将在线增量语义占据建图与离线全局地图精化统一起来，在 EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet 上取得 67.12% IoU / 58.40% mIoU 的 SOTA 在线建图性能，离线精化进一步提升至 67.79% IoU / 59.37% mIoU，并实现移动机器人零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义占据建图同时存在两类互补需求：受因果约束的在线增量建图（保持全局地图实时更新）与离线全局地图精化（利用额外算力提升场景补全质量）。现有方法往往割裂处理这两个阶段，缺乏统一表示来复用在线观测信息。

Method: 提出以持久化稀疏体素特征图为核心的统一框架：探索阶段地图随新观测区域扩张，通过可靠性引导的更新融合对齐的局部特征，并由上下文感知解码器输出当前语义占据图；探索结束后，将缓存的局部特征作为互补证据进行整合，与已完成的在线表示结合，完成多尺度空间精化。

Result: 在 EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet 上达到 67.12% IoU、58.40% mIoU，刷新在线增量建图 SOTA；固定局部预测器时，在线建图流程较 VEOcc 融合提升 3.70 IoU 与 3.74 mIoU；离线精化仅用探索期观测即将完整地图提升至 67.79% IoU、59.37% mIoU；移动机器人零样本部署验证了跨环境迁移性、机载可行性与大规模室内建图可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过持久化稀疏体素特征图串联在线增量建图与离线精化，可有效复用探索期观测并显著提升语义占据建图质量，且具备实际机器人平台上的部署能力。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy mapping supports embodied perception during exploration and detailed scene understanding afterward, giving rise to two complementary requirements: online incremental mapping for maintaining an up-to-date global map under causal constraints, and offline global map refinement for improving the completed scene representation with additional computation. We present VEOcc++, a unified voxel-centric framework that connects both stages through a persistent sparse voxel feature map. During exploration, the map expands with newly observed regions and integrates aligned local features through reliability-guided updates, while context-aware decoding produces the current semantic occupancy map. After exploration, cached local features are consolidated as complementary evidence and combined with the completed online representation for multi-scale spatial refinement. On EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet, VEOcc++ achieves 67.12\% IoU and 58.40\% mIoU, establishing new state-of-the-art performance for online incremental mapping. With the local predictor fixed, our online mapping pipeline outperforms VEOcc fusion by 3.70 IoU and 3.74 mIoU points, demonstrating substantial gains from the proposed global mapping design. Offline global map refinement further improves the completed map to 67.79% IoU and 59.37% mIoU using only the observations acquired during exploration. Zero-shot deployment on a mobile robot further demonstrates transferability to unseen environments, onboard feasibility, and scalability to large-scale indoor mapping. Project page: https://wryzju.github.io/VEOccpp/.

</details>


### [60] [CARLAR Influence: A Structured Hallucination Risk in Multimodal Cheque Extraction and an Exploratory Compressed-Image Countermeasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27192)
*Joong Ho,Choi,Aditya Paul,Nidhi Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究多模态大模型在财务文档结构化抽取中的"视觉歧义"行为：即使支票两处金额标注不一致（CARLAR效应），模型仍返回相同金额；并提出压缩图像少样本干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM越来越多用于金融文档的结构化抽取，但其在视觉歧义条件下的行为理解不足，尤其是跨字段依赖性（courtesy amount 与 legal amount）导致的错误耦合。

Method: 在10种视觉提示条件下评测GPT-4.1与Claude 4.5 Sonnet；数据为243张主支票（其中124张双金额观测不等）及另两个数据集的598个严格不匹配样本；采用bootstrap置信区间与多重比较校正；另测试一种无需微调的"压缩图像少样本"干预（将示例呈现为单张视觉表格）。

Result: courtesy字段遮蔽下GPT-4.1的CARLAR率达73.7%（95% CI 68.3–79.0，p<0.001）；预测一致时模型自报置信度仍偏高，CARLAR样本的期望校准误差达0.93。干预在243例队列上使Claude对照组配对联合抽取准确率提升11.5个百分点（95% CI 6.2–16.9，校正p=0.00054），但严格124例子集增益较小且校正后不显著；对字段一致性的影响具模型依赖性（Claude下降、GPT-4.1上升）。

Conclusion: 需采用考虑视觉上下文与字段间依赖关系的验证方法，并为多模态抽取系统制定字段依赖感知的校准与评测流程。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for structured extraction from financial documents, but their behavior under visual ambiguity is poorly understood. We study CARLAR (Courtesy Amount Recognition and Legal Amount Recognition) influence: a model returns the same amount for both cheque fields even though their annotations differ, including cases with a blank annotation encoded as zero. Across ten visual prompting conditions, we evaluate GPT-4.1 and Claude 4.5 Sonnet on 243 primary cheques (124 with two observed unequal amounts) and 598 strict mismatches from two additional datasets. Under courtesy redaction, GPT-4.1 CARLAR reaches 73.7 percent (95 percent bootstrap interval: 68.3 to 79.0; p below 0.001 versus baseline). Model-reported confidence can remain high when predictions agree; expected calibration error reaches 0.93 among CARLAR cases. We also test a compressed-image few-shot intervention that presents examples as one visual table without model fine-tuning. On the 243-case cohort, Claude's Control condition increases paired joint extraction accuracy by 11.5 percentage points (95 percent interval: 6.2 to 16.9; adjusted p = 0.00054), but its strict-124 gain is smaller and not significant after correction. The intervention's effect on predicted-field agreement is model-dependent: it decreases for Claude and increases for GPT-4.1. These results motivate validation methods that account for visual context and dependencies between extracted fields.

</details>


### [61] [PRIMFLOW: Learning Trajectory Primitives for Few-Step Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27146)
*Hongyi Fang,Benjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续轨迹原语蒸馏方法，用局部教师速度监督原语有限差分，经端点差获得全局更新，并给出局部到全局误差界与端点吸收以实现单次网络评估推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步轨迹蒸馏依赖特定轨迹参数化或离散区间预测构造步内动力学，使学习到的更新被绑定于预设时间结构，缺乏对累积位移时间演化的直接建模。

Method: 直接学习表示教师轨迹上累积位移的连续轨迹原语；以局部教师速度监督该原语的有限差分以学习累积运动，并通过端点差得到全局更新，避免预设时间基或固定查询网格；建立局部到全局误差界刻画局部回归误差对全区间位移精度的影响；提出端点吸收，在消除辅助原语分支的同时保持单次网络评估推理。

Result: 大规模文生图任务上，1 NFE 时 T-FID 为 20.92、HPSv2 为 28.35，两项指标均优于轨迹蒸馏基线；端点吸收后性能相当，T-FID 20.86、HPSv2 28.33。

Conclusion: 以连续累积位移原语为核心的蒸馏公式在不预设时间结构的前提下实现了精确少步生成，且误差界分析为局部监督到全局精度提供理论支撑；端点吸收表明辅助分支可被消除而不损失性能，实现高效单步推理。

Abstract: Few-step trajectory distillation aims to learn accurate global updates from dense local supervision along a teacher's sampling trajectory. Existing approaches construct within-step dynamics using specific trajectory parameterizations or discrete interval predictions, tying the learned update to a prescribed temporal structure. We propose \method, which directly learns a continuous trajectory primitive representing cumulative displacement along the teacher trajectory. By supervising finite differences of the primitive with local teacher velocities, \method learns the accumulated motion directly, while obtaining global updates through endpoint differences. This formulation avoids prescribing a temporal basis or fixed query grid and instead learns the temporal evolution of cumulative displacement from local supervision. We further establish a local-to-global error bound to characterize how local regression errors affect full-interval displacement accuracy, and introduce endpoint absorption to eliminate the auxiliary primitive branch while preserving one-network-evaluation inference. Experiments on large-scale text-to-image generation show that \method achieves a T-FID of 20.92 and an HPSv2 score of 28.35 at one NFE, improving both metrics over a trajectory-distillation baseline. After endpoint absorption, it retains comparable performance, achieving a T-FID of 20.86 and an HPSv2 score of 28.33.

</details>


### [62] [LVMem: Image-Augmented Memory for Long-Horizon Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27104)
*Victoria Khoruzhaya,Maksim Kalmykov,Tuong Do Minh,Tatiana Zemskova,Yixin Zhu,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LVMem，一种基于视图记忆的高效长时程时空问答框架，通过引入图像路径链接、光流帧选择（PSFR）与物体中心场景描述，并配合自适应检索工具，在 OC-NaVQA 上优于 ReMEmbR，且在二值准确率上持平或超越 4D 场景图 DAAAM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需从长观测历史中对物体、事件及位置进行语言引导的感知与问答，传统 VQA 难以覆盖长时程与持久场景记忆需求；现有基于视图的记忆系统仅存文本描述或视觉嵌入，信息不完备、采样均匀导致注释信息量低，缺乏按需视觉核查能力。

Method: 在 ReMEmbR 式视图记忆上扩展：为每条存储描述附加原始图像路径以实现描述—视觉内容链接；用基于光流的帧选择策略 PSFR 替代记忆构建中的均匀采样；存储以物体为中心的描述性场景表示而非普通 caption；为 VLM 智能体配备自适应检索工具，返回与最相关 caption 关联的图像，由智能体自行决定何时进行视觉检查。

Result: 在 OC-NaVQA 基准上，LVMem 在两种骨干网络下于二值指标与位置指标上均优于 ReMEmbR；在二值准确率上持平或超过 4D 场景图 DAAAM 基线，同时仅依赖轻量视图记忆，无需显式图构建。

Conclusion: 通过图像路径链接、光流驱动的关键帧选择与物体中心描述，并结合自适应视觉检索，可在不构建显式图结构的轻量视图记忆下有效支撑长时程时空问答，实现优于同类视图记忆方法、媲美场景图方法的效果。

Abstract: Reliable language-guided perception is essential for embodied agents, where questions about objects, events, and their locations must be answered from long observation histories. Unlike conventional visual question answering, realistic robotic settings require reasoning over extended temporal horizons and persistent scene memory. We build upon the view-based memory paradigm and propose an efficient framework for long-horizon spatio-temporal question answering. While existing view-based systems typically store either text captions or visual embeddings, we extend the ReMEmbR-style memory with image paths that link each stored description to its original visual content. To make the stored annotations more informative, we replace uniform sampling during memory construction with an optical-flow-based frame selection strategy (PSFR) and store an object-centric descriptive scene representation instead of plain captions. We further equip a VLM-based agent with an adaptive retrieval tool that returns the image associated with the most relevant caption, allowing the agent to decide when visual inspection is needed. On the OC-NaVQA benchmark, LVMem outperforms ReMEmbR on binary and positional metrics with both backbones and matches or exceeds a 4D scene graph DAAAM baseline on binary accuracy, while operating on a lightweight view-based memory that requires no explicit graph construction.

</details>


### [63] [VitronEdit: Universal Visual Editing across Images, Videos, and 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27049)
*Jing Wang,Yanlin Li,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VitronEdit 统一框架，用共享序列表示与渐进式多模态训练，将图像/视频编辑知识迁移到数据稀缺的 3D 场景编辑，并引入几何感知后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉编辑已把图像与视频编辑整合到单模型，但 3D 场景编辑仍相对独立，且受限于稀缺的成对训练数据。鉴于图像/视频编辑监督丰富且跨模态共享编辑语义，作者探究能否将这类知识迁移至数据稀缺的 3D 编辑。

Method: 采用共享序列表示（shared sequence representation）与渐进式多模态训练（progressive multimodal training）以跨模态迁移编辑知识；进一步引入几何感知后训练（geometry-aware post-training），利用预训练几何重建模型提供深度与相机位姿监督。

Result: 仅对 3D 样本施加几何监督即可取得最佳整体性能；图像与视频编辑虽未获直接几何监督也同步提升，说明几何正则通过共享骨干网络迁移。分析表明图像/视频训练主要向 3D 传递编辑语义，而 3D 监督主要提升多视图一致性（multi-view consistency）。

Conclusion: 跨模态知识迁移可有效缓解 3D 编辑的数据稀缺问题，统一骨干下的几何正则具备正向迁移效应，多模态训练与 3D 监督在语义迁移与多视图一致性上分工互补。

Abstract: Recent advances in unified visual editing have begun to consolidate image and video editing within a single model, while 3D scene editing remains largely separate and constrained by scarce paired training data. Given the rich image and video editing supervision now available and the common editing semantics shared across modalities, a natural question is whether this knowledge can be transferred to data-scarce 3D editing. We present VitronEdit, a universal framework for image, video, and 3D scene editing. VitronEdit adopts a shared sequence representation and progressive multimodal training to transfer editing knowledge across modalities. We further introduce geometry-aware post-training, which leverages a pretrained geometry reconstruction model to provide depth and camera-pose supervision. Surprisingly, applying geometric supervision only to 3D samples yields the best overall performance, while image and video editing also improve despite receiving no direct geometric supervision, suggesting that geometric regularization transfers through the shared backbone. Our analysis further shows that image and video training mainly transfers editing semantics to 3D, whereas 3D supervision primarily improves multi-view consistency. The project page: https://vitron-llm.github.io/VitronEdit/.

</details>


### [64] [Deep Auxiliary Learning via Bilevel Feature Optimization for Label-Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26954)
*Fuping Wu,Xi Jia,Le Zhang,Jiahua Li,Rima Mustafa,Thomas E. Nichols,Bartlomiej W. Papiez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于双层优化（bilevel optimization）的辅助学习框架（ALBO），其辅助任务标签由已有分割标注自动派生而无需额外人工标注，并通过特征通信模块在任务间传递知识，在三个医学图像分割基准的少标签设置下取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助学习可借助相关辅助任务提升主任务性能，但现有方法通常依赖人工标注的辅助标签，标注成本高，医学图像中专家标注尤为昂贵。作者因此探究：能否利用由现有分割标注自动派生的、无需额外标注的辅助任务来提升分割性能。

Method: 将辅助学习建模为双层优化问题，使辅助任务与主分割任务的优化相互嵌套；并设计特征通信模块实现任务间有效的知识迁移，从而增强主分割任务。

Result: 在三个医学图像分割基准的有限标签设置下进行评估，结果相对当前最先进方法取得一致提升，并表现出更优的标签效率。

Conclusion: 无需额外人工标注的自动派生辅助任务结合双层优化框架，可有效提升分割性能与标签效率，为低标注成本下的医学图像分割提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Auxiliary learning (AL), which can improve the performance of a primary task by leveraging related auxiliary tasks, is commonly regarded as a special case of multi-task learning. Existing AL approaches typically require manually annotated auxiliary labels, increasing annotation costs, particularly in medical imaging where expert labeling is expensive. In this work, we investigate whether segmentation performance can be improved using auxiliary tasks whose labels are automatically derived from existing segmentation annotations, requiring no additional manual labeling. To address this, we propose a novel auxiliary learning framework, formulated as a bilevel optimization problem. Our method enables effective knowledge transfer between tasks via a feature communication module, enhancing the primary segmentation task. We evaluate the method on three medical image segmentation benchmarks under limited-label settings. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior label efficiency. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/FupingWu90/ALBO.

</details>


### [65] [SuperClip Pyramid for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26925)
*Sanghyeok Lee,Juyeon Ko,Joonmyung Choi,Jongha Kim,Dohwan Ko,Ji Soo Lee,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCVTG，基于多尺度时间层次（SuperClip 金字塔）解决视频时序定位中语义边界与变长事件捕捉不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视频快速增长使视频时序定位（含 moment retrieval 与 highlight detection 两个子任务）愈发重要；现有 DETR 式解码器或时间金字塔结构依赖单尺度特征图或均匀下采样表示，难以捕捉语义边界与可变长度事件。

Method: 构建 SuperClip 金字塔：将相邻片段分组为语义连贯的 SuperClips 形成多尺度层次；引入 temporally adaptive kernel，以时间条件化权重聚合时长与位置各异的 SuperClips；并设计 relative saliency loss 引导模型学习片段间相对重要性。

Result: 在五个视频时序定位基准上均取得最佳性能，并带来明显提升。

Conclusion: 多尺度 SuperClip 时间层次结合自适应聚合与相对显著性损失，可有效提升视频时序定位的边界刻画与事件区分能力。

Abstract: With the rapid expansion of online video content, video temporal grounding has become essential for localizing moments based on user-specific queries, comprising two subtasks: moment retrieval (MR) and highlight detection (HD). While recent approaches adopt DETR-style decoders or temporal pyramid architectures, they rely on single-scale feature maps or uniformly downsampled representations, limiting their ability to capture semantic boundaries and variable-length events. In this paper, we present SuperClip Video Temporal Grounding (SCVTG) built on a multi-scale temporal hierarchy that better captures distinctive events at various granularities. SCVTG constructs a SuperClip pyramid by grouping adjacent clips into semantically coherent segments, termed SuperClips. Further, to aggregate these SuperClips having variable duration and temporal location, we introduce a temporally adaptive kernel that aggregates SuperClips through temporally conditioned weights, enabling flexible aggregation. On top of this, we design relative saliency losses to guide the model in learning inter-clip importance, enhancing its ability to distinguish relevant events. Extensive experiments show that SCVTG achieves the best performance across five video temporal grounding benchmarks with promising gains.

</details>


### [66] [Memory Internalization via Test-Time Training for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26910)
*Haonan Zhao,Jiaxuan Chen,Qingyang Liu,Shuochen Chang,Siyu Sun,Jiangtong Li,Li Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIT^3提出逐视频的测试时训练框架，将长视频写入紧凑的视频专属自适应参数，实现无视频、纯文本的问答；它用因果追踪选择写入层、用视觉记忆蒸馏保留视觉证据的内部效应，在三个长视频基准上以更少合成样本达到数据高效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需在同一视频上回答多问题，证据分散于长时序；常规视频语言模型对每个问题重复编码帧或检索视觉证据，成本高。参数化视频记忆只需写入一次，但现有方法主要依赖合成文本与固定适配配置，未解决"视频知识应写入何处"以及"视觉证据应如何塑造记忆"两个问题。

Method: 提出MIT^3（Memory Internalization via Test-Time Training）：1）逐视频适配，把每个长视频写为紧凑的视频专属自适应参数，供无视频纯文本问答使用；2）在紧凑多尺度监督下，用因果追踪（causal tracing）度量各层对视频落地回答的因果记忆效用，从而选择视频专属适配位置；3）视觉记忆蒸馏，训练纯文本记忆去复现相关视频片段所引发的表征变化，使视觉证据的内部效应超越文本监督被保留。

Result: 在LVBench、LongVideoBench与Video-MME上，MIT^3实现数据高效的无视频问答，所需合成样本显著少于先前参数化视频记忆方法。

Conclusion: 有效的视频内化取决于两点：知识写入的位置（层选择）与视觉证据蒸馏入模型的方式；二者结合可在无视频条件下高效完成长视频问答。

Abstract: Long-video understanding often requires answering many questions about a single video whose evidence is distributed across extended temporal contexts. Conventional video-language models repeatedly encode frames or retrieve visual evidence for every question. Parameterized video memory offers a write-once alternative, but existing methods rely primarily on synthesized text and fixed adaptation configurations, leaving open where video knowledge should be written and how visual evidence should shape the memory. We introduce Memory Internalization via Test-Time Training (MIT^3), a per-video adaptation framework that writes each long video into compact, video-specific adaptive parameters for video-free, text-only question answering. Under compact multi-scale supervision, causal tracing measures the causal memory utility of each model layer for video-grounded answering and selects video-specific adaptation locations. Visual memory distillation then trains the text-only memory to reproduce the representation change induced by the relevant video clip, preserving the internal effect of visual evidence beyond textual supervision. Across LVBench, LongVideoBench, and Video-MME, MIT^3 achieves data-efficient video-free question answering with substantially fewer synthesized samples than prior parameterized video-memory methods, demonstrating that effective video internalization depends on both where knowledge is written and how visual evidence is distilled into the model.

</details>


### [67] [CrossFit-GS: Out-of-Fold Pose Estimation for COLMAP-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28190)
*Zijian Wu,Jinliang Wang,Mingfeng Jiang,Zidian Lin,Ying Song,Ziqian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossFit-GS 提出一种延迟提交的渐进式无位姿 3DGS 框架，通过双互补子图交叉精炼位姿，抑制轨迹漂移与错误自增强循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无位姿 3DGS 直接从无位姿图像序列重建场景以绕开 SfM，但现有方法存在累积轨迹漂移，且畸变地图与位姿误差相互强化形成自增强循环。

Method: 将输入序列划分为交错子集并维护两个互补子图；每个候选位姿与一个从未用该帧优化过的冻结对侧子图交叉精炼，获得独立几何参考；用棋盘格验证与空间覆盖保护机制拒绝不可靠更新，仅提交已验证位姿至其所属子图；轨迹精炼完成后丢弃中间子图，用固定位姿从头训练最终全局表示。

Result: 多基准评测显示，相比现有方法，CrossFit-GS 显著降低累积轨迹漂移，并持续提升相机位姿估计与新视角合成质量。

Conclusion: 延迟提交与交叉参考设计有效打破畸变地图误差传播循环，为无位姿 3DGS 重建提供更稳健的位姿-几何联合优化范式。

Abstract: Pose-free 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) aims to bypass cumbersome Structure-from-Motion (SfM) by directly reconstructing scenes from unposed image sequences. In this work, we present CrossFit-GS, a deferred-commitment progressive framework that mitigates cumulative trajectory drift and breaks the self-reinforcing loop of distorted-map error propagation. Specifically, we partition the input sequence into interleaved subsets and maintain two complementary submaps. Each candidate pose is cross-refined against a frozen opposite submap that has never been optimized using the target frame, thereby providing an independent geometric reference for pose estimation. Checkerboard validation and spatial coverage safeguards further reject unreliable updates, and only verified poses are committed to their corresponding owner submap. Once the trajectory has been refined, the intermediate submaps are discarded and the final global representation is trained from scratch using the fixed refined poses. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that CrossFit-GS substantially reduces cumulative trajectory drift and consistently improves both camera pose estimation and novel-view synthesis over existing methods.

</details>


### [68] [DocLayout-YOLO: Axis-Attentive Document Layout Detector with Mesh-Guided Synthetic Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28171)
*Zhiyuan Zhao,Hengrui Kang,Guang Liang,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DocLayout-YOLO，通过合成文档预训练语料MeshLayout-1M与页面轴向布局注意力PALA，在异构文档上实现实时且高精度的版面分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YOLO虽是实时检测的主流范式，但其在异构文档版面分析上的适配尚不充分，缺乏面向多样文档布局的大规模预训练数据与有效的页面结构建模手段。

Method: 数据侧：提出Mesh-candidate BestFit算法生成合成预训练语料MeshLayout-1M，提供大规模、多样化文档布局用于预训练；模型侧：提出Page-Axis Layout Attention（PALA），沿水平与垂直页面轴向建模长程依赖且保持计算高效。

Result: 在D4LA、M6Doc、HJDataset、PRIMA-LAD四个异构文档基准上分别取得72.4%/75.5%/96.0%/73.1% mAP50:95，达到SOTA，并相较现有实时检测器具备更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 合成布局预训练与页面轴注意力可有效提升YOLO类实时检测器对异构文档的适应性，兼顾精度与效率，适用于现代文档解析系统。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient document layout analysis is critical for modern document parsing systems. However, although the YOLO framework represents a leading paradigm for the real-time detector, its adaptation to heterogeneous documents remains underexplored. To address this gap, we propose DocLayout-YOLO, a document-specialized real-time detector that integrates diverse synthetic document pretraining and page-axis context modeling. From the data perspective, we introduce MeshLayout-1M, a synthetic pretraining corpus generated by the proposed Mesh-candidate BestFit algorithm, which provides large-scale and diverse document layouts for pretraining and improves adaptation to heterogeneous document scenarios. From the model perspective, we propose Page-Axis Layout Attention (PALA), which captures long-range dependencies along the horizontal and vertical page axes while remaining computationally efficient. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DocLayout-YOLO achieves state-of-the-art performance on four heterogeneous document benchmarks, obtaining 72.4%/75.5%/96.0%/73.1% mAP50:95 on D4LA, M6Doc, HJDataset, and PRIMA-LAD, respectively. Compared with existing real-time detectors, DocLayout-YOLO establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off in document layout analysis. Code, model, and data are available at https://github.com/opendatalab/DocLayout-YOLO/tree/release-v2.

</details>


### [69] [PanoGraphGS: Unifying Panoptic Segmentation and Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27696)
*Jinghan Gao,Siyu Wang,Dongyu Feng,Yiyuan Pan,Rong Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoGraphGS：将全景3D高斯表示学习与分层3D场景图构建统一，实现跨视角一致的语义/实例预测，并发布NaviGS数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导导航依赖3D场景图，而构建此类结构化地图需要一种在跨视角下能一致区分语义区域与物体实例的场景表示。

Method: 在共享高斯几何上学习语义与实例特征：基于原型的语义学习和基于关联的实例学习获得跨视角一致的全景预测；表示支持新视角全景分割与高斯级全景解码；语义感知场景图模块恢复floor-room-stuff-object层级及包含与空间关系。

Result: 在Replica与NaviGS上，物体节点准确率平均比最强基线高50个百分点以上；2D全景分割在所评估方法中达到SOTA；并发布含3DGS、mesh、RGB、语义、深度的真实全景数据集NaviGS。

Conclusion: PanoGraphGS在分层3D场景理解与2D全景分割两方面均有效，验证了统一全景高斯表示与场景图构建框架的价值。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs support language-guided navigation by organizing physical entities and their containment and spatial relations into hierarchical maps. Building such structured maps requires a scene representation that consistently distinguishes semantic regions and object instances across viewpoints. To this end, we present PanoGraphGS, a unified framework that integrates panoptic 3D Gaussian representation learning with hierarchical 3D scene graph construction. The framework learns semantic and instance features on shared Gaussian geometry. Prototype-based semantic learning and association-based instance learning produce consistent panoptic predictions across viewpoints. The learned representation supports novel-view panoptic segmentation and direct Gaussian-level panoptic decoding. A semantic-aware scene graph module further recovers a floor-room-stuff-object hierarchy with containment and spatial relations. We further release NaviGS, a real-world dataset for panoramic scene understanding. The dataset provides 3DGS, mesh, RGB, semantic, and depth data. For 3D scene graph identification, PanoGraphGS outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline in object-node accuracy by more than 50\% points on average across Replica and NaviGS. PanoGraphGS also achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods in 2D panoptic segmentation.
