These results demonstrate the effectiveness of PanoGraphGS in both hierarchical 3D scene understanding and 2D panoptic segmentation. The project website is available at: https://github.com/gaojh0333/PanoGraphGS.

</details>


### [70] [EndoWorld-JEPA: Motion-Guided Global-Local Latent World Modeling for Endoscopic Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27890)
*Jie Wang,Shilong Yang,Tao Zhou,Juan Yu,Rong Qu,Jianfeng Ren,Zhicheng Zhang,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EndoWorld-JEPA：首个面向通用内窥镜视频表征学习的潜空间世界模型预训练框架，将被动内窥镜视频预训练重构为“因果运动引导的潜在状态预测”，以观测到的光流运动替代动作/位姿条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜视频包含由相机位移、组织形变与器械交互引发的状态转移，但现有预训练方法对动力学建模是隐式的。受动作条件化联合嵌入预测世界模型启发，且观察到内窥镜光流同时包含共享的图像平面位移与局部残余运动，作者希望显式利用运动作为预测条件。

Method: 提出光学运动引导模块，将主导位移与残余运动分离，并转化为有序时间运动条件与空间局部运动权重；全局预测器与运动局部预测器共享该输出：时间条件调制两个潜状态转移，局部权重定义辅助的运动局部状态，将预测从场景级演化扩展到空间选择性动力学；补丁级运动加权强调局部变化同时保留解剖上下文；引入过去运动窗口随机丢弃，整段丢弃运动条件以降低对不可靠运动的依赖；在表征空间中学习可预测的视觉—运动动力学，不重建随机外观伪影。

Result: 单个通用预训练检查点在四个下游基准上均取得强迁移表现，验证运动引导的全局—局部潜空间预测是有效的内窥镜视频预训练范式。

Conclusion: 以观测运动作为条件的全局—局部潜状态预测预训练范式能有效学习内窥镜视频表征，为通用内窥镜视觉基础模型提供新思路，且无需动作标签、标定位姿或机器人运动学。

Abstract: Endoscopic video contains state transitions induced by camera displacement, deformable tissue, and instrument interaction, yet existing pre-training methods treat dynamics implicitly. Inspired by action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive world models and by the observation that endoscopic optical flow contains both shared image-plane displacement and localized residual motion, we reformulate passive endoscopic video pre-training as causal motion-guided latent-state prediction. We present EndoWorld-JEPA, to our knowledge the first latent world-model pre-training framework designed for general-purpose endoscopic video representation learning. In place of recorded actions, calibrated poses, or robot kinematics, the model conditions prediction of the next latent state on motion observed up to the current state. Its Optical Motion-guided module separates dominant displacement from residual motion and converts the resulting evidence into an ordered temporal motion condition and spatial local-motion weights. Complementary global and motion-local predictors use these outputs: the temporal condition modulates both latent transitions, whereas the local weights define an auxiliary motion-local state that extends prediction from scene-level evolution to spatially selective dynamics. Patch-wise motion weighting emphasizes localized change while retaining anatomical context, and past-motion-window dropout randomly withholds the complete motion condition to reduce dependence on unreliable motion. This global--local formulation learns predictable visual--motion dynamics in representation space without reconstructing stochastic appearance artifacts. Evaluation shows that a common pre-trained checkpoint achieves strong transfer across four downstream benchmarks, supporting motion-guided global--local latent prediction as an effective pre-training paradigm for endoscopic video.

</details>


### [71] [Look Before You Reason: Implicit Visual Thinking for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27341)
*Zerui Chen,Changrui Chen,Fei Ni,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoRe框架，用单轮VLM交互实现隐式视觉思考：预测相关图像区域并强化对应KV缓存，无需显式工具调用，低开销提升细粒度多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要细粒度理解小目标或易忽略区域的推理任务上表现有限；已有的"以图思考"方法依赖多轮交互（缩放、裁剪、代码执行），导致冗余token计算、推理缓慢，且将区域检查与依赖其的推理步骤割裂。

Method: LoRe在单轮交互中：给定图像与问题，预测最相关的图像区域，将其与对应的图像patch特征对齐，并增强相关KV缓存条目，使后续解码直接利用选定的视觉线索，无需额外视觉输入或显式工具调用；并采用强化学习引导VLM定位信息量高的区域并在推理中利用它们。同时构建COCO-ObjQA大规模基准用于以对象为中心的多模态推理细粒度评测。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的多模态基准上，LoRe一致提升VLM推理性能，且额外推理开销低；并提供了COCO-ObjQA基准支持细粒度评测。

Conclusion: 隐式区域选择与KV缓存增强可在单轮交互内有效实现视觉思考，兼顾性能与效率，为细粒度多模态推理提供轻量方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal reasoning, yet they remain limited on tasks requiring fine-grained understanding of small or easily overlooked image regions. Recent thinking with images approaches mitigate this limitation by allowing models to inspect images through operations such as zooming, cropping, or code execution. While effective, their multi-turn interactions with VLMs introduce redundant token computation and slow inference, and separate region inspection from the reasoning step that depends on it. To address these issues, we propose Look Before You Reason (LoRe), an implicit visual thinking framework for efficient visual reasoning within a single-turn VLM interaction. Given an image and a question, LoRe predicts the most relevant image region, aligns it with the corresponding image patch features, and strengthens the associated key-value (KV) cache entries, enabling subsequent decoding to directly exploit the selected visual cues without additional visual inputs or explicit tool calls. We further employ reinforcement learning to guide the VLM toward identifying informative image regions and leveraging them during reasoning. To support fine-grained evaluation of multimodal reasoning, we introduce COCO-ObjQA, a large-scale benchmark for object-centric multimodal reasoning. Experiments on three challenging multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that LoRe consistently improves VLM reasoning performance with low additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [72] [Steering Vision-Language Models for Visual-Contextual Privacy in Location Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27323)
*Wenyue Chen,Yuhan Cheng,Ruixin Yang,Ethan Mendes,Alan Ritter,Jingwei Sun,Hai Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）地理定位披露中的视觉语境隐私问题，发现披露粒度可从生成前隐状态中恢复，并提出推理时"路由-引导"（route-and-steer）框架以对齐披露粒度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM能从细微视觉线索推断地理位置等敏感信息，其回答可能泄露超出视觉语境所支持的细节，形成隐私风险。核心诉求是让披露粒度与图像所描绘的视觉语境相匹配，同时避免过度披露与披露不足。

Method: 先验证语境适宜的披露粒度可从生成前隐状态中恢复（即使生成回答错位），且披露等级沿公共隐状态方向呈有序结构。据此提出推理时框架：序数粒度路由器将视觉语境隐私表征映射为披露路由，披露等级条件化引导模块在解码过程中施加路由特定的隐状态干预。

Result: 在四个VLM骨干模型上，该方法提升了视觉语境感知的披露对齐，更好地平衡过度披露与披露不足，对通用能力基准仅有适度影响；并可迁移至数据分布与隐私风险分类体系不同的另一数据集。

Conclusion: 隐状态层面的序数结构与推理时路由引导可有效实现VLM地理定位披露的语境适配隐私保护，具备跨骨干与跨数据集的泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer sensitive information such as geolocation from subtle visual cues, creating privacy risks when responses disclose more detail than the visual context warrants. We study visual-contextual privacy in geolocation disclosure, where a VLM should reveal location information only at the granularity appropriate to the visual context depicted in the image. We first show that this contextually appropriate granularity is recoverable from pre-generation hidden states, even when generated responses are misaligned, with disclosure levels exhibiting an ordered structure along a common hidden-state direction. Motivated by this, we propose an inference-time route-and-steer framework: an ordinal granularity router maps visual-contextual privacy representations to a disclosure route, and a disclosure-level-conditioned steering module applies route-specific hidden-state interventions during decoding. Across four VLM backbones, our method improves visual-context-aware disclosure alignment, better balances over- and under-disclosure, and incurs only modest changes on general capability benchmarks. It further transfers to another dataset with a distinct data distribution and privacy-risk taxonomy.

</details>


### [73] [Provisioning Robust and Efficient LiDAR Perception for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27617)
*Tingxu Han,Zijie Shen,An Guo,Lyuke Wang,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将LiDAR 3D目标检测的鲁棒性与资源效率统一建模为"受延迟与显存约束下的抗点云损坏检测器构建"问题，通过敏感性排序的难样本训练提升鲁棒性、通过窄化网络与邻域密度驱动的采样控制提升效率，在KITTI与nuScenes多架构多类别上同时提升损坏条件下AP并降低延迟与显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将鲁棒性与资源效率割裂处理：紧凑检测器在点云损坏下性能脆弱，鲁棒检测器部署代价高。自动驾驶实际部署需要同时满足抗损坏精度、推理延迟与峰值GPU显存约束，因此需要联合优化框架。

Method: 1) 构造模拟极端天气的点云损坏样本；2) 用七个异构3D检测器组成集成，通过置信度变化、BEV重叠度与未匹配检测数估计扰动敏感性，并按敏感性对干净/损坏样本排序，使检测器从难损坏样本中学到更多；3) 效率侧采用网络窄化，并借助离线邻域密度测量确定各采样阶段的点数与邻域数配置。

Result: KITTI Car（PointRCNN）上，损坏条件下Moderate AP提升3.32点，平均延迟降低19.8%，峰值分配显存降低53.9%；跨2个数据集、2种检测架构、3个类别的平均结果为：损坏AP提升4.03点，平均延迟降低14.4%，峰值显存降低38.0%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性与资源效率可在统一框架内协同优化，敏感性排序训练与密度感知的采样/网络窄化能显著改善"精度—延迟—显存"三角权衡，为可部署的LiDAR感知服务提供可行方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D object detection is a core perception service for autonomous driving, but deployment requires robustness to point-cloud corruption while meeting latency and memory requirements. Existing approaches address robustness and resource efficiency separately, resulting in compact detectors that are fragile under corruption or robust detectors that remain costly to deploy. We therefore formulate LiDAR perception provisioning as constructing a detector that remains accurate under point-cloud corruptions while meeting constraints on inference latency and peak GPU memory. To achieve robustness, we first craft corrupted samples that mimic extreme weather conditions. Then we train candidate detectors offline by ordering clean and corrupted samples by perturbation sensitivity, through which detectors learn more from hard corrupted samples. We estimate perturbation sensitivity using an ensemble of seven heterogeneous 3D detectors by tracking changes in confidence, BEV overlap, and unmatched detections. To improve efficiency, we narrow the network and use offline neighborhood-density measurements to determine how many points and neighbors each sampling stage processes. We evaluate our method using AP (Average Precision) under clean and corrupted conditions, inference latency, and peak allocated GPU memory. On KITTI for Car detection with PointRCNN, our method improves Moderate AP under corruption by 3.32 points over the original detector while reducing mean latency by 19.8% and peak allocated GPU memory by 53.9%. Across two datasets, two detector architectures, and three object classes, our method improves AP under corruption by 4.03 points on average over the corresponding original detectors, while reducing mean inference latency and peak allocated GPU memory by 14.4% and 38.0%, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/drunklibai/robust-efficient-lidar-perception.

</details>


### [74] [Causal Spatiotemporal State Evolution for Monocular 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27594)
*Qiaowei Miao,Yijing Guo,Lipei Zhang,Qifeng Cheng,Yuru Xiao,Ziqian Ni,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出前馈框架，用因果时空Mamba（CSM）实现方向性状态传播，并以统一运动监督（UMS）约束帧间状态转移，从单目视频学习因果4D视觉几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前馈模型从静态3D几何扩展到动态4D场景需把视觉几何建模为因果演化状态；现有方法采用无序时间聚合，缺乏物理时间的方向性结构，且监督中几何与运动相互割裂，缺少对时间状态转移的统一约束，导致4D表示不一致。

Method: 核心为Causal Spatiotemporal Mamba：在时间聚合中引入方向性状态传播，通过Temporal-Spatial与Spatial-Temporal互补扫描跨帧连续传播几何感知状态；并提出Unified Motion Supervision，利用相机与深度头的几何感知预测构建与底层3D结构一致的前向运动场，作为几何接地的帧间状态转移约束。

Result: 在动态基准上的大量评估显示，在视频深度、相机位姿与光流估计上均达到SOTA；显著降低时间漂移，增强时空一致性。

Conclusion: 方向性状态传播与统一运动监督的协同可有效捕捉连续场景演化，是提升4D表示时空一致性的有效途径。

Abstract: While feed-forward models excel in static 3D geometry estimation, extending them to dynamic 4D scenes requires modeling visual geometry as a causally evolving state. Current methods typically rely on unordered temporal aggregation, which lacks the directional structure of physical time. Furthermore, existing supervision often treats geometry and motion as disjoint objectives, failing to exploit their inherent physical coupling. Consequently, they lack a unified constraint to effectively supervise temporal state transitions, leading to inconsistent 4D scene representations. To address this, we propose a feed-forward framework for causal 4D visual geometry learning from monocular videos. At its core, Causal Spatiotemporal Mamba augments temporal aggregation with directional state propagation. Through complementary Temporal-Spatial and Spatial-Temporal Scans, CSM continuously propagates geometry-aware states across frames to model spatiotemporal evolution. To align this causal state evolution with observable dynamics, we introduce Unified Motion Supervision. Rather than treating motion as an independent objective, UMS leverages geometry-aware predictions from the camera and depth heads to construct a unified forward motion field that is consistent with the underlying 3D structure. This geometry-grounded supervision explicitly constrains inter-frame state transitions, enabling stable states propagation across frames. Extensive evaluations on dynamic benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across video depth, camera pose, and optical flow estimation. These results validate that the synergy between the directional state propagation of Causal Spatiotemporal Mamba and Unified Motion Supervision effectively captures continuous scene evolution, significantly reducing temporal drift and reinforcing spatial-temporal consistency in 4D representation learning.

</details>


### [75] [SynCalib: Breaking Dataset Shortcuts in LiDAR-Camera Calibration via Extrinsic-Controllable Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27547)
*Huihua Zhu,Shuyang Zhang,Dongqing Chen,Zhuo Yu,Jianhao Jiao,Yilong Zhu,Siyu Teng,Xiaopin Zhong,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向LiDAR-相机标定网络训练的高泛化数据生成框架，利用带深度真值的全景图像数据集，在严格外参约束下合成多模态标定数据，缓解现有方法的"数据集捷径"（外参多样性不足导致过拟合固定标称外参）问题，并验证其零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线LiDAR-相机标定是机器人领域的基础难题。近期基于深度学习的数据驱动方法具备高精度、无靶标标定能力，但训练数据中外参多样性不足，导致网络学习到"数据集捷径"，过拟合于固定标称外参，在真实部署中性能严重退化、泛化性差。因此需要一种能提供充分外参多样性的数据生成方案。

Method: 设计通用的数据生成框架：以现有的、带深度真值标注的全景（omnidirectional）图像数据集为基础，在严格的外参约束下合成LiDAR-相机多模态标定数据，从而在训练数据中引入丰富且可控的外参变化，用于训练标定网络。

Result: 大量实验表明，该流水线生成的数据显著提升了典型学习型标定网络的泛化性与鲁棒性；并在多种相机-LiDAR配置下开展真实世界评测，验证了仅用合成数据训练的模型具备零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过可控外参的数据合成有效缓解了数据集捷径问题，为学习型LiDAR-相机标定提供了可泛化的训练数据来源；数据生成代码、预训练模型与在线基准数据集将公开，以促进后续研究与社区使用。

Abstract: Online LiDAR-camera calibration remains a fundamental yet challenging task in the field of robotics. Recently, deep learning-based data-driven approaches have emerged, offering high-precision, targetless calibration capabilities. However, existing methods often suffer from a critical dataset shortcut phenomenon due to the lack of extrinsic diversity in their training data. Networks tend to overfit to fixed nominal extrinsic configurations, leading to severe performance degradation and poor generalization in real-world deployments. To address this limitation, we propose a highly generalizable data generation framework tailored for training LiDAR-camera calibration networks. Our framework leverages existing omnidirectional image datasets annotated with depth ground truth to synthesize multi-modal calibration data under rigorous extrinsic constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the data generated by our pipeline significantly enhances the generalization and robustness of typical learning-based calibration networks. Furthermore, we conduct comprehensive real-world evaluations across various camera-LiDAR configurations, validating the zero-shot transfer capability of models trained exclusively on our synthetic data. To facilitate future research and community use, the code for data generation, the pre-trained models, and online benchmark datasets will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [76] [Multiscale Temporal-Spectral-Spatial EEG Learning With Modality-Specific and Modality-Invariant Representations for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27521)
*Jian Zhang,Manrong Liu,Yilei Huang,Wenhua Liu,Xiaohui Yang,Lijun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GMSI-Net是一个以EEG为核心的多模态情感识别框架，通过TSSR编码器、模态特异表示分支与模态不变表示分支（自适应加权对比损失对齐），在DEAP与MAHNOB-HCI上取得约94%–96%的效价/唤醒度识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生理信号可为情感识别提供互补线索，但存在两方面挑战：一是情感相关EEG模式在时间、频带、电极与脑区上变化显著；二是不同生理模态既含模态特有信息又含共享情感信息，需兼顾建模。

Method: 提出GMSI-Net，含三部分：(1) 时空谱区（TSSR）编码器，建模多尺度时间-通道模式、微分熵特征及局部到全局脑区关系；(2) 模态特异表示（MSR）分支，采用双向跨模态注意力与可学习门控控制信息传递并保留模态特有线索；(3) 模态不变表示（MIR）分支，学习共享情感表示并用自适应加权对比损失实现跨模态对齐。外周生理信号由独立的各模态多尺度Conv-Transformer编码器处理。

Result: 在被试内随机十折交叉验证下，DEAP上效价与唤醒度分别达96.21(1.47)与96.41(1.60)；MAHNOB-HCI上分别达94.10(2.86)与94.48(3.77)。

Conclusion: 将EEG特征与互补生理信息结合可提升情感识别性能，验证了模态特异与模态不变表示联合建模的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal physiological signals provide complementary cues for emotion recognition. They also raise two related challenges. Emotion-related EEG patterns vary across time, frequency bands, electrodes, and brain regions. Different physiological modalities also contain both modality-specific and shared affective information. This paper presents GMSI-Net, an EEG-centered multimodal emotion recognition framework. It has three key components. The temporal-spatial-spectral-region (TSSR) encoder models multiscale temporal-channel patterns, differential entropy features, and local-to-global brain-region relations. The modality-specific representation (MSR) branch uses bidirectional cross-modal attention and learnable gates. It controls information transfer while retaining modality-specific cues. The modality-invariant representation (MIR) branch learns a shared affective representation. An adaptively weighted contrastive loss aligns this representation across modalities. Independent multiscale Conv-Transformer encoders process the peripheral physiological signals. GMSI-Net is evaluated on DEAP and MAHNOB-HCI using within-subject random ten-fold cross-validation. The model reaches 96.21 (1.47) and 96.41 (1.60) for valence and arousal on DEAP. It reaches 94.10 (2.86) and 94.48 (3.77) on MAHNOB-HCI. These results indicate that combining EEG features with complementary physiological information can improve emotion recognition.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-Visibility-Gap Identity Persistence for 4D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27505)
*Jiahui Hu,Li Gao,Yu Liu,Pengzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CVG-SGM记忆模块，通过"目标—载体绑定"的关系历史实现跨可见性间隙的身份保持，在受控与真实特征级数据上显著优于无记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D场景图中物体隐入不透明载体后重现时成为新检测，外观与目标运动线索不可用，需依赖关系历史恢复身份。

Method: 将跨可见性间隙身份保持建模为经由记忆中的目标—载体绑定的历史节点恢复；设计transfer–maintain–read记忆：几何加权跨节点交互将目标证据写入载体状态，共享逐节点GRU在运输中维持，双时刻读出融合载体—身份兼容性与当前检测—载体几何。

Result: 单一身份单载体受控设定下重链接准确率1.000，无记忆与参数量匹配的无写入对照均为0.570；可泛化到场景不重叠的更长间隙；真实RGB-D/SAM2特征级片段重链接36/36（最强固定载体—身份对照28/36）；反事实载体记忆交换可按绑定重定向预测。

Conclusion: 存储的关系信息对身份恢复具因果作用；局限为共享载体下的身份歧义，且未实现端到端原始视频重识别。

Abstract: 4D scene graphs require persistent object identity when an object disappears inside an opaque carrier and later reappears as a fresh detection. When appearance and target-centric motion are unavailable, identity recovery must rely on relational history. We formulate cross-visibility-gap identity persistence as historical-node recovery through a remembered target--carrier binding.
  We introduce CVG-SGM, a perception-frontend-compatible transfer--maintain--read memory. Geometry-weighted cross-node interaction transfers target-specific evidence into carrier states, a shared per-node GRU maintains this information during transport, and a two-time readout combines stored carrier--identity compatibility with current detection--carrier geometry to recover the historical identity.
  In the controlled one-identity-per-carrier setting, CVG-SGM achieves 1.000 re-link accuracy, compared with 0.570 for both no-memory and parameter-count-matched no-write controls. It also generalizes to scene-disjoint, unseen longer visibility gaps. On real RGB-D/SAM2 feature-level episodes, it correctly re-links 36/36 held-out detections, compared with 28/36 for the strongest fixed carrier--identity control. Counterfactual carrier-memory swaps redirect predictions according to the swapped bindings, supporting the role of stored relational information in identity recovery. The current approach remains limited by shared-carrier identity ambiguity and does not provide end-to-end raw-video re-identification.

</details>


### [78] [RoboJudge: Multimodal Language Models as Judges for Embodied Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27782)
*Siyuan Ma,Yu Qi,Cheng Ye,Ruotian Peng,Xinyi Xu,Zitiantao Lin,Zedong Cai,Yijian Huang,Weiyang Liu,Siyuan Wang,Yandong Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoboJudge-Bench基准系统评估MLLM作为具身视频生成裁判的能力，并构建RoboJudge-Train训练出RoboJudge-9B，在物理遵循与指令对齐两维度取得最高人类一致性（Pearson 0.719）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型要求生成的视频 rollout 在物理上有效且忠实于任务指令，MLLM 被广泛用作评判器，但其与人类判断在具身视频上的一致性缺乏系统评估，存在评测空白。

Method: 构建RoboJudge-Bench：覆盖800段人工标注视频、8个源语料库、10个视频生成器，涵盖夹爪、人手与灵巧手，评测Physical Adherence与Instruction Alignment两维度。基于诊断结果构建RoboJudge-Train（12K视频、25K人工核验评分及模型辅助理由），对9B MLLM先监督微调再强化学习，得到RoboJudge-9B。

Result: 现有裁判与人类评分一致性不均衡：通用MLLM在物理遵循上一致性偏低，通用领域专用视频裁判不能稳定迁移到具身场景。RoboJudge-9B取得最高总体一致性，Pearson 0.719，较最佳专有基线Gemini-3.7-Flash显著提升0.089；在VideoPhy2、Physion-Eval等外部基准上亦有增益。

Conclusion: 训练专用具身视频裁判可显著提升与人类判断的一致性并具备跨基准泛化性；RoboJudge-9B可作为下游评估器，按Physical Adherence与Instruction Alignment对视频生成模型进行基准测试。

Abstract: Video generation models are promising foundations for embodied world models, but only if their generated rollouts are physically valid and faithful to task instructions. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to judge these properties, yet their agreement with human judgments on embodied videos has not been systematically evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce RoboJudge-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs as judges of embodied video generation. The benchmark measures two fundamental dimensions, Physical Adherence and Instruction Alignment, across 800 human-annotated videos from 8 source corpora and 10 video generators, spanning grippers, human hands, and dexterous hands. Experiments reveal uneven alignment between existing judges and human ratings: general-purpose MLLMs show lower agreement on physical adherence, while specialized video judges designed for general-domain physical reasoning do not consistently transfer to embodied scenarios. Motivated by these findings, we construct RoboJudge-Train, comprising 12K videos annotated with 25K human-verified scores and model-assisted rationales for Physical Adherence and Instruction Alignment. We fine-tune a 9B MLLM on this dataset with supervised learning followed by reinforcement learning, yielding RoboJudge-9B. Among the model judges evaluated on RoboJudge-Bench, RoboJudge-9B achieves the highest overall agreement, reaching a Pearson correlation of 0.719 with a statistically significant 0.089 gain over Gemini-3.7-Flash, the best-performing proprietary baseline in our evaluation. Training on RoboJudge-Train also yields gains on external benchmarks, including VideoPhy2 and Physion-Eval. We further use RoboJudge-9B as a downstream evaluator to benchmark video generation models according to Physical Adherence and Instruction Alignment.

</details>


### [79] [World2HOSI: A Multi-Expert Harness for Long-Horizon Human-Object-Scene Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27725)
*Jiushu Li,Haoran Wang,Yumeng Liu,Zhiyang Dou,Yuan Liu,Yuexin Ma,Cheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出World2HOSI多专家编排框架，从3DGS场景与语言指令生成连续的长时程人-物-场景交互数据，通过持久化场景状态闭环编排现有专家并保证物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程人-物-场景交互（HOSI）对具身智能、动画与游戏至关重要，但现有HOI与HSI模型各自只覆盖单一任务技能，缺乏统一能力；而训练统一HOSI模型受限于稀缺且昂贵的数据。

Method: 核心洞见是长时程HOSI依赖编排而非单一更强模型。World2HOSI以解耦的背景与物体资产构建持久化结构化场景状态，形成闭环：编排harness在该状态上规划动作阶段，通过各专家原生条件接口调用之，并将每个动作的效果写回状态，使每次调用都锚定于演化中的世界；同时用有界残差优化与人-物接触驱动的物理仿真分别保证人体运动与物体操作的物理合理性，并保持交互意图。

Result: 在评测的HOSI任务上，World2HOSI取得最佳任务完成度与交互质量，且生成的数据多样性更高；感知用户研究进一步验证了上述结论。

Conclusion: 通过共享场景状态的编排框架，现有专家可协同完成任何单一专家无法实现的长时程任务，为统一HOSI数据生成提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon human-object-scene interaction (HOSI) is central to embodied AI, animation, and game development. Existing human-object interaction (HOI) and human-scene interaction (HSI) models excel in their respective domains, but none alone covers tasks requiring multiple interaction skills. Training unified HOSI models is hindered by scarce and costly data. We introduce World2HOSI, a multi-expert orchestration harness that generates continuous HOSI data from 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes and natural language instructions. Our key insight is that long-horizon HOSI hinges not on a stronger single model but on orchestration. Given a shared, structured scene state and an orchestration harness grounded in it, existing experts can jointly accomplish tasks beyond the reach of any of them alone. World2HOSI realizes this insight as a closed loop around a persistent scene state built from decoupled background and object assets. A harness plans action stages over this state, invokes experts via their native conditioning interfaces, and writes each action's effects back, so that every invocation is grounded in the evolving world. To reduce physical artifacts, bounded residual optimization and contact-driven physics simulation further ensure the physical plausibility of human motion and object manipulation, respectively, while preserving interaction intent. Experiments and a perceptual user study show that World2HOSI achieves the best task completion and interaction quality on the evaluated HOSI tasks, while generating more diverse data.

</details>


### [80] [Controllable Multi-Object Insertion for Detection Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26922)
*Jiyang Huang,Tianwei Zhang,Xiaoqin Zhang,Ling Shao,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneP2F：两阶段框架，将场景感知的联合规划与布局对齐生成相结合，实现可控目标插入以增广检测数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控目标插入可在类别-数量保真、标注对齐与源内容保持方面增广检测数据集，但现有方法在精确类别-数量控制、视觉一致性、跨类别/实例协调以及特定类别多目标定位上存在局限。

Method: 两阶段可训练框架。①ScenePlan：为每个请求实例分配一个类别标签查询，联合预测场景兼容的目标中心，并基于规划中心与场景上下文分别估计框尺寸；②SceneAlign-Fill：几何与外观感知条件、框-类引导的区域注意力，以及标注区域加权的流匹配目标，渲染规划目标并抑制对源内容的非预期改动。

Result: 在UAVDT、VisDrone、BDD100K三个基准上，持续优于代表性通用编辑方法与目标插入方法。

Conclusion: 协调实例级规划与生成对类别定向的检测数据增广具有明确价值。

Abstract: Controllable object insertion provides a targeted approach to augmenting object detection datasets that require category--count fidelity, annotation alignment, and source-content preservation beyond visual realism. Several approaches have been proposed to address this grand challenge, but most suffer from different constraints in precise category--count requests and visual consistency, limited coordination across categories and instances, and poor localization of multiple objects of specific category and count. We design SceneP2F, a two-stage trainable framework that connects scene-aware joint planning with layout-aligned generation leveraging explicit instance-level layouts. SceneP2F consists of two novel designs. The first is ScenePlan which allocates one class-labeled query per requested instance, jointly predicts scene-compatible object centers, and separately estimates box sizes conditioned on the planned centers and scene context. The second is SceneAlign-Fill which combines geometry- and appearance-aware conditioning, box--class-guided regional attention, and an annotation-region-weighted flow-matching objective to render the planned objects while limiting unintended changes to source content. Extensive experiments on three challenging benchmarks UAVDT, VisDrone, and BDD100K show that SceneP2F outperforms representative general-purpose editors and object-insertion methods consistently, demonstrating the value of coordinating instance-level planning and generation for category-targeted detection augmentation.

</details>


### [81] [BUMP: Body-driven Unified Multi-object Physics for HOI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27066)
*Wonjung Woo,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BUMP，一种文本驱动的多物体人-物交互生成框架，核心思想是"学习意图运动、模拟物理结果"：先生成全身人体运动，再预测少量被意图操控的驱动物体轨迹，其余物体通过物理仿真获得运动，从而避免扩大轨迹输出空间，并用异构监督缓解数据稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体HOI生成困难，因为不同运动有不同成因：人体运动源于语义意图，被操控物体需与人体协调，周围物体主要由接触与碰撞的物理后果驱动。现有方法难以统一建模这种异质性，且缺少配对的全身体多物体HOI数据。

Method: 1）由文本与场景上下文生成全身人体运动；2）以人体运动为条件，预测少量被意图操控的"驱动物体"轨迹；3）通过物理仿真得到"响应物体"运动，可适应任意数量与配置的周围物体；4）结合仅人体运动、全身体HOI、以手为中心的操控数据（配以合理身体运动）三类异构监督；5）提出结果条件化的交互编辑，从期望的响应物体行为反推驱动物体轨迹的局部修改，无需显式目标轨迹。

Result: 在单物体与多物体HOI基准上均取得良好性能；定性结果展示了涉及物体间关系与编辑的多样场景，体现统一模型表述的优势。

Conclusion: 将"意图运动学习"与"物理后果仿真"分离的统一建模范式，可有效支撑多物体HOI生成与交互编辑，并具备良好扩展性。

Abstract: Generating human-object interactions (HOIs) with multiple objects is challenging because the resulting motions arise from different underlying causes. Human motion reflects semantic intent, intentionally manipulated objects must remain coordinated with the human, while surrounding objects move primarily as physical consequences of contact and collision. We present BUMP, a text-driven framework for multi-object HOI generation based on a simple principle: learn intentional motion and simulate physical consequences. Our method first generates full-body human motion from text and scene context, then predicts trajectories for a small number of intentionally manipulated driver objects conditioned on the human motion. We then obtain responder object motions through physics simulation, allowing the framework to handle varying numbers and configurations of surrounding objects without expanding the learned trajectory output space. To address the scarcity of paired full-body multi-object HOI data, we combine heterogeneous supervision from human-only motion, full-body HOI, and hand-centric manipulation data augmented with plausible body motion. We further introduce outcome-conditioned interaction editing, which infers a local modification of the causal driver object trajectory from a desired responder object behavior without requiring an explicit target driver object trajectory. Experiments demonstrate strong performance on both single-object and multi-object HOI benchmarks. Qualitative results showcase diverse scenarios involving inter-object relationships and edits, highlighting the advantage of our unified model formulation.

</details>


### [82] [Robustness of Internal Signals for Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27041)
*Allison Chen,William Yang,Salma Abdel Magid,Jonathan Williams,Olga Russakovsky,Esin Tureci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文深入分析基于VLM内部信号检测物体幻觉的方法的鲁棒性，揭示其依赖token位置捷径、偏向语言模态幻觉等局限，并证明集成互补信号仍可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法利用视觉语言模型生成文本时的内部信号来检测物体幻觉，但这些信号可能正是导致幻觉的信号本身，其鲁棒性与可靠性存疑，亟需系统性的深入分析。

Method: （1）以生成词在响应中的token位置作为朴素基线进行对比，检验各检测方法是否把token位置作为捷径；（2）分析检测方法对语言模态与视觉模态的敏感性，考察其视觉落地程度及幻觉出现在文本较早位置时的鲁棒性；（3）将不同检测方法进行集成，验证互补信号的存在。

Result: token位置的朴素基线即可达到有竞争力的检测性能；部分方法实际依赖token位置捷径，因而视觉落地性较弱、对早期幻觉鲁棒性下降；强检测性能多由语言类幻觉驱动；集成时即便加入较弱方法，也仍能带来性能提升。

Conclusion: VLM内部信号虽可能含有可用于幻觉检测的信息，但当前利用这些信号的方法存在重要局限，需谨慎对待其性能来源。

Abstract: When given an image and a text prompt, vision-language models (VLMs) can produce seemingly sophisticated text responses. However, they are also prone to hallucinate objects in their output, which can have detrimental consequences in safety-critical applications. To detect these hallucinations, certain methods interestingly leverage internal signals from the VLM as it generates the text response; potentially, these methods use the same signals that led to a hallucination to detect it. We conduct an in-depth analysis to understand the robustness of these detection methods. First, we show that a naive baseline of a generated word's token position in the VLM text response achieves competitive performance. Further, we reveal how some detection methods rely on token position and use this heuristic as a shortcut. Second, we explore detection methods' sensitivity to language and vision modalities. Our experiments suggest that strong detection performance is often driven by detecting language-based hallucinations, and when methods rely on token position, they tend to be less visually grounded and exhibit reduced robustness when hallucinations occur earlier in a text response. Lastly, we demonstrate that different detection methods can carry complementary signals; when ensembling methods together, adding weaker methods to an ensemble of higher performing ones still offers performance improvements. Altogether, while there may be useful internal VLM signals to detect hallucinations, our investigation reveals important limitations of the current methods that try to leverage them.

</details>


### [83] [LatticeRank: Spacing-Ranked Consistency for Native-Grid 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28091)
*Jingjia Yan,Yue Zhuo,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅训练阶段使用的 LatticeRank 框架，按体素间距排序分配一致性监督，使3D分割网络可直接在原始网格上推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变体素间距改变的是离散采样格点、张量几何与可用解剖证据，候选网格并非等价视图：粗网格丢失的结构信息多于轻度重采样网格，而标准一致性目标却赋予其相同权重。

Method: LatticeRank 首先在物理空间中（而非索引空间）对齐各候选网格的后验概率，再依据体素间距顺序分配一致性监督权重：在端点匹配的候选中，辅助损失集中于所请求的最粗网格；推理阶段仍只做单次前向，不引入候选分支或集成。

Result: 在 MSD Task04 上，跨五个随机种子，粗网格 Extra Dice 较均值一致性提升 0.03436，同时满足预设的参考网格非劣性判据。消融阶梯实验显示：网格暴露带来最大恢复，后验一致性提供二次增益，按间距排序分配给出最强的直接网格结果。

Conclusion: 所请求的体素间距可作为简单、与模型无关的排序信号，用于直接网格一致性学习；该方法为纯训练期正则，不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Variable voxel spacing changes more than image resolution: it alters the discrete sampling lattice, tensor geometry, and anatomical evidence available to a 3D segmentation network. While conventional pipelines restore images to a canonical grid, direct-grid deployment requires the model itself to tolerate these changes. We observe that candidate grids are not exchangeable views -- a coarse grid removes more structural information than a mildly resampled one -- yet standard consistency objectives assign them equal influence. We therefore propose LatticeRank, a training-only framework that aligns candidate posteriors in physical space and allocates consistency supervision by voxel-spacing order: among endpoint-matched candidates, the auxiliary loss concentrates on the coarsest requested grid, while inference retains a single network forward with no candidate branch or ensemble. On MSD Task04, LatticeRank improves coarse-grid Extra Dice over mean consistency by 0.03436 across five seeds while meeting the prespecified reference-grid noninferiority criterion. A controlled ladder shows that grid exposure provides the largest recovery, posterior consistency adds a second gain, and spacing-ranked allocation yields the strongest direct-grid result; requested spacing thus provides a simple, model-independent ordering signal for direct-grid consistency learning.

</details>


### [84] [WildAIGC-Bench: Can AI Image Detectors Handle the Wild? A Benchmark of Generated and Edited Images in Online Circulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28087)
*Zijian Yu,Hangwei Zhang,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 WildAIGC-Bench，一个通过持续采集新闻网站、事实核查来源及社交媒体内容构建的、包含 407 张经人工验证的 AI 生成/编辑图像基准数据集，揭示现有检测器在真实网络条件下的显著召回缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成图像与新闻事件、网络声明关联，可能误导受众、助长欺诈或引发公共争议；而现有基准多为受控条件下构造，缺乏真实场景中压缩、截图、二次拍摄等干扰，检测器在野外场景的泛化能力不明确。

Method: 构建流水线：定期发现 + 自动筛选与初步标注 + 针对图像证据的人工复核；数据源覆盖新闻网站、事实核查机构与相关社交媒体；每张图像附带来源网页链接与证据摘要，保留网络传播中的真实退化条件（压缩、截图、重拍摄）。评估对象包括专用视觉检测器、多模态大语言模型及简单的 Perception Encoder 基线。

Result: 数据集共 407 张经核验的 AI 生成或编辑图像；实验显示上述检测方法相对于现有基准存在显著的召回率差距（recall gaps）。

Conclusion: WildAIGC-Bench 既提供贴近真实的检测挑战，也为持续的数据扩展与基于来源的验证奠定基础；数据集已开源发布。

Abstract: AI-generated images linked to news events and online claims can mislead audiences, enable fraud, or cause public controversy. We introduce WildAIGC-Bench, built through recurring collection across news websites, fact-checking sources, and related social media content. The pipeline combines broad coverage, timely updates, and human-verified labels through scheduled discovery, automatic screening and preliminary annotation, and human review of image-specific evidence. The benchmark contains 407 verified AI-generated or edited images, each accompanied by a source webpage link and an evidence summary. Images retain the conditions encountered online, including compression, screenshots, and recapture. Evaluation of dedicated visual detectors, multimodal large language models, and a simple Perception Encoder baseline reveals substantial recall gaps relative to existing benchmarks. WildAIGC-Bench provides both a practical detection challenge and a basis for continued data expansion and source-based verification. The dataset is released at https://github.com/YuZijian/WildAIGC-Bench.

</details>


### [85] [Spherical Geometry-Aware Panoptic Scene Graph Generation for Equirectangular Panoramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28033)
*Zhe Wu,Xiaoyan Fei,Xinyi Jiang,Yanxu Wei,Junming Rao,Yihong Yan,Xuewei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段球面几何感知框架，将全景PSGG从ERP投影转为多视角透视预测融合，并构建340张高清全景数据集，显著提升PQ与mR指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP全景图存在几何畸变、随纬度变化的像素面积以及人为接缝，直接用于全景场景图生成（PSGG）效果受限。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段融合重叠透视视角的预测，依据观测可靠性、球面重叠度与角邻近度加权；第二阶段关系推理采用球面面积感知聚合、加权无关系监督与双经度分数融合。同时构建340张高分辨率全景、10,571个节点、6,632条有向关系的数据集。

Result: 相同视角条件下，球面融合使PQ提升36.03%；在PredCls设定（真值掩码与物体标签）下，关系推理使mR@50提升11.20%；完整框架在SGDet设定（预测全景分割）下mR@50提升52.99%。

Conclusion: 球面几何建模对全景PSGG具有显著价值，验证了多视角融合与球面关系推理的有效性。

Abstract: Panoptic scene graph generation (PSGG) represents scene entities with segmentation masks and directed relations. Equirectangular projection (ERP) panoramas challenge PSGG through distortion, latitude-dependent pixel areas, and an artificial seam. We propose a two-stage spherical geometry-aware framework that fuses overlapping perspective-view predictions using observation reliability, spherical overlap, and angular proximity. Relation reasoning combines spherical-area-aware aggregation, weighted no-relation supervision, and dual-longitude score fusion. We also construct a dataset of 340 high-resolution panoramas with 10,571 nodes and 6,632 directed relations. Against corresponding baselines, spherical fusion improves PQ by 36.03% under matched views, while relation reasoning improves mR@50 by 11.20% under PredCls with ground-truth masks and object labels. The full framework improves mR@50 by 52.99% under SGDet with predicted panoptic segments. These results demonstrate the value of spherical geometry for panoramic PSGG.

</details>


### [86] [AdaptMIL: Domain Adaptation for Multiple Instance Learning in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28007)
*Ben Vardi,Dana Schonberger,Yuval Friedmann,Zohar Yakhini,Iris Barshack,Alexander Loebel,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaptMIL两阶段训练策略，将域适应解耦为表示级域对齐与MIL聚合学习，提升病理基础模型在跨医院分布偏移下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型（PFMs）通过多实例学习（MIL）支持全切片图像（WSI）的多种组织病理任务，但对跨医院分布偏移敏感——任务头在一家医院数据上训练后用于另一家医院时性能常显著下降。作者发现直接在MIL训练中施加域适应目标会干扰MIL聚合学习，甚至劣于标准训练。

Method: AdaptMIL：一种将域适应融入MIL训练的两阶段训练策略。第一阶段进行表示级域对齐学习，第二阶段进行MIL聚合学习，从而解耦两类目标，避免相互干扰。

Result: 在多个WSI数据集、多种任务、多种PFM、多种MIL架构及多种域适应目标上的实验表明，AdaptMIL提升跨医院性能并优于其他适应方法。

Conclusion: 解耦域对齐与MIL聚合学习是缓解PFMs跨医院分布偏移的有效途径；代码将在论文发表后开源。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) support a wide range of histopathology tasks on whole-slide images through multiple instance learning (MIL). Despite recent progress, these models remain sensitive to cross-hospital distribution shifts: performance often degrades when a task head (e.g., classification) is trained on data from one hospital and then used on data from another hospital. To address this challenge, we propose AdaptMIL, a training strategy for integrating domain adaptation into MIL training. AdaptMIL is motivated by our finding that directly applying domain adaptation objectives during MIL training can interfere with MIL aggregation learning, often resulting in performance degradation over standard training. AdaptMIL resolves this by decoupling representation-level domain alignment learning from MIL aggregation learning through a two-stage training procedure. We show that AdaptMIL improves cross-hospital performance and outperforms alternative adaptation methods across experiments spanning multiple WSI datasets, tasks, PFMs, MIL architectures, and domain adaptation objectives. AdaptMIL's code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [87] [Edit in Sync: Unified End-to-End Audio-Visual Editing for Human-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27947)
*Ziye Yuan,Minghong Cai,Xu He,Ruchang Yao,Xiao Fu,Kaixin Ding,Ming Zhang,Xiangyu Du,Tianpeng Lan,Da Xie,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个统一的端到端音视频编辑框架AVE，无需空间掩码即可联合完成基于参考的人物替换、插入与删除，并构建了包含10万对含外貌与音色参考的音视频编辑数据集AVE-Dataset。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以人为中心的音视频编辑方法采用独立的视频与音频流程，缺乏跨模态交互，导致唇音同步差、流水线误差累积；且多依赖掩码修复，存在掩码尺寸的两难问题。

Method: 设计统一的端到端框架，联合建模音频与视频模态；构建合成数据流水线生成AVE-Dataset（含外貌与语音参考的10万对数据）；采用“合成到真实重建”策略结合预先音频预热，缓解合成数据局限并改善音视频token不平衡；全程不使用空间掩码。

Result: 在编辑真实感、时间一致性和音视频对齐三项指标上均优于音视频分离的编辑流水线。

Conclusion: 统一跨模态建模范式可有效消除分离式流水线的缺陷，实现高质量人物替换/插入/删除的音视频协同编辑；合成数据与预热策略可弥补真实数据稀缺。

Abstract: Human-centric audio-visual editing requires precise alignment of lip movements, gestures, and expressions with speech. Existing methods process inputs through independent video and audio pipelines, preventing cross-modal interaction between visual editing and audio dubbing. Many of these pipelines further rely on mask-based inpainting. As a result, they suffer from poor lip-audio synchronization, cumulative pipeline errors, and an inherent mask-size dilemma. We present AVE, the first unified end-to-end framework that jointly performs reference-guided person swapping, insertion, and deletion across both audio and video modalities without requiring spatial masks. To overcome data scarcity, we design a synthesis pipeline and introduce AVE-Dataset, the first audio-visual editing dataset to provide 100K pairs with appearance and voice references. By combining Synthetic-to-Real reconstruction with preliminary audio warm-up, our framework transcends synthetic pipeline limits and mitigates audio-video token imbalance. AVE outperforms modality-separate audio-visual editing pipelines in editing realism, temporal consistency, and audio-visual alignment.

</details>


### [88] [Counting Contributions, Not Views: Occlusion-Aware Densification for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27108)
*Marcel Rogge,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3DGS自适应密度控制中视锥可见性过滤忽略遮挡的问题，提出遮挡感知过滤与平均贡献归一化，显著提升遮挡密集场景的图像质量并降低高斯数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS通过自适应密度控制（ADC）从稀疏初始化生长稠密场景，其每高斯统计量来自渲染过程。现有统计量仅经视锥可见性过滤，对遮挡不敏感：被完全遮挡的高斯仍被计为"被观测"，从而稀释平均梯度信号，并在几何被遮挡处抑制致密化。

Method: 提出遮挡感知过滤，将致密化统计量限制在真正有贡献的视图中；并用"平均贡献"归一化替代准则中原始的视图计数归一化。二者集成于基于StopThePop的框架中。

Result: 在遮挡密集场景上，各图像质量指标均优于遮挡盲基线（LPIPS最多降低6%），且所需高斯数量不足视图计数归一化方案的一半。在长调度下，大多数准则发散而本方法数量保持有界；在小型基准上以远少于对比方法的数量保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 遮挡感知的统计过滤与平均贡献归一化能有效修正ADC的信号偏差，在提升重建质量的同时实现高斯数量的高效控制与长调度下的稳定性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting grows a dense scene from a sparse initialization through adaptive density control (ADC), driven by per-Gaussian statistics gathered during rendering. These statistics pass a frustum-based visibility filter blind to occlusion: fully occluded Gaussians still count as observed, diluting the averaged gradient signal and suppressing densification precisely where geometry is occluded. We correct this with an occlusion-aware filter that restricts densification statistics to views in which a Gaussian actually contributes, and replace the criterion's raw view-count normalizer with an average-contribution one. Integrated into a StopThePop-based framework, the two together improve every image-quality metric over the occlusion-blind baseline on occlusion-heavy scenes (up to 6% lower LPIPS), at less than half the Gaussians a view-count normalizer requires. Under the longer schedules these large scenes require, the count stays bounded where most criteria diverge, and on smaller benchmarks our method stays competitive at a fraction of the competitors' count.

</details>


### [89] [On the Limits of Scale: Inverse Scaling in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27360)
*Rohit Saxena,Alessandro Suglia,Marek Rei,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统揭示视觉语言模型（VLM）在子任务层面存在"逆向缩放"现象：同一模型家族内更大模型在特定问题类别上反而不如更小模型，且该现象由任务驱动、可跨架构复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律通常暗示模型规模、数据与算力增加会带来性能单调提升，但这多成立在聚合层面。作者希望检验子任务层面是否同样成立，即是否存在"越大越差"的特定问题类别。

Method: 评测4个模型家族共19个模型，覆盖8个多模态基准（9个评测划分），每个模型回答29,386道题，共558,334次模型-问题评估；在每个家族内对所有模型对进行逐题配对分析，识别逆向缩放的子任务-模型对组合，并检验其跨架构可复现性。

Result: 识别出375个逆向缩放子任务-模型对组合，涉及4,342个唯一查询（小模型答对而大模型答错），且该模式在独立开发的架构间可复现；发现13个子任务-数据集对在多个家族中均出现逆向缩放，支持任务驱动解释。受影响最严重领域为多图空间推理、视觉感知、大学级STEM知识，准确率差距在多例中超过20个百分点，而图表理解相对更稳健。

Conclusion: 增大模型规模并不必然一致提升多模态能力，聚合指标掩盖了子任务层面的退化；模型选择应具备任务感知性（task-aware）。

Abstract: Scaling laws suggest that increasing model size, training data, and compute generally yields monotonic performance improvements. While this often holds at the aggregate level, we present systematic evidence that vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit inverse scaling at the subtask level: specific question categories on which larger models perform worse than smaller counterparts within the same model family. We evaluate 19 models from four distinct model families across eight multimodal benchmarks (nine evaluation splits), answering 29,386 questions per model, 558,334 model-question evaluations in total. Using per-question pairwise analysis across all model pairs in each family, we identify 375 subtask-model-pair combinations exhibiting inverse scaling, spanning 4,342 unique queries where a smaller model answers correctly but a larger model fails, and we find that these patterns replicate across independently developed architectures. We identify 13 subtask-dataset pairs that show inverse scaling across multiple families, consistent with the phenomenon being task-driven rather than model-specific. The most affected domains in our study include multi-image spatial reasoning, visual perception, and university-level STEM knowledge, with accuracy gaps exceeding 20 percentage points in several cases, while diagram understanding appears comparatively resistant. These findings challenge the assumption that increasing model size uniformly improves multimodal capabilities and suggest that model selection should be task-aware.

</details>


### [90] [PointForward V2: Point-Aligned Interaction for Feedforward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28120)
*Xianqi Wang,Cheng Chi,Yuechuan Pu,Hongcheng Luo,Junda Cheng,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Bing Wang,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointForward V2 通过紧凑的场景结构化寄存器（静态场景寄存器与动态实例寄存器）实现点对齐查询之间的时空交互，避免全局注意力，在 Waymo、nuScenes、Argoverse 2 上取得 SOTA 驾驶场景重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式动态驾驶场景重建需将多视角、多时刻观测融合为一致的 3D 表示。PointForward 通过将图像观测提升为稀疏 3D 点查询实现点对齐表示，但各查询基本独立处理，缺乏跨空间与时间的信息交换；而数万个点查询间的直接全局注意力计算代价过高。

Method: 将点对齐表示升级为点对齐交互：以紧凑的场景结构化寄存器作为查询交互的中介。静态背景方面，逐帧 Static Scene Registers 压缩点信息、跨帧通信并将聚合上下文回传至查询；动态实例方面，将不同帧的查询对齐到以对象为中心的规范化空间，通过实例专属 Dynamic Instance Registers 交互；并采用结合对齐空间坐标与原始时间戳的 4D 位置编码，实现空间对齐但时间可区分的交互。

Result: 在 Waymo、nuScenes 与 Argoverse 2 等大规模驾驶基准上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: PointForward V2 无需稠密全局注意力即可在提升后的 3D 点查询间实现可扩展的时空推理，验证了寄存器介导交互范式的有效性。

Abstract: Feedforward reconstruction of dynamic driving scenes requires integrating observations across multiple views and time steps into a coherent 3D representation. PointForward established state-of-the-art feedforward driving reconstruction by lifting image observations into sparse 3D queries, demonstrating the effectiveness of point-aligned scene representations. However, these queries are largely processed independently, limiting information exchange across space and time, while direct global attention among tens of thousands of point queries is computationally prohibitive. In this work, we present \textbf{PointForward V2}, which advances point-aligned representation to point-aligned interaction. Our key idea is to mediate query interaction through compact, scene-structured registers. For the static background, frame-wise Static Scene Registers compress point information, communicate across frames, and propagate the aggregated context back to the queries. For each dynamic instance, queries from different frames are aligned in an object-centric canonical space and interact through instance-specific Dynamic Instance Registers. We further employ 4D positional encoding that combines aligned spatial coordinates with original timestamps, enabling spatially aligned yet temporally distinguishable interactions. In this way, PointForward V2 enables scalable spatio-temporal reasoning directly among lifted 3D queries without dense global attention. Experiments on Waymo, nuScenes, and Argoverse 2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across large-scale driving benchmarks.

</details>


### [91] [Auditing Where Concept Bottleneck Accuracy Comes From on CUB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28059)
*Sascha Saralajew,Eleonora Poeta,Eliana Pastor,Ammar Shaker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文审计CUB-200-2011上概念瓶颈模型(CBM)与概念嵌入模型(CEM)的准确率来源，发现大部分类别信息通过非局部特征与内部嵌入泄漏进入预测，CUB标注噪声使准确率差距难以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBM承诺可解释、可人工纠错，但社区仅以任务准确率与概念准确率衡量进展；这两者无法说明标签是否真正来自暴露的概念分数，也无法说明概念是否由对应视觉证据而非共现的物种线索支撑。

Method: 审计CUB标注与准确率上限；用泄漏探针检验CEM预测能否由其概念分数重建；归纳类别信息进入概念分数的四条设计轴（感受野、共享vs概念特定表示、范数依赖打分、任务监督）；以ResNet50与局部BagNet补丁的消融定量各来源贡献；并用定位与遮挡实验检验概念分数是否真的落地。

Result: 类别级训练标签与逐图标注仅约82%一致，仅用逐图标注训练的模型物种准确率仅约37%；CEM预测无法由概念分数重建，缺失类别信息存于内部嵌入；将骨干限制为局部补丁后，从概念分数可恢复的准确率由72%降至43%，接近真值标注支持的水平，联合训练可回升但概念准确率下降；局部化概念分数在类别级标签下以头部代理身体/翅/尾概念，CEM概念分数对遮挡其支撑部位几乎无响应。

Conclusion: 任务与概念准确率应作为CBM评估的辅助而非主导指标；应加入泄漏探针、定位与遮挡等落地性检验；鉴于CUB标注噪声，其上的准确率差距不应被过度解读。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise predictions that humans can inspect and correct through concepts such as "curved beak". Yet on CUB-200-2011, their standard benchmark, progress is measured like that of ordinary classifiers: by task and concept accuracy. Neither reveals whether the label is predicted from the exposed concept scores, or whether a concept is supported by visual evidence of that concept rather than by co-occurring features such as cues to the species as a whole. We audit where the accuracy of CBMs and Concept Embedding Models (CEMs) on CUB comes from. The CUB annotations are too noisy to explain it: class-level training labels agree with per-image annotations on only ~82% of image--concept pairs, and classifiers trained on per-image annotations reach only ~37% species accuracy. Leakage probes show that CEM predictions cannot be reconstructed from their concept scores; the missing class information sits in internal embeddings. We identify four design axes through which class information can enter concept scores---receptive field, shared versus concept-specific representations, norm-dependent scoring, and task supervision. An ablation shows that most of it enters through non-local features: restricting a ResNet50 backbone to local BagNet patches lowers the accuracy recoverable from concept scores from 72% to 43%, close to what ground-truth annotations support, while joint training raises it again at the cost of concept accuracy. Localization and occlusion show that locality makes grounding measurable but does not guarantee it: under class-level labels, local concept scores use the head as a proxy for body, wing, and tail concepts, and CEM concept scores barely respond to occluding their supporting part. Task and concept accuracy should thus support, not drive, the evaluation of CBMs, and given the noisy annotations, CUB accuracy gaps should not be overinterpreted.

</details>


### [92] [A Process-Informed, Photorealistic Digital Twin for Vision-Based Inline Inspection of C-SMC Strip Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28050)
*Patrick Flore,Fabian Röder,Kevin Chen,Andreas Gebhard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于过程信息的照片级数字孪生，用于生成碳纤维SMC条带铺放的大规模、精确标注图像数据集，以缓解在线质检中数据标注瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMC制造在线质检缺乏大规模、精确标注的图像数据集，而多重叠条带分布的联合分布无法解析建模，人工标注难以提供可靠真值。

Method: 结合刚体仿真（复现沉降、摩擦、传送带运动等铺放运动学）与物理偏振渲染模型生成真实外观；每帧同步输出有向包围框、点标注、密度图和偏振渲染（AoLP/DoLP）等互洽标签。

Result: 生成3300帧、超16000张图像，重现近各向同性纤维取向（轴向集中度r<0.05）；纯合成数据训练YOLOv11与CSRNet检测mAP50达0.91，有向框检测器mAP50=0.83；合成-真实差异（KS距离0.46，分布重叠0.56）与两批真实采集序列间差异（0.41，0.57）同量级。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生方法可显著降低纤维取向与体积含量相机在线检测的标注负担，并验证合成数据可直接训练检测与密度估计模型。

Abstract: Inline quality assurance for Sheet Molding Compound (SMC) manufacturing is hindered by the lack of large-scale, accurately labeled image datasets for training machine-learning models. This work presents a process-informed, photorealistic digital twin for carbon-fiber SMC strip deposition. A rigid-body simulation reproduces the deposition kinematics (settling, friction, belt motion), while a physically grounded polarization-rendering model produces realistic appearance, so that every image carries exact, mutually consistent labels in synchronized formats: oriented bounding boxes, point annotations, density maps, and polarization renderings (Angle and Degree of Linear Polarization). Because the joint distribution of many overlapping strips cannot be specified analytically, simulation yields exact, scalable ground truth that manual annotation cannot provide. The twin produces over 16,000 images across 3,300 frames, and the synthetic data replicate near-isotropic fiber orientation (axial concentration r < 0.05) consistent with as-deposited commercial SMC. Trained purely on synthetic data, YOLOv11 and CSRNet reach detection mAP50 up to 0.91, with a dedicated oriented-bounding-box detector confirming the oriented labels are directly trainable (mAP50 = 0.83); on real manufacturing images, the synthetic-to-real difference in edge and texture structure (Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance 0.46, distribution overlap 0.56) is of the same magnitude as the difference between two real acquisition series of the same material (0.41, overlap 0.57). The approach substantially reduces the labeling burden for camera-based inline inspection of fiber orientation and volume content.

</details>


### [93] [Recast the Past: Stable Long Video Generation with Full Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27986)
*Yukuo Ma,Cong Liu,Junke Wang,Junqi Liu,Haibin Huang,Zuxuan Wu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReCam：将历史潜变量的局部时空块压缩为记忆 token 并直接投影至 DiT 隐藏空间，配合双指针去噪缓解时间漂移，实现长视频生成的高效历史建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程视频生成需保留不断扩张的历史，但直接以其增长后的 token 序列作为条件代价高昂；现有方法对历史上下文做限制或压缩，可能丢失未来生成所需的信息。

Method: 将历史潜变量的局部时空块聚合为向量，并直接投影到 Diffusion Transformer 的高维隐藏空间，模型通过原生自注意力学习使用这些记忆 token，从而在降低 token 数量的同时保留对完整历史的块级访问；此外引入双指针去噪，对近期上下文与目标帧采用独立噪声调度以抑制自回归推演中的时间漂移。

Result: 在所评估方法中取得最高 VBench-Long 综合分 80.1，并在 R2M-Bench 上具备有竞争力的静态场景重访表现；在离屏主体重现的全部四项一致性指标上领先，其中服装一致性为 4.09，最佳基线为 3.03。

Conclusion: 以块级记忆 token 建模压缩历史、并以双指针去噪控制漂移，可在保持全历史可访问性的同时降低条件开销，显著提升长视频生成的综合质量与主体一致性。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation requires retaining an ever-expanding history, but conditioning on its growing token sequence is costly. Existing methods restrict or compress historical context, potentially losing information needed for future generation. This work proposes ReCam, which groups local spatiotemporal blocks of historical latents into vectors and projects them directly into the high-dimensional hidden space of a Diffusion Transformer. The model learns to use the resulting memory tokens through native self-attention, reducing token count while retaining block-level access to the full history. To mitigate temporal drift during autoregressive rollout, we further introduce dual-pointer denoising with separate noise schedules for recent context and target frames. Among the evaluated methods, ReCam achieves the highest VBench-Long overall score of 80.1 and competitive static-scene revisitation performance on R2M-Bench. It also leads on all four consistency metrics for off-screen subject reappearance, including clothing consistency of 4.09 versus 3.03 for the best baseline.

</details>


### [94] [In PRAISE of Attributes: Grounding Retrieval Geometry with Query-Conditioned Subspaces for Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27944)
*Rajarshi Bhattacharya,Aryan Shukla,Shakeeb Murtaza,Christian Desrosiers,Jose Dolz,Maguelonne Heritier,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRAISE，一种面向VLM-based ReID的查询侧测试时自适应框架，将属性提示词用于构建属性条件低秩子空间以重塑查询-图库相似度，而非直接当作检索分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I2I ReID受视角、光照、遮挡、背景变化干扰，干扰项可能比真实匹配更相似；T2I ReID中描述不完整或模糊会丢失关键视觉证据。VLM中服装颜色、携带物等属性提示可提供细粒度匹配线索，但直接用属性提示词相似度作检索分数不可靠：模板化提示存在偏置、相关性及校准不良问题。

Method: PRAISE（Prompt-guided Attribute-based Inference-time Similarity Enhancement）：查询侧测试时自适应。使用可迁移的属性提示库，由最兼容的属性提示诱导出查询相关的低秩子空间——属性条件子空间（ACS）；ACS提供受控的属性条件几何，用于重塑查询-图库亲和度，并在无身份与属性标签条件下引导轻量级推理时更新。

Result: 在多个I2I与T2I ReID基准上，PRAISE稳定提升VLM检索骨干性能，并优于通用及ReID专用的TTA基线。

Conclusion: 将属性提示词从"分数来源"转为"几何构造工具"是更可靠的VLM ReID适配路径；查询侧、无标签的推理时自适应可有效增强检索，具备跨骨干与跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) ranks an image gallery using either an image query or a textual query. In image-to-image (I2I) ReID, viewpoint, illumination, occlusion, and background changes can make distractors appear more similar than the true match. In text-to-image (T2I) ReID, incomplete or ambiguous descriptions may omit key visual evidence. We study this problem for vision-language model (VLM) based ReID, where attributes such as clothing color and carried objects provide useful fine-grained matching cues. However, directly using attribute-prompt similarities as retrieval scores is unreliable because templated prompts can be biased, correlated, and poorly calibrated. We therefore propose \textbf{PR}ompt-guided \textbf{A}ttribute-based \textbf{I}nference-time \textbf{S}imilarity \textbf{E}nhancement (\texttt{PRAISE}), a query-side test-time adaptation framework for VLM-based ReID. Instead of treating prompts as scores, \texttt{PRAISE} uses a transferable attribute-prompt bank to construct an \emph{Attribute-Conditioned Subspace} (ACS): a query-dependent low-rank subspace induced by the most compatible attribute prompts. ACS provides a controlled attribute-conditioned geometry for reshaping query--gallery affinities and guiding lightweight inference-time updates without identity or attribute labels. Experiments across multiple I2I- and T2I-ReID benchmarks show that \texttt{PRAISE} consistently improves VLM retrieval backbones and outperforms both generic and ReID-specific TTA baselines.

</details>


### [95] [ICIBS: Increasing the Context for Ice Boundary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27912)
*Marcel Dreier,Dakota Pyles,Nora Gourmelon,Moritz Koch,Fei Wu,Thorsten Seehaus,Matthias Braun,Andreas Maier,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在深度学习模型中融入额外空间与地理空间上下文信息的多种扩展，以改善温性冰川雷达回波数据中冰底的自动 delineation（识别），在IceAnatomy基准上取得SOTA，并对Patagonia新数据表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山地冰川与冰盖快速消融，需用探地雷达测量冰厚以评估体积和厚度分布并预测未来。雷达回波数据人工标注耗时（测区可达数百公里）；现有自动方法难以刻画冰底，尤其温性冰川因含水量高导致散射和衰减增强、冰底反射弱且模糊、数据噪声大，视觉特征不明确。

Method: 对深度学习模型提出若干扩展，将额外的空间上下文与地理空间上下文信息引入预测网络，从而增强对模糊冰底特征的判别。

Result: 在IceAnatomy基准数据集上取得新的SOTA：单一组合系统在FAU子集平均误差10.9 m，CReSIS子集43.4 m；在Patagonia新测数据上泛化良好，较基线优49.6%。

Conclusion: 引入空间与地理空间上下文可有效提升温性冰川冰底自动 delineation 的精度与泛化能力；模型代码与新数据集已开源（GitHub: ki7077/ICIBS；Zenodo: records/23392580）。

Abstract: Mountain glaciers and ice sheets are undergoing rapid loss of ice mass. To assess their current volume and ice-thickness distribution, and to accurately forecast their future, ground-penetrating radar systems are employed to measure ice thickness. These instruments produce radio-echo sounding data that reveal the internal structure of the ice and its boundaries at the surface and beneath the ice body. However, delineating these interfaces is time-consuming as the surveyed area can span several hundred kilometers. To accelerate the labelling process, automated methods have emerged, but they often struggle to delineate the ice bottom, particularly in temperate glaciers. The high water content inherent in temperate ice increases both scattering and attenuation of the radar signal, resulting in weak and ambiguous reflections at the ice bottom and generally noisier data. As a result, the visual features corresponding to the ice bottom become ambiguous. To address this issue, we propose several extensions to deep learning models that incorporate additional contextual information into the prediction. Specifically, we integrate modifications that incorporate additional spatial and geo-spatial contextual information into the network. With these enhancements, we achieve a new state-of-the-art performance on the IceAnatomy benchmark dataset, with mean errors of 10.9 m for the FAU subset and 43.4 m for the CReSIS subset in a single combined system. We also demonstrate that our method generalises well to previously unseen data, outperforming the baseline by 49.6 % on new campaign data from Patagonia. The model code and the new dataset are available at https://github.com/ki7077/ICIBS/tree/main and https://zenodo.org/records/23392580.

</details>


### [96] [SGLDet: Spectral-aware Global-Local Dynamic Modeling for Multispectral Aerial Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27883)
*Peng Zhang,Shuaihao Han,Tingfa Xu,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGLDet，一个用于多光谱航空目标检测的谱感知全局-局部动态建模框架，通过稀疏相似匹配、场景记忆上下文推理与输入依赖的专家核聚合，同时解决前景背景失衡与局部尺度/方向/结构变化两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法聚焦谱-空解耦与目标感知的谱-空融合，但忽视两点：1）大场景覆盖导致严重前景-背景失衡，损害可靠目标识别；2）航空目标的尺度、方向与结构存在显著局部变化，阻碍精确定位。作者观察到目标语义由全局谱一致性与场景上下文共同决定，而精确���位需要以输入谱和空间属性为条件的动态特征调制。

Method: SGLDet由三个协同模块构成：1）谱驱动全局动态建模（Spectral-driven Global Dynamic Modeling），通过相似度引导的稀疏匹配在全场景聚合谱相关特征；2）多尺度全局上下文集成（Multi-scale Global Context Integration），构建紧凑场景记忆用于场景引导的上下文推理；3）谱感知输入依赖校准聚合（Spectral-aware Input-dependent Calibration Aggregation），以场景引导的谱-空校准执行专家核聚合，实现自适应局部特征调制。

Result: 在MODA、HOD3K与DroneVehicle三个数据集上的大量实验表明，SGLDet以有竞争力的计算开销取得更优性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 结合全局谱一致性与局部动态调制的设计可有效缓解航空多光谱检测中的失衡与定位难题，验证了全局-局部协同动态建模的有效性。

Abstract: Multispectral aerial object detection aims to exploit spectral-spatial cues to address object detection challenges in complex aerial scenes. Existing methods mainly focus on spectral-spatial decoupling and object-aware spectral-spatial fusion to improve spectral utilization. However, two critical issues remain underexplored: 1) severe foreground-background imbalance caused by large scene coverage hinders reliable object recognition; 2) aerial objects exhibit substantial local variations in scale, orientation, and structure, which hinder accurate localization. To address these issues, we propose SGLDet, a spectral-aware global-local dynamic modeling framework for multispectral aerial object detection, which is motivated by the observation that object semantics are jointly determined by global spectral consistency and scene context, while precise localization requires dynamic feature modulation conditioned on the input spectral and spatial properties. To realize these properties, SGLDet consists of three coordinated components: a Spectral-driven Global Dynamic Modeling module that aggregates spectrally related features across the scene via similarity-guided sparse matching; a Multi-scale Global Context Integration module that constructs a compact scene memory for scene-guided contextual reasoning; and a Spectral-aware Input-dependent Calibration Aggregation module that performs expert-kernel aggregation with scene-guided spectral-spatial calibration to enable adaptive local feature modulation. Extensive experiments on MODA, HOD3K, and DroneVehicle demonstrate that SGLDet achieves superior performance with competitive computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Riiluo/SGLDet.

</details>


### [97] [LaLa-World: Scaling Latent World Models with Natural Language Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27851)
*Junha Song,Geonmo Gu,Jeongeun Park,Heejeong Nam,Lucas Maes,Byeongho Heo,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语言条件潜世界模型LaLa-World，用语言统一描述动作与视觉变化，实现跨域数据联合训练，规划更快更省，并可微调为LaLa-Policy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式潜世界模型（LWM）虽高效，但通常按领域或任务分别训练，难以利用异构数据源（仿真、真机轨迹、图像与视频），限制了规模化与泛化。

Method: 借鉴视频生成模型的语言条件接口，构建LaLa-World：(1) 将数值动作与视觉变化均用语言表述，使单一未来预测器可在异构数据集上联合训练；(2) 以语言模型为骨干构建预测器，在预测未来潜状态时解析语言条件；预训练后作为初始化用于LaLa-Policy，联合预测动作与未来视觉表征。

Result: 平均MPC规划性能优于按领域/任务单独训练的现有LWM；加入图像与视频数据进一步提升平均规划性能及对动作措辞的鲁棒性；与RAEv2视频生成基线相比，规划性能相当，训练成本降低6倍、规划速度提升13.9倍；LaLa-Policy在LIBERO与RoboCasa上与Cosmos Policy竞争力相当，推理最快提速5.5倍。

Conclusion: 语言作为跨域统一接口可使潜世界模型在多样数据上有效且高效地学习，为想象式规划与下游策略微调提供通用基础。

Abstract: This paper shows that JEPA-style latent world models (LWMs) can learn across diverse domains and data sources by adopting the language-conditioned interface of video generation models. We establish this approach as an effective and efficient basis for planning with imagination and downstream policy fine-tuning. To this end, we introduce LaLa-World, a Language-conditioned Latent World model that learns from simulation and robot trajectories together with broader image and video data. Two designs make this work. First, we describe both numerical actions and visual changes in language, enabling joint training of a single future predictor across heterogeneous datasets. Second, we build the predictor on a language model, allowing it to interpret language conditions while predicting future latent states. The resulting predictor achieves higher average MPC planning performance than existing LWMs trained separately for each domain or task. Adding image and video data further improves average planning performance and robustness to action phrasing. In a matched comparison with an RAEv2-based video generation model baseline, LaLa-World reaches comparable planning performance with $6\times$ less training cost and $13.9\times$ faster planning. Beyond planning, the pretrained LaLa-World serves as an initialization for LaLa-Policy, which jointly predicts actions and future visual representations. LaLa-Policy performs competitively with Cosmos Policy on LIBERO and RoboCasa, with up to $5.5\times$ faster inference.

</details>


### [98] [RLSplat: Resource-Linear Budget Control for Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27712)
*Injae Kim,Minseok Joo,Jihwan Park,Sehyung Kim,Minseong Bae,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RLSplat 提出资源线性、预算可控的前馈 3D 高斯泼溅框架，使表示规模、推理延迟与峰值显存随高斯预算减少而线性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算可控 FF3DGS 方法只控制输出表示大小，却忽略推理本身开销：需先预测稠密高斯再剪枝/选择，导致延迟与峰值显存几乎与预算无关，在低预算场景下不实用。

Method: 用高斯分配策略替代剪枝/选择：学习将预算分配到局部场景区域，直接生成恰好所需数量的高斯；随后进行资源线性高斯解码，每个高斯仅通过高效层次化邻域搜索关注邻近高斯，使解码开销随高斯数线性下降。

Result: 零样本评测中，RLSplat 在各预算下均优于先前预算可控方法；在高斯数量少 4 倍时仍超越最强基线，低预算下运行速度快至 4.1 倍、峰值显存低至 9.6 倍。

Conclusion: 通过将预算控制从"输出端剪枝"转向"生成端分配与线性解码"，RLSplat 实现了表示规模、延迟与显存的同步线性伸缩，显著提升 FF3DGS 在受限资源场景下的实用性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (FF3DGS) reconstructs 3D scenes from images in a single forward pass, making it well-suited to applications that must operate within limited resource budgets. To further improve its practicality, recent budget-controllable FF3DGS methods allow the number of Gaussians to be adjusted at inference, so that the output meets a given storage limit or rendering-latency constraint. However, these methods overlook the cost of inference itself: to produce a representation with the target number of Gaussians, they first predict a dense set of Gaussians and then prune or select from it, requiring nearly constant latency and peak memory regardless of the budget. We therefore propose RLSplat, a resource-linear budget-controllable FF3DGS framework whose representation size, inference latency, and peak memory all decrease linearly as the Gaussian budget is reduced. Instead of pruning or selection, RLSplat learns a Gaussian allocation policy that distributes the budget across local scene regions, so that exactly the requested number of Gaussians is generated. These Gaussians are then refined through resource-linear Gaussian decoding, in which each Gaussian attends only to nearby Gaussians found via an efficient hierarchical neighbor search, so that the decoding cost decreases linearly with fewer Gaussians. In zero-shot evaluation, RLSplat outperforms prior budget-controllable methods at every budget, surpassing the strongest baseline even with 4$\times$ fewer Gaussians, while running up to 4.1$\times$ faster with 9.6$\times$ less peak memory at low budgets.

</details>


### [99] [DART: Scaling a Shape Representation Across Anatomical Classes and Imaging Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27605)
*Koushik Swarna,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Lam Ngo,Wenxuan Li,Jiancheng Yang,Alan Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DART，一种基于文本引导扩散的解剖形状重建方法，仅从分割概率学习跨类别共享形状表示，无需图像或新体素级标注，即可在13个基准上修正分割错误并实现跨类别、跨模态迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型在最难分割的结构上仍产生解剖学上不合理的形状（如血管断裂、腺体碎片化）。学习式形状表示能捕捉结构形态并纠正此类错误，但多数方法局限于固定类集合，扩展类别与成像模态通常需要昂贵的体素级标注。

Method: DART（Diffusion for Anatomical Reshaping with Text）以分割概率而非医学图像为输入，借助解剖文本描述条件化扩散模型，学习一个跨类别共享的形状表示，并在推理时对分割概率进行形状层面的重塑与纠正，因而不需要图像监督。

Result: 在13个公开基准上，CT胆囊单例绝对Dice提升达31%，MRI前列腺单例提升10%；利用现有分割预测，将单一形状表示从50类扩展到377类，且无需为纠正训练新增体素级标注；可纠正训练中未出现的4类病灶，并在不重训练的情况下从CT迁移至MRI；纠正后的标签还能反哺分割模型，使某模型Dice由57.1%升至67.5%；同一流程可支持45,000例CT扫描的标注。

Conclusion: 在类别内与跨类别共享形状信息，可显著降低将分割扩展到更多解剖类别与成像模态时对新体素级标注的依赖，为可扩展的解剖形状先验学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Current state-of-the-art segmentation models still produce anatomically implausible shapes, such as broken vessels and fragmented glands, on the structures that are hardest to segment. Learned shape representations capture what structures look like and can help correct these errors, but most cover a fixed set of anatomical classes. Extending this coverage across classes and imaging modalities is costly because it often requires new voxel-level annotations. We propose Diffusion for Anatomical Reshaping with Text (DART), which learns a shared shape representation from segmentation probabilities. It uses anatomical descriptions to correct these probabilities without receiving medical images. Across 13 public benchmarks, absolute Dice gains reach 31% for individual gallbladder cases in CT and 10% for individual prostate cases in MRI. Using existing segmentation predictions, we scale a single shape representation from 50 to 377 anatomical classes without new voxel-level annotations for correction training. The shared representation also corrects four lesion classes absent from training and transfers from CT to MRI without retraining. Corrected labels also improve segmentation models, raising one model's Dice from 57.1% to 67.5%. The same pipeline supports annotations for 45,000 CT scans. These results suggest that sharing shape information within and across classes can reduce the need for new voxel-level annotations when extending segmentation to more anatomical classes and imaging modalities. The model is available at https://huggingface.co/Koushik45/DART-Seg

</details>


### [100] [Real In, Real Out: What If We Only Use Real Data for Scene Text Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27592)
*Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Jiaxin Zhang,Chong Sun,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TextRIRO：通过将每张真实文本图像自身"破坏"成配对监督（Shuffle&Crop构造自配对数据），在条件扩散框架上构建强基线，摆脱对合成配对数据的依赖，缓解合成到真实的域差距，并构建TextRIRO-3M大规模真实数据集用于长文本识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景文本编辑（STE）需在保持原风格的前提下修改文本内容，但现有方法高度依赖合成配对数据与复杂解耦策略，难以忠实反映自然场景的多样性与复杂性，常产生伪影或幻觉，存在明显的合成-真实域差距。

Method: 挑战常规做法，探索将每个真实样本转化为其自身的配对监督。具体尝试多种图像破坏方式（裁剪Crop、打乱Shuffle、掩码Mask）构建自配对训练数据，并基于条件扩散架构建立强基线TextRIRO；实验表明"字符列打乱+图像裁剪"组合能有效破坏文本语义同时保留关键风格线索，使模型可直接从真实文本图像学习编辑行为。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，TextRIRO在准确性与视觉真实性上均取得有前景的表现，显著缓解合成到真实的域差距；并据此通过拼接编辑图与原图构建TextRIRO-3M大规模真实长文本识别数据集，用其训练识别模型可显著提升识别准确率。

Conclusion: TextRIRO以简洁且面向真实数据的设计将STE提升到真实、可靠、广泛适用的新水平，并为下游识别任务提供高价值训练资源。

Abstract: Aiming to modify textual content while preserving the original style, Scene Text Editing (STE) has significant practical value in many applications. STE typically requires paired images before and after editing for training. However, recent approaches rely heavily on synthetic paired data and complex disentanglement strategies. They struggle to faithfully capture the diversity and complexity of natural scenes, often producing artifacts or hallucinations. In this work, we challenge the common practice and investigate whether STE can be driven by turning each real sample into its own paired supervision. Concretely, we explore multiple ways to disrupt the original image (e.g., Crop, Shuffle, Mask) to construct self-paired training data, and build a strong baseline (TextRIRO}) upon a conditional diffusion architecture. Our experiments show that combining character-column shuffling with image cropping (Shuffle&Crop) effectively destroys text semantics while preserving key style cues, enabling the model to learn editing behaviors directly from the real text image. With this concise yet real-oriented design, TextRIRO achieves promising performance in both accuracy and visual realism across real-world benchmarks, substantially mitigating the synthetic-to-real domain gap. Furthermore, we leverage TextRIRO to build TextRIRO-3M, a large-scale realistic dataset for long-text recognition by concatenating edited and original images. Experimental results show that training recognition models with TextRIRO-3M significantly improves recognition accuracy, demonstrating that TextRIRO provides highly useful training resources for downstream tasks. Overall, TextRIRO lifts STE to realistic, reliable, and widely applicable new levels. Code is available at https://github.com/YesianRohn/TextRIRO.

</details>


### [101] [LoRASpace: A Pool-Wide Shared Substrate for Static and Dynamic Multi-LoRA Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27444)
*Thamirawaran Sathiyalogeswaran,Navindu De Silva,Chamikara Siriwardane,W. K. Alwis,Jason Mars,Udaya Sampath K. Perera Miriya Thanthrige,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现真实 LoRA 池中堆叠低秩因子所需方向远少于秩之和，据此提出免训练方法 LoRASpace，将池共享子空间复用于静态/动态两种推理模式，在扩散池上以 merge 级成本达到 composite 质量，并比六 LoRA composite 快 6 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多 LoRA 组合存在两难：权重合并只需一次前向但不同概念的 LoRA 相互干扰、质量下降；逐 LoRA 独立前向（composite）质量好但推理时间随激活 LoRA 数线性增长。作者希望利用此前多 LoRA 工作忽视的 LoRA 池性质来同时获得质量与效率。

Method: 先实证研究 LoRA 池的低秩重叠性：对堆叠低秩因子做 SVD，求保留 99% 因子能量的最小子空间，并统计所需秩占秩总和的比例及其随池规模的增长关系；在扩散池、LoRA-Hub/FLAN-T5 子池与 LoRA-Retriever 基准上比较重叠度。据此提出训练-free 的 LoRASpace：每个池只计算一次子空间并复用，提供静态模式（merge 成本）与面向输入相关路由的动态模式。

Result: 三个扩散池仅需总秩的 46%–57% 即可保留 99% 能量，子空间随池规模亚线性增长；LoRA-Hub 的 48-LoRA 子池重叠度 62%，而多样性筛选的 LoRA-Retriever 接近无重叠基线（96%），说明池构成而非模态决定可压缩性。扩散池上静态 LoRASpace 以 merge 成本匹配 composite 质量；17-LoRA 池中激活 6 个 LoRA 时，动态 LoRASpace 比 composite 快 6 倍以上，主要来自单次而非六次骨干前向。同秩静态变体与 LoRASpace 相当，说明质量增益不依赖池级基；在近正交的 LoRA-Retriever 上压缩有限，增益来自与外部检索的耦合。

Conclusion: LoRA 池中存在显著的低秩方向重叠，可被一次性提取并复用于多 LoRA 推理，从而在保持组合质量的同时将成本降至 merge 级别；该收益取决于池内的概念冗余程度，对多样性筛选的近正交池效果有限，需结合外部检索。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) attaches small low-rank matrices to a frozen backbone, and public repositories host many LoRAs per backbone, each for a different concept or task. Combining several LoRAs in one generation forces a trade-off. Merging their weights into one update needs a single forward pass, but LoRAs trained for different concepts interfere and quality drops. Running each LoRA in its own forward pass (composite) preserves quality, but inference time grows linearly with the number of active LoRAs. We study a property of LoRA pools overlooked by prior multi-LoRA work. In real LoRA pools, the stacked low-rank factors need far fewer directions than their summed ranks. Using singular value decomposition, we find the minimal subspace that keeps 99% of the factor energy. On three diffusion pools, only 46% to 57% of the summed LoRA ranks is needed, and the subspace grows sub-linearly with pool size. A 48-LoRA sub-pool of LoRA-Hub on FLAN-T5 tasks shows comparable overlap (62%), while the diversity-curated LoRA-Retriever benchmark sits near the no-overlap baseline (96%), so pool composition rather than modality predicts where compression is large. We build on this overlap with LoRASpace, a training-free method that computes the subspace once per pool and reuses it in two inference modes, a static mode at merge cost and a dynamic mode for input-dependent routing. On the diffusion pools, static LoRASpace matches composite quality at merge cost, and dynamic LoRASpace is more than 6x faster than composite with six active LoRAs on a 17-LoRA pool, mainly because it runs one backbone pass instead of six. In an ablation, static variants at the same rank match LoRASpace, so the quality gain does not depend on the pool-wide basis. On the near-orthogonal LoRA-Retriever benchmark, compression is small and gains come from coupling with external retrieval.

</details>


### [102] [Test-time Adaptation for Point Cloud Denoising under Unseen Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27410)
*Kun Jiang,Shida Fu,Fanhao Yi,Weiqing Li,Zhiyong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出测试时自适应去噪框架TTAD，使预训练点云去噪模型在未知噪声分布下无需额外监督或重训练即可自适应，并扩展出持续版本CTTAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习点云去噪方法在测试点云噪声分布偏离训练集时性能显著下降，缺乏针对未知噪声的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 残差驱动的噪声建模模块(RDNM)：通过分析噪声输入与初始模型去噪得到的伪干净版本之间的偏差，估计样本特定的噪声分布；2) 噪声感知对比自适应模块(NACA)：基于去噪点云构建样本特定的"噪声-更噪"测试时训练对，通过对比学习在每个样本上执行少量测试时自适应步骤更新模型参数；3) 自然扩展为持续TTAD(CTTAD)，在噪声分布一致的连续测试样本上渐进式精修并降低单样本计算开销。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著提升现有去噪模型在未见噪声下的泛化能力，在多项评价指标上取得更优性能。

Conclusion: TTAD为点云去噪提供了一种无需源数据重训练、无需额外监督的通用测试时自适应方案，并可高效扩展至持续测试场景。

Abstract: Although deep learning-based point cloud denoising methods have demonstrated impressive results in recent years, their effectiveness usually degrades substantially in real-world scenarios where the noise distribution of test point clouds deviates from that of the training set. To address this challenge, we propose a test-time adaptive denoising (TTAD) framework that enables existing denoising approaches to adapt to unknown noise patterns at test time without relying on additional supervision or retraining from source data. Taking a pre-trained denoising model and a test noisy point cloud as input, TTAD first employs a residual-driven noise modeling (RDNM) module to estimate the sample-specific noise distribution through analyzing the deviation between the noisy input and its pseudo-clean version denoised by the initial model. Then, a noise-aware contrastive adaptation (NACA) module is designed to build sample-specific noisy-noisier test-time training pair from the denoised point cloud, and update the model parameters over a few adaptation steps per sample at test time through contrastive learning. Furthermore, TTAD naturally extends to a continual TTAD (CTTAD) setting, facilitating efficient progressive refinement across sequential test samples with consistent noise distributions, while reducing the computational cost per sample. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly improves the generalization ability of existing denoising models under unseen noise, achieving superior performance across multiple evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [103] [Rate as a Feature, Not a Nuisance: Curriculum FiLM Training for Event-Based Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26933)
*Kavyansh Tyagi,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出事件相机的事件率随场景而变（N-Caltech101上变化约26倍），标准GNN流程通过归一化时间戳或训练不变性丢弃了这一信息，导致精度损失。作者将每条记录的log事件率作为标量，经FiLM调制PointNet++的每一阶段（初始化为恒等），配合两阶段课程学习，在N-Caltech101上达67.2%（+6.3pp）、DVS128 Gesture上82.5%（+14.0pp），仅增加约0.2M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件率由场景运动、物体纹理、光照、对比度阈值等决定，在单相机采集的N-Caltech101中变化约26倍。现有事件图神经网络流程要么做每条记录的时间戳窗口归一化（不可逆，损失约6pp自然速率精度），要么训练对事件率不变（用精度换鲁棒性），二者都浪费了可利用的信息。作者希望直接以事件率为条件来利用这一信息。

Method: 提出per-recording log-rate标量，通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）调制PointNet++骨干网络的每个阶段，FiLM初始化为恒等映射使训练从原始基线开始；采用两阶段课程：先自然事件率训练，再用速率增强做微调。并设置等容量对照实验以排除参数增加的贡献。

Result: N-Caltech101达67.2%（+6.3pp），DVS128 Gesture达82.5%（+14.0pp），仅额外约0.2M参数；等容量对照实验表明增益来自速率条件化而非模型容量增加。per-recording归一化带来约6pp自然速率精度损失。

Conclusion: 事件率是可利用的信息而非需消除的干扰；通过轻量的log-rate FiLM条件化与速率课程学习，可在几乎不增加容量的前提下显著提升事件图神经网络性能，代码将在录用后公开。
