Abstract: An event camera emits events at a rate given by the scene motion, object texture, illumination, its own contrast thresholds, and other settings. Across the N-Caltech101 dataset, recorded by a single event camera, the event rate varies by roughly 26x. Standard event-based Graph Neural Network GNN pipelines do not make use of the variable event rate. They either normalize each event's timestamp into a fixed window or train the network to be invariant to the event rate. We argue that both choices lose information that could instead be used to improve the performance of a pipeline. Per-recording normalization is non-invertible, incurring about 6pp (percentage points) of natural-rate accuracy loss in our setting. And invariance training trades accuracy for robustness to shifts in the event rate. We instead take a different path and condition on the event rate directly. We add a per-recording log-rate scalar that modulates every stage of a PointNet++ backbone through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), initialized to identity, so that training begins at the unmodified baseline. A two-stage curriculum (natural event rates first, then rate-augmented fine-tuning) reaches 67.2% on N-Caltech101 (+6.3pp) and 82.5% on DVS128 Gesture (+14.0pp) with an additional ~0.2M parameters. A matched-capacity control experiment shows that the gain is not from the increase in model capacity but from the rate conditioning. We will make the implementation publicly available upon acceptance of this work.

</details>


### [104] [Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring for Diffusion Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27719)
*Nattanat Chatthee,Tagon Sompong,Ekapol Chuangsuwanich,Supasorn Suwajanakorn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring（PPAA），在交叉注意力空间中对目标概念表征进行"推—拉"式位移，并用有界余弦相似度约束锚定擦除强度，实现无重训练的概念擦除，兼顾擦除效果与非目标生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型快速发展带来隐私、有害内容和版权风险；现有概念擦除方法依赖启发式强度缩放（如无分类器引导、负提示缩放），导致语义漂移不可控、过度，并损害非目标生成质量。

Method: 在交叉注意力空间中提出PPAA机制：位移目标表征的同时，通过有界余弦相似度约束锚定擦除幅度；采用相对相似度比值而非绝对差值构建自适应阈值，使阈值表示语义保留的有意义百分比，从而以单一数值在所有层间统一适用。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4上，针对名人、艺术风格、裸体及知识产权等多类概念的广泛实验表明，该方法相较已有方法取得更优的权衡，并验证了其在FLUX等现代架构上的适用性。

Conclusion: PPAA以有界约束替代启发式强度缩放，实现了可控、可统一配置的概念擦除，在擦除有效性与非目标生成保真度之间取得良好折衷，并具备跨架构的可迁移性。

Abstract: Rapid advances in diffusion models have raised concerns about privacy, harmful content, and copyright violations. Concept erasure methods aim to address these risks by removing undesirable concepts from pretrained models without full retraining. However, existing approaches often degrade non-target generation quality through heuristic strength scaling (e.g., classifier-free guidance or negative prompt scaling), leading to uncontrolled and excessive semantic drift. To address this, we propose Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring (PPAA), a mechanism in the cross-attention space that displaces target representations while anchoring the scale of erasure via bounded cosine-similarity constraints. By using a relative similarity ratio instead of an absolute difference, our adaptive threshold represents a meaningful percentage of semantic retention, facilitating a single value to be applied uniformly across layers. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple concept categories, including celebrities, artistic styles, nudity, and intellectual property. Our method achieves favorable trade-offs against prior approaches on Stable Diffusion v1.4 and demonstrates its applicability to modern architectures such as FLUX.

</details>


### [105] [PsycheMME: A Psychology-Informed Benchmark for Social Understanding and Interaction in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28625)
*Heran Hu,Yuhang Dong,Zhucun Xue,Kaiwei Chen,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PsycheMME——一个基于心理学构建的视频基准，用八项能力（涵盖grounding、状态、需求、交互决策、记忆与风险）将"理解人类"与"选择回应"两类判断分离评估，并引入 Reasoning-Verified 协议按显式理由的支持度与逻辑一致性加权给分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估用聚合准确率掩盖了"理解人类需求"与"选择回应"这两类本质不同判断之间的差异，无法定位模型究竟在感知、状态推断、需求理解还是回应决策环节失败。

Method: 基于 Social Information Processing 与 Perception-Action Cycle 理论构建八类能力的组织框架；从630个episode中经独立作答（隐去参考答案）与专家审核构建1000道题；提出 Reasoning-Verified 协议，依据显式理由对照标注线索的支持度与逻辑一致性对答案给分；在17个模型上评估，并进行输入控制与 SFT/GRPO 实验考察上下文依赖性与适应性。

Result: 17个模型中 Timing 能力的 Verified 分数与得分保留率最低，且正确答案的理由支持判断更弱；Need 分数相近的模型在开放题上的答案完整性仍有差异；输入控制与 SFT/GRPO 揭示了模型的上下文依赖与适应行为。

Conclusion: 将答案与理由联合评估能够在"人类理解—回应选择—理由论证"三个层面精确定位模型失败之处，表明聚合准确率不足以刻画社会认知能力，需以过程化、理论驱动的基准进行细粒度诊断。

Abstract: Understanding human needs and selecting responses require distinct judgments that aggregate accuracy can obscure. We introduce PsycheMME, a video benchmark informed by psychology, with eight capabilities spanning grounding, states, needs, interaction decisions, memory, and risk. Drawing on Social Information Processing and the Perception-Action Cycle, this organization separates human understanding from response selection. We construct 1,000 questions from 630 episodes through independent answering with proposed references withheld and expert review. Our Reasoning-Verified protocol weights answer credit by the support and logical consistency of explicit rationales against annotated cues. Across 17 models, Timing has the lowest Verified scores and credit retention, with weaker support judgments for correct Timing answers. Models with similar Need scores still differ in answer completeness on open questions. Input controls and SFT/GRPO examine context dependence and adaptation. Evaluating answers alongside rationales locates where models fail across understanding, response selection, and justification.

</details>


### [106] [Neural Implicit Energy Optimization for LiDAR Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28598)
*Hrishikesh Viswanath,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Chi Lin,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将神经LiDAR建图表述为神经隐式表示参数空间上的能量泛函优化，基于SDF水平集的形状算子（曲率）定义能量泛函正则项，以提升大规模稀疏噪声LiDAR重建的平滑性且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经隐式表示（如SDF）为大规模LiDAR场景提供内存高效的参数化，但从稀疏且常含噪声的输入恢复的几何存在抖动与伪影；经典几何优化用表面能量泛函（其极小化对应光滑曲面）来消除此类伪影，因此希望将该原理引入神经LiDAR建图。

Method: 把神经LiDAR建图形式化为神经隐式表示参数空间上的能量泛函优化；具体地，用定义于SDF水平集形状算子（shape operator，表征曲面曲率）上的能量泛函对SDF进行正则化，从而沿曲面促进平滑，并将经典几何先验推广至百万点级LiDAR地图的尺度。

Result: 在大规模数据集上，重建结果平滑性提升，同时保持几何保真度，且不引入额外推理时开销。

Conclusion: 基于形状算子的能量泛函正则化可将经典几何先验有效适配到大规模神经隐式LiDAR建图，在保持几何精度的前提下改善平滑性，且具零推理开销；代码已公开。

Abstract: Neural implicit representations provide memory-efficient signed distance field (SDF) parameterizations for large-scale LiDAR scenes, but the geometry recovered from sparse and often noisy inputs carries jitter and artifacts. Classical geometric optimization techniques resolve such artifacts with surface energy functionals, whose minimizers correspond to smooth surfaces. Motivated by this principle, we formulate neural LiDAR mapping as the optimization of energy functionals over the parameter space of neural implicit representations. Specifically, we regularize the signed distance field with an energy functional defined on the shape operator of its level set, which represents the curvature of the surface, thus encouraging smoothness along the surface. This adapts classical geometric priors to the scale of million-point LiDAR maps. Experiments on large-scale datasets show reconstructions with improved smoothness at preserved geometric fidelity, and no additional inference-time cost. The code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/HrishikeshVish/nieo}{https://github.com/HrishikeshVish/nieo}

</details>


### [107] [Latent Bridge: From Video Latents to 3D Geometry via Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28521)
*Shuai Zhang,Zhanpeng Ouyang,Zhengqing Chen,Ruohong Mei,Shaoyu Chen,Weiqiang Ren,Hui Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Bridge，将视频生成潜空间与几何特征空间直接对齐，实现从视频潜变量直接预测相机、深度和3D点云，无需中间 RGB 往返。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成与前馈3D重建通常使用不同的表示，需先由生成潜变量解码 RGB、再视觉重编码以恢复几何，流程冗余且低效。

Method: 为 Wan2.1 与 V-RAE 分别构建表示专用 bridge，在冻结视频编码器与几何模型的前提下，仅用 RGB 视频做特征蒸馏训练；无需真值几何，也无需运行下游几何聚合器或预测头。

Result: 在 Co3Dv2 与 WildRGB-D 上取得有竞争力的重建质量，端到端生成-重建高效，潜变量到几何解码更快；同一 bridge 可复用于同潜空间的 I2V 与 T2V 生成而无需重训。

Conclusion: 潜空间对齐是连接视频生成与3D重建的实用、可复用接口。

Abstract: Video generation and feed-forward 3D reconstruction typically use separate representations, requiring RGB decoding and visual re-encoding to recover geometry from generated latents. We propose Latent Bridge, which connects video generative latent spaces with geometric feature spaces to directly predict 3D geometry from video latents. We develop representation-specific bridges for Wan2.1 and V-RAE, enabling feed-forward prediction of cameras, depth, and 3D point clouds without the intermediate RGB pathway. Each bridge is trained on RGB videos through feature distillation, while the video encoder and geometry model remain frozen. Training requires neither ground-truth geometry nor execution of the downstream geometric aggregator or prediction heads. At inference, the same video latents support both RGB decoding and geometry prediction. The same trained bridge can be reused for image-to-video and text-to-video generation within the same video latent space, without bridge retraining. Experiments on Co3Dv2 and WildRGB-D demonstrate competitive geometric reconstruction quality, efficient end-to-end generation and reconstruction, and faster latent-to-geometry decoding, highlighting latent alignment as a practical and reusable interface between video generation and 3D reconstruction. Our code is available at: https://github.com/hustvl/Latent-Bridge.

</details>


### [108] [Answering in the User's Variety: Calibrated Accommodation Pairs for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28474)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Heming Li,Jian Yang,Junyu Luo,Jingshu Peng,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文追踪SFT→DPO→RL各后训练阶段对"语域/方言顺应"倾向的影响，提出校准顺应偏好对（CAP）数据，使模型按用户方言密度顺应，同时不损失标准英语用户的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会随对话者调整语言变体，但聊天模型倾向用标准英语统一回复；统一输出既掩盖训练是否差别对待不同方言用户，也使输出审计失效，因此需要从"更新层面"而非仅输出层面审查对齐过程。

Method: 在10个开放模型系谱上逐阶段（SFT、DPO、强化学习）测量对方言线索的顺应倾向；以内容匹配的成对回复（一条顺应用户、一条不顺）反推各更新的偏好，并对回复的既有似然进行校准。随后构建CAP：校准的顺应偏好对，奖励匹配用户方言密度同时保留标准英语用户的标准英语。

Result: 后训练将所有非正式线索统一标准化；但校准后DPO更新仍在10个系谱上更偏好匹配英式/澳式拼写而非AAE与Singlish，且模型被要求时仍能产出AAE特征。在完全开放模型上，CAP在未训练特征上提升收敛度，且未检测到标准英语用户方言增加或指令遵循下降；单边偏好对只能通过"过度顺应"强加方言来获得类似增益。

Conclusion: 统一回复会掩盖选择性更新，审计应同时读取更新与输出；CAP提供一种双向校准的偏好数据方案，可实现顺应而不越界。

Abstract: People adapt how they talk to the person in front of them. However, chat models tend to respond in Standard English regardless of the dialect used by the user. A uniform answer can hide whether training treats these users alike, and output audits cannot tell as well. We trace how each post-training stage, from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to preference optimization (DPO) and reinforcement learning, changes a model's tendency to match the user, and read what each update prefers from two content-matched replies, one matching the user and one not. Across 10 open model lineages, post-training standardizes replies to every informal cue, yet after adjusting for how likely each reply already was, the DPO update still favours matching British and Australian spelling over AAE and Singlish on 10 lineages, and models still produce AAE features when asked. We then introduce calibrated accommodation pairs (CAP), preference data that reward matching users' dialect density while preserving Standard English for Standard-English users. On a fully open model, CAP raises convergence on features it was not trained on without a detectable increase in dialect for Standard-English users or a cost in instruction following. In contrast, one-sided pairs reach a similar gain only by overshooting the user and imposing dialect. Our findings show that uniform replies can hide selective updates and audits should read the update as well as the output. Code: https://github.com/kouzhizhuo/cap-accommodation.

</details>


### [109] [VisualEvolve: A Skill Self-Evolution Framework for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28386)
*Jiawei Wang,Yucun Li,Xueqi Li,Shenghao Wang,Xinquan Gong,Dan Zhang,Zheqi Lv,Yuquan Le,Shaozu Yuan,An Zhang,Da Cao,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisualEvolve 自进化框架，通过 Select–Execute–Evolve–Evaluate 循环在不更新模型参数的前提下进化可复用的视觉操作与工具使用技能，缓解技能进化带来的性能回退与证据过载问题，在 24 个设置中 23 个优于初始技能库，最大提升 15.1 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉工具使用可帮助 VLM 获取任务相关证据，但其效果受限于预定义的视觉操作和训练中习得的工具使用策略。将这些操作与策略表示为可复用的外部技能，可通过自进化在不更新参数的情况下改进；但技能改进可能引入性能回退，且同时检查累积轨迹与技能进化历史会导致“进化证据过载”。

Method: 提出 VisualEvolve，一个 Select–Execute–Evolve–Evaluate 循环的自进化框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) Skill-Library Snapshot Selection：利用与执行任务不相交的固定验证集上的表现，选择有效的历史技能库快照用于后续进化，限制性能回退的传播；(2) Progressive Evidence Access (PEA)：以由粗到细的层级引导能力差距诊断，依次为 Skill-Performance Report、Evolution Log、Trajectory Summaries 及相关原始轨迹片段。

Result: 在 4 个 VLM 骨干和 3 个基准的 6 个评测集上，VisualEvolve 在 24 个设置中有 23 个优于初始技能库；使用 Qwen3.5-Plus 在 VisualProbe Hard 上最大提升 15.1 个百分点。消融实验支持 Snapshot Selection 与 PEA 的贡献；技能可借助不同数据设置的经验改进，并能直接跨骨干复用；相比代表性 RL 方法，任务求解轨迹用量减少一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: VisualEvolve 实现了有效的技能自进化：在不更新模型参数的情况下提升 VLM 视觉工具使用能力，同时通过快照选择抑制性能回退、通过渐进式证据访问缓解证据过载，并具备跨数据设置与跨骨干的技能可复用性与较高的样本效率。

Abstract: Visual tool use enables vision-language models (VLMs) to acquire task-relevant evidence, but its effectiveness can be limited by predefined visual operations and the tool-use strategies learned during training. Representing these operations and strategies as reusable external skills allows them to be refined through self-evolution without updating model parameters. However, skill refinement can introduce performance regressions. Meanwhile, inspecting accumulated task execution trajectories together with skill evolution history may lead to evolution evidence overload. To address these challenges, we propose VisualEvolve, a self-evolving framework that evolves executable visual operations and tool-use strategies for VLMs through a Select--Execute--Evolve--Evaluate cycle. Skill-Library Snapshot Selection uses performance on a fixed validation set disjoint from execution tasks to select an effective historical snapshot for subsequent evolution, limiting the propagation of performance regressions. Progressive Evidence Access (PEA) guides capability-gap diagnosis through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy comprising the Skill-Performance Report, Evolution Log, Trajectory Summaries, and relevant raw trajectory segments. Across four VLM backbones and six evaluation sets from three benchmarks, VisualEvolve improves over the Initial Skill Library in 23 of 24 settings, with a maximum gain of 15.1 percentage points on VisualProbe Hard using Qwen3.5-Plus. Ablation studies support the contributions of Snapshot Selection and PEA, while further experiments show that skills can be improved using experience from different data settings and directly reused across backbones. Moreover, VisualEvolve enables effective skill self-evolution with over an order of magnitude fewer task-solving trajectories than estimated for representative RL-based methods.

</details>


### [110] [RayCo4D: Ray-Conditioned Continuous Motion Model for Monocular 4D Dynamic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28351)
*Yuanxiang Ni,Xianliang Huang,Ruihan Hou,Feng Xie,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayCo4D提出一种射线条件化的连续运动模型用于单目4D重建，将视觉观测与潜在3D运动状态显式分离：每条长时轨迹绑定一个限定在相机射线上的参考时刻3D查询，经局部多对多关联约束共享的潜在运动节点；每个节点在稀疏支撑时刻持有SE(3)位姿与体坐标系速度，以白噪声-加速度先验耦合相邻状态并定义连续时间插值；推断出的共享运动经双四元数混合迁移到高斯图元，并结合参考锚定的局部SE(3)残差建模细尺度非刚性形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频动态3D重建需从含噪且间歇可见的观测中推断运动。长时轨迹能提供有价值的几何约束，但其时间覆盖是碎片化的，难以整合进统一的连续运动表示，导致快速运动与轨迹不完整情形下的结构与时间一致性不足。

Method: (1) 解耦视觉测量与潜在3D运动状态：每条轨迹的参考时刻3D查询被约束在其相机射线上，图像与深度观测通过局部多对多关联约束共享潜在运动节点。(2) 连续运动表示：每个节点在稀疏支撑时刻维护SE(3)位姿与body-frame速度，采用white-noise-on-acceleration过程先验耦合相邻状态并给出连续时间插值，使不同可见区间内的观测共同约束同一潜在状态序列。(3) 运动传递与形变补偿：共享运动经dual-quaternion blending迁移至高斯图元，并用参考锚定的局部SE(3)残差刻画超出共享骨架的细尺度非刚性形变。(4) 支持连续时间渲染。

Result: 在多种单目动态场景上，RayCo4D取得有竞争力的新视角合成质量；在快速运动与轨迹不完整条件下改善了结构一致性与时间一致性；并支持连续时间渲染。

Conclusion: 通过射线条件化查询与带过程先验的连续SE(3)运动节点，可将碎片化长时轨迹统一到共享连续运动序列中，再经双四元数混合与局部残差迁移至高斯表示，实现兼顾一致性与连续性的单目4D重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from monocular video requires inferring motion from noisy and intermittently visible observations. Long-range tracks provide valuable geometric constraints, but integrating their fragmented temporal coverage into shared, continuous motion remains challenging. We introduce \textbf{RayCo4D}, a ray-conditioned continuous motion model for monocular 4D reconstruction that explicitly separates visual measurements from latent 3D motion states. Each track is associated with a reference-time 3D query constrained to its camera ray, and its image and depth observations constrain shared latent motion nodes through local many-to-many associations. Each node maintains an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ pose and body-frame velocity at sparse support times. A white-noise-on-acceleration process prior couples neighboring states and defines continuous-time interpolation, allowing observations across visibility intervals to jointly constrain the same latent state sequence.The inferred shared motion is transferred to Gaussian primitives through dual-quaternion blending, while reference-anchored local $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ residuals capture fine-scale non-rigid deformation beyond the shared scaffold. Experiments on diverse monocular dynamic scenes show that RayCo4D achieves competitive novel-view synthesis quality, improves structural and temporal consistency under rapid motion and incomplete tracks, and supports continuous-time rendering.

</details>


### [111] [PI-MLLM: Psychophysics-Inspired Multimodal Large Language Model for Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28264)
*Yuheng Zhang,Jie Zhang,Yuecong Min,Yunwei Zhao,Meichen Liu,Qiankun Li,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建了Oddly Satisfying Video Dataset（OSVD，12,000个标注5秒片段）以支持视频情感分析（OSVEA），并提出受心理物理学启发的多模态大模型PI-MLLM，通过模态特定动态对齐（MSDA）与基于联盟的音视频归因（CAVA）提升观众情感预测与感官解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSV通过重复运动、视觉触感与类ASMR声音引发强烈观众反应，但缺乏显式语义情感线索；现有VEA方法过度依赖语义内容，忽视塑造观众反应的物理感官动态，因此需构建专门数据集与方法。

Method: 构建OSVD数据集：12,000个5秒片段，每片段在纯视频、纯音频、音视频三种条件下提供观众情感标签，并给出基于视觉与听觉感官证据的解释。提出PI-MLLM：MSDA通过辅助预测目标将模态表征与视觉运动和声学能量起始动态对齐；CAVA跨模态联盟评估情感分数并用Shapley归因识别主导模态、判断视听证据为协同或冲突。

Result: 实验表明PI-MLLM在观众侧情感预测与感官依据解释质量两方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 物理感官线索对视频情感分析具有重要价值，模态动态对齐与联盟归因能有效建模OSV中的情感反应。

Abstract: Oddly Satisfying Videos (OSVs) can evoke strong viewer responses through repetitive motion, visual tactility, and ASMR-like sounds despite containing few explicit semantic emotion cues. This makes OSVs a challenging setting for Video Emotion Analysis (VEA), since most existing methods focus on semantic video content and under-model the physical sensory dynamics that shape viewer responses. To support Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis (OSVEA), we construct the Oddly Satisfying Video Dataset (OSVD), comprising 12,000 annotated 5-second clips. Each clip provides viewer-emotion labels under video-only, audio-only, and audio-visual conditions, together with explanations grounded in visual and acoustic sensory evidence. Building on OSVD, we propose PI-MLLM, a psychophysics-inspired multimodal large language model that integrates Modality-Specific Dynamics Alignment (MSDA) and Coalition-Based Audio-Visual Attribution (CAVA). MSDA uses auxiliary prediction objectives to align modality representations with visual motion and acoustic energy-onset dynamics. CAVA evaluates emotion scores across modality coalitions and applies Shapley attribution to identify the dominant modality and determine whether visual and auditory evidence is synergistic or conflicting. Experiments show that PI-MLLM improves both viewer-side emotion prediction and sensory-grounded explanation quality, suggesting the value of physical sensory cues to video emotion analysis.

</details>


### [112] [SplatFlux: Feed-Forward 4D Gaussian Reconstruction and Generation from Video Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28257)
*Runmin Zhang,Binglin Ren,Zhu Yu,Fudong Wang,Jiangke Lin,Yichun Shentu,Si-Yuan Cao,Yi Yuan,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatFlux是一个前馈的latent-to-4DGS框架，统一实现基于视频的4D重建与条件4D生成，通过静态-动态解耦4D高斯表示与蒸馏策略达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到观测视频与条件生成内容可表示在同一预训练视频潜空间中，从而两类任务可统一为latent-to-4DGS解码问题；同时需避免昂贵的动态标注。

Method: 提出静态-动态解耦4D高斯表示，显式预测高斯基元的动态概率，将静态结构与运动区域分离，通过静态聚合与动态运动插值前馈构建完整4D场景；并设计辅助蒸馏策略提供伪动态掩码与光流线索来学习分解与运动。

Result: 在3D/4D重建与生成任务上的大量实验表明达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 统一潜空间解码与静态-动态解耦表示可高效、紧凑且时序一致地完成4D重建与生成，且无需昂贵标注。

Abstract: This work presents SplatFlux, a feed-forward latent-to-4DGS framework for both video-based 4D reconstruction and conditional 4D generation. Our key observation is that observed videos and conditionally generated content can be represented in the same pretrained video latent space, allowing both tasks to be formulated as latent-to-4DGS decoding. To construct compact and temporally coherent 4D scenes, SplatFlux introduces a static-dynamic decoupled 4D Gaussian representation that explicitly predicts dynamic probabilities for Gaussian primitives. This representation separates static scene structures from moving regions, enabling complete 4D scene construction in a feed-forward manner through static aggregation and dynamic motion interpolation. To learn static-dynamic decomposition and dynamic Gaussian motion without costly annotations, we further develop an auxiliary distillation strategy that provides pseudo dynamic masks and optical-flow cues. Extensive experiments across 3D and 4D reconstruction and generation demonstrate that SplatFlux achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [113] [AEON: Unifying Video and 3D World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28631)
*Weiqi Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Junsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AEON 提出统一框架，在文本/图像条件视频潜空间内生成可渲染的 4D Gaussian Splatting 表示，兼得视频世界模型的真实感与 3D 世界模型的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显式世界模拟分两路：视频生成模型（如 Sora）视觉真实但缺乏 3D 一致性；3D 生成模型（如 Marble）几何一致但静态、真实感不足。作者希望融合两者优势。

Method: 核心是在视频潜空间中进行生成，并输出可渲染的 4DGS 作为显式世界表示。用单目视频训练 4D 重建模型，从视频同时预测 4DGS 与相机位姿，作为初始化的 4D 解码器；再设计域适配器，通过对视频 VAE 的蒸馏将生成式视频潜变量转换为重建式 4D 潜变量。

Result: AEON 可在预测的相机轨迹上渲染 4DGS 以支持视频式世界建模，并构建含场景动态的持久 4D 环境；在标准基准上取得 SOTA 性能，支撑多样化世界建模应用。

Conclusion: 通过在视频生成潜空间与 4DGS 显式表示之间建立桥接，AEON 统一了视频式与 3D 式世界模型，实现兼具几何一致性与真实感、且支持动态的持久世界模拟。

Abstract: Current world models for explicit world simulation typically follow two distinct pathways: (1) video generative models (e.g., Sora), which simulate realistic appearances but fail to preserve 3D consistency; and (2) 3D generative models (e.g., Marble), which generate geometrically consistent scenes but often yield static, less realistic observations. We present AEON, a unified framework that bridges video-based and 3D-based world models and combines the benefits of both. The key insight of AEON lies in a generative model that operates within the text- or image-conditioned video latent space while producing renderable 4D graphics primitives---specifically, 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS)---as the explicit world representation. We train a 4D reconstruction model to predict both 4DGS and camera poses from monocular videos, serving as our initialized 4D decoder. Furthermore, we design a domain adapter that translates generative video latents into reconstructive 4D latents through a distillation process on the video VAE. AEON naturally supports video-based world modeling by rendering 4DGS at predicted camera trajectories, and constructs a persistent 4D environment with scene dynamics for realistic world simulation. Consequently, AEON empowers a diverse range of world modeling applications and achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks. Project Page: https://weiqi-zhang.github.io/AEON

</details>


### [114] [OverSeer: Multi-Agent World Models Beyond What Agents See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28554)
*Runjia Li,Kam Woh Ng,Minghao Chen,Junlin Han,Jianyuan Wang,Zhaochong An,Juan-Manuel Perez-Rua,Andrea Vedaldi,Shikun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OverSeer 通过引入空间固定的辅助相机视角作为世界中心表示的代理，并结合 delta-VAE 压缩，显著提升多智能体视频世界模型的时间一致性与智能体间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体视频世界模型需生成多路智能体专属视频流，并要求其在时间上及智能体之间保持一致，现有方法难以做到。作者观察到以自我为中心的视角会因智能体位置微小变化而剧烈改变，不利于维持一致性，因此需要一种更稳定、更能对应世界状态变化的表示。

Method: 在自我中心视角之外增加空间位置固定的辅助相机视角，作为世界中心表示的代理，因为固定视角的变化直接对应世界状态的局部变化；由于辅助视角具有冗余和半静态特性，采用 delta-VAE 仅编码相对于静态参考图像的差异，从而减少处理这些视角所需的 token 数量。

Result: 在 Minecraft 和 Doom 上的实验显示世界一致性与智能体一致性均有提升；真实世界多相机拍摄的定性结果说明方法不局限于游戏引擎；压缩分析表明 delta-VAE 大幅降低辅助视角生成的计算开销。

Conclusion: 空间固定的辅助视角为改进多智能体世界模型提供了一种简单而高效的方式。

Abstract: Despite the success of video world models, extending them to multiple agents is difficult because it requires generating several agent-specific video streams that are consistent in time and between agents. To address this gap, we introduce OverSeer, a multi-agent world model that augments egocentric agent views with auxiliary camera views. We suggest that, by fixing the auxiliary views in space, they act as a proxy for a world-centric representation: Unlike the agents' views, which can change entirely due to a simple change in agent position, changes in the auxiliary views directly map to localized changes in the world state. We show that these fixed views substantially help the model to maintain consistency. We also show that, due to their redundant and semi-static nature, the auxiliary views can be efficiently compressed with a delta-VAE that encodes only the changes relative to a static reference image, reducing the number of tokens required to process them. Experiments in Minecraft and Doom demonstrate improved world and agent consistency, with qualitative results on real-world multi-camera captures indicating that the approach is not tied to game engines. Our compression analysis further shows that delta-VAE substantially reduces the computational overhead of auxiliary-view generation. We conclude that these fixed views provide a simple and efficient way to improve multi-agent world models.

</details>


### [115] [Learning from Automatic 3D Gaussian Splatting Edits for Online Bayesian Scene Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28490)
*Changrui Zhu,Simon Julier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于广义贝叶斯证据融合的在线场景变化检测框架，通过离线编辑3DGS自动生成监督信号，学习场景特定证据模型与先验，结合累积3D高斯场与物体引导精化输出变化掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线场景变化检测需将新观测与参考场景融合以判定变化区域，但变化先验与证据模型事先未知，且数值贝叶斯推断计算代价高，难以满足在线需求。

Method: 利用对参考3DGS模型的自动编辑生成带标签训练视图（无需人工标注），联合未变化参考观测监督学习场景特定证据模型并估计有效像素先验与响应统计；在线阶段将当前证据与历史支持融合，由学习到的响应统计参数化的闭式局部贝叶斯因子更新累积3D高斯场，再用物体引导精化得到最终变化掩码。

Result: 在PASLCD与CCS3D上达到SOTA，mIoU分别为67.64%与75.82%；在PASLCD完整查询序列上全流程运行速度17.2 FPS，为所评估在线方法中最快。

Conclusion: 以3DGS自动编辑替代人工标注实现自监督学习，并用闭式局部贝叶斯因子替代昂贵数值推断，可在保证精度领先的同时实现高效在线变化检测。

Abstract: Online scene change detection requires integrating incoming observations to determine which regions have changed relative to a reference scene. We update change beliefs as new images arrive using generalised Bayesian evidence fusion. However, the prior probability of change and evidence models are unknown beforehand, and numerical Bayesian inference can be costly. We address these challenges by learning offline from automatic edits to a reference 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model. These edits generate labelled training views without manual annotations. Together with unchanged reference observations, they provide supervision for learning scene-specific evidence models and estimating the effective pixel prior and response statistics. Online prediction combines current evidence with historical support, while closed-form local Bayes factors, parameterized by the learned response statistics, update an accumulated 3D Gaussian field. Object-guided refinement produces the final change masks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on PASLCD and CCS3D, with $67.64\%$ and $75.82\%$ mIoU, respectively. On PASLCD, the complete pipeline runs at $17.2$\,FPS over complete query sequences, the fastest among the evaluated online methods.

</details>


### [116] [Gated Prototype Transfer for Zero-Support Medical Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28350)
*Sumaiya Fazal,Rizwan Qureshi,Laleh Behjat,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练模块GProtoT，通过门控知识引导的原型迁移，为无支持样本的低患病类补足视觉原型，在糖尿病视网膜病变one-shot试验中显著提升缺失类召回并抑制负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型在真实少样本适应中，低患病类可能在支持集中完全缺失标签（零支持）。此时免训练的SS-Text+求解器虽仍可用，但其零支持原型无法获得视觉适应，只能退化为纯文本类原型，导致这些类别缺乏视觉信息。

Method: GProtoT是一个前置抑制（pre-repulsion）模块：利用VLM相似度与基于本体的临床距离，将受支持类的视觉残差迁移到缺失类；并设计无需验证集（validation-free）的证据门控，依据语义相关性与残差一致性缩放迁移强度，从而限制缺乏支撑的外推。方法基于SS-Text+，无需梯度、验证数据或额外模型推理。

Result: 在MESSIDOR数据集+FLAIR特征的50次配对真实one-shot试验中（重度与增殖性糖尿病视网膜病变在所有支持集中均缺失）：相对SS-Text+，门控ProtoT将缺失类召回提升5.23个百分点、平均类准确率提升1.37个百分点、缺失/支持召回调和均值提升1.83个百分点；负迁移频率由无门控迁移的46–48%降至2%；代价为受支持类召回下降1.20个百分点、top-1准确率下降0.45个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于证据的门控为知识源提供了安全机制；GProtoT在监督不完整的临床场景下，为可问责的模型适应提供了一条低算力路径。

Abstract: Realistic few-shot adaptation of medical vision-language models (VLMs) can leave low-prevalence classes without any labeled support. The training-free SS- Text+ solver remains operational in this setting, but its zero-support prototypes receive no visual adaptation and fall back to a purely textual class prototype. We in- troduce Gated Knowledge-Guided Prototype Transfer (GProtoT), a pre-repulsion module that transfers supported-class visual residuals to missing classes using VLM similarity and an ontology-based clinical distance. A validation-free evidence gate scales transfer based on semantic relevance and residual agreement, thereby limiting unsupported extrapolation. In 50 paired realistic one-shot trials on MESSIDOR with FLAIR features, severe and proliferative diabetic retinopathy were absent from every support set. Relative to SS-Text+, gated ProtoT improved missing-class recall by 5.23 points, average class accuracy by 1.37 points, and the missing/supported recall harmonic mean by 1.83 points. It reduced negative- transfer frequency from 46-48% for ungated transfer to 2%, while at the cost of reductions of 1.20 percentage points in supported-class recall and 0.45 percentage points in top-1 accuracy. These results indicate that evidence-based gating provides a safety mechanism for the knowledge source. GProtoT is based on SS- Text+ and requires no gradients, validation data, or additional model inference, of- fering a low-compute path toward accountable adaptation under incomplete clini- cal supervision. Code is available: https://github.com/sumaiyaafridi/GProtoT

</details>


### [117] [HistoLoop: Iterative Contextual Inference with a Looped Transformer for Histology-Based Spatial Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28339)
*Ziang Liu,Jie Liu,Shuai Li,Yigui Feng,Qinglin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HistoLoop将"从H&E图像预测空间基因表达"重构为迭代式上下文推断，用共享权重的循环Transformer在多尺度上反复由更新后的spot状态重建上下文，在HEST-Bench HVG50上取得六项任务中最高的宏观平均基因级Pearson相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽在空间上下文建模上有所进展，但"如何由早期上下文交互更新后的spot状态来构建后续上下文"这一环节研究不足，导致多尺度/多轮上下文推断未被充分利用。

Method: 提出HistoLoop——循环（looped）Transformer：每一精化步将当前spot状态聚合为对应分辨率的上下文token，经共享空间Transformer处理，再通过cross-attention为每个spot检索其专属上下文；采用记忆介导的更新将该信息写入持续存在的spot状态，并据此在下一更细分辨率上重建上下文。

Result: 在HEST-1K中五个器官、六项癌症相关任务上，按HEST-Bench HVG50协议评估，HistoLoop的宏观平均基因级Pearson相关为对比方法中最高；消融实验证实循环状态精化与"由更新后spot状态在递进更细分辨率上重建上下文"二者均重要；基于表达的无监督聚类显示其更能刻画空间组织的多基因模式。

Conclusion: 将预测建模为迭代上下文推断、并让上下文由被更新过的spot状态逐级重建，是提升H&E到空间转录组预测性能与空间模式刻画能力的有效范式。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from H&E images offers a scalable complement to experimental spatial transcriptomics. However, despite progress in spatial context modeling, how to construct subsequent context from spot states updated by earlier contextual interactions remains underexplored. We introduce HistoLoop, a looped Transformer that formulates prediction as iterative contextual inference. At each refinement step, HistoLoop aggregates the current spot states into context tokens at the corresponding resolution, processes them with a shared spatial Transformer, and retrieves spot-specific contextual information through cross-attention. A memory-mediated update integrates this information into persistent spot states, which are then used to construct context at the next, finer resolution. Under the HEST-Bench HVG50 evaluation protocol, HistoLoop achieves the highest macro-averaged gene-wise Pearson correlation among the compared methods across six cancer-specific tasks spanning five organs in HEST-1K. Ablation studies support the importance of recurrent state refinement and reconstructing context from updated spot states at progressively finer resolutions. Expression-derived clustering further suggests that HistoLoop better captures spatially organized multigene patterns.

</details>


### [118] [Towards Consistent Geospatial Representations: Aligning One Encoder to Many Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28313)
*Arthur Ouaknine,David Rolnick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Pangaea-Latents 基准（5 个地理空间基础模型在 11 个数据集上的冻结嵌入）与 GeoMixture 框架，通过多表征对齐目标使单个 ViT 继承多个 GFM 的互补性，在 9 个数据集上平均排名并列最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型（GFM）在预训练目标、模态与分辨率上差异很大，且没有任何单一模型能在所有基准上领先。作者希望验证这些模型的表征是否互补，以及单个编码器能否继承这种互补性。

Method: 构建 Pangaea-Latents：作为 Pangaea 基准的扩展，提供 5 个 GFM 在其 11 个数据集上的冻结嵌入及复现的基线。提出 GeoMixture：按数据集将单个 ViT 与多个 GFM 对齐，采用多表征对齐目标 mREPA。

Result: GFM 嵌入既非冗余也非完全不相交，具有互补性；GeoMixture 编码器在 9 个 Pangaea 数据集上的平均排名与最佳单一 GFM 持平，即以牺牲峰值性能换取跨任务一致性。

Conclusion: 多个 GFM 的表征可被单一编码器有效融合，跨任务一致性可作为替代追求单一峰值性能的可行设计目标；该工作为 GFM 表征分析与融合提供了基准与初步框架。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) differ widely in pretraining objective, modality and resolution, and none leads across benchmarks. We ask whether their representations are complementary, and whether a single encoder can inherit that complementarity. We introduce Pangaea-Latents, an extension of the Pangaea benchmark providing frozen embeddings of $5$ GFMs over its $11$ datasets, along with reproduced baselines, and find GFM embeddings to be neither redundant nor disjoint. We then propose GeoMixture, a preliminary framework to align a ViT to several GFMs per dataset through a multi-representation alignment (mREPA) objective. Our encoder ties the best GFM in average rank across nine Pangaea datasets, trading peak performance for cross-task consistency.

</details>


### [119] [Convergent Performance, Divergent Mechanisms in LoRA-Adapted Vision Foundation Models for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28255)
*Cecilia Diana-Albelda,Fiona Kekwick,Raghav Mehta,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Paul M. Matthews,Jesus Bescos,Álvaro García-Martín,Wenjia Bai,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 同等分割性能下，四个ViT-B/16视觉基础模型经LoRA适配后内部表征变化机制迥异，且跨任务迁移表现分化；将LoRA限制于中早层可在保持分割性能的同时减少分类退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将视觉基础模型（VFM）通过LoRA适配到医学影像已成常规做法，但骨干模型的选择几乎完全由下游任务指标决定，性能相近的骨干被视为可互换。作者质疑"同等性能是否意味着同等的内部表征变化"，并探究不同预训练目标（自蒸馏 vs. 对比视觉-语言）与数据域（自然 vs. 医学）的模型在适配后内部动态与跨任务迁移上的差异。

Method: 选取四个ViT-B/16骨干：DINOv3、SigLIP2、MerMED-FM、BiomedCLIP，分别用LoRA适配到脑肿瘤分割任务，并在六个数据集上评估分割与阿尔茨海默病分类。采用三类机制分析工具：注意力散度（attention divergence）、权重变化分析（weight-change analysis）、激活修补（activation patching），以刻画内部表征变化及其因果重要性。

Result: 四个模型分割性能趋同（平均Dice 78–81%），但内部动态显著不同；跨任务迁移出现分化——分割适配提升了DINOv3的阿尔茨海默病分类性能，却使其余三个骨干性能下降。发现修改幅度与因果重要性之间存在解离：被改动最大的层并非因果相关层，中早层足以恢复分割性能。

Conclusion: 将LoRA限制于中早层可达到或接近全层分割性能，同时减轻三个受影响骨干的分类退化，提供了一种有机制依据、更省参数且更鲁棒的适配策略；也表明仅凭下游指标选择骨干是不够的，需考虑内部表征变化与跨任务迁移特性。

Abstract: Adapting Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to medical imaging via LoRA has become standard practice, yet model selection is guided almost entirely by downstream task metrics, treating backbones with similar performance as interchangeable. We investigate whether equal performance implies equal internal representational shift. We analyse four ViT-B/16 backbones, DINOv3, SigLIP2, MerMED-FM, and BiomedCLIP, pre-trained with different objectives (self-distillation vs. contrastive vision-language) and on different data domains (natural vs. medical images). We adapt each to brain tumour segmentation and evaluate on six datasets covering both segmentation and Alzheimer's disease classification. Using attention divergence, weight-change analysis, and activation patching, we find that the four models converge to similar segmentation performance (mean Dice 78-81%) through markedly different internal dynamics, yet diverge in cross-task transfer: segmentation adaptation improves Alzheimer's disease classification for DINOv3 but degrades it for the other three backbones. We reveal a dissociation between modification magnitude and causal importance: the most heavily modified layers are not the causally relevant ones, with early-to-mid layers identified as sufficient for segmentation recovery. Restricting LoRA to these layers matches or approaches full-layer segmentation while reducing classification degradation in the three affected backbones, providing a mechanistically grounded strategy that is more parameter-efficient and more robust than standard full-layer adaptation.

</details>


### [120] [Region-Guided Food-Package Evidence Extraction and Abstention-Aware Draft R3337 Screening with Selective VLM Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28211)
*Mayimunah Nagayi,Alice Scaria Khan,Tamryn Frank,Rina Swart,Clement Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向南非R3337草案的区域引导证据抽取与“弃权感知”筛查流程：三分类检测器定位营养标签、配料表与产品标识区，OCR与结构化抽取恢复证据，按确定性规则判定；证据不全/冲突时弃权（Review/Insufficient evidence），选择性VLM复审减少弃权。442个产品（3929张图）、65个产品人工验证：自动覆盖率51.7%（选择性准确率96.8%），经VLM复审升至65.0%（97.4%），无漏报警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将R3337警告标签判据应用于食品包装照片，需同时获得营养、配料与产品形态三类证据；单一OCR或端到端模型难以保证证据完整与判定可靠性，误报与漏报代价不对称，因此需要可控的抽取—判定—弃权机制。

Method: 三分类检测器定位营养信息面板、配料表与产品标识区域；PaddleOCR配合结构化抽取恢复营养值、配料证据与产品形态信息；证据聚合后按确定性R3337草案规则判定；证据不完整、不确定或冲突时弃权（Review/Insufficient evidence）；对符合条件的未决样本，用Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct进行选择性VLM复审，合并可接受证据后重新应用同一规则；在442个产品（3929张图像）上运行，并以65个产品人工验证集评估。

Result: VLM复审前：60个可判定产品中31个获得自动结论、30个正确，覆盖率51.7%，自动结论的选择性准确率96.8%。经选择性VLM复审新增8个产品结论，覆盖率升至65.0%；39个自动结论中38个正确，选择性准确率97.4%。唯一误报警源自仅规则流程，复审后无漏报警（不安全省略）；但证据级评估显示VLM恢复的营养、配料与产品形态证据存在错误。

Conclusion: 选择性VLM复审可有效恢复未决证据并提升覆盖率，但确定性筛查与显式弃权对可靠的基于图像的R3337筛查仍属必要；应同时在结论级与证据级评估系统可靠性。

Abstract: Applying South Africa's draft R3337 warning criteria to food-package photographs requires nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. This study presents a region-guided evidence extraction and abstention-aware screening workflow for draft R3337. A three-class detector localises nutrition information panels, ingredient lists and product-identity regions, after which PaddleOCR and structured extraction recover nutrient values, ingredient evidence and product-form information. Recovered evidence is aggregated and assessed using deterministic draft R3337 rules. When evidence is incomplete, uncertain or conflicting, the workflow abstains through Review or Insufficient evidence rather than forcing an automatic outcome. For qualifying unresolved cases, a selective vision-language model (VLM) review stage using Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct reassesses unresolved evidence. Accepted evidence is merged and the same deterministic rules are reapplied. The workflow was applied to 442 products represented by 3,929 images. Evaluation used a manual validation set of 65 products. Before selective VLM review, 31 of 60 decidable products received automatic outcomes and 30 were correct, giving 51.7% coverage and 96.8% accuracy among automatic outcomes (selective accuracy). Selective VLM review resolved eight additional products, increasing coverage to 65.0%. Of 39 automatic outcomes, 38 were correct, giving 97.4% selective accuracy. The single false warning was retained from the rules-only workflow, while no unsafe warning omissions occurred after selective review. Evidence-level evaluation nevertheless identified incorrect VLM-recovered nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. These findings show that selective VLM review can recover unresolved evidence, while deterministic screening and explicit abstention remain necessary for reliable image-based draft R3337 screening.

</details>


### [121] [Learn to Modify: Self-evolving Multi-agent Refinement Framework for Conversational Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28210)
*Jianan Li,Jiawei Zhao,Yangtao Zhou,Zhifu Zhao,Shaoxin Wu,Yutao Wang,Dezhen Wang,Hua Chu,Fu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出对话式动作生成（conversational motion generation）新范式，要求系统协调指令理解、动作生成、局部编辑与质量评估；为此提出自进化多智能体精炼框架 SMART，含语义引导掩码动作编辑机制与基于记忆的自进化策略，实现多轮对话下的高效局部精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法多为单次生成（single-shot）：用户对局部动作细节不满时只能整段重新生成，计算低效且会破坏已建立的运动学上下文。理想系统应支持迭代式用户反馈，仅精炼受影响的时空区域并保持其余动作不变。

Method: 构建对话式动作生成范式，需协同指令理解、动作生成、局部编辑与质量评估；基于 LLM 驱动的多智能体角色分工设计 SMART 框架。其中：(1) 语义引导掩码动作编辑机制，将自然语言反馈精确接地到时空动作区域，实现局部精炼而不改动未编辑区域，保持语义一致性；(2) 基于记忆的自进化策略，从交互历史中动态积累动作编辑知识并复用历史成功经验，减少冗余探索。

Result: 大量实验表明，SMART 能有效支持多轮对话式动作生成，并实现高效的动作精炼。

Conclusion: SMART 通过多智能体协同、语义接地式局部编辑与记忆驱动的自进化，将动作生成从单次范式推进到可迭代反馈的对话式范式，在保持未编辑区域的同时提升精炼效率。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in text-to-motion generation, existing methods are commonly designed for single-shot generation. When users are dissatisfied with specific motion details, they are often forced to regenerate the entire sequence from scratch, which is computationally inefficient and potentially destroys previously established kinematic context. A practical generation system should instead support iterative user feedback and refine only the affected spatio-temporal regions while preserving the remaining motion. Therefore, we introduce a conversational motion generation paradigm that requires the system to coordinate instruction understanding, motion generation, localized editing, and quality assessment. Recent advances in LLM-driven multi-agent systems provide a promising foundation for such role-specialized coordination. To tackle these challenges, a Self-evolving Multi-Agent RefinemenT Framework (SMART) is proposed for conversational motion generation. Within SMART, to preserve semantic consistency, we design a semantic-guided masked motion editing mechanism, which precisely grounds natural language feedback into spatiotemporal motion regions to enable localized refinements without altering unedited regions. To reduce redundant exploration, we propose a memory-based self-evolving strategy that dynamically accumulates motion editing knowledge from interaction history and reuses past successful experience. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SMART effectively supports multi-turn conversational motion generation and enables efficient motion refinement.

</details>


### [122] [WorldSR: Harnessing World Knowledge Search for Grounded Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28423)
*Junyuan Deng,Xinyi Wu,Zhenyao Wu,Song Wang,Yu Wang,Congchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldSR提出一种以显式、实体对齐的视觉世界知识增强超分的框架：将输入分解为语义实体，从大规模外部语料中做实体级检索，经记忆复用与多步过滤筛选参考图，再由多参考超分模型融合生成细节与结构，在真实场景基准上优于现有生成式超分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成先验式超分依赖大模型隐式知识，但先验本身随机、面向多样生成，与超分所需的确定性高保真重建相冲突；且其与基座模型绑定、更新代价高，并在真实数据演化时易产生幻觉。因此需要引入外部、可更新的显式知识来补充模型内在先验。

Method: 1) 自动将输入分解为语义实体；2) 对每个实体在大规模外部图像语料中检索结构与语义对齐的参考图；3) 引入记忆机制复用历史检索结果以提升效率，并采用多步过滤流程精炼候选参考；4) 通过新型多参考超分模型将选定的“世界知识”参考整合进细节合成与结构恢复。

Result: 在真实世界超分基准上，WorldSR在结构保真度与视觉质量上均稳定提升，性能优于现有生成式超分方法。

Conclusion: 实体级的外部视觉世界知识grounding是模型内在生成先验的一种简单而有效的补充范式，可缓解随机性与幻觉问题，并摆脱对单一基座模型的强绑定。

Abstract: Generative-prior-based super-resolution (SR) methods leverage implicit knowledge learned from large-scale models to enhance low-resolution inputs. However, such priors are inherently stochastic and optimized for diverse generation, conflicting with the deterministic, high-fidelity reconstruction required for SR. It is also tied to the base model, costly to update, and prone to hallucinations as real-world data evolves. To address these limitations, we introduce WorldSR, a novel framework that augments SR with explicit, entity-aligned visual world knowledge. WorldSR automatically decomposes the input into semantic entities and performs entity-wise retrieval of structurally and semantically aligned images from a large-scale external corpus. For efficiency and reliability, we incorporate a memory mechanism to reuse previously retrieved results and a multi-step filtering process to refine candidate references. The selected references as world knowledge are then integrated through a novel multi-reference super-resolution model, which provides fine-grained constraints for detail synthesis and structural recovery. Experiments on real-world SR benchmarks show that WorldSR consistently improves structural fidelity and visual quality, outperforming existing generative super-resolution methods. Our results suggest that entity-level grounding in external visual world knowledge provides a simple and effective complement to model-bound generative priors.

</details>


### [123] [KoalaGS: Complexity-Aware Continuous Level of Detail for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28615)
*Radu Beche,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KoalaGS 利用优化前的图像复杂度先验引导 3D 高斯原语的分配与重定位，使单一高斯集合在连续 LoD 与缩减算力预算下平滑降级并保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算力预算收缩、以更短前缀渲染时，3D Gaussian Splatting 场景需要同一套原语集合仍然有效；核心难题是决定预算下降时哪些高斯应保留容量。作者先行的试点研究发现：由输入视图在优化前计算出的图像复杂度可预测重建误差在图像与 3D 空间的集中位置；同时简单区域的高斯数量过多，且在低预算下退化程度与复杂区域相当，尽管其本应更易表示。

Method: 在 KoalaGS 训练中引入基于图像复杂度的先验，把原语重定位偏向重建较差（高复杂度）区域，同时保证跨 LoD 的平滑退化；再结合原语重要性评分，支持低容量推断并保持场景结构。

Result: 在三个数据集上测试，KoalaGS 在保持与基线相当的全容量质量的同时，显著提升整个预算区间的质量，跨 LoD 过渡更平滑，并支持更快的渐进式流式传输。

Conclusion: 以优化前的图像复杂度作为先验来指导高斯原语的容量分配与重定位，是提升 3DGS 连续 LoD 与受限算力部署质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Deploying a trained 3D Gaussian Splatting scene under shrinking compute budgets requires one primitive set to remain effective when rendered at smaller prefixes. In this continuous level of detail (LoD) setting, the challenge is to decide which Gaussians should retain capacity as the budget decreases. A pilot study on 3DGS-MCMC reconstructions shows that image complexity, computed from the input views before optimization, predicts where reconstruction error concentrates in image and 3D space. It also reveals that simple regions contain too many Gaussians and degrade as much as complex ones at lower budgets, despite being inherently cheaper to represent. Subsequently, in KoalaGS, during training we use this complexity prior to bias relocation toward poorly reconstructed areas while ensuring smooth degradation across LoDs. A primitive importance score then enables inference at low-capacity, while preserving the scene structure. Tested across three datasets, KoalaGS maintains competitive full-capacity quality while significantly improving quality across the budget spectrum, yielding smoother transitions across LoDs, and enabling faster progressive streaming.

</details>


### [124] [Predictive Latents for Autoregressive Language Models: Possibility, Gauge, and Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28540)
*Yuzhou Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该笔记在统一的潜在过程上区分三种风险（token 风险、teacher-forced 潜在风险、开环风险），并给出潜在宽度-$k$线性最小二乘加重新拟合 unembedding 精确匹配贝叶斯下一 token 预测的条件，同时指出若干尖锐的不可匹配障碍（常数潜在态、零空间游走、小损失不构成凭证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期自回归模型除发 token 外还会迭代不输出 token 的隐藏状态（continuous thoughts、pause steps、recurrent depth），但缺乏对“潜在计算是否真正担保下一 token 预测”的理论刻画，作者希望厘清同一潜在过程上的多重风险与可识别性。

Method: 构建三分风险框架：$R_{\mathrm{tok}}$ 评估 readout；$R_1$ 评估见到真实隐藏态的一步映射（teacher forcing）；$R_H$ 评估同一映射在自身输出上迭代后的开环误差。在贝叶斯下一 token 后验为 $k$ 维统计量（协方差满秩）的假设下，构造宽度-$k$潜在态、线性最小二乘转移与重新拟合的 unembedding，并分析可识别性（仅到可逆 gauge）与匹配障碍。

Result: 当后验依赖满秩 $k$ 维统计量时，宽度-$k$潜在、线性最小二乘转移与重拟合 unembedding 可匹配贝叶斯 token 预测器；统计量仅在可逆 gauge 意义下可识别，whitening 残差流在重拟合 unembedding 后不改变 token 分布。障碍是尖锐的：常数潜在态使 $R_1\to 0$ 但 token 交叉熵退化为边缘熵；完美 token 预测器仍可隐藏零空间游走，使开环误差随时域线性增长。

Conclusion: 小的下一潜在损失不能作为下一 token 预测的凭证，小的下一 token 损失也不能作为自由潜在 rollout 的凭证——除非在陈述数值时同时给出秩或单射性条件。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are trained by next-token cross-entropy, yet several recent systems also iterate a hidden state without emitting a token: continuous thoughts, pause steps, and recurrent depth. This note separates three risks on one latent process. The token risk $R_{\mathrm{tok}}$ scores a readout. The teacher-forced latent risk $R_1$ scores a one-step map that sees the true hidden state. The open-loop risk $R_H$ scores the same map after it has been iterated on its own outputs. If the Bayes next-token posterior is a function of a $k$-dimensional statistic with full-rank covariance, a width-$k$ latent, a linear least-squares transition, and a refit unembedding match the Bayes token predictor. The statistic is identified only up to an invertible gauge: whitening the residual stream does not change the token law once the unembedding is refit. The matching obstructions are sharp. A constant latent drives $R_1$ to zero while token cross-entropy falls back to the marginal entropy. A perfect token predictor can still hide a null-space walk whose open-loop error grows linearly in the horizon. A small next-latent loss does not certificate next-token prediction, and a small next-token loss does not certificate free latent rollout, unless a rank or injectivity condition is stated with the number.

</details>


### [125] [CoGaussian: Open-Vocabulary Gaussian-based Collaborative 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28530)
*Om Shivam Verma,Shaya Farahmand,Lingjun Zhao,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGaussian：基于基础模型对齐稀疏3D高斯的开放词汇协同语义占据预测框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同语义占据方法依赖稠密3D体素标注，且仅能识别预定义语义类别内的目标，难以实现跨视角的开放词汇场景理解。

Method: 将场景表示为与基础模型对齐的稀疏3D高斯集合，作为多智能体间传递与融合几何、语义信息的紧凑表示，实现开放词汇协同语义占据预测。

Result: 在Semantic-OPV2V基准上优于现有开放词汇单智能体方法，验证了开放词汇协同场景理解的有效性。

Conclusion: 以稀疏高斯替代稠密体素表示可同时缓解标注依赖与类别封闭问题，协同感知在开放词汇场景理解中具备显著增益。

Abstract: Collaborative perception enables agents to retrieve the scene semantics and geometry beyond the field of view of any individual agent. This broader scene understanding enhances transportation safety and improves efficiency in multi-robot systems. However, existing collaborative semantic occupancy methods rely on dense 3D voxel annotations and are limited to recognizing objects within a predefined set of semantic classes. To overcome these limitations, we present CoGaussian, an open-vocabulary Gaussian-based collaborative semantic occupancy prediction framework. CoGaussian represents a scene as a set of foundation model-aligned sparse 3D Gaussians, which serve as a compact representation for communicating and fusing geometric and semantic information across multiple agents. On the Semantic-OPV2V benchmark, our method outperforms existing open-vocabulary single-agent approaches, highlighting the effectiveness of open-vocabulary collaborative scene understanding.

</details>


### [126] [Touch-JEPA: Tactile Representation Learning from Hand-Object Video with Limited Tactile Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28519)
*Minseong Kweon,Qiao Feng,Yiming Huang,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Touch-JEPA：利用手-物视频进行自监督预训练以学习触觉表征，有限触觉监督可进一步提供物理接地，冻结特征上的简单读出即优于监督基线并可迁移至机械手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解手-物交互中的接触与触觉强度是实现物理智能系统的关键，但触觉数据采集成本高、覆盖范围有限，制约了触觉表征学习。

Method: 提出 Touch-JEPA 框架。首先设计仅依赖视频的自监督预训练目标：借助视频推断的手部几何，在对应手表面位置依据时空上下文预测被遮掩的视觉特征，促使表征捕获潜在接触动态。随后扩展为联合表征学习：对所有视频施加遮掩预测，仅对标注子集施加触觉监督，使共享表征与实测接触及强度对齐。

Result: 仅自监督预训练下，冻结 Touch-JEPA 特征上的简单监督读出在三个人手基准的接触判别与触觉强度估计上优于监督基线，并在无机器人触觉训练数据情况下迁移至机械手；加入有限触觉监督的联合学习进一步提升人手基准上的接触与强度估计性能。

Conclusion: 手-物视频可为触觉表征学习提供可扩展的自监督信号，而有限的触觉测量则提供互补的物理接地，二者结合有效。

Abstract: Understanding contact and tactile magnitude in hand-object interaction is a key step toward physically intelligent systems, yet learning such representations is limited by the cost and restricted coverage of tactile measurements. We present Touch-JEPA, a framework for learning tactile representations from hand-object videos with limited tactile supervision. We first introduce a self-supervised pretraining objective that learns from video alone without tactile supervision. Using video-derived hand geometry, Touch-JEPA predicts masked visual features at corresponding hand-surface locations from spatial and temporal context, encouraging the representation to capture latent contact dynamics. We then extend this formulation to joint representation learning, where masked prediction is applied to all videos while tactile supervision is available only for a labeled subset, grounding the shared representation in measured contact and magnitude. With self-supervised pretraining alone, simple supervised readouts on frozen Touch-JEPA features outperform supervised baselines in contact discrimination and tactile magnitude estimation across three human-hand benchmarks, and transfer to robotic hands without robot tactile training data. Joint learning with limited tactile supervision further improves contact and magnitude estimation on the human-hand benchmarks. Together, these results show that hand-object video can provide scalable self-supervision for tactile representation learning, while limited tactile measurements provide complementary physical grounding.

</details>


### [127] [SenseAlign: Understanding Vision and Touch through Language at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28511)
*Chuyang Ye,Qinting Jiang,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SenseAlign，一种在测试时利用共享文本语义参考做模态加权（Alignment Trust）与特征校正（Anchor Realignment）的无监督自适应方法，缓解触觉-视觉-语言模型在部署中因光照、接触质量或硬件变化导致的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉-视觉-语言模型通过共享语言空间融合视觉与触觉，但部署时光照、接触质量或传感器硬件变化会引起模态漂移；当两模态共同参与预测时，不可靠模态会压过另一模态的正确证据。部署阶段通常无标注数据，因此需要测试时自适应来恢复性能。

Method: 1) 发现：将累积观测与候选类文本嵌入比较，可揭示置信度无法捕捉的模态可靠性差异；对竞争类做特征精炼优于单纯几何对齐。2) Alignment Trust：以对共享预测类别的支持度为依据为各模态加权。3) Anchor Realignment：用同一类文本嵌入拟合共享特征校正，将预测类与替代类分离。4) 两模块共用无标注历史，所有编码器保持冻结。

Result: 在多样数据集上，于自然分布偏移与受控数据损坏两种设定下均取得较强识别性能。

Conclusion: 以文本嵌入作为共同语义锚点，可在无标签、冻结编码器的条件下同时实现可靠的模态加权与判别性特征校正，有效提升触觉-视觉-语言模型在测试时的鲁棒识别能力。

Abstract: Tactile-visual-language models combine vision and touch through a shared language space to recognize objects and materials. In real-world deployment, changes in lighting, contact quality or sensor hardware can shift visual or tactile inputs and degrade recognition performance. When both modalities contribute to a prediction, an unreliable modality can override correct evidence from the other. Since labeled data are generally unavailable during deployment, test-time adaptation offers a way to recover recognition performance from unlabeled observations. In this setting, we find that comparing accumulated observations with the text embeddings of candidate classes reveals modality reliability differences that confidence can miss. Refining features against competing classes also improves recognition beyond geometric alignment alone. These findings motivate SenseAlign, which uses a common semantic reference for test-time modality weighting and feature correction. Alignment Trust weights each modality according to its support for shared predicted classes. Anchor Realignment uses the same class text embeddings to fit a shared feature correction that separates predicted classes from alternatives. Both modules use the same unlabeled history while keeping all encoders frozen. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate strong recognition performance under both natural shifts and controlled corruptions.

</details>


### [128] [CountVideoBench: A Benchmark to Evaluate Prediction Biases on the Object Counting task by Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28356)
*Marc Chapus,Carlos Crispim-Junior,Véronique Eglin,Atilla Baskurt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CountVideoBench 视频计数基准（2600 条合成视频，覆盖 5–50 共 26 个基数），揭示现有 VideoQA 基准真值分布严重偏小导致计数评估失真；16 个视频语言模型零样本 Exact Match 均不超过 27.6%，且呈现模型特有的"偏好整数"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中的物体计数是多模态大模型能力中被刻画不足的一项。现有 VideoQA 基准虽含计数题，但真值分布高度偏向小数值（所调查九个基准的中位数不超过 5，最高三个取值合计占 45%–93% 视频），使平均准确率由高频计数主导，稀有计数上的偏差几乎不可见，因而无法诊断模型的计数能力。

Method: 构建 CountVideoBench：2600 条合成视频，覆盖区间 [5, 50] 内 26 个基数（每个 100 条），按连续计数分为六个簇以支持近邻比较。每条视频仅含单一类别静态物体并施加受控相机运动，排除物体运动与识别作为混淆因素。对来自九个架构家族的 16 个视频-语言模型进行零样本评估。

Result: 无模型 Exact Match 超过 27.6%。准确率随基数的变化模式因模型而异，从单调下降至非单调的"峰值-崩塌"均有出现，说明"难度随计数增大"不能作为计数失败的主因。此外，每个模型都将预测集中于自身偏好的整数集合，准确率峰值恰与这些值重合，并在相邻计数处骤降。

Conclusion: 现有基准的偏斜分布掩盖了视频计数能力的真实缺陷；模型存在值依赖的系统性偏差而非单纯难度递增。需采用分布均衡、具备诊断性的计数基准来可靠评估这一基础视觉能力。

Abstract: Object counting in videos remains a poorly characterized capability of multimodal large language models. Counting questions are included in many existing VideoQA benchmarks, but the ground-truth count distributions in these benchmarks are heavily skewed toward small values. Across the nine benchmarks we survey, the median ground-truth count is at most five, and the three most frequent values together account for between 45% and 93% of the videos. The resulting mean accuracy is then dominated by the most frequent counts; a value-dependent bias at a rare count contributes negligibly to the average and remains invisible. We introduce CountVideoBench, a benchmark of 2,600 synthetic videos covering 26 cardinalities in the interval [5, 50] (100 videos each), grouped into six clusters of consecutive counts that span the range while enabling near-neighbor comparisons. Each video contains a single class of static objects under a controlled camera motion, removing object motion and reducing recognition as confounds. We evaluate sixteen video-language models from nine architecture families in a zero-shot setting. No model exceeds 27.6% Exact Match. The dependence of accuracy on cardinality varies across models, ranging from monotonic decline to a non-monotonic peak-and-collapse pattern, which rules out increasing difficulty with cardinality as the dominant explanation of counting failure. Every model further concentrates its predictions on its own set of preferred integers, with accuracy peaks coinciding with these values and collapsing at adjacent counts. These findings highlight the need for balanced, diagnostic counting benchmarks to reliably assess this fundamental visual capability.

</details>


### [129] [PACE: Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit for Training-Free Response Timing in Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28323)
*Siqi Guo,Yuxia Chen,Wenbo Zhu,Yangguang Ji,Zhenxiang Jiang,Teng Zhong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE：无需训练的流式视频理解框架，通过"规划—关注—检查—输出"显式建模三类chunk级流式错误，在ProactiveVideoQA与OVO-Bench Forward上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解不仅要决定"答什么"，还要决定"何时答"。证据逐步到达，而近期基准显示：在查询事件尚未结束时就提前作答（premature response）是常见失败模式。现有方法多需额外训练学习响应时机；免训练方案则依赖标量门控信号，难以显式表征"答案何时定型"。

Method: 作者将chunk级流式错误归为三类：采样波动导致答案不稳定；答案稳定但缺乏已观测视频证据支撑；答案虽被当前证据支持但事件尚未完成。据此提出PACE（Plan, Attend, Check, Emit）免训练框架：给定问题先起草计划，明确"看什么"与"何以为完成信号"；随流推进在信息量大的时刻调用现成视频LLM；对每次草稿从稳定性、所引帧的grounding、完成度三方面检验；将验证通过的观测记入event ledger（事件台账）。

Result: 在ProactiveVideoQA与OVO-Bench Forward上，于闭源与开源骨干模型上，相较官方协议最高提升5.9个平均PAUC点与6.2个平均准确率点，达到state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 显式建模"过早作答/证据不足/事件未完成"三类失败模式，并以规划+检查+事件台账实现免训练的动态响应时机控制，可在多种骨干上稳定提升流式视频理解性能。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires a model to decide not only what to answer but also when to answer. Evidence arrives progressively, and recent benchmarks identify premature responses, issued before the queried event has concluded, as a recurring failure mode. Existing approaches often learn response timing through additional training, while training-free alternatives typically rely on scalar gating signals that do not explicitly represent when an answer becomes final. We organize chunk-wise streaming errors into three patterns: sampling variation can make an answer unstable, a stable answer may lack support in the observed video, or an answer may be supported by the current evidence while the event remains unfinished. We propose PACE (Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit), a training-free framework designed around these three failure modes. Given a question, PACE drafts a plan specifying what to observe and what will signal completion. As the stream unfolds, it invokes an off-the-shelf video LLM at informative moments, evaluates each draft for stability, grounding in its cited frames, and completion, and records verified observations in an event ledger. Experiments on ProactiveVideoQA and OVO-Bench Forward show that PACE improves over the official protocols by up to 5.9 average PAUC points and 6.2 average accuracy points across proprietary and open-source backbones, yielding state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [130] [AGGR-Mamba: Mamba-based Network with Attention-Guided Gated Residual Refinement for Undersampled MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28291)
*Yue Zhuo,Wenbin Deng,Lingjun Liu,Xuhang Chen,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AGGR-Mamba，一种建立在Mamba主干之上的轻量级输出端残差精炼方法，用于欠采样脑部MRI重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠采样MRI的k空间缺失会导致残余混叠伪影、边缘模糊与局部结构细节丢失，需要一个能在不破坏主干重建的前提下修正局部误差的后处理式精炼机制。

Method: Mamba主干先输出初始重建，随后由输出端精炼模块以受控残差方式修正局部误差与高频伪影；其中包括Attention Refine Head（通过通道与空间注意力重校准局部解剖结构、边缘纹理及残差误差区域）与GDFN风格的门控残差精炼分支（选择性保留有信息量的残差特征、抑制冗余或不稳定响应）。

Result: 在IXI脑部MRI数据集（T1、T2加权图像，加速因子R=4与R=8）上，AGGR-Mamba在不同重建设置下取得有竞争力的PSNR与SSIM。

Conclusion: 注意力重校准与门控残差选择相结合的输出端精炼策略能有效改善局部结构恢复，验证了该轻量级精炼范式的有效性。

Abstract: Undersampled magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction aims to recover high-quality images from partially acquired k-space data, thereby reducing scan time. However, missing frequency-domain measurements often lead to residual aliasing artifacts, blurred edges, and loss of local structural details. In this paper, we propose AGGR-Mamba, a lightweight output-stage residual refinement method built upon a Mamba backbone for undersampled brain MRI reconstruction. The Mamba backbone first produces an initial reconstruction, and the proposed refinement modules then correct the remaining local errors and high-frequency artifacts in a controlled residual manner. Specifically, an Attention Refine Head is introduced to recalibrate local anatomical structures, edge textures, and residual-error regions through channel and spatial attention. In addition, a GDFN-style gated residual refinement branch is designed to selectively preserve informative residual features while suppressing redundant or unstable responses. By combining attention-based recalibration with gated residual selection, AGGR-Mamba improves local structure recovery without substantially disturbing the backbone reconstruction. Experiments are conducted on the IXI brain MRI dataset with T1- and T2-weighted images under acceleration factors of R=4 and R=8. The results show that AGGR-Mamba achieves competitive PSNR and SSIM performance across different reconstruction settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed output-stage refinement strategy.

</details>


### [131] [HRoPE: Homography Relative Positional Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28284)
*Huachen Gao,Yang Yang,Xiaoqian Liang,Xingyu Chen,Youjia Zhang,Qi Zhang,Baorui Ma,Anpei Chen,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HRoPE：以斜面三维平面表示每个patch的局部几何，将平面诱导的token级单应变换作为相对变换注入注意力，实现表面感知的几何位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何相对位置编码以传感器为中心（相机级视锥或光线步进深度），仅描述token"从何处被观测"，未刻画token所对应场景区域的表面几何。

Method: 借鉴经典多视图立体的倾斜支撑窗假设，将每个patch的局部几何表示为倾斜3D平面；把token级、平面诱导的单应变换作为相对变换注入注意力，为transformer提供矫正透视畸变、跨视角对齐patch特征的几何先验。平面参数无需真值，可在纯视图合成目标下端到端预测，或由基础模型导出。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、CO3Dv2和DL3DV上，最佳HRoPE变体在新视图合成任务中持续优于所评估的几何位置编码；大量消融与分析验证了所提编码的有效性。

Conclusion: 从传感器中心转向表面感知的位置编码是可行且有效的，平面诱导单应作为相对变换可作为强几何先验，且无需几何真值监督。

Abstract: Geometric Relative Positional Encoding has become the primary mechanism for injecting spatial inductive biases into multi-view transformers. Existing designs condition attention on camera-level frustums or ray-marched depth, associating cross-view tokens through the sensor geometry. Such sensor-centric formulations, however, describe only where a token is observed from, leaving the rich surface geometry of the scene region depicted by the token. In this paper, we advocate a shift toward surface-aware positional encoding that explicitly grounds patch tokens in the geometry of physical surfaces. We introduce HRoPE, which represents the local geometry of each patch as a slanted 3D plane, following the slanted support-window assumption of classical multi-view stereo. We inject token-level, plane-induced homographies into attention as relative transformations. This equips the transformer with a geometric prior for rectifying perspective distortions and aligning patch features across viewpoints. Our encoding requires no ground-truth geometry: the plane parameters can be predicted end-to-end under a view-synthesis objective alone, or derived from foundation models. Extensive experiments on RealEstate10K, CO3Dv2, and DL3DV show that our best variant of HRoPE consistently outperforms the evaluated geometric positional encodings for novel view synthesis. Extensive ablations and analysis show the effectiveness of the proposed encoding. Please refer to our project page at: https://gaohchen.github.io/HRoPE

</details>


### [132] [VGL-stGen: Learning a shared multimodal model for lung histology and spatial transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28266)
*Haenara Shin,Dongjoo Lee,Sungwoo Bae,Kwon Joong Na,Daeseung Lee,Hongyoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGL-stGen：基于配对肺H&E与空间转录组（含单细胞参考）适配的多模态医学视觉-语言模型，单一checkpoint支持13项组织/细胞类型分类、基因预测与条件表达补全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组织形态学与分子状态，利用空间转录组与H&E配对数据把转录组层面的细胞类型监督迁移到组织学图像，构建可共享生成建模的统一模型。

Method: 以预训练医学视觉-语言模型为骨干，用配对肺H&E-空间转录组及单细胞参考进行适配训练；整合图像-表达与表达-细胞类型配对，并通过基因条件伪标签将转录组细胞类型监督迁移至H&E；任务特定解码输出类别标签、基因列表与结构化表达水平；条件补全中固定目标基因身份并自回归预测其表达水平；受控适配中对表达水平token施加限制性监督。

Result: 13项任务表现良好；自回归结构化预测提升条件补全效果，限制监督至请求的表达水平token可进一步改善图像条件补全；图像置换实验显示正确配对的H&E在观测表达之外提供额外信息。

Conclusion: 医学视觉-语言骨干可通过空间转录组与组织学共享生成建模，将组织形态与分子状态关联；外部分类部分依赖共享注释谱系的计算参考，部分H&E来源在癌症训练或模型开发中已暴露，结论需在此条件下解读。

Abstract: VGL-stGen is a multimodal large language model adapted from a pretrained medical vision-language model using paired lung H&E and spatial transcriptomics with single-cell references. Training integrates image-expression and expression-cell-type pairs, and gene-conditioned pseudo-labels transfer transcriptomic cell-type supervision to histology images. One deployed checkpoint supports thirteen evaluated tasks spanning tissue and cell-type classification, gene prediction and conditional expression completion from combinations of histology, cell identity and observed gene expression. Task-specific decoding produces categorical labels, gene lists and structured expression levels. For conditional completion, fixing queried gene identities and predicting their levels autoregressively improves structured prediction. Controlled adaptation shows that restricting supervision to requested expression-level tokens can further improve image-conditioned completion. Image-permutation experiments indicate that correctly paired histology adds information beyond observed expression. External classification relies partly on computational references with shared annotation lineage, and some H&E sources were exposed during cancer training or model development. Within these conditions, VGL-stGen demonstrates how a medical vision-language backbone can learn from spatial transcriptomics and histology to connect tissue morphology and molecular states through shared generative modeling.

</details>


### [133] [Attractive Faces Skew Text-to-Image Reward Models and Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28195)
*Harvey Mannering,Zhiwu Huang,Adam Prügel-Bennett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示人类偏好评判中"吸引力偏见"会压倒图像质量，并从排行榜、偏好模型、生成结果三方面展示其下游危害，最后探讨缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好已成为文本到图像模型评估、引导、微调与排序的核心，但人们对图像生成中偏好易受偏见影响这一问题研究不足，尤其在人脸场景下偏好判断是否被"感知吸引力"支配尚不明确。

Method: 通过受控用户研究（含真实照片与AI生成光照/保真度控制对比）验证吸引力对质量判断的支配作用；用用户研究数据模拟Elo排名；在五个人类偏好模型（PickScore、MPS、HPSv2.1、HPSv3等）上单独编辑40个标注人脸属性做相关与回归分析；并考察偏好对齐生成对年龄、性别特征及显式提示的偏移效应，最后测试数据采集与后训练两方面的缓解策略。

Result: 标注者会把更有吸引力的人脸评为更高质量，即使将不吸引人的真实人脸与吸引人的AI生成人脸对比亦然；Elo模拟显示吸引力可翻转排行榜，使合成的吸引力人脸超越真实但缺乏吸引力的人脸；在五个偏好模型中的四个里，吸引力是分数的最强相关因素且在回归中是最强预测因子；偏好对齐生成会使人脸偏向更年轻、更女性化，并可覆盖针对"不吸引人"的显式提示，削弱用户控制。

Conclusion: 人类偏好模型内嵌了认知偏见，无法可靠区分图像质量与吸引力，损害评估与可控性；需要能解耦图像质量与人类认知偏见的偏好模型，提升评估可靠性，并可通过数据采集规范与偏好模型后训练进行部分缓解。

Abstract: Human preference is now central to the evaluation, guidance, finetuning, and ranking of text-to-image models. However, the susceptibility of human preferences to bias in image generation remains underexplored. We identify a key observation: for human faces, perceived attractiveness overrides image quality in preference judgments, even when comparing real photographs of identical fidelity. In a controlled user study, annotators rank more attractive faces as higher quality, even when unattractive real faces are compared with attractive AI-generated ones. We examine the downstream consequences of this bias. Firstly, simulating Elo rankings from user study data shows attractiveness can invert a leaderboard's ranking, elevating synthetic attractive faces over real, unattractive ones. Secondly, the same bias reappears in the human preference models. Editing 40 annotated facial attributes in isolation, we find that attractiveness is the strongest correlate of score for four of five human preference models (PickScore, MPS, HPSv2.1, HPSv3), and the strongest predictor of score in regression analysis. We further show that preference aligned generation shifts faces toward younger, more feminine faces, and can override explicit prompts for unattractive faces, thereby limiting user control. Finally, we explore mitigation strategies, both for data collection and post training on human preference models. These findings highlight the need for preference models that disentangle image quality from human cognitive bias, enabling more reliable evaluation.

</details>


### [134] [RACE: Learning Multimodal Search through Referential Abstraction and Compositional Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28382)
*Eric Tao,Minhao Sun,Liyan Liu,Zhijing Wu,Xiaohan Yu,Lang Mei,Li Huang,Chao Feng,Jinchao Ma,Sichen Kang,Siyu Mo,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RACE，一个基于图像条件的多模态搜索智能体任务合成框架，通过构造"图像→目标"和"目标证据→答案"的依赖关系，消除文本身份线索和未使用目标证据导致的捷径；仅用12K教师轨迹微调Qwen3.5-27B，即可将十个多模态基准的平均准确率从53.5提升到56.0（相对提升约4.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态搜索智能体需要"必须依赖视觉定位才能确定答案"的任务，但现有合成问题中存在文本身份线索（identity-revealing text）和未被使用的目标证据，使得模型可通过文本捷径作答，从而弱化了对视觉搜索与证据整合能力的监督。

Method: RACE框架包含两种操作：（1）指称抽象（Referential Abstraction, RA）：用具体视觉指称或需在图中候选间检索才能消解的关系型歧义指称，替换暴露身份的文本；（2）组合证据（Compositional Evidence, CE）：将同一图像中多实体或跨图像实体的检索事实链接成联合问题，答案必须依赖每一份指定证据。两种操作同样适用于搜索过程中新发现的图像，使新的视觉观察决定后续搜索目标或提供答案证据。

Result: 仅使用RACE生成的12K条教师轨迹对Qwen3.5-27B进行微调，在十个多模态基准上的平均准确率由53.5提升至56.0，相对匹配的基线智能体相对提升约4.7%。

Conclusion: 通过消除文本身份捷径并强制进行视觉定位与多证据联合整合，图像条件化的任务合成能有效增强多模态搜索智能体的训练信号；代码、数据与模型将公开。

Abstract: Training multimodal search agents requires tasks that make visually grounded search necessary for determining the answer. Textual identity clues and unused target evidence allow shortcuts in synthetic questions, weakening supervision for visual search and evidence integration. We propose RACE, an image-conditioned task synthesis framework that constructs dependencies from images to targets and from target-specific evidence to answers. Referential Abstraction (RA) replaces identity-revealing text with specific visual references or ambiguous relational references resolved by searching among depicted candidates. Compositional Evidence (CE) links retrieved facts about multiple entities in one image or entities from different images into joint questions whose answers require every designated contribution. Both operations also apply to images found during search, allowing new visual observations to determine subsequent search targets or supply answer evidence. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-27B on only 12K teacher-generated trajectories from RACE raises average accuracy across ten multimodal benchmarks from 53.5 to 56.0, a relative improvement of approximately 4.7% over the matched base agent. Our code, data, and model will be publicly available.

</details>


### [135] [Auto-Director: Leveraging Multi-Agent Collaboration for Autonomous Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28346)
*Yuxia Chen,Wenbo Zhu,Yangguang Ji,Zhenxiang Jiang,Teng Zhong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出工具增强的多智能体框架 Auto-Director，模拟专业后期制作流程实现开放资源自主视频编辑，并构建 VEdit-Bench 基准与五维评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界资源下的自主视频编辑仍具挑战：检索式方法受限于固定素材、缺乏创作灵活性；生成模型难以维持长时程连贯性且常偏离用户意图；二者均无法复现专业后期制作所依赖的结构化多阶段推理。

Method: 四智能体协同：Planning Agent 通过检索增强规划将抽象主题分解为有事实依据的脚本；Material Agent 经镜头分割与视觉标注索引开放世界素材；Editor Agent 借助 MLLM 驱动的空间适配组装语义对齐片段；Critic Agent 主导迭代式“反思—修订”循环，检查场景级对齐与节奏。

Result: 构建 VEdit-Bench：100 个专家精选主题、人工标注 Golden Script 及覆盖叙事逻辑、事实准确性、语义对齐、构图质量与风格一致性的五维指标。实验显示 Auto-Director 在自动指标与人工评估上均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 多智能体+工具增强的范式有效弥合业余与专业级视频制作间的差距，为该新范式提供了可严谨评估的基准。

Abstract: Autonomous video editing from open-world resources remains a challenging problem in multimedia content creation. Retrieval-based methods are constrained by fixed source material and lack creative flexibility, while generative models struggle with long-horizon coherence and often diverge from user intent. Both paradigms fail to replicate the structured, multi-stage reasoning that underlies professional post-production. To address these limitations, we present Auto-Director, a tool-augmented multi-agent framework that mirrors professional editorial workflows: a Planning Agent decomposes abstract topics into fact-grounded scripts via retrieval-augmented planning; a Material Agent indexes open-world footage through shot segmentation and visual tagging; an Editor Agent assembles semantically aligned clips with MLLM-driven spatial adaptation; and a Critic Agent governs an iterative Reflect-and-Revise loop that checks scene-level alignment and pacing. To rigorously evaluate this new paradigm, we introduce VEdit-Bench, a benchmark of 100 expert-curated topics with human-annotated Golden Scripts and a five-dimensional metric suite covering Narrative Logic, Factual Accuracy, Semantic Alignment, Compositional Quality, and Stylistic Cohesion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Auto-Director significantly outperforms baseline methods in both automated metrics and human evaluation, effectively bridging the gap between amateurs and professional-grade video production.

</details>


### [136] [ABACUS: A Benchmark for Image Editing Models to Assess Object Category and Counting Under Scene Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28316)
*Aymen Lassoued,Angelique Loesch,Romaric Audigier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ABACUS 基准，用于评测基于指令的图像编辑中的数值（对象计数）编辑能力。它采用无 VLM 裁判的协议，在实例级别对照全景真值检验编辑是否达到精确目标计数并保持场景其余部分不变，揭示出现有编辑器在复杂场景与高计数下的显著数值编辑失败，而这些失败此前被整体相似度指标或计数能力不佳的 VLM 评判所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑模型在增删指定数量对象或改变类别时经常产出错误数量，且错误随场景复杂度与请求计数增加而急剧放大。现有基准以整体相似度指标或自身计数能力薄弱的 VLM 评判打分，导致数值错误被忽略或错判，编辑模型的真实数值能力从未被准确测量。

Method: 提出 ABACUS：新基准、评测协议与专用评分指标。协议为 judge-free：不让 VLM/MLLM 打分或计数（VLM 仅用于确认罕见歧义裁剪块的类别）；改为在实例级别对照经检测器共识确认的全景（panoptic）真值逐实例校验，覆盖 add、remove、change 三类操作，同时核查是否精确达到所请求计数以及场景其余部分是否保持。

Result: 在主流图像编辑器上的评测揭示出大量数值编辑失败，且失败程度随场景复杂度与对象计数上升而加剧；这些失败在以往基准中常被忽视或误判。

Conclusion: 数值编辑能力需以实例级、无裁判的精确计数校验来评测；ABACUS 提供了可靠的度量手段，并表明当前编辑模型在计数敏感编辑上仍存在系统且随复杂度放大的缺陷。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models can now add, remove a specified number of objects or change their category while preserving the scene. Yet these models often fail to produce the correct number, and these failures grow sharply as scenes become more complex and the requested count increases. Current benchmarks largely miss this problem: they score edits with holistic similarity metrics or with vision-language model (VLM) judges that are themselves poor at counting, so numerical errors go unnoticed or are graded incorrectly. As a result, the true numerical ability of today's editing models has never been accurately measured. We address this issue with ABACUS, a new benchmark, evaluation protocol and dedicated scoring metric for numerical image editing. Our protocol is judge-free: no VLM or MLLM scores an edit or counts objects (a VLM only confirms the category of rare ambiguous crops). Instead, it verifies each edit at the instance level against panoptic ground truth confirmed by detector consensus, checking both that the exact requested count was achieved and that the rest of the scene was preserved, across the add, remove and change operations. By evaluating leading image editors, our method reveals substantial numerical editing failures that worsen with increasing scene complexity and object count; failures that prior benchmarks often overlooked or misjudged.

</details>


### [137] [Reliability-Aware Reconstruction of V-Shaped Surfaces with a Low-Cost Multi-Zone SPAD Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28258)
*Yohanssen Pratama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向V形表面的可靠性感知深度重建框架，利用两种TMF8821采集配置与基于置信度、峰值幅度、峰值锐度的逐点可靠性学习，结合图优化降低角度与RMSE误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本多区SPAD传感器（如TMF8821）虽提供紧凑的飞行时间测量，但采样稀疏且在几何不连续处深度估计不可靠，限制了其对V形等折面的重建精度。

Method: 采用两种互补的TMF8821采集配置；从传感器置信度、瞬态峰值幅度与峰值锐度学习逐点可靠性，并将其嵌入图优化框架进行深度重建；以鲁棒的two-plane fitting与垂直截面评估重建几何。

Result: 在60°、90°、120°目标上，平均V角误差由11.21°降至9.66°（13.8%）；代表性90°目标的组合point-to-plane RMSE由14.56 mm降至13.65 mm。

Conclusion: 多配置采集与可靠性加权的图优化可有效缓解稀疏采样与不连续处误差，实现低成本SPAD对折面的更稳健三维重建。

Abstract: Low-cost multi-zone single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) sensors provide compact time-of-flight measurements but are limited by sparse sampling and unreliable depth estimates near geometric discontinuities. This letter presents a reliability-aware reconstruction framework for V-shaped surfaces using two complementary TMF8821 acquisition configurations. Point-wise reliability is learned from sensor confidence, transient peak amplitude, and peak sharpness, and is incorporated into a graph-based depth optimization. The reconstructed geometry is evaluated by robust two-plane fitting and a perpendicular cross-section. Experiments on 60-degree, 90-degree, and 120-degree targets reduce the mean V-angle error from 11.21 degrees to 9.66 degrees (13.8%). For the representative 90-degree target, the combined point-to-plane RMSE decreases from 14.56 mm to 13.65 mm.

</details>


### [138] [Joints, Camera, Action! Co-Generating 3D Motion and Video for Arbitrary Rigs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28632)
*Noam Rotstein,Yoad Tewel,Chen Tessler,Haggai Maron,Gal Chechik,Or Litany,Yoni Kasten,Yuval Atzmon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JCA模型，将3D运动保持为独立模型并支持任意骨架，通过少量交叉注意力层与预训练视频模型耦合，实现给定rig的联合视频-3D运动生成及相机配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型能生成逼真运动但仅停留在像素层面，无法把运动交还给特定3D资产并在3D场景中重放（如猴子跨栏视频无法转为可重播的3D运动）。现有视频-运动模型要么仅处理人体骨架且需增加数十亿参数，要么从生成的2D姿态图三角化恢复3D，泛化与质量受限。

Method: 1) 解耦架构：3D运动由独立模型处理，支持任意骨架，经少量cross-attention层接入预训练视频模型；2) 分离使运动模型可先仅用运动数据学习骨架运动（比配对视频易获取、易增广）；3) 模型学习在视频帧中定位各关节，从而恢复相机并把3D运动置于场景中；4) 用生成式视频编辑器把动画3D资产放入与其动作匹配的场景，筛选使已知运动仍有效的编辑，构建近200K训练对（含人与机器人视频）。

Result: 相比video-first流水线，模型能从文本更忠实地驱动多样rig，且生成运动能响应周围场景；支持视频→rig运动、rig运动→视频的互转，并实现3D运动的关键帧控制。

Conclusion: 将3D运动与视频生成解耦并用轻量交叉注意力耦合，是一种可扩展、可支持任意骨架且可场景配准的联合生成范式，为视频运动的可重放3D化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Video generation models can now depict remarkably realistic motion. Yet it is unclear how to use this motion beyond pixels: to animate a specific 3D asset and replay the animation in its 3D scene. For instance, a video model can show a monkey jumping over a crossbar, but it cannot return that jump as replayable 3D motion placed in the 3D scene.
