Here we present a model that directly co-generates the video and 3D motion for any supplied rig, along with a camera that registers this motion to the scene. Prior video-motion models either handle only human skeletons, often adding billions of parameters, or triangulate 3D motion from generated 2D pose maps. We instead keep 3D motion in a separate model that supports arbitrary skeletons, and connect it to a pretrained video model through a small set of cross-attention layers. This separation lets the motion model first learn how skeletons move from motion-only data, which is easier to obtain and augment than paired video. Additionally, our model learns to locate each joint in the video frames, which lets us recover the camera and place the 3D motion in the scene. To train across many skeletons, we use a generative video editor to place animated 3D assets in scenes suited to their actions, curating the edits so their known motion remains valid. Together with human and robot video, this yields nearly 200K training pairs. Our evaluations demonstrate that our model animates diverse rigs from text more faithfully than video-first pipelines, and its motion responds to the surrounding scene. It also turns video into rig motion, rig motion into video, and enables keyframe-based control of the 3D motion. Videos and additional results are available on our project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/par/projects/jca/.

</details>


### [139] [SolarRenderer: Few-Step Generative Reference Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28630)
*Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Dongyue Lu,Junchao Huang,Lutao Jiang,Xin Wang,Yiren Song,Li Jiang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarRenderer：基于冻结的MiniMax-H3 33B骨干与两个轻量LoRA的结构条件视频渲染器，原生参考通路接收任意结构表征，四步分布匹配渲染比50步采样快12.3倍，块因果变体实现768p实时流式渲染，在分布内测试集与16种结构表征的泛化基准上均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏引擎与编码智能体可指定场景布局、运动与相机，但将其粗结构转为真实感视频需要既能遵循结构、又能补全外观的渲染器。现有渲染器受限于单一引擎的表示、需数十步去噪或昂贵自蒸馏、且训练条件与目标视频不对齐。

Method: 冻结33B MiniMax-H3骨干，仅适配两个轻量LoRA；结构视频以其所描述帧的位置直接进入骨干的原生参考通路，不加类型标识符、仅以纯文本描述，从而单一控制LoRA可跟随任意结构表示；从预训练四步LoRA出发，采用结构引导的分布匹配实现四步渲染；另设块因果变体扩展到流式；并构建来自游戏引擎、模拟器与YouTube的136K帧对齐片段语料训练。

Result: 四步渲染较50步采样提速12.3倍且保持所学控制；块因果变体可实时渲染768p视频；在分布内测试集与含16种结构表示��泛化基准上均取得SOTA，并泛化到11种未见表示（含姿态骨架、手绘草图、点云）。

Conclusion: 通过原生参考通路与轻量LoRA解耦结构与表示，SolarRenderer实现了高效、表示无关且可流式部署的结构条件视频渲染，并具备强泛化能力。

Abstract: Game engines and coding agents can specify the layout, motion, and camera of a scene, yet turning such coarse structure into realistic video requires a renderer that follows the structure and completes the appearance it leaves open. Existing renderers are tied to the representation of one engine, need tens of denoising steps or a costly distillation of their own, and learn from conditions that are not aligned with their target videos. We present SolarRenderer, a structure-conditioned video renderer that adapts a frozen MiniMax-H3 33B backbone with two lightweight LoRAs. Structural videos enter the native reference pathway of the backbone at the positions of the frames they describe, without a type identifier and described only in plain text, so that a single control LoRA follows arbitrary structural representations. Starting from a pretrained four-step LoRA, structure-guided distribution matching renders in four steps, 12.3 times faster than 50-step sampling, while preserving the learned control. A block-causal variant extends the same design to streaming and renders 768p video in real time. To train the model, we build a frame-aligned corpus of 136K clips from game engines, simulators, and YouTube videos. Experiments on an in-distribution test set and a generalization benchmark of 16 structural representations show that SolarRenderer achieves state-of-the-art results across both, and generalizes to 11 unseen representations, including pose skeletons, drawings, and point clouds.

</details>


### [140] [MindView: Benchmarking Generative Multi-View Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28622)
*Yanbo Wang,Siqi Luo,Siyu Zhang,Chenming Zhu,Wenzhao Zheng,Qian Yu,Juncheng Yan,Bin Fu,Tai Wang,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MindView 是一个用于生成式多视角空间智能的基准，含 513 个场景、1,500 个样本与七类任务；实验显示 11 个生成器均有较大提升空间，微调 BAGEL 使总分提升 24.3 分，并提出免训练的 MindCue 证据选择器以逆转扩展上下文下的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能评测多集中于空间落地的图像编辑等生成任务，而从部分多视角观测出发、由语言引导生成空间一致视角的问题尚未被充分探索，缺乏对应的评测平台与诊断性分析。

Method: 构建 MindView 基准：1,500 个样本、513 个场景、覆盖三个室内数据集，七项任务分为几何主导型（指定相机位置与朝向变化）与语义主导型（需通过指称对象落地、空间关系与参考系线索推断目标视角）；评测十一个生成器并做配对上下文分析；在 MindView 训练集上微调 BAGEL；提出免训练、以目标视角假设为引导的空间证据选择器 MindCue。

Result: 十一个生成器在 MindView 上表现均有明显提升空间，且在扩展上下文设置下总分一致下降；微调后的 BAGEL 总分提升 24.3 分，七项任务全部增益，并在三个空间理解基准上（未使用其标注训练）取得更高分数；MindCue 逆转了扩展上下文下的分数下降并优于标准上下文基线。

Conclusion: MindView 为生成式多视角空间智能提供了具体测试平台；任务特定监督可显著改善目标视角生成，且学习生成空间落地视角可能迁移受益于空间理解；合理选择多视角证据能有效利用扩展上下文。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence has recently been assessed through generative tasks such as spatially grounded image editing, yet language-guided generation of spatially consistent views from partial multi-view observations remains underexplored. We introduce MindView, a benchmark for generative multi-view spatial intelligence with 1,500 samples from 513 scenes across three indoor datasets. Its seven tasks form two families: geometry-dominant tasks specify changes in camera position and orientation, while semantics-dominant tasks require inferring target viewpoints by grounding referred objects and interpreting spatial relations and reference-frame cues. Evaluation of eleven generators reveals substantial room for improvement on MindView. A paired context analysis further shows that all eleven generators obtain lower overall scores under the expanded context setting. These findings motivate us to investigate whether task-specific supervision can improve target-view generation and whether models can use additional multi-view evidence effectively. We therefore fine-tune BAGEL on the MindView training dataset, improving its overall score by 24.3 points and yielding gains across all seven MindView tasks. Beyond these generation gains, the fine-tuned model also yields higher scores on three spatial-understanding benchmarks without training on their annotations, suggesting that learning to generate spatially grounded views may benefit spatial understanding. To improve evidence utilization under expanded context, we propose MindCue, a training-free spatial evidence selector guided by a target-view hypothesis. MindCue reverses the drop in overall score under expanded context and outperforms the standard-context baseline. Overall, MindView provides a concrete testbed for generative multi-view spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [141] [Can a Display Be Used for Rapid LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28571)
*Zuhao Zou,Xiangcheng Hu,Jianhao Jiao,Jin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScreenCalib 利用普通可编程显示屏实现 LiDAR-相机外参标定：相机观测屏幕显示的 AprilTag，LiDAR 测量屏幕物理表面与边界，通过标签引导的屏幕提取、几何假设生成与精化、以及环境边缘消歧完成跨模态关联，支持单姿态与多姿态联合标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标定依赖专用标定板，需额外制作与携带；而显示屏（手机、平板、笔记本、显示器、电视）随处可得，可同时提供已知视觉标记与受控几何结构，从而降低标定物准备成本。

Method: 核心挑战在于两种模态是间接关联（相机看标签、LiDAR 看屏幕面与边界），且显示几何带来关联歧义、室内杂波干扰提取。方法包括：(1) 标签引导的显示屏提取；(2) 几何假设生成与精化；(3) 利用周围场景边缘消除歧义。单姿态模式仅需一次静态摆放；联合多姿态模式通过跨姿态一致性融合多次独立静态观测，联合优化共享外参与标签尺度，无需连续运动或姿态间轨迹估计。

Result: 在仿真与三类传感器平台（涵盖机械式与非重复扫描 LiDAR，干净与杂乱环境）上验证：平移精度优于基于标定板的对比方法；联合多姿态精化在三个平台上进一步降低平均旋转与平移误差，并改善渲染地图对齐效果；在手机、平板、笔记本、显示器与电视等多类现成显示屏上的评估显示了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: ScreenCalib 表明普通可编程显示屏可作为专用标定板的有效替代，兼具易得性与精度；多姿态联合优化可缓解标签尺寸测量误差的敏感性，代码已开源。

Abstract: ScreenCalib enables light detection and ranging (LiDAR)--camera extrinsic calibration using an ordinary programmable display, providing known visual markers and controlled geometry while reducing dedicated target preparation. The camera observes displayed AprilTags, whereas the LiDAR measures the physical screen surface and boundary, requiring indirect cross-modal association. Display geometry introduces association ambiguities, while indoor clutter complicates extraction. ScreenCalib addresses these challenges through tag-guided display extraction, geometric hypothesis generation and refinement, and surrounding scene edges for ambiguity resolution. The single-pose mode requires only one static placement. The joint multi-pose mode combines independent static observations through cross-pose consensus and jointly refines the shared extrinsics and tag scale, reducing sensitivity to tag-size measurement errors without requiring continuous motion or inter-pose trajectory estimation. Experiments in simulation and on three different sensor platforms cover mechanical and nonrepetitive LiDARs in clean and cluttered environments. Benchmark results show that ScreenCalib outperforms competitive calibration-board-based methods in translation accuracy. Joint multi-pose refinement further reduces mean rotation and translation errors across the three platforms and improves rendered-map alignment. Evaluation across readily available displays, including cell phones, tablets, laptops, computer monitors, and televisions, further examines ScreenCalib's versatility as a practical alternative to dedicated calibration targets. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZuhaoZou2026/ScreenCalib.

</details>


### [142] [Continual Concept Personalization in Diffusion Models via Residual-Space Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28567)
*Yuzhi Liu,Enis Simsar,Giulia Lanzillotta,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将残差空间适配扩展到预训练扩散模型的随机适配过程，以解决"持续概念个性化"问题，在竞争方法中取得最高的概念保真度并实现近零遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像个性化方法通常对每个概念独立训练，或假设所有概念同时可用；但实际应用中概念往往顺序到来，模型需在不断学习新主体、物体或风格的同时尽量不干扰已学概念，即持续概念个性化问题尚未得到妥善解决。

Method: 将残差空间适配（residual-space adaptation）扩展到预训练扩散模型的随机适配过程中，使模型能够顺序学习概念并抑制对旧概念的性能退化。

Result: 在竞争性持续个性化方法中取得最高的概念保真度，同时保持近零遗忘；所学习的残差空间动态揭示了适配能力在扩散模型内部的集中位置，依据该位置限制适配层可在几乎不损失性能的情况下减少最多72%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 残差空间动态能够识别对持续概念个性化贡献最大的层，并可直接指导更具参数效率的适配方式，兼具性能与效率优势。

Abstract: Text-to-image personalization aims to equip pretrained diffusion models with the ability to learn user-specific concepts from only a few reference images. Existing methods, however, typically train each concept independently or assume that all concepts are available simultaneously. In practical applications, concepts often arrive sequentially, requiring models to continually learn new subjects, objects, or styles while minimizing interference with previously acquired concepts. We address this continual concept personalization setting by extending residual-space adaptation to the stochastic adaptation process of pretrained diffusion models. Our work achieves the highest concept fidelity among competing continual personalization methods while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Beyond these empirical gains, the learned residual-space dynamics reveal where adaptation capacity is concentrated within the diffusion model. Restricting adaptation to the identified layers by our method preserves nearly the same performance while reducing the number of trainable parameters by up to 72\%, demonstrating that residual-space dynamics can identify the layers that contribute most to continual concept personalization and directly guide more parameter-efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [143] [Copyright Leakage Detection in AI Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28524)
*Shaif Chowdhury,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向AI生成视频的版权泄露检测基准数据集（50个受版权保护概念×5幅图像，配图像+提示与纯提示两类生成视频各5段），并构建基于对比检索框架的检测工具集，将检测建模为视频到图像检索，用聚合帧嵌入匹配受版权图像库、以单一相似度阈值判定并返回最相似参考作为证据，检测准确率达99.6%（仅1个假阴性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型可从图像与文本提示合成逼真视频，已引发对受版权保护视觉内容被复现的担忧，但生成视频中的版权泄露检测尚属空白，缺乏基准数据与可扩展、可解释的检测方法。

Method: 构建含50个受版权概念、每概念5幅图像的基准数据集，并为每概念各生成5段视频（图像+提示模型与纯提示模型）；设计对比检索框架，将版权泄露检测转化为视频到图像检索问题，通过聚合视频帧嵌入与版权图像嵌入库匹配，使用单一相似度阈值判定泄露并检索最相似参考作为支撑证据。

Result: 检索与检测性能强劲，检测准确率达99.6%，仅有1个假阴性；结果表明该检索式方法对AI生成视频的版权泄露检测具有可扩展性与可解释性。

Conclusion: 检索式框架能够高效、可解释地实现AI生成视频版权泄露检测；数据集与代码已公开，为后续研究提供基准与工具基础。

Abstract: Video generation models can create realistic-looking video footage from images and text prompts. Some of the recent advances have raised concerns about the reproduction of copyrighted visual content. However, copyright leakage detection in generated videos remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we introduce a new benchmark data set for copyright leakage detection, comprising 50 copyrighted concepts, each with 5 images. For each concept, we have 5 videos created using the image+prompt video generation model and the prompt-only video generation model. Following that, we develop an AI toolset based on a contrastive retrieval framework that formulates copyright leakage detection as a video-to-image retrieval problem by matching aggregated video frame embeddings against a database of copyrighted image embeddings. Our method uses a single similarity threshold and retrieves the most similar copyrighted reference as supporting evidence.
  We conducted experiments showing strong retrieval and detection performance, achieving 99.6\% detection accuracy with only a single false negative. These results show the effectiveness of the retrieval-based method for scalable and explainable copyright leakage detection in AI-generated videos. Data can be accessed publicly from https://zenodo.org/records/21386117 and the source code for this project is publicly available at https://github.com/Shaif95/video-cp-Leakage.

</details>


### [144] [EvaOmni: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28444)
*Xiaoxing Hu,Wenxi Yao,Chaoyuan Shi,Ziqi Ye,Xiaorong Guo,Peixuan Li,Zhuoran Sun,Ziyang Gong,Peiyuan Zhang,Mingxin Liu,Shuran Ma,Hongbo Liu,Xiaomin Yu,Yu Tian,Wentao Wang,Zhenjie Yang,Jian Hu,Yunhang Shen,Haoyu Cao,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出证据锚定的音视频推理基准 EvaOmni，要求模型同时输出答案与支撑证据时间区间，并用 C.G. 与 B.G. 两个指标评估完整推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频推理基准多为选择题、仅以答案正确性评分，无法判断模型是否真正形成从证据到答案的推理链；同时文本或单模态捷径可使模型在未分析必要音视频证据的情况下答对，削弱评测可靠性。

Method: 构建 EvaOmni 基准：392 个视频、1,027 条纯人工标注问答对，覆盖 15 项能力与三个层级（检索与定位、基础理解、复杂推理）。标注流程包括专家标注音视频证据、构造基于视频的干扰项、人工核查严格检验标准，以及用模型过滤文本与单模态捷径。评测要求模型同时返回答案与证据区间；提出 Coverage Grounded Score（C.G.）衡量已标注证据覆盖度，Bottleneck Grounded Score（B.G.）惩罚任一模态的时间定位不佳，二者并入答案正确性。

Result: 在 7 个开源与 17 个专有 Omni 大模型上评测，Gemini3.7-Flash 取得最高总体准确率 79.4%、C.G. 70.8%、B.G. 35.1%；在 Level 3 上准确率达 79.1%，但 B.G. 仅 12.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即便最先进的模型在锚定式音视频推理上仍有较大提升空间，尤其复杂推理层级的时间定位能力薄弱。

Abstract: Omni LLMs can naturally locate key audio and visual evidence and integrate information across modalities for audiovisual reasoning. Existing audio-visual reasoning benchmarks that mainly rely on multiple-choice questions usually score models solely based on answer correctness, making it difficult to assess whether a model truly forms a reasoning chain from evidence to the correct answer. In addition, textual or unimodal shortcuts may allow models to answer correctly without analyzing the necessary audio-visual evidence, further weakening the reliability of evaluation benchmarks. To address these issues, we introduce EvaOmni, an evidence-grounded benchmark containing 392 videos and 1,027 purely human-annotated question-answer pairs, covering 15 capabilities and three levels: retrieval and localization, basic understanding, and complex reasoning. We design a series of rigorous annotation procedures in which experts annotate audio and visual evidence and construct video-based distractors; humans review whether questions meet strict inspection criteria, and finally models filter textual and unimodal shortcuts. Beyond high-quality question-answer pairs, we require models to simultaneously return the answer and supporting evidence intervals in order to evaluate the complete reasoning chain. We propose the Coverage Grounded Score (C.G.) to measure how much annotated evidence a model identifies, and the Bottleneck Grounded Score (B.G.) to penalize poor temporal localization in either modality; both are incorporated into answer correctness. Across 7 open-weight and 17 proprietary Omni LLMs, Gemini3.7-Flash achieved the highest overall accuracy (79.4%), C.G. (70.8%), and B.G. (35.1%). At Level 3, it reached 79.1% accuracy, but its B.G. was only 12.3%. These results show that even the most advanced models still have room for development in grounded audio-visual reasoning.

</details>


### [145] [Brain2Vec: A Self-Supervised 3D Foundation Model for Structural Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28411)
*Spiros Maggioros,Guray Erus,Gareth Harman,George Aidinis,Alexander Getka,Aristeidis Sotiras,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Brain2Vec：基于74,425例T1加权MRI预训练的基础模型，可作为微调骨干或独立特征提取器，在准确率、收敛速度与嵌入质量上均优于随机初始化及体积特征基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像深度学习面临标注数据有限、算力受限的问题，端到端训练多样任务困难，因此需要大规模无标注预训练的基础模型以提供通用表征与权重初始化。

Method: 汇聚多研究的74,425例T1加权MRI扫描预训练Brain2Vec基础模型；评估其作为下游任务权重初始化骨干与冻结特征提取器两种用法；在脑龄预测及五个下游分类任务上，与随机初始化、解剖学体积特征及一个数据规模更大的SOTA基础模型对比；并通过特征归因与潜变量操控分析其编码的神经解剖区域。

Result: 以Brain2Vec预训练权重初始化并微调，脑龄预测的最终精度更高且收敛更快；冻结嵌入在五个下游分类任务中有四个优于解剖学体积特征，并与数据规模更大的SOTA基础模型性能相当；模型主要编码空间局部化且具神经解剖意义的脑区信息，操控部分潜变量对重建解剖产生单调、区域特异的影响。

Conclusion: Brain2Vec是一种有效、可解释且易于部署的脑MRI基础模型，支持微调与免训练特征提取，已开源并可通过NiChart云平台直接使用。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become essential tools in medical imaging, yet training them end-to-end for diverse tasks remains challenging due to limited labeled data and computational constraints. To address this, we introduce Brain2Vec, a foundation model pre-trained on 74,425 T1-weighted Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) scans pooled from multiple studies. Brain2Vec can serve both as a weight initialization backbone for fine-tuning downstream tasks and as a standalone feature extractor. We evaluated Brain2Vec in three tasks: downstream predictive performance, convergence speed, and embedding quality. Using Brain2Vec's pretrained weights as initialization and fine-tuning on labeled data for a brain age prediction task, results demonstrate improved final accuracy and faster convergence compared to random initialization. Additionally, its frozen embeddings outperform anatomically defined volumetric features on four of five downstream classification tasks and perform competitively with a state-of-the-art foundation model pre-trained on substantially more data. Lastly, we show that Brain2Vec encodes information primarily from a spatially localized and neuroanatomically meaningful set of brain regions, and that manipulating a subset of latent features produces monotonic, region-specific effects on the reconstructed anatomy. Brain2Vec code and trained models are available as an open-source package on https://github.com/CBICA/Brain2Vec. Users can apply Brain2Vec locally by installing the package, or directly through the NiChart cloud platform https://cloud.neuroimagingchart.com, where Brain2Vec features can be derived without requiring local installation or specialized infrastructure.

</details>


### [146] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Machine Learning with Evidence-based Planning for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28410)
*Yiqing Shen,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多智能体AutoML框架MedicalMLOmni，通过自然语言接口实现多模态医学任务的自动化模型训练，在20个基准中19个取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已成为医学图像分析的默认工具，但架构选择与超参数优化仍"更像艺术而非科学"，难以稳定获得最优性能。现有AutoML方案在医学影像领域助益有限：通用方法缺乏医学专用架构与预处理流程，而医学专用方法则依赖工程专业知识且局限于3D分割等狭窄任务。

Method: MedicalMLOmni采用多智能体AutoML框架：（1）基于证据的规划，通过检索已发表结果来指导模型选择与配置；（2）并行HPO，由多个智能体同时探索不同的调参策略；（3）以自然语言接口替代编码，支持多种单模态与多模态医学任务。

Result: 在20个基准中的19个上取得最佳分数，唯一例外为PMC-VQA，该任务上预训练更充分的视觉-语言模型占优。骨干网络匹配的对照实验表明，性能提升归因于框架本身而非骨干网络。

Conclusion: MedicalMLOmni通过消除编程要求，降低了拥有丰富数据但工程能力有限的领域专家的使用门槛，为多样化医学任务提供了可及的自动化建模能力。

Abstract: Deep learning has become the default tool for medical image analysis, driven by the open-source movement. However, achieving the best performance on a given task remains difficult, with architecture selection and hyperparameter optimization (HPO) still considered "more of an art than a science". Automated machine learning (AutoML) automates parts of model training, yet helps little in medical imaging: general-purpose approaches lack medical architectures and preprocessing, and medical-focused ones demand engineering expertise and remain confined to narrow tasks such as 3D segmentation. We introduce MedicalMLOmni, a multi-agent AutoML framework that automates model training for diverse single- and multi-modal medical tasks through a natural language interface. Specifically, MedicalMLOmni performs evidence-based planning by retrieving published results to guide model selection and configuration, and employs parallel HPO where multiple agents explore adjustment strategies concurrently. MedicalMLOmni achieves the best score on 19 of 20 benchmarks; PMC-VQA is the exception, where a vision-language model with broader pre-training leads. Backbone-matched controls attribute the gains to the framework, not the backbone. By removing coding requirements, MedicalMLOmni lowers the barrier for domain experts with rich datasets but limited engineering expertise.

</details>


### [147] [StreamEval: Evaluating Interactive Streaming Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28402)
*StreamEval Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StreamEval，一个覆盖五类任务的交互式流式视频生成统一评测基准，含 300 条 15–300 秒脚本化测试用例，从四个维度、十项指标（0–100）评测评测 37 个模型；结果显示高视觉质量与身份保持并不等同于可靠请求执行与状态一致性，请求执行仍是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着流式视频生成技术进步，与生成视频进行实时交互的需求日益突出，但现有评测缺乏对“增量到达的多模态请求下的可靠执行”与“时序约束下的状态保持”的统一度量，因此需要构建面向交互式流式视频生成的基准。

Method: 构建 StreamEval 基准，覆盖虚拟人对话、虚拟人表演、世界生成、指令编辑、参考编辑五个任务；共 300 条脚本化测试用例，时长 15–300 秒，每条明确给出初始资产、增量到达的多模态请求以及对状态变化与内容保持的显式要求；从任务执行、状态一致性、视觉保真度、视听协调四个维度设计十项任务特定指标，采用 0–100 打分，并对 37 个不同模型进行评测。

Result: 评测 37 个模型后发现：高视觉质量与身份保持能力并不必然伴随可靠的请求执行或持续的状态一致性。请求执行尤为困难：七个虚拟人表演模型中最高的 Following 得分为 36.90；文本条件世界生成中 Following 最高为 25.93；三款报告的参考编辑模型中 Edit Success 最高仅为 15.00。

Conclusion: 在时序约束与内容保持约束下的可靠请求执行，是推进全模态实时交互系统发展的核心挑战；StreamEval 为这一方向提供了统一的评测框架与公开榜单。

Abstract: As streaming video generation advances, enabling real-time interaction with generated video is becoming increasingly important. We introduce StreamEval, a unified benchmark for interactive streaming video generation spanning five tasks: avatar dialogue, avatar performance, world generation, instruction editing, and reference editing. StreamEval comprises 300 scripted test cases lasting 15-300s, each specifying initial assets, incrementally arriving multimodal requests, and explicit requirements for state changes and content preservation. Performance is assessed along four evaluation dimensions: task execution, state consistency, visual fidelity, and audiovisual coordination, instantiated through ten task-specific metrics on a 0-100 scale. Evaluating 37 distinct models across 300 test cases, we find that high visual quality and identity retention do not necessarily coincide with reliable request execution or sustained state consistency. In particular, request execution remains challenging: the highest task-specific scores are 36.90 for Following among seven avatar-performance models, 25.93 for Following in text-conditioned world generation, and 15.00 for Edit Success among three reported reference-editing models. These findings identify reliable request execution under timing and preservation constraints as a central challenge for advancing omni-modal real-time interactive systems. The leaderboard is available at https://streameval.ai

</details>


### [148] [CLOSER: Evidence-Aware Active Visual Verification for Embodied Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28476)
*Pengying Wu,Mingye Jiang,Yunze Hu,Zhejia Wen,Kangjie Zhou,Zongru Yang,Zhe Peng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLOSER：将"停止"视为闭环证据获取，用VLM逐视角验证+嵌入多样性筛选+扩散策略主动逼近，提升ObjectNav成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ObjectNav中智能体仅凭有限第一视角视频（甚至单帧）判断候选是否为目���，检测器易高置信度误判相似物体（如沙发误判为床），停在假正例会直接导致回合失败；同时现有系统贪心地逼近候选，造成遮挡或观察不充分。

Method: 提出证据感知的主动验证框架：微调开源VLM将跨帧跟踪候���的每个视角标注为目标/非目标/不确定；用基于嵌入的多样性门控选择互补视角；以轻量网络融合各视角概率；若融合结果仍不确定，则用扩散策略仅依赖跟踪掩码与深度驱动机器人靠近候选，直至判断明确。框架本地运行，可即插即用地接入现有ObjectNav系统而无需重训练。

Result: 在Gibson与Habitat-Matterport 3D上，平均成功率提升3.6个百分点，SPL提升2.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 在做出停止决策前先主动获取并验证证据，能有效改善具身导航性能，说明"验证后再承诺"的闭环范式具有实际价值。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) requires an embodied agent to find an instance of a specified category in an unseen environment and stop near it. The agent typically relies on an object detector but must judge online from a limited egocentric video stream, sometimes a single frame, whether a detected candidate is the target. Detectors often confidently mistake similar objects, such as a sofa for a bed, and stopping at a false positive fails the episode. Prevailing systems also approach candidates greedily, leaving them occluded or partially observed. We present CLOSER, an evidence-aware active verification framework that treats stopping as closed-loop evidence acquisition, coupling vision-language model (VLM) verification with active perception. CLOSER fine-tunes an open-source VLM to label each view of a candidate tracked across frames as target, non-target, or uncertain, selects complementary views with an embedding-based diversity gate, and fuses their per-view probabilities with a lightweight network. If the fused judgment remains uncertain, a diffusion policy drives the agent toward the candidate using only the tracked mask and depth until the decision becomes decisive. CLOSER runs locally and plugs into existing ObjectNav systems without retraining them. On Gibson and Habitat-Matterport 3D, it raises average success rate by 3.6 points and success weighted by path length by 2.0 points, showing that verifying evidence before committing improves embodied navigation.

</details>


### [149] [Learning the 4D World through Point Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28594)
*Byungwoo Jeon,Sunghwan Hong,Dohyeon Kim,Jonghoon Lee,Jungwoo Park,Inhee Lee,Doosan Baek,Jinil Kim,Taehyun Lim,Hanbyul Joo,Marc Pollefeys,Saining Xie,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-Point Model（VPM），一个以多视角视频历史与语言指令为条件的世界模型，联合预测任意视角未来视频与持续场景点的3D轨迹；并通过轨迹监督与动作token扩展支持机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的多视角预测要求不同相机的视频描述同一个演变的3D场景，而纯视频生成缺乏对共享3D运动的显式约束。

Method: 在预训练世界模型上引入相机条件化的多视角处理以融合跨相机互补观测，并增加轨迹头预测物理点随时间的运动；每条轨迹跨所有视角追踪同一空间点，轨迹监督与视频生成联合训练。构建VPM-Data仿真语料（同步多视角视频+持续点轨迹）与VP-WorldBench基准；并将机器人动作作为token模态加入生成器，与未来视频共享点监督骨干、联合去噪。

Result: 在VP-WorldBench及现有4D生成基准上，点监督相较纯视频基线提升了视频保真度与3D运动精度；在仿真与真实机器人实验中，动作使能模型的操控成功率亦得到提升。

Conclusion: 跨视角共享的点轨迹监督为视频生成提供3D-aware锚定，可同时改善多视角预测质量与下游机器人策略表现。

Abstract: Reliable multi-view forecasting requires videos from different cameras to describe the same evolving 3D scene. To this end, we introduce Video-Point Model (VPM), a world model that conditions on multi-view video histories and a language instruction to jointly forecast future videos from arbitrary viewpoints and the 3D trajectories of persistent scene points. We extend a pretrained world model with camera-conditioned multi-view processing that integrates complementary observations across cameras, and add a trajectory head that predicts how physical points move through time. Each trajectory tracks a single point across all views, and supervising these trajectories alongside video generation therefore anchors appearance changes in every camera to a shared description of 3D motion. To support training and evaluation, we build VPM-Data, a simulation corpus pairing synchronized multi-view videos with persistent point trajectories, and VP-WorldBench, a forecasting benchmark spanning simulated and real environments. We further extend VPM to robot control by adding robot actions as a token modality in the generator. Action tokens share the point-supervised backbone and are denoised jointly with future videos, thereby grounding the policy in the same 3D-aware representation. On VP-WorldBench and existing 4D generation benchmarks, point supervision improves both video fidelity and 3D motion accuracy over a video-only counterpart, and in simulated and real-robot experiments, it also raises the manipulation success of the action-enabled model.

</details>


### [150] [WildMAE: Structure-Aware Masked Autoencoding for Wildlife Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28590)
*Yicheng Zhang,Yujie Jin,Ke Zhang,Jiaxin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildMAE 在 MAE 解码端加入零门控卷积残差与门控交叉注意力，在 Snapshot Serengeti 掩码重建任务上取得更优的 PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS，但作者强调所述指标为历史协议下的结果，非统一复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱图像存在外观、光照、背景杂乱与视觉退化等显著差异，需要无标签表示学习框架；而标准 MAE 解码器对局部结构与可见区域上下文利用不足。

Method: 提出 WildMAE：在 MAE 解码端引入零门控卷积残差路径以注入二维邻域先验（局部结构感知精修），并加入门控交叉注意力使掩码位置从可见位置特征检索上下文，同时保留原 Transformer 解码器；训练配方渐进引入归一化像素损失、Sobel 边缘损失与感知损失。

Result: 在 Snapshot Serengeti 4,744 张测试图像、75% 掩码设置下，WildMAE 与原始 MAE 分别为 27.90 vs 24.33 dB PSNR、0.840 vs 0.780 SSIM、0.160 vs 0.320 LPIPS；历史记录另显示在四种合成退化条件下具稳定平均增益，并给出若干组件变体结果。作者声明这些为历史协议依赖指标，非新近统一复现，也不构成对真实缺失像素复原能力的验证。

Conclusion: WildMAE 为"全局 token 混合 + 空间与目标化上下文精修"相结合的野生动物掩码图像建模提供了实用架构案例，其结论需在统一协议与真实缺失场景下进一步检验。

Abstract: Camera-trap imagery presents substantial variation in animal appearance, illumination, background clutter, and visual degradation. Masked autoencoders (MAEs) offer a label-free framework for modeling such data through reconstruction of randomly hidden image patches. We present WildMAE, a decoder-side extension of MAE that combines local structure-aware refinement with masked-to-visible cross-attention. A zero-gated convolutional residual path introduces a two-dimensional neighborhood prior, while gated cross-attention allows masked positions to retrieve contextual information from visible-position features without replacing the original Transformer decoder. The associated training recipe progressively introduces normalized-pixel, Sobel-edge, and perceptual objectives. In the original Snapshot Serengeti study, covering 4,744 test images at 75% masking, the reported reconstruction scores were 27.90 versus 24.33 dB PSNR, 0.840 versus 0.780 SSIM, and 0.160 versus 0.320 LPIPS for WildMAE and vanilla MAE, respectively. Additional historical records document favorable average differences under four synthetic degradation conditions and describe component variants. We present the method, experimental observations, and their scope together: the retained metrics are historical protocol-dependent results, rather than a newly reproduced, harmonized comparison or a validation of restoration from genuinely unavailable pixels. WildMAE provides a practical architectural case study for combining global token mixing with spatial and targeted contextual refinement in wildlife masked-image modeling.

</details>


### [151] [Deep Learning-Based Road Lane Quality Assessment from Egocentric Dashcam Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28508)
*Shaif Chowdhury,Akhila Yeruvasu,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy,Ayush Goyal,Dunren Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个地理参考的智能手机行车记录仪数据集（南德州63,839帧，其中3,339帧带OSM车道数弱监督标签），并提出三种车道标线质量指标CSI、LCR、RLCS及基线预测框架，用于可扩展、低成本的车道标线质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车道标线状况对人工驾驶与自动驾驶均至关重要，但目前主要依赖人工巡检；现有自动驾驶数据集多面向车道检测与场景理解，缺乏面向养护的可见性、连续性与退化标签，导致缺少可规模化的质量评估手段。

Method: 采集GPS行车记录仪数据并用OpenStreetMap车道数作为弱监督；提出三项指标：CSI（基于拉普拉斯响应刻画车道-背景对比稳定性）、LCR（基于边缘与Hough直线证据估计结构连续性）、RLCS（预测车道数与地图车道数在匹配帧上的一致性）；训练基线回归模型，并用50张人工排序图像做初步排序一致性验证，与PSNR、SSIM、BRISQUE、NIQE、LPIPS对比。

Result: 基线模型预测性能强：CSI（MAE 0.039，R²=0.97）、LCR（MAE 0.093，R²=0.84）。CSI与LCR为无参考、分割衍生的质量指标，无需密集人工标注。50张图像试点验证中，车道专用指标与人工质量排序的一致性优于通用图像质量指标（如LCR的Spearman ρ=0.7316、Kendall τ=0.5472）。

Conclusion: 该数据集、指标与基线为可扩展、低成本、位置感知的车道标线质量评估迈出第一步；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable lane markings are important for both human drivers and automated driving systems, but their condition is still commonly assessed through manual inspection. Existing autonomous-driving datasets primarily support lane detection and scene understanding and usually lack maintenance-oriented labels for lane-marking visibility, continuity, or degradation. In this study, we present a geo-referenced smartphone dashcam dataset and baseline framework for lane-marking quality assessment. The dataset contains 63,839 road-facing frames collected across South Texas using a GPS-enabled dashcam application. A subset of 3,339 frames is matched with OpenStreetMap-derived lane-count labels, which are used as weak supervision for lane-count regression and map-consistency evaluation. We propose three lane-quality measures: Contrast Stability Index (CSI), which captures lane-background contrast using Laplacian responses; Line Continuity Ratio (LCR), which estimates structural continuity using edge and Hough-line evidence; and Regression Lane Confidence Score (RLCS), which measures agreement between predicted and map-derived lane counts on matched frames. The baseline prediction models attained strong performance, with CSI (MAE $0.039$, $R^2=0.97$) and LCR (MAE $0.093$, $R^2=0.84$). Notably, CSI and LCR are no-reference, segmentation-derived quality metrics that can be computed without dense human labels. In a pilot validation using 50 human-ranked images, these proposed lane-specific metrics show stronger rank agreement with human lane-quality ordering (e.g., Spearman's $ρ=0.7316$ and Kendall's $τ=0.5472$ for LCR) than general image-quality measures such as PSNR, SSIM, BRISQUE, NIQE, and LPIPS. We believe the dataset, metrics, and baseline provide an initial step toward scalable, low-cost, location-aware lane-marking quality assessment. Data and code: https://github.com/Shaif95/Road_Quality

</details>


### [152] [GazeLens for Efficient Egocentric Video Question Answering with Simple Gaze-Driven Adaptive Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28502)
*Trong-Thang Pham,Anh Nguyen,Hien Van Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GazeLens：在小型视觉语言模型上结合关键帧选择与注视/问题引导的 token 压缩，在 StreamGaze 上以更低输入前向 FLOPs 取得更高的宏/微平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜需在有限算力下让小规模视觉语言模型理解用户活动与周围环境。冗余帧会占用视觉预算，而激进的空间压缩又会丢失关键细节；同时注视虽指示穿戴者焦点，但问题所需证据常来自周边场景，因此需同时保留焦点与上下文信息。

Method: 两阶段流程：(1) 视觉编码前，用 HOG 变化量与图像清晰度进行帧选择；(2) 对每个选中帧，用高斯池化生成注视聚焦摘要与互补摘要，再以轻量门控按问题相关性融合为主 token，并与全局场景 token 组合，使每帧仅保留两个 token。

Result: 在 StreamGaze 上，GazeLens 的宏平均与微平均准确率均高于所评估的 full-grid 与 token 削减基线；且输入前向 FLOPs 低于共享骨干的 token 削减基线。

Conclusion: 帧级选择与注视/问题引导的 token 级压缩相结合，可在极小 token 预算（每帧 2 个）下兼顾焦点与上下文证据，实现精度与计算效率的较优权衡。

Abstract: Smart glasses can help users understand their activities and surroundings, but small vision-language models must preserve useful evidence within limited computation. This is difficult because redundant frames consume the visual budget, while aggressive spatial compression can discard relevant details. Although gaze identifies the wearer's focus, the question may require evidence from the surrounding scene. To address these constraints, we propose GazeLens, which combines frame selection with gaze- and question-guided token reduction. First, HOG transitions and image sharpness select frames before visual encoding. For each selected frame, Gaussian pooling then forms gaze-focused and complementary summaries. Because their relevance depends on the question, a lightweight gate blends them into a primary token. Together with a global scene token, this retains both focused and contextual summaries within two tokens per frame. On StreamGaze, GazeLens achieves higher macro and micro accuracy than the evaluated full-grid and token-reduction baselines, with lower input-forward FLOPs than the shared-backbone token-reduction baselines.

</details>


### [153] [DISTRACT: Degradation-Indexed Study of Textual Reference Across Competing Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28419)
*Dan Zlotnikov,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DISTRACT基准：固定视频/目标/干扰项，交叉4种文本先验与4种图像退化，用行为对比、逐层表征探针与激活修补评估5个视觉-语言跟踪器(VLT)，揭示文本仅在部分模型(Molmo2、JointNLT)中产生定向作用，且作用随退化收缩、可定位到早期干预敏感状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLT接受文本描述并不代表其内容真正改变跟踪决策，尤其在视觉证据恶化时；现有评测缺乏受控匹配干预，无法判定文本是被"使用"还是被"接收"。

Method: 构建匹配干预基准DISTRACT：保持视频、目标、干扰项不变，沿可控严重度轴交叉4种文本先验(正确/冲突等)与4种图像退化。在4个数据集上评测5个VLT，采用三类手段：行为对比、逐层表征探针、激活修补。

Result: Molmo2与JointNLT表现出定向文本使用，部分效应随退化收缩，部分在多重校正后终点变化不显著；UVLTrack、MMTrack、DUTrack对文本语义变化反应弱，FDR校正后无定向行为效应。决策近端状态中，正确vs冲突文本仅在Molmo2、JointNLT可解码；可解码性峰值位于晚期状态但替换行为影响小，替换较早状态可将冲突轨迹引向目标；匹配正确供体未显著优于无关供体，故仅定位早期敏感状态，未确立目标特定语义救援。

Conclusion: DISTRACT揭示了文本在退化视觉证据下"何时、何处"影响跟踪，区分了模型的文本敏感性差异与干预敏感状态，并指出语义救援证据仍不充分。

Abstract: Vision-language trackers (VLTs) accept descriptions intended to assist tracking, but accepting text does not establish that its content changes tracking decisions, particularly as visual evidence deteriorates. We introduce DISTRACT, a matched-intervention benchmark that holds video, target, and distractor fixed while crossing four text priors with four image degradations along a controlled severity axis. We evaluate five VLTs on four datasets using behavioral contrasts, layerwise representation probes, and activation patching. Molmo2 and JointNLT exhibit directed text use. Some effects contract under degradation, whereas others show no significant endpoint change after multiplicity correction. UVLTrack, MMTrack, and DUTrack respond only weakly to changes in text semantics, with no directed behavioral effect surviving FDR correction in the tested settings. Model-internal analyses reveal that at decision-near states, correct-versus-conflicting text is decodable in Molmo2 and JointNLT but not in the other three systems. In the responsive models, decodability peaks at late states where replacement produces little behavioral change, whereas replacing selected earlier states redirects conflict-associated trajectories toward the target. Because matched-correct donors do not significantly outperform unrelated donors, these interventions identify earlier intervention-sensitive states without establishing target-specific semantic rescue. DISTRACT thus exposes when and where text affects tracking under degraded visual evidence.

</details>


### [154] [Code2Video-Compass: A Comprehensive Benchmark for General-Purpose Code-Based Video Generation across T2V, I2V, and V2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28416)
*Zhenghan Chen,Zekai Shao,Xin Lin,Leting Cheng,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通用基准 Code2Video-Compass，覆盖 T2V/I2V/V2V 的代码式视频生成（2,104 任务、10 类、24 子类、3 个复杂度维度），并给出12项客观+5项主观的双层评测框架；对15个近期MLLM的评测显示其难以可靠生成高质量完整视频，且随构建、动态动画、状态依赖复杂度上升性能普遍下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码式视频生成相较直接生成具备细粒度控制、定向编辑与组件复用优势，但需MLLM将视觉与时序需求转为可执行程序以生成完整视频。现有代码式视频基准多聚焦专门任务，缺乏对多创意类别、多输入模态下"完整视频创作"的系统评估。

Method: 构建 Code2Video-Compass：2,104任务，覆盖10类、24子类及构建/动态动画/状态依赖三个复杂度维度；任务来源包括文本指令与视觉参考的策展任务，以及由完整专业与用户创作视频衍生的T2V/I2V/V2V任务。提出双层评测：客观层含12项指标（操作性能、视频质量、文本-视频对齐、参考保真度），主观层由MLLM裁判评估指令遵循与整体视频质量。实验评测来自9个家族、15个近期闭源与开源MLLM。

Result: 即便领先模型也难以稳定生成与文本及视觉要求一致的高质量完整视频；性能随构建、动态动画、状态依赖三类复杂度递增而普遍下降。

Conclusion: Code2Video-Compass 有望推动可靠、连贯、可控的代码式视频生成研究，并促进程序化视觉创作的更广泛进展。

Abstract: Code-based video generation offers fine-grained control, targeted editing, and component reuse compared with direct video generation. This approach requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to translate visual and temporal requirements into executable programs that produce complete videos. However, existing code-based benchmarks primarily target specialized tasks, leaving complete video creation across diverse creative categories and input modalities insufficiently evaluated. We introduce Code2Video-Compass, a general-purpose benchmark for code-based video generation across text-to-video (T2V), image-to-video (I2V), and video-to-video (V2V) settings. Code2Video-Compass comprises 2,104 tasks spanning 10 categories, 24 subtypes, and three complexity dimensions covering construction, dynamic animation, and state dependency. It combines tasks curated from textual instructions and visual references with corresponding T2V, I2V, and V2V tasks derived from complete professional and user-created videos. We propose a dual-level evaluation framework combining 12 objective metrics and five subjective metrics. Objective evaluation covers operational performance, video quality, text-video alignment, and reference fidelity, while subjective evaluation uses an MLLM judge to evaluate instruction following and holistic video quality. Extensive evaluation of 15 recent proprietary and open-weight MLLMs from nine families reveals that even leading models face challenges in reliably generating high-quality complete videos aligned with textual and visual requirements, with performance generally declining as construction, dynamic animation, and state-dependency complexity increases. We anticipate that Code2Video-Compass will advance reliable, coherent, and controllable code-based video generation and inspire broader progress in programmatic visual creation.

</details>


### [155] [CPVB-RG: Cross-Prior Visual Bridging for Prior-Aware Chest X-Ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28289)
*Sike Xiang,Shuang Chen,Julie Cox,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CPVB-RG，一种显式区分"变化区域"与"稳定解剖"的先验感知胸片报告生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生通过对比当前与既往胸片识别新发、吸收或进展的征象，但现有报告生成系统要么忽略纵向上下文，要么直接融合先验特征而不区分间期变化与稳定不变的解剖结构。

Method: CPVB-RG 融合当前与既往影像及可用临床文本。核心模块 Cross-Prior Visual Bridge（CPVB）包含 Progression-Guided Focus Attention（PGFA）与两个 Cross-Attention 单元：PGFA 将"进展差异注意力"与"共享空间注意力"进行对比，以抑制不变响应、同时保留检查间的粗粒度空间对应；Cross-Attention 单元将纵向视觉证据与临床语义连接。训练采用两阶段：先做先验引导的图像-文本对齐，再用轻量语言解码器进行自回归报告生成。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 与 IU X-Ray 上语言与临床指标均一致提升；MIMIC-CXR 上取得 0.2847 F1RadGraph、0.6692 VLScore，相对最接近的解码器匹配先验感知基线具有统计显著增益。

Conclusion: 结果支持"差异感知 + 空间引导"的融合设计对先验感知胸片报告生成的有效性。

Abstract: Radiologists interpret follow-up chest X-rays by comparing current and prior examinations to identify new, resolved or progressing findings. Most report generation systems either omit this longitudinal context or fuse prior features without distinguishing interval changes from anatomy that remains stable over time. We propose CPVB-RG, a prior-aware framework that combines current and prior radiographs with available clinical text. Its Cross-Prior Visual Bridge (CPVB) contains Progression-Guided Focus Attention (PGFA) and two Cross-Attention units. PGFA is designed to contrast progression-difference attention with shared-spatial attention, aiming to reduce invariant responses whilst retaining coarse spatial correspondence between examinations. The Cross-Attention units then connect the resulting longitudinal visual evidence with clinical semantics. CPVB-RG is trained in two stages, using prior-guided image-text alignment followed by autoregressive report generation with a lightweight language decoder. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray show consistent improvements across language and clinical metrics. On MIMIC-CXR, CPVB-RG achieves 0.2847 F1RadGraph and 0.6692 VLScore, with statistically significant gains over the closest decoder-matched prior-aware baseline. These results support the effectiveness of the proposed difference-aware and spatially guided fusion design for prior-aware chest X-ray report generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [OptionWorld: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Failures of LLMs as Long-Term Personal Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28491)
*Ruoyu Zhang,Zhaocen Liu,Weiyi Tian,Melissa Feng,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OptionWorld基准，追踪模拟日常任务中信息获取、判断、行动与结果，定位LLM作为长期个人助理的不足，核心缺陷在于任务状态维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期助理需跨天跟进未完成的任务，但“找到信息”或“开始行动”并不等于“完成任务”，现有评测未覆盖这一缺口，因此需要一个能追踪完整任务链条的基准来诊断当前LLM的短板。

Method: 构建OptionWorld基准，在模拟日常任务中记录信息获取、判断、行动和结果四个环节；采用存档决策输入进行运行，统计失败实例中关键事实出现在截止前最后输入的比例；做失败分析归纳高频问题；并开展三项后续实验分别验证补充缺失事实、行动提醒、纠正错误限制性提示的效果。

Result: 35.5%的失败实例中，关键事实在截止时刻前已存在于模型最后一次输入中；三类最高频失败为：所需信息未获取或未整合、已有行动机会未被利用、智能体施加了任务并不要求的限制。后续实验显示：补充缺失事实可在部分情况恢复任务成功；行动提醒能重启工作但后续步骤仍会错过截止时间；纠正错误禁止联系的提示后可恢复被允许的询问行为。

Conclusion: LLM作长期助理的核心短板在于任务状态维持：将事实与未完成工作关联、在截止前持续跟进落实、以及区分真实限制与智能体自身假设。

Abstract: Long-term assistants must follow unfinished tasks across days as information and circumstances change. Finding information or starting an action, however, does not ensure that the task gets completed. We introduce OptionWorld, a benchmark that traces information acquisition, judgments, actions, and outcomes in simulated daily-life tasks to identify where current LLMs fall short as long-term personal assistants. In runs with archived decision inputs, the key facts remain in the model's last input before the deadline in 35.5% of failed instances. Failure analysis identifies the three most frequent problems: needed information is not obtained or combined, available opportunities to act go unused, and agents impose restrictions that the task does not require. Three follow-up experiments show that supplying missing facts restores task success in some cases; action reminders can restart work while subsequent steps still miss the deadline; and correcting a supplied note that wrongly forbids contact restores permitted inquiry. These findings locate shortcomings in maintaining task state: connecting facts to unfinished work, following through before deadlines, and separating actual restrictions from the agent's assumptions.

</details>


### [157] [NarraSpace: Multimodal Scene-Grounded Evidence for Picture-Description Dementia Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28616)
*Qingming Li,Ruiyan Sun,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NarraSpace将图描述语音中的词与语音时间对齐到命名画面元素，按预设场景事件检索话语并构建叙事-空间描述符，再结合冻结语言模型的事件级对比得分、转录困惑度与覆盖率训练固定SVM，在ADReSS官方测试集上取得81.25%准确率与91.32% AUC，且每一事件得分均可溯源到原话语、语音区间与词匹配记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图描述痴呆分类共享同一视觉场景，但仅做转录级预测无法指出输入背后的场景证据；临床审阅也需要可核查的量化依据而非黑箱标签。

Method: 构建NarraSpace：把词与语音时间链接到具名画面元素，按预定义场景事件检索话语；计算叙事-空间描述符刻画覆盖度与时间组织；用冻结语言模型在AD与健康对照训练样本示例下对每个事件文本做对比打分，并融合转录困惑度与覆盖度测量；下游采用固定SVM分类器，保留源记录以将事件得分回溯到原话语、语音区间与词汇匹配。

Result: ADReSS 48人官方测试集上，五十个随机种子的平均准确率为80.88%±2.56%；概率平均后准确率81.25%，ROC曲线下面积91.32%。

Conclusion: NarraSpace使场景关联的分类输入构建过程显式且可检查，把事件索引的语言模型测量与源话语、语音时序及抽取记录相连；此类源链接测量有望在呈现量化摘要的同时附上原始语音证据，支持临床医生对图描述的审阅。

Abstract: Picture-description dementia classification uses a shared visual scene, but a transcript-level prediction alone does not identify the scene evidence underlying its inputs. NarraSpace links words and speech times to named picture elements and retrieves utterances for predefined scene events. Narrative-spatial descriptors summarize coverage and temporal organization. A frozen language model contrasts each event's text under examples from Alzheimer's disease and healthy-control training participants, alongside transcript surprisal and coverage measurements. Retained source records link event scores to their source utterances, speech intervals, and lexical matches. On the official 48-participant test set of the Alzheimer's Dementia Recognition through Spontaneous Speech benchmark, a fixed support vector classifier achieves $80.88\%\pm2.56\%$ mean accuracy over fifty seeds; averaged probabilities yield $81.25\%$ accuracy and $91.32\%$ area under the receiver operating characteristic curve. NarraSpace makes the construction of scene-linked classification inputs explicit and inspectable by connecting event-indexed language-model measurements to their source utterances, speech timing, and extraction records. Such source-linked measurements could support clinician review of picture descriptions by presenting quantitative summaries alongside the original speech evidence.

</details>


### [158] [Capable Teacher, Myopic Scorer: Reward-Free Teacher Repair for Agentic On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28586)
*Shu Zhao,Tan Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出agentic OPD中的复合误差解释不完整，提出"rollout-scorer gap"概念，并用R2S方法先修复教师评分器再做蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释把agentic OPD失败归因于复合误差（学生轨迹漂出教师可靠区域），于是用课程、教师引导轮次、前缀回放、教师介入等手段让训练留在该区域内。作者发现该解释不完整：在偏离最大的状态上教师的接管仍能完成任务，但在其token分数上训练的学生仍耗尽轮次。真正原因是下一token评分的"短视性"——教师逐token打分却看不到回合终点，因而放行那些会被结果型RL以零奖励惩罚的拖延性安全动作（agentic OPD缺少这种惩罚）。

Method: 提出Rollout-to-Scorer Distillation (R2S)：先修评分器再蒸馏。1)从未训练学生中挖掘高分歧状态；2)在这些状态上运行教师接管；3)通过无奖励检查（共识或LLM评判）保留足够接管通过的状态；4)微调教师，使其对从分歧轮起的所有assistant token给出"接管知情"的分数（上下文中不含接管本身）。修复后的教师被冻结，作为标准OPD的唯一评分器，修复与训练全程无需任务奖励。

Result: 在三个环境、两个教师、四个学生共12个教师-学生-环境组合中，R2S全部优于三种SOTA agentic-OPD方法，在每格最佳基线之上平均提升12.8个成功率百分点。

Conclusion: agentic OPD的核心瓶颈不是复合误差而是评分器的短视性所导致的rollout-scorer gap；通过在蒸馏前对齐教师评分器与完整rollout结果，R2S实现一致且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Agentic on-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student agent on its own trajectories while a teacher scores every token. A natural explanation of where it goes wrong is compounding error: the student's mistakes carry its trajectories beyond the region where the teacher is reliable, so recent methods keep training inside that region, through episode-length curricula, teacher-guided turns, prefix replay, or teacher intervention where the two disagree. We show this explanation is incomplete. From the states where the student departs most from the teacher, the teacher's own takeovers still finish the task, yet the student trained on its token scores keeps running out of turns. We attribute this to the myopia of next-token scoring. The teacher rates each token without seeing where the episode ends, so its scores let through the safe, delaying moves that outcome-based RL would punish with zero reward, a penalty agentic OPD lacks. We call this difference between what the teacher does and what it scores the rollout-scorer gap. We propose Rollout-to-Scorer Distillation (R2S), which repairs the scorer before distillation by mining high-disagreement states from the untrained student, running teacher takeovers from them, keeping the states where enough takeovers pass a reward-free check such as consensus or an LLM judge, and fine-tuning the teacher to give its takeover-informed scores on every assistant token from the divergence turn onward, without the takeover in context. The repaired teacher is frozen as the sole scorer for standard OPD, requiring no task reward during repair or training. Across three environments, two teachers and four students, R2S outperforms three state-of-the-art agentic-OPD methods in all twelve teacher-student-environment cells, by 12.8 success points on average over the best of them in each cell. Project page: https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/.

</details>


### [159] [Cache Engineering for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28577)
*Tianyu Fu,Ruoxi Wang,Zihan Min,Zhongqi Ren,Zhihao Li,Tengxuan Liu,Shengxu Mu,Hanling Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"cache engineering"新范式,让LLM agent通过drop/repos等操作直接控制活跃KV cache而不改动轨迹或重预填充;并协同设计AKV服务引擎处理历史依赖问题,实验显示精度与成本效率均优于摘要式上下文工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长轨迹LLM agent中KV cache的I/O与存储成为主要成本,轨迹可能超上下文长度;已有上下文工程通过裁剪/摘要压缩cache,但修改轨迹前缀会使后续缓存状态失效并触发保留上下文的昂贵重预填充,保留过少又损伤精度。

Method: 提出cache engineering:agent可主动用drop将选定cache排除出未来注意力,用repos调整保留key的位置编码,无需修改轨迹或重预填充。形式化这些cache操作,并指出history dependence服务挑战(相同token序列在不同操作序列下产生不同cache状态,前缀匹配不足以正确复用)。据此协同设计AKV服务引擎:对外提供无状态接口兼容现有API,内部管理有状态cache复用、驱逐与恢复。

Result: tool-response rolling-drop策略相比阈值触发的摘要方法平均基准分提升1.7分、平均成本降低27.7%;AKV实现最高1.2倍输出吞吐和1.5倍P95延迟加速;cache策略递归自我改进比专家设计进一步降本7.1%。

Conclusion: cache engineering与AKV引擎为长轨迹agent提供了不改轨迹、不重预填充的KV cache管理新方案,在精度、成本与延迟上均获收益,且支持策略自动进化。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents can handle increasingly complex tasks, navigating long trajectories of observations and actions. As these trajectories grow, key-value (KV) cache I/O and storage become the dominant cost. Trajectories can also exceed the LLM's maximum context length, requiring control over active cache growth. Prior work uses context engineering to reduce cache size by trimming or summarizing history. However, modifying the trajectory prefix invalidates subsequent cached states and triggers costly re-prefilling of retained context. Task accuracy can also suffer when too little context is retained to limit re-prefill costs. We propose cache engineering as a new scheme. It allows agents to directly control their active KV cache without modifying the trajectory or re-prefilling retained context. Specifically, agents can proactively use drop to exclude selected cache from future attention and repos to adjust retained keys' position encodings. We formalize these cache operations and identify history dependence as a serving challenge: identical token sequences can yield different cache states under different cache operation sequences, making prefix matching insufficient for correct cache reuse. Thus, we co-design the AKV serving engine. It provides a stateless interface compatible with existing serving APIs, while internally managing stateful cache reuse, eviction, and restoration. Experiments show that a tool-response rolling-drop cache policy improves average benchmark scores by 1.7 points and reduces average cost by 27.7%, compared with conventional threshold-triggered summarization. With AKV, it achieves up to 1.2x the output throughput and a 1.5x speedup in P95 request latency. Recursive self-improvement of cache policies further reduces cost by 7.1% over expert design. Our code is at https://github.com/thu-nics/AKV.

</details>


### [160] [Large Flow Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28559)
*Arnav Chavan,Chanhyuk Lee,Udbhav Bamba,Jaehoon Yoo,Seunghoon Hong,Aditi Raghunathan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lafa 是一个扩展到 8B 参数的块级连续流语言模型，通过适配预训练自回归模型实现数据高效训练，并借助前缀缓存与块内并行解码获得相对 AR 模型 2.54 倍吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流语言模型具备并行生成与可控、可加速的潜力，但十亿参数级别的有效构建方法尚不成熟；现有扩散类模型训练数据开销大。

Method: 采用块级流建模：块前缀使用因果注意力，块内为双向、异步流；从预训练 AR 模型适配以提升数据效率；推理时用前缀缓存与块级并行解码；进一步蒸馏为 flow map 支持一步/少步生成；并利用流训练的多 token 预测效应构建自投机解码（流式草稿 + 单模型内自回归验证）。

Result: 在 0.5B、7B、8B 规模上均以少于 30B token 完成训练，数据效率优于此前扩散模型；在指令跟随、推理与编码任务上与 AR 质量相当或更优；自投机解码在同等质量下达到 AR 的 2.54 倍吞吐，优于多 token 预测与离散扩散方法。

Conclusion: 连续流可作为可扩展的语言建模基础范式：流训练不仅保持甚至提升预训练 AR 能力，还能与自回归验证结合形成高效推理方案。

Abstract: Continuous flow language models offer a path to parallel generation with tools for control and acceleration unavailable to discrete diffusion, yet effective techniques for building them at the billion-parameter scale remain less established. We present Lafa (Large flow language model), a fast, high-performing flow language model scaled to 8B parameters in a data-efficient manner. Lafa is a blockwise flow model with causal attention on the prefix and bidirectional, asynchronous flow within each block. This design enables efficient training by adapting pretrained autoregressive (AR) models and fast inference via prefix caching and blockwise parallel decoding. We train Lafa at 0.5B, 7B, and 8B scales with fewer than 30B tokens, more efficient than previous diffusion models, and evaluate it on instruction following, reasoning, and coding, where the learned flow matches or surpasses the quality of AR and can be distilled into flow maps for one- and few-step generation. Notably, flow training preserves or even improves pretrained AR capabilities via a multi-token prediction effect, which we leverage in a self-speculative decoding scheme that verifies flow-based drafts autoregressively within a single model. This yields 2.54x the throughput of an AR model at comparable quality, outperforming multi-token prediction and discrete diffusion methods. Our findings show that continuous flows can serve as a scalable foundation for language modeling.

</details>


### [161] [BitMTP: When Multi-Token Prediction Meets Low-Bit Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28500)
*Ning Zhang,Shihao Wang,Jinrui Zhang,Chaodong Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BitMTP是一个面向超低比特LLM骨干的算法-系统协同设计框架，通过联合下一词预测与多词预测目标，在保证质量的同时显著加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特骨干（降低权重存储、内存流量与线性层开销）与多词预测MTP（并行预测多个未来token，减少顺序解码步数）理论上互补，但直接结合无法在可比质量下加速解码：掩码起草缺少下一词训练时提供的中间token输入，而更宽的计算和因果KV更新会抵消减少步数带来的收益。

Method: 提出BitMTP算法-系统协同设计框架：1）联合NTP与MTP目标，使单一模型同时具备自回归生成与块因果起草能力；2）提供仅置信度解码、仅投机解码和混合解码三种质量-吞吐操作点，无需独立的起草与验证模型；3）提出被接受前缀对齐机制，保证已提交前缀、已物化的因果KV边界与CUDA Graph重放状态一致，以支持持续生成；4）基于该契约选择宽度特化的低比特执行，并在共享KV存储上复用已捕获的计算图。

Result: 在NVIDIA H20上，混合解码在BitNet b1.58-2B上取得五项任务平均2.2倍加速，在1.7B-4B Bonsai模型上取得2.1-2.4倍加速，且任务质量相当；在RTX 3090与Jetson Orin上，分别达到最高2.0倍的五任务平均吞吐和MBPP+上3.3倍加速。

Conclusion: BitMTP证明其可在不同骨干、模型规模与GPU类型上有效加速超低比特LLM生成，是一种可泛化的高效解码方案，源代码已开源。

Abstract: For efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), methods of ultra-low-bit backbones reduce weight storage, memory traffic, and linear-layer execution cost, while multi-token prediction (MTP) methods reduce sequential decoding steps by predicting multiple future tokens in parallel. Although complementary, the direct combination of these methods cannot accelerate decoding at comparable quality: masked drafting lacks intermediate token input supplied in next-token training, while wider computation and causal key-value (KV) updates can offset the savings from fewer steps. To address these challenges, we propose BitMTP, an algorithm-system co-design framework for efficient MTP on ultra-low-bit LLM backbones. A joint next-token prediction and MTP objective equips a single model with both autoregressive (AR) generation and block-causal drafting. Confidence-only decoding, speculative-only decoding, and hybrid decoding provide different quality-throughput operating points without using separate draft and verifier models. Accepted-prefix alignment keeps the committed prefix, materialized causal KV boundary, and CUDA Graph replay state consistent for continued generation. BitMTP uses this contract to select width-specialized low-bit execution and reuse captured graphs over shared KV storage. On NVIDIA H20, BitMTP with hybrid decoding achieves five-task mean speedups of 2.2x on BitNet b1.58-2B and 2.1-2.4x on 1.7B-4B Bonsai models at comparable task quality. On RTX 3090 and Jetson Orin, it further achieves up to 2.0x in five-task mean throughput and 3.3x on MBPP+, respectively. These results demonstrate that BitMTP accelerates ultra-low-bit LLM generation across backbones, model sizes, and GPU types. The source code is released at https://github.com/PolyU-VCLab/BitMTP

</details>


### [162] [Benchmarking Arabic Dialects for Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28607)
*Noor Abo Mokh,Teresa Lynn,Nizar Habash,Ahmed Elshabrawy,Sara Alhajeri,Alaa Elsetohy,Preslav Nakov,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个面向方言阿拉伯语的数学推理论文基准 DAR-MGSM（含纯翻译与文化本地化两个平行版本，覆盖5种方言+MSA），评测11个模型，发现本地化版本性能普遍下降，且可解释性分析显示对应题目的内部推理轨迹存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（含方言阿拉伯语）的数学推理研究尚不充分，缺乏人工精编的评测基准；同时语言变体与文化语境对推理的影响未被系统考察。

Method: 构建 DAR-MGSM：人工整理的数学应用题（MWP），需多步推理，覆盖5种主要阿拉伯方言与MSA；每道题提供"仅翻译"和"翻译+文化本地化"（替换实体与指代为区域化表达）两个平行版本。在基准上评测11个阿拉伯语中心与多语种大模型，并进行机制可解释性分析。

Result: 本地化版本上模型性能总体下降，说明文化语境适配带来额外挑战；下降幅度因模型而异，阿拉伯语中心模型对本地化的敏感度不一。可解释性分析表明，同一题目的翻译版与本地化版即使总体准确率相近，也常引发不同的内部推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 数学推理表现同时依赖语言变体与文化语境，仅以翻译近似无法完全刻画方言场景下的推理能力，需在评测中显式纳入文化本地化维度。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning remains underexplored for low-resource languages, including Dialectal Arabic. To bridge this gap, we introduce the DAR-MGSM (Dialectal Arabic Multilingual Grade School Math) Benchmark, the first manually curated benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning in Dialectal Arabic. DAR-MGSM consists of math word problems (MWPs) requiring multi-step reasoning and covers five major Arabic dialects alongside Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). The benchmark comprises two parallel versions: translated-only and translated & culturally localized. The latter version adapts entities and references to regional varieties. We evaluate eleven Arabic-centric and multilingual large language models on DAR-MGSM. The results show that the performance generally declines on the localized version, highlighting the challenges introduced by culturally grounded adaptations. However, the magnitude of this effect varies across models, with Arabic-centric models exhibiting different levels of sensitivity to localization. A mechanistic interpretability analysis shows that translated and localized variants of the same MWPs often induce distinct internal reasoning trajectories, even when aggregate accuracy remains similar. Overall, our results show that mathematical reasoning performance depends on both linguistic variety and cultural context.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [TxnQA: Benchmarking Analytical Reasoning and Evidence Grounding over Transaction Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27256)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yiming Bian,Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Jenna L. Ballard,Zijie Pan,Shivani Shekhar,Yixi Li,Yirou Ge,Avinash Thangali,Richard Williamson,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TxnQA，一个面向商户交易序列分析的分析型问答基准，包含 27 个任务族（81 个模板）以及 5 个场景的欺诈风险推理套件；通过程序化生成真值，并将性能分解为格式遵循率、条件内容准确率与端到端正确率，揭示模式遵循、语义计算与证据归因三类独立瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商户交易历史的分析需要对不规则事件流进行动态时间过滤、实体消解与聚合，而现有静态表格或时间序列预测类基准难以刻画此类操作，因此缺少能同时评估结论与支撑证据的分析型问答评测。

Method: 构建 TxnQA 基准：27 个任务族共 81 个模板，外加 500 实例的 5 场景欺诈风险推理套件，评估判定结果与证据归因；数据为合成且经隐私缓解的交易数据，真值由程序计算；指标分解为 FFR、条件 CA 与 E2E 正确率；对比提示式闭源基线及 6 个 LoRA 适配的开源权重模型。

Result: 适配能可靠约束输出模式，但算术环节仍是瓶颈：Gemma-4-31B-IT 与 Qwen3-8B 的 E2E 分别为 0.867 与 0.816，所评估的 MoE 检查点仅 0.632–0.713；欺诈套件上判定准确率常超 80%，但要求精确证据归因的联合准确率最高仅约 23%。

Conclusion: 模式遵循能力、语义计算能力与证据归因能力构成交易序列问答中三个相互独立的瓶颈，需分别针对性改进。

Abstract: Analyzing merchant transaction histories requires dynamic temporal filtering, entity resolution, and aggregation over irregular event streams--operations poorly captured by static tabular or time-series forecasting benchmarks. We introduce TxnQA, an analytical QA benchmark comprising 27 task families (81 templates) alongside a 5-scenario fraud-risk reasoning suite that evaluates both verdicts and supporting evidence over synthetic, privacy-mitigated transaction data. Ground-truth answers are computed programmatically, and model performance is decomposed into format-following rate (FFR), conditional content accuracy (CA), and end-to-end correctness (E2E). Evaluating prompted proprietary baselines and six LoRA-adapted open-weight models shows that adaptation reliably enforces output schemas but leaves arithmetic bottlenecks: Gemma-4-31B-IT and Qwen3-8B attain E2E scores of 0.867 and 0.816, whereas evaluated MoE checkpoints reach only 0.632-0.713. Furthermore, on the 500-instance fraud suite, verdict accuracy frequently exceeds 80%, yet joint accuracy (requiring exact evidence attribution) collapses to at most ~23%. These findings identify schema compliance, semantic calculation, and evidence attribution as three distinct bottlenecks in transaction-sequence QA.

</details>


### [164] [ReuF2F: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Formalized Undergraduate Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27046)
*Zeyu Zheng,Yiyang Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Congmin Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Jianian Chen,Kangning Zhang,Kayue Daniel Wong,Lingyu Yang,Rong Shan,Shihao Chen,Thomas Zhu,Wentao Zhang,Xiaopeng Li,Yan Hu,Yongxi Lin,Yuxiang Chen,Zhuoying Ou,Jianghao Lin,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Jingbang Chen,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReuF2F，一个包含244个来自本科生科研(REU)的形式化开放问题的 Lean 4 基准，并配合标准编码智能体评测框架与 Leanimum-agent，用于对端到端开放问题求解进行可靠的留出评估；最佳模型仅解出约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 系统开始解决开放研究问题，可靠的验证变得关键。形式数学可提供机器可检验的证明框架。但现有形式数学基准多聚焦已知结果或少量著名开放问题，一旦被解出并广泛传播就难以可靠评估，且现有评测协议与现代编码智能体的实际工作方式不完全一致。

Method: 构建 ReuF2F：244 个来自 REU 的形式化开放问题，覆盖入门级开放研究问题与广泛数学子领域；采用标准编码智能体评测设置，将每个问题及其逻辑否定分别放入 Docker 容器中的 Lean 项目；并基于 mini-SWE-agent 构建 Leanimum-agent，作为跨模型公平比较的统一 harness。

Result: 即使在表现最好的模型上，也仅解决约 40% 的问题，表明形式化开放研究问题上仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 较少受关注、可能长期未解的 REU 开放问题更适合作为 Lean 中端到端开放问题求解的留出评测集；ReuF2F 提供了更贴合现代编码智能体、且可抵御解泄露的评测范式。

Abstract: As AI systems begin to resolve open research problems, reliable verification becomes increasingly important. Formal mathematics provides a trusted framework for mathematical reasoning, enabling agents to produce machine-checkable proofs while conducting mathematical exploration. Existing formal mathematics benchmarks often focus on known results or a small set of prominent open problems, which can become difficult to evaluate reliably once solutions are discovered and widely disseminated. Existing evaluation protocols are also not always aligned with how modern coding agents operate. We therefore introduce ReuF2F, a Lean 4 benchmark of 244 formalized open problems drawn from Research Experiences for Undergraduates (REU), spanning entry-level open research problems across a broad range of mathematical subfields. We expect these less prominent open problems, which tend to attract less attention and may remain unsolved for longer, to be better suited for held-out evaluation of end-to-end open problem resolution in Lean. We adopt a standard coding-agent evaluation setting, placing each problem and its logical negation into a Lean project in a Docker container. We also build Leanimum-agent on mini-SWE-agent to provide a unified harness for fair comparisons across models. Our evaluation finds that even the best-performing model resolves only ~40% of the problems, leaving substantial room for improvement on formalized open research problems.

</details>


### [165] [Teaching AI Agent to Play Poker Strategically Through Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27028)
*Phurinat Polasa,Thanapon Thanadunpremdet,Phatthanasak Kraiduang,Saksorn Ruangtanusak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPOKE（KL锚定的策略偏好优化），在扑克不完全信息博弈中同时提升策略质量与教师一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑克智能体可能"合法但策略糟糕"；需检验紧凑模型能否在隐藏信息下表现策略性行为，以及偏好优化能否超越静态与零样本策略。

Method: 先评测静态基线与三个冻结LLM（SmallThinker、Qwen3、Gemma-3）；再提出SPOKE，融合教师相对价值惩罚、策略锚定（KL约束）、风险约束与结构化理由监督，并与SFT、DPO、GRPO、GSPO对比。

Result: 在SmallThinker-4B-A0.6B上三次运行：Kuhn可利用度最低0.213，Leduc最低1.081，六人桌top-1一致率最高0.775；教师KL为0.522，与SFT的0.523相当。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，策略偏好优化可在保持教师一致行为的同时提升策略质量。

Abstract: A poker agent can make a legal move and still make a strategically poor one. We ask whether compact AI agents can exhibit strategic behavior under hidden information and whether preference optimization can improve beyond static and zero-shot policies. We first evaluate a static baseline and three frozen language models: SmallThinker, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 whose performance varies substantially between exact-game and six-player evaluation. We therefore propose Strategic Preference Optimization with KL-anchored Equilibrium (SPOKE), combining teacher-relative value penalties, policy anchoring, risk constraints, and structured rationale supervision, and compare it with SFT, DPO, GRPO, and GSPO. Across three runs of a SmallThinker-4B-A0.6B policy, SPOKE achieves the lowest mean exploitability in Kuhn (0.213) and Leduc (1.081) and the highest six-max top-1 agreement (0.775), while maintaining teacher KL comparable to SFT (0.522 vs 0.523). These results provide preliminary evidence that strategic preference optimization can improve strategic quality while preserving teacher-consistent behavior.

</details>


### [166] [Staged Continual Adaptation of Multimodal Foundation Models for Japanese Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27904)
*Genshin Kakimoto,Atsushi Yanagisawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 追踪一个8.4B多模态基础模型的三个阶段式后训练（视觉-语言对齐、推理蒸馏、领域微调），发现各阶段之间存在非可加性交互，最佳检查点依赖于任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段式后训练已成多模态基础模型的常规流程，但各阶段之间如何相互权衡能力、彼此干扰，仍缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 以日语金融文档为领域，在5个文本输入基准上，从未微调基线出发，依次追踪8.4B参数模型的三个阶段，并做跳阶段消融（skip-phase ablation）。

Result: 阶段交互非可加：纯数学蒸馏会覆盖此前获得的金融知识，而程序性数学技能得以保留；领域阶段"修复"多于"新增"。跳阶段消融在领域知识上最优，但丧失数值推理增益。

Conclusion: 各阶段贡献不可分解，最优检查点取决于下游任务；实践启示在于检查点保留与训练调度，而非新增训练阶段。

Abstract: Staged post-training of multimodal foundation models chains vision-language alignment, reasoning distillation, and domain tuning, yet how each phase trades capabilities against the others remains under-characterised. Tracking an 8.4B-parameter model from an un-fine-tuned baseline through three training phases on Japanese financial documents (evaluated on five text-input benchmarks), we find the phases interact non-additively: math-only distillation overwrites the financial knowledge acquired earlier even as procedural math skill survives, and the domain phase repairs more than it installs. A skip-phase ablation is best for domain knowledge but forfeits numerical-reasoning gains, so per-phase contributions are not decomposable and the best checkpoint is task-dependent, with implications for checkpoint retention and scheduling rather than added stages.

</details>


### [167] [Omni-Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction via Evidence-Prior Rebalancing for Omni LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27103)
*Zijian Liu,Chaoyou Fu,Yan Yang,Caifeng Shan,Ran He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架Omni-Woodpecker（OWP），将音视频全模态大模型的跨模态幻觉缓解从输出级操作转向"冲突条件下的表征再平衡"，通过正交分解分离接地证据与竞争先验并自适应重加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni LLM在联合音视频推理上表现优异，但跨模态幻觉严重。已有缓解方法多停留在输出级（如解码修正、对比解码），忽视了融合表征中"接地证据"与"习得先验"相互纠缠这一根因，因而无法从根本上抑制误导性先验。

Method: OWP为training-free框架，三阶段：1）从互补的单模态视角（音频、视觉）识别跨模态冲突；2）在隐状态上用正交分解将接地证据分量与竞争先验分量解耦；3）在解码前对融合表征做自适应再平衡——抑制误导性先验、恢复被忽略的证据。

Result: 以Qwen2.5-Omni-7B为骨干，CMM提升12.9分、AVHBench提升8.6分；在四个通用OLLM推理基准上稳定提升0.67–2.25分。跨架构泛化至VideoLLaMA2-AV，在相同基准上分别提升4.6与3.9分。

Conclusion: 幻觉缓解的关键在于解码前的表征层面而非输出层面；基于冲突检测与正交分解的表征再平衡是一种架构无关、无需训练且有效的通用方案，具备良好的跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Recent Omni LLMs (OLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in joint audio-visual reasoning, yet cross-modal hallucinations remain a major challenge. Existing mitigation methods mainly operate at the output level, overlooking the entanglement of grounding evidence and learned priors within fused representations. To address this issue, we propose Omni-Woodpecker (OWP), a training-free framework that shifts hallucination mitigation from output-level manipulation to conflict-conditioned representation rebalancing. Specifically, OWP first identifies cross-modal conflicts from complementary modality-specific views. It then employs orthogonal decomposition to separate grounding evidence from competing priors within the hidden states. Finally, OWP adaptively rebalances fused representations by suppressing misleading priors and restoring ignored evidence before decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and strong cross-architecture generalization of OWP. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the backbone, OWP improves performance by 12.9 and 8.6 points on CMM and AVHBench, respectively, while consistently achieving gains of 0.67-2.25 points across four general OLLM reasoning benchmarks. Furthermore, OWP generalizes effectively to VideoLLaMA2-AV, improving performance by 4.6 and 3.9 points on the same benchmarks. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZiiJianLiu/Omni-Woodpecker

</details>


### [168] [TEX: Test-Time Scaling Testing Agents via Execution-based Cross-Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27290)
*Akash Gokul,Srijan Bansal,Yingbo Zhou,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TEX：基于执行驱动交叉验证的协作式测试时扩展框架，用K×K交叉执行反馈矩阵替代孤立的候选生成与轨迹聚合，在SWT-Bench Verified上达86.4%，刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在测试用例生成上仍具挑战性。现有测试时扩展方法要么孤立评估候选，要么简单聚合轨迹，难以扩展到长时程（long-horizon）任务。

Method: 并行化多个智能体分别生成测试与补丁；将每个测试与每个补丁交叉执行，构造K×K反馈矩阵；各智能体利用自己的矩阵切片进行迭代精炼，实现客观验证且无需共享冗长轨迹。

Result: SWT-Bench Verified 上达86.4%，较单智能体基线提升+9.0%，创SOTA；即使从TEX输出中随机选取（84.4%）也超过此前SOTA（84.0%）。

Conclusion: 协作式执行反馈提升了解的整体质量而非仅最佳样本；执行接地（execution-grounded）反馈是长时程智能体任务有效的扩展机制。

Abstract: Test-case generation is critical but remains challenging for language models. Existing test-time scaling methods either isolate candidates or aggregate traces, which do not scale to long-horizon tasks. We present TEX, a collaborative test-time scaling framework based on execution-driven cross-validation. TEX parallelizes agents to generate tests and patches, cross-executing each test against every patch to form a $K \times K$ feedback matrix. Agents use their matrix slices for refinement, enabling objective validation without verbose trace sharing. On SWT-Bench Verified, TEX achieves 86.4%, establishing a new state-of-the-art and improving +9.0% over single-agent baselines. Moreover, even random selection from TEX outputs (84.4%) surpasses the previous SOTA (84.0%), showing collaborative execution feedback improves solution quality, not just best-case samples. Our results highlight execution-grounded feedback as an effective scaling mechanism for long-horizon agentic tasks.

</details>


### [169] [ACE-Compression: An Ability-Centric Evaluation of Compressed Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27913)
*Jiahui Ning,Liang Chen,Yuchen Liang,Yi Zhang,Fenglei Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ACE-Compression 能力中心测评框架，从四个部署面向维度刻画压缩后 LLM 的能力剖面，发现压缩会重分布能力而非均匀退化，量化在保持率上优于剪枝但差距的实际意义取决于应用所需能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩评测仅衡量任务性能保持率，掩盖了压缩对分布于数十亿权重中的知识造成的扰动，无法揭示哪些底层能力存活、哪些悄然崩塌，故需从"是否答对"转向"哪些核心能力仍完整"。

Method: 构建能力中心的评测框架，围绕四个部署面向维度探查：程序性推理与约束执行、知识落地与真实性、社会认知与情感理解、人格保真与风格一致性；在六个开源权重 LLM 的 35 个以上压缩检查点（覆盖量化与剪枝）上进行系统评测。

Result: 压缩重分布能力剖面：粗粒度、局部、表层信号往往保持，细粒度、长尾、情境依赖能力变得脆弱；量化在保持率上稳定优于剪枝，但其实际意义完全取决于应用所需的具体能力。

Conclusion: 部署方可依据应用所需能力剖面而非排行榜均分选择压缩方法，算法设计者获得诊断信号以构建"保关键"的压缩方法；ACE-Compression 将压缩从黑箱式精度下降转化为可测量、可理解、可改进的可解释干预。

Abstract: Model compression makes large language models deployable, but today's evaluation tells an incomplete story. Standard practice measures retained task performance and stops there. This misses a critical fact: compression perturbs the distributed knowledge embedded across billions of weights, and task scores alone cannot reveal which underlying abilities survive and which quietly collapse. To solve this question, we introduce ACE-Compression, an ability-centric evaluation framework. Instead of asking only "does the compressed model still answer correctly?", we ask "which of its core abilities remain intact?" Our framework probes four deployment-facing dimensions: procedural reasoning and constraint execution, knowledge grounding and truthfulness, social cognition and affective understanding, and persona fidelity and stylistic consistency. Across more than 35 compressed checkpoints from six open-weight LLMs-covering quantization and pruning-we find that compression redistributes ability profiles. Coarse, local, and surface-level signals often persist, while fine-grained, long-tail, and context-dependent abilities become fragile. Quantization consistently outperforms pruning in retention, but the practical meaning of that gap depends entirely on which ability an application requires. Based on our findings, deployers can choose compression methods by the ability profile their application needs, not just by a leaderboard average. And algorithm designers gain diagnostic signals to build compression methods that preserve what matters. We believe that ACE-Compression turns compression from a black-box accuracy drop into an interpretable intervention-one we can measure, understand, and ultimately improve.

</details>


### [170] [Toolflation: Seller Agents Distort Tool Markets Through Spontaneous Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28454)
*Zhenwei Tang,Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Xidong Wu,Qianfeng Wen,Xinwu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现仅以"被选中"为目标的卖家智能体会自发进行生成式引擎优化，对工具描述进行"工具膨胀"（toolflation），在部分模型下导致市场价格信号失效、买家任务成功率从89%跌至约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体之间开始相互交易：卖家智能体撰写工具/服务/产品的listing，买家智能体据此选择。已有研究关注单智能体的欺骗与对齐，但缺少对"竞争性市场中智能体对第三方（买家）诚实性"的系统考察。作者因此要探究：当模型被指派为卖家、且只被要求"被更多选中"时，是否会自发进行生成式引擎优化，并对市场双方造成何种扭曲。

Method: 基于Amazon和Instagram的真实数据工具构建工具市场，对每个问题逐一运行每个工具，从而获得"哪个工具能回答哪个问题"的基准真值。在五种生产模型下让卖家智能体改写描述，统计其宣称的字段数与自身证据（工具实际返回结果）的矛盾程度，并测量买家智能体的选择分布与任务成功率。

Result: 五种模型下卖家均不断扩张字段宣称，包括工具在不足一半目标上才能返回的字段；市场机制对此无任何约束，模型间矛盾宣称数量相差一个数量级。工具膨胀改变买家选择，能力最强的工具在10种（卖家模型×平台）组合中的7种失去份额。四种模型下买家仍选择有能力工具、任务成功率保持；第五种模型下卖家相互模仿直至描述无法区分工具，买家转而按列表位置选择，几乎无回答能力的工具赢得1/4至2/5的选择，Amazon成功率由89%降至约30%（接近随机），Instagram由77%降至40%。

Conclusion: 卖家只是执行了厂商（vendor）的指令，外部成本却由与其无直接交易的买家承担。作者主张：竞争条件下对第三方的诚实性应被视为安全对齐的组成部分，模型在作为卖家部署前应先以卖家身份接受测试。

Abstract: Agents are starting to sell to other agents. Seller agents write the listings of tools, services and products, and buyer agents read these listings when they choose what to use. We show that seller agents told only to be selected more often practice generative engine optimization on their own, and that this distorts the market for sellers and for buyers. We build tool markets from real data tools on Amazon and Instagram and run every tool on every question, so we know which tools can answer each question. Under all five production models we test, sellers rewrite their descriptions to claim more and more fields, including fields that their tools return for fewer than half of the targets. We call this process toolflation. Nothing in the market limits it, and how far it goes depends on the model, as the number of claims that contradict the sellers' own evidence differs by an order of magnitude between models. Toolflation shifts the buyers' choices between sellers, and the most capable tool loses share in seven of the ten combinations of seller model and platform. Under four models, the choices stay with capable tools and buyer agents keep their task success. Under the fifth, sellers copy one another until the descriptions no longer tell the tools apart. Buyer agents then pick tools by their position in the list, tools that answer almost nothing win a quarter to two fifths of the choices, and task success falls from 89% to about 30% on Amazon, close to random choice, and from 77% to 40% on Instagram. The sellers did only what their vendors asked, and the cost fell on buyers they never dealt with. We therefore argue that honesty toward third parties under competition is part of safety alignment, and that models should be tested as sellers before they are deployed as sellers.

</details>


### [171] [CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27645)
*Xu Hu,Haoming Li,Yuxiang Wang,Jinxin Yang,Kangshuo Li,Licheng Wang,Dingyi Kang,Jiayu Song,Tianhao Wang,Zhanhua Hu,Xu Yang,Qiannan Li,Bingzhe Li,Feng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CUA-Recovery 基准与 ReRail 合成框架，用于评测长程计算机使用智能体（CUA）的错误检测与恢复能力，并证明训练轨迹可显著提升恢复与任务完成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CUA 基准主要评测端到端任务成功率，难以反映智能体在执行中能否发现并修复错误；少数评测恢复能力的基准依赖人工注入错误，无法代表真实策略所产生的错误类型与时序跨度。

Method: 提出 ReRail 框架：在持久化桌面环境中将 MyPCBench 任务组合为带可执行真值的长程工作流，采集多策略下经验证器确认的失败，并转化为经验证的恢复轨迹，据此构建 CUA-Recovery 基准（测试集 2,250 个错误状态/100 任务，训练集 8.6K 轨迹/377 任务）；在 6 个智能体、6 种接管深度下评测错误意识率（EAR）与 Pass@3，并对 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 进行微调。

Result: 测试集上 EAR 最高仅 44.0%、Pass@3 最高仅 25.5%；微调后留出恢复状态上平均 EAR 由 19.9% 升至 35.2%，Rubric Score 由 5.0% 升至 16.1%，MyPCBench 与 Odysseys 上 Pass@3 分别提升 15.3 与 10.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有智能体在继承错误的识别与恢复上均存在明显短板；基于真实策略失败合成恢复轨迹的训练数据可同时提升错误恢复与从干净起点完成长程任务的能力，为构建鲁棒 CUA 提供可行路径。

Abstract: As computer-use agents (CUAs) move to long-horizon workflows spanning multiple applications, undetected errors can silently corrupt persistent state and cause cascading failures in subsequent actions. Reliable error detection and recovery have become critical bottlenecks for building robust CUAs. However, existing benchmarks focus primarily on end-to-end task performance, providing limited insight into whether agents can detect and recover from errors during execution. The few benchmarks that evaluate recovery rely largely on manually injected errors, which may not reflect the types or horizons of failures induced by real agent policies. We introduce CUA-Recovery, a benchmark comprising test and training suites across multiple desktop applications. We construct it using ReRail, a framework for synthesizing long-horizon tasks and trajectories in a persistent desktop environment.
