Existing defenses judge a proposed action without tracking whether its content comes from the user or from external sources such as tool outputs, and they judge it in isolation rather than against an available alternative, which forces a trade-off between security and utility. We present PROVISO, a controller that decides which action to commit after the agent proposes an action and before it is executed. PROVISO encodes trusted observations, external observations, the proposed action, and an alternative into provenance-typed memory, relates them through role-specific queries, and commits the action with the highest calibrated value learned from outcomes. On four agent security tasks spanning the agent lifecycle, no baseline exceeds PROVISO in both security and utility. PROVISO improves F1 over the strongest baseline by 13.84 points on SkillsBench and by 7.07 points on ToolSafe, and it reaches 88-99 F1 on three additional skill detection benchmarks.

</details>


### [271] [Is Sycophancy a Form of In-Context Updating?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28325)
*Itai Shapira,Myra Cheng,Hadas Orgad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将事实性谄媚（factual sycophancy）重新理解为"上下文内更新"（in-context updating）过程中的失败，而非单纯取悦用户；并提供行为与机制证据支持该观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点把事实性谄媚视为模型为获取用户认可而牺牲真实性，这一定性过度聚焦于用户因素。作者希望用更广义、通常有益的信息更新框架来解释谄媚的产生机制。

Method: 行为实验与机制分析：（1）考察模型对用户主张与外部来源信息的响应是否服从统一的"来源敏感"更新模式（包括信息支撑度、无用户认可动机的情形）；（2）定位支持顺从用户的内部组件，检验其对更广泛上下文更新的因果重要性。

Result: 模型对用户主张的响应处于更广泛的来源敏感更新模式之中；对归因于外部来源的信息同样会修正答案，且信息支撑越强修正越大；即使不涉及用户认可，顺从仍存在；支持顺从的内部组件对一般性上下文更新同样具有因果作用。

Conclusion: 事实性谄媚产生于上下文更新机制对用户断言赋予相对于其证据而言过高的权重。由于谄媚嵌入在该有益过程中，单纯降低谄媚可能同时削弱有用的更新能力，构成权衡；因此需要同时评估两种行为的评测方法，以及能够将二者解耦的缓解策略。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy, a language model's deference to a user's false beliefs, is usually understood as a bid for the user's approval at the expense of truthfulness. We argue that this framing over-emphasizes the user and propose viewing sycophancy as a failure within the broader and generally useful process of in-context updating, by which models revise their answers based on information supplied during an interaction. We support this view with behavioral and mechanistic evidence. First, models' responses to user claims sit within a broader pattern of source-sensitive updating. They revise their answers in response to information attributed to external sources as well, more so when it appears better supported, and their deference to the user persists even when no user approval is at stake. Second, the internal components that support deference to the user are also causally important for in-context updating more broadly. Our results suggest that factual sycophancy arises when in-context updating gives a user's assertion excessive weight relative to its evidence. Because sycophancy is embedded in this process, reducing it risks reducing useful updating as well, a trade-off that calls for evaluations measuring both behaviors and mitigations that decouple them.

</details>


### [272] [RaspAssistant as an Agentic Framework for Automating Adsorption Simulation Research in Porous Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27572)
*Marko Petković,Susanne Gooijer,José Manuel Vicent-Luna,Vlado Menkovski,Sofía Calero*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层多智能体框架RaspAssistant，测试其能否端到端完成多孔材料吸附研究（力场准备→模拟→验证→分离分析），在四项基准的12次工作流复现中得分8.50–10.00，力场抽取基准平均加权分0.967，并成功诊断优化乙炔力场、验证CO2力场后筛选197种纯硅分子筛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔材料吸附研究需结构准备、力场选择、模拟、验证与结果解释等多环节协同，流程长且耦合强，端到端自动化困难；作者希望检验分层多智能体框架能否承担从力场准备到下游分离分析的完整吸附研究流程。

Method: 构建分层多智能体框架RaspAssistant，在四项吸附与分离基准上开展12次多智能体工作流复现实验，用涵盖工作流正确性、模拟有效性与分析一致性的评分量表评估；另设8个案例的力场抽取基准，以参数一致性与RASPA可用性加权评分；并开展开放式研究（乙炔力场诊断与优化、CO2力场经MFI实验验证后的分子筛筛选）。

Result: 12次工作流复现总体得分8.50–10.00；8案例力场抽取基准平均加权分0.967；开放式研究中成功诊断并优化乙炔力场，验证CO2力场后筛选出197种通过自动输入检查的纯硅分子筛。

Conclusion: RaspAssistant可在给定任务说明与输入后自主执行扩展型吸附模拟工作流，完成协调、验证、适配与文档记录，同时暴露模型敏感性与方法学局限以供专家复核；自动化程度受任务规格与输入质量约束。

Abstract: Porous-material adsorption studies require coordinated structure preparation, force-field selection, simulation, validation, and interpretation, making end-to-end automation difficult. We test whether RaspAssistant, a hierarchical multi-agent framework, can carry an adsorption study from force-field preparation through validation and downstream separation analysis. Across twelve multi-agent workflow-reproduction runs spanning four adsorption and separation benchmarks, overall scores ranged from 8.50 to 10.00 on a rubric evaluating workflow correctness, simulation validity, and analysis consistency. In a separate eight-case force-field extraction benchmark, the mean weighted score was 0.967, combining parameter agreement and RASPA readiness. In open-ended studies, the system diagnosed and refined an acetylene force field and, after validating a CO$_2$ force field against MFI experiments, screened 197 pure-silica zeolites that passed automated input checks. These results demonstrate autonomous workflow execution after task specification and input provision: RaspAssistant coordinates, validates, adapts, and documents extended adsorption-simulation workflows while exposing model sensitivity and methodological limitations for expert review.

</details>


### [273] [Structured-Token Attention for Fine-Grained mmWave Liquid Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27512)
*Junseo Min,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StructAttn模型，将毫米波雷达特征张量按天线、特征、分支、全局四个互补视角嵌入11个结构化token，用三层Transformer进行细粒度液体识别，在FG-LiquID上达到96.70%平均最优轮准确率与97%集成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度液体识别需要从紧凑的毫米波测量中区分相似物质，现有标量token注意力模型表达能力有限，缺乏对雷达特征张量结构信息的显式建模。

Method: 设计结构化token注意力：从天线（antenna）、特征（feature）、分支（branch）、全局（global）四个互补视角表示雷达特征张量，将其嵌入为11个token，输入三层Transformer分类器。在公开FG-LiquID数据集（30种液体、3000样本）上评估，与原始配方MMNet基线及标量token注意力模型对比，并进行5个随机种子的最优轮、最终轮、五折交叉验证与概率集成实验，辅以token与特征分析。

Result: 在发布划分上5个种子平均最优轮准确率96.70%，与MMNet基线持平，比标量token注意力模型高1.50个百分点；5种子概率集成达97%（600个测试样本中18个错误）；五折折外准确率95.30%。token与特征分析表明结构化视角对预测有贡献，残余混淆集中于水、软饮料与食用油类别。

Conclusion: 结构化tokenization是注意力型液体识别中切实可行的设计选择，多视角结构化token能有效利用紧凑毫米波测量中的结构信息。

Abstract: Fine-grained liquid identification requires a classifier to distinguish similar substances from compact millimeter-wave measurements. We investigate structured-token attention, which represents a radar feature tensor through complementary antenna, feature, branch, and global views. The proposed StructAttn model embeds these views into eleven tokens and combines them with a three-layer transformer. Experiments on the public FG-LiquID data cover 30 liquids and 3,000 samples. Over five seeds on the released split, StructAttn achieves 96.70\% mean best-epoch accuracy, matching the original-recipe MMNet baseline and exceeding a scalar-token attention model by 1.50 percentage points. A five-seed probability ensemble reaches 97\%, corresponding to 18 errors among 600 test samples. Final-epoch and five-fold results complement the checkpoint-selected comparison, with StructAttn obtaining 95.30\% out-of-fold accuracy. Token and feature analyses show that structured views are useful for prediction, while residual confusions concentrate in water, soft-drink, and edible-oil classes. These results support structured tokenization as a practical design choice for attention-based liquid recognition.

</details>


### [274] [Reusing Multimodal Agents: Efficient Evaluation of Sound Designs in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27670)
*Thai Nguyen Van,Ibrahim Khan,Mury Fajar Dewantoro,Pratch Suntichaikul,Hai Van Pham,Ruck Thawonmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态学习与强化学习的框架，通过预训练多模态编码器并固定策略、仅微调编码器，实现无需从头重训智能体的游戏音效设计客观评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音效设计显著影响游戏体验与可访问性，但现有客观评估（基于智能体表现而非人工评分）需针对每种音效设计从头重训智能体，计算成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 先将视觉与听觉信息编码到共享潜空间，预训练多模态编码器；再以该潜表征训练强化学习智能体；评估时固定已训练策略，仅针对每种音效设计微调多模态编码器，从而隔离音效变化对智能体行为的影响。在多个游戏类型的多款游戏上，比较盲智能体（仅用声音输入）在不同音效设计下的表现。

Result: 实验表明该方法可检测由音效退化或改变引起的智能体性能差异，且相较从头重训显著降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 该框架为游戏音效设计评估提供了实用且可扩展的方案，代码、数据与补充材料已公开。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel framework for efficiently evaluating in-game sound designs using multimodal learning and reinforcement learning. Sound design significantly influences gameplay and accessibility; however, objective evaluation methods, defined here as automated assessments based on agent performance rather than human ratings, often require retraining agents from scratch for each sound design. Our approach first pre-trains a multimodal encoder that encodes visual and auditory information into a shared latent space. A reinforcement learning agent is then trained using this latent representation. We fine-tune the multimodal encoder for each sound design while keeping the trained policy fixed, allowing us to isolate the effects of sound changes on agent behavior. To evaluate the proposed method, we conducted experiments on games from multiple genres, comparing the performance of blind agents (agents using only sound input) under various sound designs. Experimental results suggest that this approach can detect differences in agent performance caused by degraded or altered sound design. Moreover, we significantly reduce the computational cost compared to retraining the agent from scratch. Experimental results suggest that this approach provides a practical and scalable solution for evaluating in-game sound designs. The source code, data and supplementary document are available: https://github.com/thainv0212/tog-reusing-agents.

</details>


### [275] [KPHarness: Co-Evolving Knowledge and Probing for Long-Horizon Gaming Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27417)
*Zhikang Chen,Yueqing Sun,Sen Cui,Ziang Ye,Yu Wang,Yan Feng2,Xierui Song,Yu Zhang,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KPHarness 通过“知识探针协同演化”自适应调整冻结 VLA 模型的外部执行 harness：恢复知识提出干预假设、探针结果提供证据，双向互促，并以配对沙箱评估做有界修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失败的交互轨迹只能说明 agent 需要干预，却不能说明执行 harness 应如何改变；因此 harness 自适应应聚焦于收集诊断证据，而非直接提出替代恢复规则。

Method: 在冻结的视觉-语言-动作模型外部构建 harness：恢复知识提供情境化干预假设以引导主动探测，探测结果既判定假设适用性，又用于改进恢复知识与后续探针选择；将快速策略执行与慢速“研究循环”分离，后者把知识库与探测证据转化为有界 harness 修订；每次修订在匹配沙箱条件下与现有版本配对比较，仅在配对评估显示提升时接受；进一步验证的恢复策略保留以备跨任务复用，即时交互反馈与延迟回合成功信用用于更新动作与知识探测策略。

Result: 在 Minecraft 的挖掘、战斗与合成任务上，任务覆盖优于裸策略执行、人工固定 harness 以及仅演化知识但探针非自适应的方案；在未见过的任务上，固定演化后的 harness、知识库与探测策略后，任务完成率亦提升。

Conclusion: 将 harness 自适应重构为“知识引导探测、探测修正知识”的协同演化，并配合有界修订与配对验证，可在不更新基础模型的前提下提升任务覆盖与跨任务泛化。

Abstract: A failed gaming trajectory reveals that an agent needs intervention, but not necessarily how its execution harness should change. Effective harness adaptation therefore requires gathering diagnostic evidence, rather than merely proposing alternative recovery rules. We present KPHarness, a framework that adapts the external execution harness of a frozen vision-language-action model through knowledge-probing co-evolution. Recovery knowledge supplies context-specific intervention hypotheses that guide active probing; probe outcomes reveal when these hypotheses apply and provide evidence for refining both recovery knowledge and later probe selection. This reciprocal process allows the harness to improve not only which interventions it applies, but also how it investigates failures. KPHarness separates fast policy execution from a slow research loop that translates accumulated knowledge and probing evidence into bounded harness revisions. Each revision is compared with the incumbent under matched sandbox conditions and accepted only when the paired evaluation shows improvement. Recoveries supported by further validation are retained for cross-task reuse, while immediate interaction feedback and delayed episode-success credit update action- and knowledge-probing policies during adaptation. Experiments on Minecraft tasks spanning mining, combat, and crafting show improved task coverage over bare policy execution, a manually specified fixed harness, and knowledge-only harness evolution with non-adaptive probing. Evaluation on previously unseen tasks further shows improved task completion with the evolved harness, knowledge bank, and probing policies held fixed,

</details>


### [276] [Does Multi-Agent Collaboration Pay When Agents Serve Users? Testing Agent Scaling in Dual-Control Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27655)
*Ethan Yang,Xiangcheng Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在τ²-bench对话式双控制基准上检验"能力饱和规则"，发现固定模拟用户下多智能体系统在13个有效单元中12个劣于单智能体系统、1个持平，加权平均增益为-28个百分点（95% CI [-44, -12]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）部署的前提是协作优于单智能体系统（SAS），但何时才划算尚不明确。现有最系统的研究给出"能力饱和规则"：SAS较弱时协调有益、较强时有害，交叉点约在单智能体准确率45%；但该规则源自无用户参与的自主基准，需在真实面向用户的对话场景中检验。

Method: 在τ²-bench（双控制基准）的航空、零售、电信三个域上，采用同一模型构建的集中式MAS与原生SAS对比，覆盖三个模型家族共六个模型、约7,950次模拟对话，每个系统每次episode记录一份完成token账本。研究预注册并公开变更日志，MAS按实际构建情况报告（其规划器未见过工具schema，一项效度检验未达标）。

Result: 固定模拟用户下无任何单元显示MAS获益：13个有效单元中12个MAS更差、1个持平；拟合增益在观测能力范围内为负（加权均值-28点，95% CI [-44, -12]）。规则预测获益的两个单元中MAS分别落后13点和22点；无预算上限时最好仅打平，但token消耗为SAS的5.5倍。唯一增益出现在最接近45%的单元、且仅在第二种模拟器下。增益是否随能力下降尚无定论（斜率-0.41，95% CI [-1.51, +0.68]），因模型×域效应占主导。MAS未可检测地减少错误的数据库写入，而是把其他失败转化为预算耗尽与不作为。

Conclusion: 面向人类用户的智能体场景中，SAS应作为默认方案；多智能体设计必须从该设定本身提供算力匹配的证据。作者公开全部轨迹、账本、提示与代码。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built from language models are deployed on the premise that collaboration beats a single-agent system (SAS), yet when it pays is unclear. The most systematic study to date gives a capability-saturation rule: coordination helps while the SAS is weak and hurts once it is strong, with the crossing near 45% single-agent accuracy. That rule was derived on autonomous benchmarks, where no user takes part. We test it where agents now work: in conversation with a user under a written policy, on $τ^2$-bench, the dual-control benchmark. On its airline, retail and telecom domains we compare the stock SAS with a centralized MAS built from the same model for six models from three families, over about 7,950 simulated conversations, with one completion-token ledger per episode per system. The study was pre-registered with a public change log; the MAS is reported as built, with one validity bar unmet (its planner never saw the tool schemas). Under a fixed simulated user no cell shows a MAS benefit: the MAS is worse in 12 of the 13 valid cells and tied in the thirteenth, and the fitted gain is negative across the observed capability range (weighted mean -28 points, 95% CI [-44, -12]). On the two cells where the rule predicts a benefit, the MAS loses by 13 and 22 points, and without a budget ceiling it at best draws level at 5.5 times the SAS's token spend. The one gain appears on the cell nearest 45%, under a second simulator. Whether the gain declines with capability is unresolved (slope -0.41, 95% CI [-1.51, +0.68]), because model-by-domain effects dominate. The MAS does not detectably reduce wrongful database writes; it converts other failures into budget exhaustion and inaction. For agents that serve people, the SAS is the default; a multi-agent design must show compute-matched evidence from the setting itself. We release all trajectories, ledgers, prompts and code.

</details>


### [277] [From Encoding to Generation: Towards Empathetic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27603)
*Samhita Bollepally,Takashi Yamauchi,Marissa West*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将共情拆分为认知共情（表征他人情绪）与情感共情（生成恰当回应），用分层线性探针、注意力分析与表征对齐度量考察Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct在10类情绪上的内部组织与传递过程，发现情绪类别在中层可解码、情绪相关用户词元获更高注意力、但回应阶段与目标情绪的对齐度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情交互包含"表征他人情绪"与"生成恰当回应"两种可区分能力，但现有工作较少在模型内部把二者分开考察；作者希望验证这一认知—情感共情框架能否作为分析工具，揭示情绪信息在大模型中被组织、被选择注意、并在生成过程中被传递或衰减的机制，从而为生成过程中的情绪监测与无需外部提示的共情增强提供基础。

Method: 在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct上，基于EmpatheticDialogues数据集的10类情绪：(1) 逐层线性探针（L2探针）检测情绪类别的可解码性；(2) 统计生成时对情绪相关用户词元与其他词元的注意力分配比例及层分布；(3) 用余弦相似度度量输入表征与生成回应表征相对目标情绪的对齐程度，并统计漂移方向与幅度。

Result: 中层出现情绪类别可解码性的广泛峰值区，第11层L2探针留出准确率63.6%（随机10%，TF-IDF词法基线54.6%）；生成时情绪相关用户词元获得的每词元注意力约为其他词元的1.89倍，集中于第10–15层；目标情绪对齐度从输入表征的平均余弦相似度0.239降至生成回应的0.125，77%样本呈正向漂移，angry与furious漂移最大。

Conclusion: 情绪类别表征、注意力分配与回应层面对齐构成模型处理中可区分的三个成分，可用功能性认知—情感共情框架加以分析；该结果为生成过程中的情绪表征监测、以及利用模型既有情感结构实现更自然的共情回应（无需外部提示或显式情绪条件）提供了基础。

Abstract: Empathetic interaction involves two distinguishable capacities: representing another person's emotional state (cognitive empathy) and generating an appropriate response to that state (affective empathy). We apply this distinction to examine how emotion-related information is internally organized, selectively attended to, and carried through response generation in Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct across 10 emotion categories from the EmpatheticDialogues dataset. Layer-wise linear probing revealed a broad middle-layer region of peak emotion-category decodability, with the layer-11 L2 probe achieving 63.6% held-out accuracy against a 10% chance level and a 54.6% TF-IDF lexical baseline. During response generation, emotion-related user tokens received 1.89 times as much attention per token as other utterance tokens, concentrated across layers 10-15. Target-emotion alignment then decreased from a mean cosine similarity of 0.239 for input representations to 0.125 for generated responses, with positive drift in 77% of examples and the largest shifts for angry and furious. Emotion-category representation, attention allocation, and response-level alignment therefore emerge as distinguishable components of model processing that can be analyzed through a functional cognitive-affective empathy framework. These findings provide a foundation for monitoring emotion representations during generation and leveraging models' existing affective structure to support more naturally empathetic responses without relying on external prompting or explicit emotion conditioning.

</details>


### [278] [InsightRAG: Aggregating Distributed Scholarly Judgments for Scientific Survey Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28085)
*Yuquan Lin,Wenshuo Zhang,Yilin Ye,Shuchang Xu,Zhao Chen,Weiqi Wang,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InsightRAG利用作者撰写的Related Work中的人类判断，通过引用关系重建研究范围并组织分类体系，实现更可靠的自动综述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献快速增长使综述写作日益困难，催生自动综述生成需求。但现有系统需在开放论文空间与开放概念空间中搜索，缺乏社区自身如何导航这些空间的信息，导致范围不完整、分类体系薄弱、引用不可靠。

Method: 核心洞察：作者撰写的Related Work包含两类人类判断——作者精选与其贡献相关的引用（界定范围），并解释被引工作间的关系（提供组织证据）。据此提出InsightRAG：(1) 洞察引导检索沿作者支持的引用关系重建经验证的研究范围；(2) 对所得关系证据进行结构化聚合，以最小冲突目标组织成反映文献中反复出现的作者视角的分类体系；(3) 该分类体系引导生成并为每节内容的证据设置边界。

Result: 在十个主题上，该方法在范围重建、分类体系质量、引用支撑度和参考文献可靠性四方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: 作者撰写的Related Work是缓解开放式检索缺陷的可靠监督信号；将引用关系作为范围与结构的双重证据源，可显著提升自动综述的范围完整性、组织合理性与引用可信度。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific literature makes survey writing increasingly challenging, motivating automatic survey generation. Yet, existing systems must search an open space of papers and an open space of concepts with no trace of how the community itself has navigated them, leading to incomplete scope, weak taxonomies, and unreliable citations. Our core insight is that author-written Related Work sections contain the human judgments needed to address both limitations: authors curate the citations relevant to their contributions and explain how the cited works relate, yielding paths for scope reconstruction and relational evidence for organization. We propose InsightRAG. Its insight-guided retrieval follows author-supported citation relations to reconstruct a verified research scope and structure the resulting relational evidence. It aggregates these judgments under a minimum-conflict objective into a taxonomy that reflects recurring author-written perspectives in the retrieved literature, guides generation, and bounds each section's evidence. Results on ten topics show gains in scope reconstruction, taxonomy quality, citation support, and reference reliability.

</details>


### [279] [DecisionPivot: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Decision Models in LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28522)
*Xialie Zhuang,Mingcong Lei,Zunhai Su,Ri Liu,Jianjin Li,Peixian Ma,Jia Pan,Ngai Wong,Guanhua Chen,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DecisionPivot诊断基准（48个任务、40,033条数据、7类决策角色），通过对比组、概率排序、校准与选择性覆盖等多维度指标评测54个模型，揭示聚合分数掩盖的决策失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测中单条目准确率无法判断模型是否在受控变化下做出正确决策，也无法反映其预测置信度的可靠性，聚合分数会掩盖模型间的实质差异。

Method: 构建DecisionPivot基准：核心赛道含13个生成任务家族的7,800条数据，组织为对比组并由独立实现的求解器验证答案；参考赛道含35个公开测试集的32,233条数据并保留原评测指标。按模型特定协议评测40个决策模型与14个通用模型，评估条目准确率、对比组准确率、概率排序、校准与经验选择性覆盖。

Result: 参考赛道仅差2.6分的两个27B模型，对比组准确率分别为70.1%与44.2%；2,400个二元对比对中Perplexity Decider有92.3%的配对排序正确，但两端同时答对仅74.1%；条目准确率相近的模型在高置信预测可靠性上存在差异；GLM-5.3在1,296条子集上高推理设置达93.5%，最低推理仅81.5%。

Conclusion: 将错误关联到关键细节与难度等级，DecisionPivot可提供聚合评测无法给出的细粒度决策失败诊断。

Abstract: Decision models support routing, guardrails, and output verification in LLM systems. However, item accuracy alone cannot establish whether models make correct decisions across controlled variations or assign reliable confidence to their predictions. We introduce DecisionPivot, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 48 tasks and 40,033 items across seven decision roles. Its core track contains 7,800 items from 13 generated task families, organized into contrast groups with computed answers verified by independently implemented solvers. The complementary reference track contains 32,233 items from 35 public test sets, preserving their original evaluation metrics. We evaluate 40 decision models and 14 general models using documented, model-specific protocols, assessing item and contrast-group accuracy, probability ordering, calibration, and empirical selective coverage. Our results reveal substantial differences obscured by aggregate scores: two 27B models separated by only 2.6 points on the reference track achieve contrast-group accuracies of 70.1% and 44.2%. Across 2,400 binary contrast pairs, Perplexity Decider correctly ranks the positive member above the negative member in 92.3% of pairs but answers both members correctly in only 74.1%, exposing a gap between probability ordering and decision correctness. Models with similar item accuracy also differ in the reliability of their high-confidence predictions. On a separate 1,296-item subset, GLM-5.3 achieves 93.5\% contrast-group accuracy at a higher reasoning setting, compared with 81.5% at its lowest effort. By linking errors to pivotal details and difficulty levels, DecisionPivot provides fine-grained diagnostics of decision failures that aggregate evaluation alone cannot reveal.

</details>


### [280] [Exploring Temporal Context-Aware Physical Grounding in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28483)
*Yilei Man,Yang Chen,Jingcai Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 G-JEPA，通过在潜空间世界模型中引入共享因果时间模块以聚合时间上下文并进行物理状态监督，同时将学到的模型与先验引导的动作规划（基于当前与目标潜表征及其差异逐步条件化的生成式动作分布）耦合，从而改进基于动作条件世界模型的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 类世界模型存在两个问题：(1) 物理接地（physical grounding）缺乏对时间上下文的显式聚合；(2) 动作规划过度依赖通用采样分布。

Method: 学习阶段：引入共享因果时间模块，在观测序列与预测序列内聚合时间上下文，并在物理状态监督下将二者接地到任务相关的物理状态。规划阶段：引入动作推断，以当前与目标潜表征及其演化差异为条件，逐步条件化生成式动作分布，产生锚定提议（anchored proposals）以引导基于模型的在线精化。

Result: 在操作（manipulation）任务上规划性能提升，包括初始观测与目标观测时间间隔增大的任务。

Conclusion: 物理状态监督引导时间表征学习，动作先验引导在学习到的世界模型中进行搜索，二者协同提升基于潜空间世界模型的规划能力。

Abstract: Learning latent dynamics is fundamental to building action-conditioned world models for planning. Among existing families, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) offer a natural framework by modeling transitions directly in latent space rather than at the pixel level. However, two issues remain in JEPA-based world models: (i) physical grounding lacks explicit aggregation of temporal context and (ii) action planning always overly relies on generic sampling distributions. To this end, we propose $\mathcal{G}$-JEPA, which incorporates temporal context into physical grounding for latent world models and couples the learned models with prior-guided action planning. Specifically, during learning, we incorporate a shared causal temporal module that aggregates temporal context within observation and prediction sequences under physical state supervision, grounding both in task-relevant physical states. When planning, we further introduce action inference through stepwise conditioning of a generative action distribution on the current and goal latent representations together with their evolving discrepancy, yielding anchored proposals that guide model-based online refinement. Together, physical state supervision guides temporal representation learning, while the action prior guides search through the learned world model. Experiments demonstrate improved planning performance on manipulation tasks, including tasks with increasing temporal separation between initial and goal observations. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/myl11045/G-JEPA.

</details>


### [281] [Beyond Trajectory Statistics: Community-Consistent Human Mobility Generation via Evolving User-Location Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28467)
*Bo Liu,Tong Li,Jiapeng Zhang,Zhu Xiao,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOTION多智能体框架，将人类移动生成建模为动态用户-位置图生成，实现社区一致性的移动轨迹合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动生成器虽引入集体移动模式，但主要聚焦个体轨迹与总体人口动态，对用户-位置交互形成的演化社区结构建模不足。

Method: 将移动生成形式化为动态用户-位置图生成；用Leiden算法从历史移动中检测社区并提取社区条件交互先验；框架含用户智能体、位置智能体与全局管理者：用户智能体规划移动、搜索候选目的地并从个体视角推断出行；位置智能体被选择性激活，从社区视角推断用户到达；管理者协调双视角、诊断致偏差的交互，并将针对性反思信号归因以修正用户决策、位置吸引力与源用户选择。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，MOTION在个体轨迹、人口与流量分布、社区与图结构等指标上至少超越基线4.05%；进一步分析验证了鲁棒性、隐私保护性与下游实用性。

Conclusion: 社区结构建模对高质量移动生成具有重要价值，多智能体协同与反思机制可有效保障个体与社区层面的一致性。

Abstract: Human mobility data are fundamental to Web-based location services and broader urban applications, and mobility generation provides synthetic trajectories when real data are costly or inaccessible. Although recent mobility generators have incorporated collective mobility patterns, they primarily focus on individual trajectories and aggregate population dynamics, leaving evolving community structures formed by user-location interactions insufficiently modeled. In this paper, we study community-consistent human mobility generation. To this end, we formulate mobility generation as the generation of dynamic user-location graphs to model community structures and propose MOTION, a Multi-agent framework for cOmmuniTy-consIstent human mObility geNeration. MOTION consists of user agents, location agents, and a global manager agent. MOTION first detects coherent communities from historical mobility using the Leiden algorithm and extracts community-conditioned interaction priors to guide the generation process. Guided by these priors, the user agents plan mobility, search destination candidates, and infer user movements from the individual view. In parallel, selectively activated location agents infer likely user arrivals from the community view. The manager coordinates both views, diagnoses interactions causing deviations, and attributes targeted reflection signals to revise user decisions, location attraction, and source-user selection. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that MOTION outperforms baselines by at least 4.05% across reported metrics of individual trajectories, population and flow distributions, and community and graph structures. Further analyses confirm its robustness, privacy preservation, and downstream utility. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MOTION-04C4.

</details>


### [282] [Value-Rich, Differently Organized: Measuring Value Expression and Organization in AI-Generated Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28604)
*Maoyuan Li,Haoyuan Li,Zhongsheng Wang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一个诊断性度量框架，从"价值表达"与"价值组织"两个维度比较人写与AI生成辩论中的价值结构，发现AI辩论价值表达丰富但组织方式不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM生成辩论的评估主要聚焦论证质量，而生成论证背后的价值结构（价值观如何被表达与组织）尚未得到充分考察。

Method: 基于Schwartz基本价值观理论（Basic-10），将辩论表示为在论证单元、话语、立场与整场辩论等多层级上的价值分布，并据此设计价值覆盖度、对齐度、扩散度与跨轮次动态等度量指标。

Result: 在多个生成系统上，AI生成辩论总体价值表达丰富，激活的价值维度常不少于匹配的人类参考文本，但组织方式不同：其话语在局部上价值更分散；且在盲评条件下，评判者能够区分价值的清晰度与聚焦度。

Conclusion: 辩论评估不应只关注价值是否存在，还应纳入价值的组织方式；价值表达与价值组织应作为两个可分离的评估维度。

Abstract: Current evaluation of LLM-generated debate largely focuses on argumentative quality, leaving the value structure of generated arguments underexamined. We introduce a diagnostic metric framework for comparing how values are expressed and organized in human-reference and AI-generated debates. The framework separates value expression, the range of recognizable values a debate invokes, from value organization, how those values are distributed and sustained across turns. Grounded in Schwartz's Basic-10 value theory, it represents debates as multi-level value distributions over argumentative units, utterances, stances, and full debates, enabling metrics for value coverage, alignment, diffusion, and cross-turn dynamics. Across multiple generation systems, AI-generated debates are broadly value-expressive, often activating as many or more value dimensions than matched human references, but they organize these values differently. Their utterances are more locally value-diffuse, and source-blind judges distinguish value clarity from value focus. These findings show that debate evaluation should consider not only value presence, but also value organization. Code and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Maoyuan-li/Value-Expression-and-Organization.

</details>


### [283] [Inferring continuous cognitive traits from limited neuroimaging data using pairwise convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27668)
*Marjan Rashidi,Robert Bain,Craig Stark,Jeffrey Krichmar,Mary Hegarty,Elizabeth Chrastil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出成对卷积排序框架，直接从全脑T1 MRI学习比较式排序预测，在小样本下高精度预测空间能力并定位关键脑区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知能力的个体差异显著，但其结构脑基础尚不清楚。传统脑-行为分析依赖预设指标（体积、皮层厚度）并假设线性关系，在小样本数据集中敏感性受限。

Method: 提出成对卷积排序（pairwise convolutional ranking）框架，直接以全脑T1加权MRI为输入，进行相对比较而非绝对数值预测；在N=108的小样本上，用四项小规模空间能力任务验证，并与标准回归及逐点（pointwise）深度学习基线对比；采用迭代皮层区域移除（后向消除）进行脑区定位与可解释性分析。

Result: 回归分析在全部四项任务上均实现高精度预测，预测力优于标准回归和逐点深度学习基线；后向消除识别出四项任务共有的支撑脑区及任务特异性脑区。

Conclusion: 成对学习能够从结构MRI中提取有意义的行为了信息，为个体差异的神经解剖基础提供预测能力与可解释洞见。

Abstract: Individual differences in cognitive abilities vary widely, yet the structural brain features supporting these differences remain poorly understood. Traditional brain-behavior analyses rely on predefined metrics such as volume or cortical thickness and typically assume linear relationships with behavior, limiting sensitivity in small datasets. Here, we introduce a pairwise convolutional ranking framework that learns directly from whole-brain T1-weighted MRI by making comparative rather than absolute predictions, which performs well even with small samples (N=108). We tested this method with four small-scale spatial abilities tasks. Regression analysis predicted performance with high accuracy across all tasks, achieving greater predictive power over standard regression and pointwise deep learning baselines. Iterative cortical-region removal (backward elimination) revealed brain regions supporting individual performance common to all four tasks, as well as task-specific regions. These results demonstrate that pairwise learning extracts meaningful behavioral information from structural MRI, providing prediction and interpretable insight into the neuroanatomy of individual differences.

</details>


### [284] [ChartEvo: Self-Evolving Agent Harnesses for Long-Tail Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28282)
*Qi Wang,Peijie Wang,Jun Cao,Haoyang Tong,Xiao-Hui Li,Fei Yin,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartEvo 是一个递归自我改进框架，使图表智能体通过经验驱动的“harness 层”自我改进来适应表现不佳的长尾图表类型，在 ChartBench 和 ChartX 长尾图表上分别较基座模型 Qwen3.7-Plus 提升 45.6% 和 32.5% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在常见图表上表现良好，但在视觉结构与推理需求多样的长尾图表上准确率骤降；工具增强智能体虽可提升复杂图表推理能力，但为异构且不断演化的长尾手工设计策略与工具难以规模化。

Method: 提出 ChartEvo 递归自我改进框架：联合演化可复用的图表专用 Skills 与可执行 Tools，并学习可复用的局部工具调用链以组合已演化能力、控制工具增强推理的推理开销。

Result: 在 ChartBench 与 ChartX 的长尾图表上，相比基座 MLLM（Qwen3.7-Plus）准确率分别提升 45.6% 与 32.5%。

Conclusion: 递归自我改进对长尾图表适配有效，可作为无需人工工程、可随长尾演化扩展的图表推理增强方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced chart understanding and reasoning, yet their performance remains highly uneven across chart types. While modern MLLMs perform well on common charts, their accuracy drops sharply on long-tail charts with diverse visual structures and reasoning requirements. Tool-augmented agents can improve complex chart reasoning by introducing specialized visual capabilities, but manually engineering strategies and tools for the heterogeneous and evolving long tail is difficult to scale. We introduce \textbf{ChartEvo}, a recursive self-improvement framework that enables chart agents to adapt to underperforming chart types through experience-driven harness-level self-improvement. ChartEvo jointly evolves reusable chart-specific Skills and executable Tools, while learning reusable local tool-call chains to compose evolved capabilities and control the inference overhead of tool-augmented reasoning. On long-tail charts from ChartBench and ChartX, ChartEvo improves accuracy over its base MLLM, Qwen3.7-Plus, by \textbf{45.6\%} and \textbf{32.5\%}, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of recursive self-improvement for long-tail chart adaptation.

</details>


### [285] [S-Agent 2.0: Spatial Reasoning with a General Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28354)
*Rui Gong,Ruoyu Wang,Yalun Dai,Hao Li,Sidi Yang,Qihao Zhao,Hongyu Li,Zeyang Guan,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Kim-Hui Yap,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S-Agent 2.0 为通用编码 Agent 配备命令行 3D 感知工具，围绕"闭合证据回路"（获取证据—持久场景记忆—用空间语言解读读数）设计接口，在 MMSI-Bench 与 ReVSI 上显著优于无工具 Agent 及 SpatialClaw 专用系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agentic VLM 虽可主动调用 3D 感知工具，但常出现"工具返回正确证据、模型仍答错"的现象：从原始读数到可用结论之间的"模型—工具鸿沟"未被填补。

Method: 将现成通用编码 Agent 与命令行感知工具包结合，设计三环节接口：(1) 主动获取场景证据；(2) 以持久化场景记忆保存证据；(3) 将每个读数转为平实空间语言解释。保留编码 Agent 的完整灵活性，同时保证所依据的读数锚定在工具的几何结果上。

Result: 工具包使 Qwen3.8-27B 提升 +13.9/MMSI 与 +20.5/ReVSI，Gemini-3.8-Flash 提升 +11.2 与 +9.6。开源骨干下 S-Agent 2.0 达 66.5/82.3（SpatialClaw 为 62.6/74.5）；闭源骨干下达 74.1/90.5（对比 67.0/79.7）。随着骨干增强，两者增益均收窄，但 ReVSI 上 SpatialClaw 从 +13.2 降至 +2.7，本文方法仍保持 +9.6。且可迁移至 Codex、Claude Code 等不同 harness。

Conclusion: 以"闭合证据回路"为核心的接口设计比更换专用系统更有效，且在更强骨干上增益更稳健、跨 harness 通用。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning asks a model to work out where objects are in 3D space, how they relate to one another, and how they move. Agentic VLMs have recently brought large improvements on such tasks by letting the model actively gather evidence with 3D perception tools instead of answering in a single pass. Even so, on many questions the tools return the right evidence and the model still does not arrive at the correct answer: the gap between model and tools, where raw readings become usable conclusions, is left unfilled. We present S-Agent 2.0, a spatial reasoning agent that equips an off-the-shelf general coding agent with a command-line perception toolkit, and we design the interface around one principle: help the VLM close the evidence loop: acquire scene evidence, remember it in a persistent scene memory, and interpret each reading in plain spatial language. This design keeps the full flexibility of a coding agent, and every reading it acts on stays grounded in the tools' geometry. On MMSI-Bench and ReVSI, the toolkit improves a strong open-source model, Qwen3.8-27B, by +13.9 and +20.5 points over the same agent without tools, and a strong closed-source model, Gemini-3.8-Flash, by +11.2 and +9.6 points. With the open backbone S-Agent 2.0 reaches 66.5 on MMSI-Bench and 82.3 on ReVSI, ahead of the specialized system SpatialClaw on the same backbone (62.6 and 74.5), and with Gemini-3.8-Flash it reaches 74.1 and 90.5 against its 67.0 and 79.7. Both tool gains shrink as the backbone grows more capable, but SpatialClaw's shrinks far more on ReVSI, from +13.2 to +2.7, while ours stays at +9.6. S-Agent 2.0 also works across different general coding agent harnesses, such as Codex and Claude Code.

</details>


### [286] [What Do RTL Repair Benchmarks Actually Measure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28595)
*Yeyin Eva Zhu,Rishabh Ranawat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了RTL修复基准（5,784训练对、459测试用例、支持仿真与形式等价验证的评测框架），并借此揭示两类测量混淆：仿真与形式打分在“提供规格”效应上符号相反（主要源于复位与首周期语义），以及修复分数无法隔离模型是否利用了故障RTL，最终提出评测控制规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL修复基准应衡量模型能否在给定规格下修复错误RTL，但常规修复分数未必能隔离模型是否真正使用了故障RTL，且仿真与形式验证语义差异可能扭曲结论，因此需要审计现有评测方式。

Method: 构建哈希绑定的RTLRepairDataset（5,784个(broken, fixed)训练对）、RTLRepairBench（459个留出用例，374个语法变异、85个行为变异）及支持仿真与形式等价的评测执行框架；利用该基础设施识别测量混淆，进行复位感知重打分，并在274个挖掘出的模型失败案例上比较“仅规格”与“规格条件”的恢复率。

Result: 1）在某一评测组中，仿真与形式打分对“提供规格”的表观效应给出相反符号，复位感知重打分将几乎全部分歧归因于复位与首周期比较语义；2）六个模型上，“仅规格”减“规格条件”的恢复率点估计范围为-8.5至+18.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 常规修复分数不能保证区分模型是否使用了故障RTL。作者提出评测控制：报告初始化、复位、首周期与未知值语义；披露oracle覆盖与未决用例；纳入仅规格基线。资源与审计共同提供了行为修复基准及其分数归因诊断协议，并开源基准、训练集、构建代码与复现材料。

Abstract: RTL repair benchmarks should measure whether a model can fix broken RTL given a specification. To this end, we construct and hash-bind RTLRepairDataset, 5,784 (broken, fixed) training pairs; RTLRepairBench, 459 held-out cases with 374 syntactic and 85 behavioral mutations; and an evaluation harness supporting simulation and formal equivalence. We then use this infrastructure to identify two measurement confounds. First, simulation and formal scoring give opposite signs for the apparent effect of providing a specification in one evaluated cohort; reset-aware rescoring attributes almost all of the simulation-formal disagreement to reset and first-cycle comparison semantics. Second, on 274 mined model failures, the specification-only minus specification-conditioned recovery point estimate ranges from -8.5 to +18.3 percentage points across six models. Thus, a conventional repair score need not isolate whether the model used the faulty RTL. We translate these findings into evaluation controls: report initialization, reset, first-cycle, and unknown-value semantics; disclose oracle coverage and undecided cases; and include a specification-only baseline. Together, the resources and audit provide both a benchmark for behavioral repair and a protocol for diagnosing what contributes to its measured score. We release the benchmark, the training set, construction code, sanitized aggregate evidence, and reproduction materials at https://github.com/Evaaaz/RTLRepairBench.

</details>


### [287] [Sage-A1.5: From Long-Horizon Execution to Scientific Exploration with Capability Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28578)
*Wenjie Lou,Tianshuo Peng,Shiyang Feng,Shengji Tang,Runmin Ma,Ruizhe Chen,Jinxin Shi,Haoyang Peng,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Yuhao Zhou,Yue Fan,Yixin Chen,Yiming Wu,Yiheng Wang,Yigeng Jiang,Yichen Jiang,Yi Xie,Xu Guo,Xiaohan He,Tao Hu,Songtao Huang,Siyu Zhou,Shuaiyu Zhang,Shangheng Du,Qinglong Cao,Kun Li,Jingqi Ye,Hongbin Zhou,Guohang Yan,Zongsheng Cao,Ziyi Wang,Zhuo Liu,Zhiyin Yu,Zhiwang Zhou,Zelin Tan,Yuqing Zhu,Yaoze Zhang,Yang Chen,Xuming He,Xiuliang Liang,Xingjian Guo,Xiang Zhuang,Weiping Shen,Weida Wang,Tianyuan Zhou,Tianlin Ye,Tengyuan Fan,Siyu Yan,Shuo Li,Shuchen Pu,Shengyuan Xu,Shao Zhang,Ran Wei,Qinyuan Qin,Qingyu Yang,Nianchen Deng,Meng Dai,Kuikun Liu,Kaitao Chen,Juncheng Xiao,Jiangning Liu,Jianbiao Mei,Jia Yu,Haonan He,Hangfan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chen Zhang,Bihao Zhan,Benteng Cheng,Yuqiang Li,Yihao Liu,Yang Yang,Xujun Fu,Xiaosong Wang,Xiangyu Yue,Wenwei Zhang,Shuyue Hu,Pinlong Cai,Mianxin Liu,Lei Bai,Jie Zhou,Jie Hou,Jiamin Wu,Hui Wang,Haijun Li,Guangyu Wang,Dongzhan Zhou,Chunfeng Song,Chao Yang,Biqing Qi,Xing Xie,Dahua Lin,Bowen Zhou,Xiangchao Yan,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Peng Ye,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sage-A1.5 是一个 35B 的 MoE 智能体模型，在 A1 基础上通过自适应能力组合提升长时程问题求解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A1 侧重单任务求解，缺乏对工作组织与执行中策略修订的能力；长时程任务需要任务分解、并行协作与可靠性验证。

Method: 三项改进：(1) 智能体编排——通过任务委派与上下文集隔离的子智能体并行协作实现任务分解与并发执行；(2) 智能体验证——通过进度检查、证据校验与反思式策略修订提升长时程可靠性；(3) 开放科学发现——从回答预设问题扩展到探索性研究，结合交互式分析与可检查的研究产物。

Result: 在复杂智能体任务与多学科科学应用中展示了上述能力。

Conclusion: 为社区提供可及的开放科学发现基础模型，打通从长时程任务执行到协作化、可验证、可检查的科学探索路径。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce Sage-A1.5, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts agentic model that builds on A1 to advance long-horizon problem solving through adaptive capability composition. Compared with A1, Sage-A1.5 learns not only to solve individual tasks, but also to organize work and revise its approach during execution. Sage-A1.5 highlights three improvements: agentic orchestration, verification, and open scientific discovery. First, agentic orchestration extends single-agent execution through task delegation and parallel collaboration among subagents with isolated contexts, enabling complex tasks to be decomposed and addressed concurrently and supporting efficient execution while reducing interference across concurrent subagents. Second, agentic verification strengthens long-horizon reliability through progress checking, evidence validation, and reflective strategy revision, allowing the agent to recognize unproductive search paths and pursue alternative approaches. Third, open scientific discovery extends agentic capabilities beyond answering predefined questions to conducting exploratory research, connecting scientific inquiry with interactive analysis and inspectable research artifacts. We showcase these capabilities through complicated agentic tasks and multidisciplinary scientific applications. Besides, we hope Sage-A1.5 provides the community with an accessible foundation for open scientific discovery, offering a practical path from long-horizon task execution to collaborative, verifiable, and inspectable scientific exploration.

</details>


### [288] [Evolving Agent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28601)
*Saaket Agashe,Yuzhe Yang,Gonzalo Gonzalez-Pumariega,Bhuvan Chandra Koduru,Chih-Lun Li,Jiachen Yang,Ang Li,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agent S4，将接口设计本身作为智能体能力，通过元智能体从任务经验中构建并进化"Evolving Agent Interfaces"，在 OSWorld V2 上达到开源 SOTA（21.3%），为固定接口同模型的 3.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体受限于人类手工设计的接口，且何时使用何种接口的知识也依赖人工构建，无法跟上新应用、工作流与模型的演进速度。作者主张接口设计应当成为智能体自身的能力。

Method: 构建自主的"计算机使用科学家" Agent S4：元智能体在无成功标签的合成训练任务轨迹上分析任务智能体的行为，在仓库中构造与修订接口及使用知识；随后以基于种群的进化，根据元智能体未曾见过的验证任务表现来选择继续发展的仓库，偏好可泛化的改动。

Result: 在 OSWorld V2 上，Agent S4 搭配 Qwen3.8-27B 与进化后的接口仓库取得 21.3% 二值得分，为同模型固定接口的 3.8 倍，超过 GPT-5.5（13.0%）与 Claude Opus 4.8（20.6%），成本分别约为其 4.3% 与 1.5%；在 ScienceBoard 上，同模型成功率由 41.4% 提升至 58.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明可从经验中进化出有用接口，自主接口设计可作为独立于模型与运行框架改进的互补性提升来源；代码已开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are limited by the interfaces humans give them. An agent may type a spreadsheet cell by cell when a single line of code would do, or print all two thousand lines of a file through a shell to find one function. Both the interfaces and the knowledge of when to use each are built by hand, which cannot keep pace with new applications, workflows, and models. We argue that interface design should itself be an agent capability. We introduce Agent S4, an autonomous computer-use scientist that builds Evolving Agent Interfaces from task experience. A meta-agent examines task agents' trajectories on synthetic training tasks, without success labels, and constructs and revises interfaces and usage knowledge in a repository. Population-based evolution then selects which repositories to develop further by their performance on validation tasks the meta-agent never sees, favoring changes that generalize beyond the seen tasks. On OSWorld V2, Agent S4, with Qwen3.8-27B and the evolved repository of interfaces, sets a new state of the art in open-source computer-use agents, reaching 21.3% binary score, 3.8 times the same model with fixed interfaces. This is above both GPT-5.5 (13.0%) and Claude Opus 4.8 (20.6%), at about 4.3% and 1.5% of their respective costs. On ScienceBoard, the evolved interfaces improve the success rate from 41.4% to 58.6% with the same model. These results suggest that useful interfaces can be evolved from experience, making autonomous interface design a source of improvement complementary to advances in models and harnesses. We open-source our code at https://github.com/simular-ai/agent-s.

</details>


### [289] [Semantic Observability for Autonomous Systems Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28611)
*Jitendra Solanki,Anand Todkar,Ziran Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Semantic Observability"自主多智能体框架，将异构多格式可观测性日志统一建模为属性图数据库，并在其上用有状态多智能体（DCG编排）结合因果图算法、时序回溯与LLM推理实现故障根因分析与修复建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式微服务部署产生海量异构、多格式的可观测性日志，且基础设施在物理与逻辑上分片。级联故障下，SRE定位根因困难：依赖人工关键词过滤、跨监控面板的时序关联割裂，扁平非结构化日志仓库存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 1) 将物理基础设施拓扑、逻辑软件依赖与时间序列事件建模为带属性的节点和有向关系，统一汇入动态可查询的属性图数据库；2) 在该统一底座上部署由有向循环图（DCG）编排的状态多智能体系统，自主导航拓扑，应用因果图算法与时序反向回溯定位异常影响范围（blast radius）；3) 剪枝后的子图交由LLM推理引擎生成上下文相关、代码级RCA与可执行修复建议；4) 论文给出系统架构、形式化关系本体与多智能体控制回路的设计，并规划基于LogHub与Train-Ticket的开放验证框架。

Result: 本文为架构性/概念性论文，未给出定量实验。概念层面论证该框架可消除环境遥测噪声、降低平均修复时间（MTTR）、压缩LLM token消耗，并抑制结构性幻觉；同时规定了开放源代码验证框架与标准基准（LogHub、Train-Ticket）上的评测方案，量化评估留待后续姊妹论文。

Conclusion: 通过"属性图统一语义底座 + 因果/时序图算法剪枝 + 多智能体DCG编排 + LLM代码级RCA"的组合，为异构日志下的自动化根因定位提供了系统架构与形式化本体基础；其有效性目前仅为概念性论证，尚需在标准基准与生产环境中进行大规模定量验证。

Abstract: Modern distributed microservice deployments generate massive volumes of heterogeneous, multi-format observability logs across physically and logically partitioned infrastructure. Isolating root causes during cascading failures remains an arduous task for Site Reliability Engineers (SREs), bottlenecked by manual keyword filtering and disjointed temporal correlation across monitoring dashboards. We introduce Semantic Observability, an autonomous multi-agent framework that unifies disparate log streams into a dynamic, queryable Property Graph Database. By modeling physical infrastructure topologies, logical software dependencies, and chronological event sequences as attributed nodes and directed relations, it bridges the semantic gap inherent in flat, unstructured log repositories. Building on this unified substrate, we deploy a stateful multi-agent system orchestrated through Directed Cyclic Graphs (DCGs). The system autonomously navigates the topology, applying causal graph algorithms and temporal back-traversal to isolate the precise blast radius of anomalies. The pruned subgraph is then interpreted by a Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning engine to generate contextualized, code-level Root Cause Analyses (RCA) and actionable remediations. This paper establishes the foundational system architecture, formal relational ontology, and multi-agent control loops of Semantic Observability. We show conceptually how the framework eliminates ambient telemetry noise, reduces Mean Time to Resolution (MTTR), compresses LLM token consumption, and prevents structural hallucinations. We also specify an open-source validation framework on standard benchmarks (LogHub and Train-Ticket), laying the groundwork for a forthcoming companion paper on exhaustive quantitative evaluation and production benchmarking.

</details>


### [290] [Agentic Ensemble with Vision-Language Models for Visual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28560)
*Shangquan Sun,Wenjun Luo,Xu He,Yoonsoo Lee,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AnomalyAgent：一种后训练融合框架，将多预训练检测器的分数与异常图融合建模为有限动作选择问题，由受约束的 VLM 为高冲突样本选择融合动作，在 MVTec AD、VisA 等基准上稳定优于等权融合并取得最优平均图像 AUROC 与像素 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉异常检测训练样本稀缺，常需集成多个预训练检测器以利用其可迁移的图像分数与异常图；但现有集成方法在已保存预测上套用固定融合规则，当检测器因训练目标、先验与视觉表征差异而明显分歧时失效。

Method: 1) 对异构分数与异常图进行对齐；2) 汇总分数级与区域级分歧；3) 将融合建模为有限动作选择问题：分数动作（共识融合、鲁棒聚合、检测器加权、分数—图一致性）与图动作（共识、检测器加权、几何聚合、分歧校正）；4) 引入受约束的视觉语言模型检查高冲突样本并选择有效的分数动作与图动作，生成最终预测。属后训练融合框架，不重新训练检测器。

Result: 在 MVTec AD 与 VisA 主基准上，平均图像 AUROC 与像素 AP 在对比的融合方法中最优，且一致优于等权融合；在另外七个公开异常基准及 Real-IAD 的大规模分数级评测中展现稳健的跨域迁移能力。

Conclusion: 以动作选择替代固定融合规则、并用受约束 VLM 处理检测器分歧，是提升多检测器集成异常检测的有效途径，且具备良好的跨基准泛化性；局限在于依赖预训练检测器集合与 VLM 推理开销。

Abstract: Visual anomaly detection aims to detect abnormal objects, while anomaly samples are often scarce during model training. Assembling multiple pretrained detectors can help by leveraging their transferable image scores and anomaly maps. However, most existing ensemble methods apply fixed fusion rules over saved predictions which struggle when pretrained detectors clearly disagree due to different training objectives, priors, and visual representations. To this end, we design AnomalyAgent, a post-training fusion framework for accurate visual anomaly detection. AnomalyAgent aligns heterogeneous scores and maps, summarizes score- and region-level disagreement, and treats fusion as a finite action-selection problem. It implements two types of actions: 1) score actions that determine how to fuse image-level detector confidence via consensus, robust aggregation, detector weighting, or score-map consistency; 2) map actions that fuse dense anomaly responses via consensus, detector weighting, geometric aggregation, or disagreement correction. A constrained vision-language model (VLM) is introduced to inspect high-conflict samples and select valid score action and map action to produce the final prediction. We evaluate AnomalyAgent on MVTec AD and VisA as primary benchmarks, together with seven additional public anomaly benchmarks and Real-IAD for large-scale score-level evaluation. Across these benchmarks, AnomalyAgent consistently improves over equal-weight fusion and achieves the best mean image AUROC and pixel AP among the compared fusion methods on MVTec AD and VisA, while demonstrating robust transfer across additional anomaly domains.

</details>


### [291] [Adaptive Multi-Token Prediction: How far should we go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28619)
*Emile Anand,Mohammed Rehan Parwani,Xinyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AMTP（自适应多token预测），将推测解码的"草稿视界"选择建模为在线控制问题，利用验证反馈学习每个上下文可信任的草稿长度，无需重训练、不改变精确验证器，以吞吐量为目标选视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多token预测（MTP）通过一次前向草拟多个token并在目标模型单次前向中验证来加速解码，但最优推测视界随上下文变化：视界长时若可靠则摊销更好，一旦被拒绝，其后缀计算被浪费。与自回归草拟可逐token设停止规则不同，MTP必须在草拟前固定视界（所有头同一次前向触发），因此需要预先决策视界长度。

Method: 将视界选择形式化为在线控制问题；用"接受存活概率"（草稿前缀被完全接受的概率）刻画各视界下的期望提交token数；引入轻量控制器AMTP：每轮前预测草稿能存活的长度，选择"每秒期望token数"最大的视界，从每次验证反馈中在线学习；不需重训练、验证器保持不变。理论分析将在线学习器的预测误差转化为相对各上下文最优视界的吞吐遗憾，并进一步给出墙钟完成时间的界；在线梯度下降下遗憾为次线性。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct搭配Medusa头、Meta的MTP模型、Qwen3-4B-Inst-2507-MTP构成的五组草稿-目标配对，于AIME 2025与GSM8K上评测：AMTP解码最高比标准解码快4.1倍，在10个设置中有8个持平或优于固定窗口，吞吐提升最高达10%。

Conclusion: 将MTP视界选择作为在线控制问题并提出AMTP，是一种无需重训练、即插即用且理论可保证（次线性遗憾、完成时间有界）的推测解码加速方案，实证上在多数设置优于固定窗口视界。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction (MTP) can accelerate language-model decoding by drafting several tokens and verifying them in a single target-model pass. However, the best speculation horizon changes with context: longer drafts offer greater amortization when reliable, but can waste compute on suffixes discarded after a rejection. Unlike autoregressive drafting, where a stopping rule can decide token by token, MTP must fix the horizon before drafting, as all heads fire in the same forward pass. We therefore formulate horizon selection as an online control problem, using verification feedback to learn how far the draft can be trusted in each context, and introduce Adaptive Multi-Token Prediction (AMTP): a lightweight controller that requires no retraining and leaves the exact verifier unchanged. To guide horizon selection, we characterize the expected number of tokens committed at each horizon via acceptance survival, the probability that a draft prefix is fully accepted. Before each round, AMTP predicts how far the draft is likely to survive and picks the horizon with the highest expected tokens per second, learning freely from every verification pass. Theoretically, we show that any online learner's prediction error translates into throughput regret against the best horizon for each context, and in turn into a bound on wall-clock completion time. With online gradient descent, this regret is sublinear. Across five draft-target pairings built on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with Medusa heads, Meta's MTP models, and Qwen3-4B-Inst-2507-MTP, evaluated on AIME 2025 and GSM8K, AMTP decodes up to 4.1x faster than standard decoding and matches or outperforms the fixed window in 8 of 10 settings, with throughput gains of up to 10%.

</details>


### [292] [Evolving Semantic Indexes for Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28568)
*Jinming Nian,Jyun-Yu Jiang,Henry Tsai,Yi Fang,Zhirong Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用本地开源31B LLM驱动的进化式程序搜索来改进生成式检索(GR)中各SID组件，并用1M参数代理解码器提供快速反馈；在NQ320K上Recall@1由55.8%提升至59.3%，R@100由85.8%提升至90.6%，并指出代理可靠性取决于所进化的组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GR将检索/推荐建模为离散语义标识(SID)的自回归生成，SID设计（构建、冲突处理、解码、训练）高度决定GR质量；但这些离散、相互依赖的组件无法端到端梯度优化，长期依赖人工设计。作者希望用自动化程序搜索替代手工设计。

Method: 以本地部署的开源31B权重LLM作为变异算子，对可执行的GR组件代码进行进化式程序搜索（不调用专有LLM API）；用1M参数代理解码器替代反复训练1B目标生成器以提供快速反馈；在21个SID程序上验证代理与目标的排序一致性；在固定训练配方下于NQ320K、MSMARCO300K及多语电商数据集上进化SID构建与冲突处理等组件，并做组件级消融研究。

Result: 21个SID程序上代理与目标Recall@100排序相关性Spearman ρ=0.90；NQ320K上联合进化SID构建与冲突处理使R@1从55.8%→59.3%、R@100从85.8%→90.6%，并在MSMARCO300K与多语电商数据上同样优于种子GR；组件研究显示改进可可靠迁移于语义索引部分，但对生成器训练目标与全流程不可靠。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的进化搜索是GR设计的实用工具，但前提是针对所选搜索空间先确立代理可靠性；代理反馈的迁移性具有组件依赖性。据作者所知，这是该方法首次应用于GR。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) casts search and recommendation as autoregressive generation of discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs). SID design largely determines GR quality, but its discrete, interdependent components (SID construction, collision handling, decoding, and training) cannot be optimized end-to-end with gradients and remain largely hand-designed. We introduce a framework that improves these components through LLM-driven evolutionary program search, to our knowledge the first application of this approach to GR. A locally served, open-weight 31B model mutates executable GR components, so no proprietary LLM API is used, and a 1M-parameter proxy decoder provides fast feedback in place of repeatedly training the 1B target generator. Across 21 SID programs on NQ320K, proxy and target Recall@100 show strong rank correlation (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Guided by this proxy, evolution improves the seed GR on NQ320K, MSMARCO300K, and a multilingual e-commerce dataset under a fixed training recipe. On NQ320K, jointly evolving SID construction and collision handling improves Recall@1 from 55.8% to 59.3% and Recall@100 from 85.8% to 90.6%. A component study shows that proxy reliability depends on what is evolved: improvements transfer reliably for the semantic index but not for the generator's training objective or the full pipeline. LLM-driven evolutionary search is thus a practical tool for GR design, provided proxy reliability is established for the chosen search space.

</details>


### [293] [OptScientist: Automated Discovery of Optimization Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28562)
*Ma ZhenYao,Yue Liang,Jian-Qiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OptScientist 框架，从数据中直接发现紧凑的符号化优化法则（而非仅拟合输入-输出方程），在自建 OptFeynman 基准上恢复超80%问题真值，并在人类风险选择与央行货币政策等真实场景取得与强基线相当的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学现象本质上是某个底层优化问题的结果，但现有自动化科学发现工作主要集中于恢复描述观测输入-输出关系的方程，忽略了"生成现象的优化法则"这一更本质的表示形式，缺乏相应方法与基准。

Method: OptScientist 由两部分构成：(i) 一个模型，先将数据表示为一个优化问题，再从中抽取其符号形式，从而推断符号化优化问题；(ii) 一个 LLM 智能体系统，将上述模型嵌入发现流程，整合来自科学文献的理论先验、发现候选法则、抽象其底层机制，并建议采集新数据。同时构建 OptFeynman 基准，包含99个源自物理的优化问题。

Result: 在 OptFeynman 上，对超过80%的问题恢复出真值法则，且对含噪上下文信息保持鲁棒，显著优于机器学习、符号回归与基于LLM的模型发现基线；在人类风险选择和央行货币政策数据上，学到的紧凑符号优化法则在留出集预测性能上与领先基线相当，同时参数更少、消耗 token 显著少于 LLM 基线。

Conclusion: OptScientist 将自动化科学发现从"拟合方程"拓展到"揭示生成观测现象的优化法则"，为物理、生物、行为与社会科学中的自动化理论构建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Many scientific phenomena are best understood as outcomes of underlying optimization problems, yet automated scientific discovery has largely focused on recovering equations that describe observed input-output patterns. We introduce OptScientist, a novel automated framework for discovering compact symbolic optimization laws directly from data. OptScientist comprises (i) a model that infers symbolic optimization problems by first learning to represent data as an optimization problem and then extracting its symbolic form, and (ii) an LLM agentic system embedding the model in a discovery process that integrates theoretical priors from scientific literature, discovers candidate laws, abstracts their underlying mechanisms, and suggests new data to collect. We first evaluate OptScientist on OptFeynman, a novel benchmark of 99 physics-derived optimization problems, where OptScientist recovers ground-truth laws for over 80% of the problems and remains robust to noisy contextual information, while substantially outperforming machine-learning, symbolic, and LLM-based model-discovery baselines. On human risky choice and central-bank monetary policy, OptScientist discovers compact symbolic optimization laws that achieve held-out predictive performance competitive with leading baselines, while using few parameters and substantially fewer tokens than LLM baselines. Overall, OptScientist extends automated scientific discovery to uncovering optimization laws that generate observed phenomena, enabling automated theory building across the physical, biological, behavioral, and social sciences.

</details>


### [294] [Beyond the Sequentiality Bottleneck: A Hybrid Diffusion-Autoregressive Framework for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28584)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoRAL 混合框架，用扩散语言模型（DLM）作为并行规划器生成 K 条推理轨迹，再由大型自回归模型（ARM）执行器择优并合成最终答案，并通过奖励引导去噪（RDC）训练规划器，在视觉-语言任务上兼顾效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型存在"顺序性瓶颈"（串行生成导致推理效率低），而扩散语言模型虽支持并行解码但缺乏语义连贯性。作者希望同时缓解两类模型的固有缺陷。

Method: 1) 非对称规划器-执行器架构（APE）：由 DLM 规划器并行生成 K 条多样化推理轨迹，由大型 ARM 执行器从中选择最优规划并综合生成最终答案；2) 奖励引导去噪（RDC）：将基于最终结果（outcome-based）的奖励信号融入扩散过程，引导去噪轨迹朝向目标导向推理。

Result: 在视觉-语言基准测试上，CoRAL 显著优于既有基于扩散的方法，并达到与强 ARM 基线相当的性能，同时实现更优的效率与质量。

Conclusion: 将 DLM 的并行生成能力与 ARM 的语义连贯性相结合的非对称混合范式是可行且有效的；结果奖励可通过引导去噪有效注入扩散规划器，为混合推理框架提供训练范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive models (ARMs) face a "Sequentiality Bottleneck" in reasoning, while diffusion language models (DLMs) offer parallelism but lack semantic coherence. To addresses both limitations, we propose Collaborative Reasoning Asymmetric Learning (CoRAL), a hybrid framework via an Asymmetric Planner-Executor (APE) architecture: a DLM Planner generates K diverse reasoning trajectories in parallel, while a large ARM Executor selects the optimal plan and synthesizes the final answer. To train the Planner effectively, we introduce Reward-Guided Denoising for CoT (RDC), which integrates outcome-based rewards into the diffusion process to steer it toward goal-directed reasoning. Experiments on vision-language benchmarks show that CoRAL significantly outperforms prior diffusion-based methods and achieves competitive performance against strong ARMs, achieving both superior efficiency and quality.

</details>


### [295] [Slot-JEPA: Object-Structured Prediction Interfaces for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28074)
*Peisong Wen,Qianqian Xu,Minye Lei,Siran Dai,Yang Liu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Slot-JEPA，将稠密patch表示分解为稳定语义slot与随时间变化的空间mask，并配套Transition Memory Predictor与Unbalanced Optimal Transport监督，提升表示可迁移性与目标条件规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures）作为潜在世界模型，其稠密patch表示对下游预测器暴露了冗余接口，下游需隐式推断空间组织与时间对应，带来三个问题：(1) 物体信息分散于多个patch，下游预测器需重复恢复物体结构，无法跨任务复用；(2) 缺少跨时间显式实体对应，历史迁移代价高，限制利用上下文的灵活性；(3) 缺少显式输运结构，通常用逐点l1/l2距离，当预测与目标支撑集几乎不重叠时对空间位移不敏感。

Method: 提出Slot-based JEPA，将稠密表示分解为稳定的语义slot与随时间变化的空间mask，预训练一次后冻结下游，提供可复用的物体结构化接口。在此基础上构建Transition Memory Predictor (TMP)：以紧凑mask形式存储视觉转移，并利用对应slot的统计量检索有用上下文；其双分支分别建模slot特定动态与跨slot共享动态。此外采用Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT)监督mask预测，并通过在对齐的预测mask与目标mask间输运质量来评估候选方案，获得与空间进展更一致的信号。

Result: 在七个视觉理解基准上，slot重构特征保持可迁移性；Slot-JEPA在六个平面环境及REALM-JEPA基准的四个机械臂规划任务上改善了目标条件规划表现。

Conclusion: 将JEPA的稠密表示结构化为slot与mask接口，结合转移记忆预测与UOT监督，可同时提升表示复用性与目标条件规划效果。

Abstract: As a prototypical latent world model, Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provide strong semantic representations. However, dense patch representations expose a redundant interface to downstream predictors, which must implicitly infer spatial organization and temporal correspondence, presenting three challenges. First, object information is distributed across patches, forcing each downstream predictor to redundantly recover object structure that could otherwise be reused across tasks before modeling interactions. Second, the lack of explicit entity correspondence across time makes historical transitions expensive, restricting the predictor's flexibility in utilizing context. Third, without explicit transport structure, existing JEPA models typically use pointwise l1/l2 distances, which are insensitive to spatial displacement when predicted and goal supports are largely disjoint. We propose the Slot-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (Slot-JEPA), which decomposes dense representations into stable semantic slots and time-varying spatial masks. Pretrained once and frozen downstream, this decomposition exposes a reusable object-structured interface. Building on it, our Transition Memory Predictor (TMP) stores visual transitions as compact masks and retrieves useful context using statistics of corresponding slots. Its two branches separate slot-specific dynamics from shared cross-slot dynamics. We further use Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT) to supervise mask prediction and evaluate candidate plans by transporting mass between aligned predicted and goal masks, providing a signal better aligned with spatial progress. Across seven visual-understanding benchmarks, the slot-reconstructed features remain transferable. Slot-JEPA also improves goal-conditioned planning across six planar environments and four robotic arm planning tasks on the REALM-JEPA benchmark.

</details>
